### [75] [Calculation of neutron electric dipole moment from Lattice QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22587)
*Thomas Blum,Fangcheng He,Taku Izubuchi,Luchang Jin,Hiroshi Ohki,Sergey Syritsyn*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文通过在含有背景电场的格点（lattice）QCD上用非微扰方法结合Feynman-Hellmann定理和变分分析，首次确定了中子电偶极矩（nEDM）对θ项的敏感度，得到物理外推后的结果$d_n=-0.0050(4)(8)\barθ$ e·fm，并结合实验上限给出了强CP问题的约束$|\barθ|\lesssim 10^{-11}$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中子电偶极矩实验约束可能暗示QCD中的强CP问题（strong-CP problem），即QCD θ角小得不自然。作者旨在通过第一性原理的格点QCD计算，非微扰地确定nEDM对θ项的敏感度，以检验是否存在强CP问题。

Method: 利用含背景电场的格点上的非微扰QCD计算，并借助Feynman-Hellmann定理，从局域拓扑荷密度（topological charge density）在由电场空间极化的核子基态之间的矩阵元计算nEDM。这些核子基态具有混合空间宇称，通过变分分析（variational analysis）构造获得。

Result: 在2+1味动力学畴壁费米子（domain wall fermions）格点上，对应π介子质量为340、420、576 MeV、格距$a\approx0.11$ fm时获得统计显著信号；物理点外推后得到$d_n=-0.0050(4)(8)\barθ$ e·fm，与实验上限比较给出$|\barθ|\lesssim10^{-11}$的约束。

Conclusion: 该结果证实了QCD中强CP问题的存在，并表明中子电偶极矩可以通过局域拓扑荷密度在系统效应良好控制下可靠地确定，且该方法可直接推广到其他CP破坏相互作用。

Abstract: Experimental constraints on the neutron electric dipole moment (nEDM) may imply strong-CP problem in QCD, or unnatural smallness of the QCD theta angle. In this work, we present a novel determination of the neutron electric dipole moment (nEDM) $d_n$ sensitivity to theta term from nonperturbative QCD on a lattice with background electric field. Using Feynman-Hellmann theorem, we compute nEDM from the matrix element of local topological charge density between nucleon ground states spatially polarized by an electric field. These states have mixed spatial parity, and we construct them using variational analysis. We obtain statistically significant signal for the theta induced nEDM from lattices with 2+1 dynamical domain wall fermions corresponding to pion masses of 340, 420, and 576 MeV and lattice spacing $a\approx 0.11~\text{fm}$. After extrapolating to the physical point, we obtain $d_n=-0.0050(4)(8)\barθ$ e$\cdot$fm. Comparison with the current experimental bound on nEDM implies constraint $|\barθ|\lesssim 10^{-11}$, which confirms existence of the strong-CP problem in QCD. Our pioneering work demonstrates that neutron EDM can be reliably determined from the local density of topological charge with robust control of systematic effects, and can be directly extended to other CP-violating interactions.

</details>

Conclusion: 与烧结Nd-Fe-B和Sm-Co磁体相比，Sm-Fe-N粘接磁体不仅能有效抑制涡流，还使紧密拼接、高密度充填的磁路布局成为可能，从而为MRI系统提供更均匀的静磁场，验证了其在低场永磁MRI中的应用价值。

Abstract: In low-field, lightweight, pole-pieceless permanent-magnet MRI systems built with sintered Nd-Fe-B or Sm-Co magnets, the rapid switching of gradient fields readily induces eddy currents in the sintered magnets, leading to image artifacts. To address this, we report for the first time a Sm-Fe-N permanent-magnet MRI system based on anisotropic Sm-Fe-N bonded magnets, whose high electrical resistivity reduces the eddy currents in the X, Y and Z directions to 0.093%, 0.172% and 2.38%, respectively, while a magnetic field inhomogeneity below 150 ppm is achieved at the boundary of a 220 mm diameter of spherical volume (DSV). Compared with sintered Nd-Fe-B and Sm-Co magnets, using Sm-Fe-N bonded magnets as the source of the static magnetic field not only suppresses eddy currents but also makes a closely tiled, densely packed magnetic-circuit layout feasible, providing a more uniform static magnetic field for the MRI system. Imaging results free of obvious geometric distortion and banding artifacts further indicate that the Sm-Fe-N magnet system delivers low eddy currents and high static magnetic field homogeneity.

### [77] [From Maxwell Fluid to Kelvin Voigt Solid: A Transient Network Model of Condensate Aging and Morphology Transition in Phase Separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23076)
*Bhanjan Debnath*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 本研究通过构建瞬态网络模型（transient-network model）和连续介质框架（continuum framework），将相分离（phase separation）、分子构象切换（molecular switching）与动态交联（dynamic cross-linking）耦合，揭示了生物分子凝聚体（biomolecular condensates）因生化环境改变而发生的物质性质与空间组织的转变机制。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物分子凝聚体可因生化环境变化从液态过渡为凝胶状或固态聚集体，但分子相互作用如何导致这种转变仍未被阐明，本文旨在解决这一核心未解问题。

Method: 将生化环境表示为三相混合物（triphasic mixture），包括液态蛋白质富集相、网状蛋白质富集相和溶剂；蛋白质分子可在两种构象状态间可逆切换，切换后暴露交联域并促进瞬态网络形成。作者建立了耦合相分离、分子切换和动态交联的瞬态网络模型与连续介质框架。

Result: 瞬态网络模型预测非老化（non-aging）网络表现为Maxwell流体特性；缓慢老化网络表现出Maxwell样行为及依赖等待时间的松弛；深度老化网络则表现出类似Kelvin-Voigt固体的弹性回弹特征。耦合模型表明分子切换与动态交联的相互作用决定了凝聚体相的空间组织。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一条机制性路径，说明了构象切换和分子交联如何调控凝聚体的材料性质与形态结构。

Abstract: Biomolecular condensates can undergo striking changes, such as transitioning from a liquid-like to a gel- or a solid-like aggregate due to changes in molecular interactions in response to changes in the biochemical environment. The question of how modified molecular interactions lead to such a transition in the material properties and spatial organization of condensates has not yet been elucidated. To address this question, we represent the biochemical environment as a triphasic mixture comprising a liquid-like protein-rich phase, a network-like protein-rich phase, and solvent. Owing to a change in the biochemical environment, protein molecules can reversibly switch between two conformational states. In a switched conformational state, the cross-linking domains of molecules are exposed which promote transient network formation in phase separated states. We develop a transient-network model and a continuum framework that couples phase separation, molecular switching, and dynamic cross-linking to predict condensate morphology and mechanics. The transient-network model predicts that a non-aging network behaves like a Maxwell fluid. When a network slowly ages via stabilization of cross-links, it shows Maxwell-like behavior and waiting time-dependent relaxation. However, a strongly aged network shows elastic recoil like characteristic of a Kelvin-Voigt solid. Our coupled continuum model demonstrates that the interplay of molecular switching and dynamic cross-linking in network formation shapes the spatial organization of condensate phases. In summary, this work demonstrates a mechanistic route explaining how conformational switching and molecular cross-linking regulate material properties and morphology of condensates.

### [78] [Pair Filters in an Extended Hubbard Model at Resonance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21870)
*Ensuo Ma,Zhi Song*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本论文研究了共振条件下扩展哈伯德模型（extended Hubbard model）中束缚对（bound pairs）的动力学行为，发现相邻的doublon对可作为散射中心，实现单费米子与单态束缚对的完美分离：束缚对完全透射而单费米子被完全反射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究束缚对在强关联系统中的动力学性质，以及寻找一种能在动力学层面生成和操控束缚对的机制。

Method: 基于扩展哈伯德模型在共振条件下的解析分析，对比单费米子与单态束缚对的色散关系（dispersion relations）和散射行为，并辅以数值模拟验证。

Result: 单费米子和单态束缚对具有完全相同的色散关系，但两者在强相互作用区的散射行为截然不同：在遇到被动力学钉扎（dynamically pinned）的相邻doublon对时，束缚对发生完全透射，而单费米子被完全反射，数值模拟与解析预测完全一致。

Conclusion: 相邻doublon对可作为高效的过滤器（filter），在动力学上实现束缚对与单粒子的分离，为生成和操控束缚对态提供了一种新的动力学机制。

Abstract: We investigate the dynamics of bound pairs in an extended Hubbard model at resonance. We show that a single fermion and a singlet bound pair possess identical dispersion relations and scatter off each other. In contrast, a neighboring doublon pair is dynamically pinned in the strong-interaction regime. Remarkably, a single fermion and a singlet bound pair exhibit fundamentally different scattering behaviors when encountering a pinned doublon pair acting as a scattering center. While the singlet bound pair undergoes perfect transmission, the single fermion is completely reflected. These results demonstrate that a neighboring doublon pair functions as an efficient filter that separates bound pairs from single particles. Numerical simulations fully support the analytical predictions. Our findings provide a dynamical mechanism for generating and manipulating bound-pair states.

</details>

Conclusion: DyPtBi是一种新型反铁磁拓扑绝缘体候选材料，其拓扑电子态与多极自由度（multipolar degrees of freedom）相互纠缠，可通过单轴应力实现磁畴壁导电性的原位控制，为拓扑性质的可调控应用提供了有前景的途径。

Abstract: Antiferromagnetic topological insulators provide a fertile platform where symmetry-breaking magnetic order is intertwined with topological electronic states. In particular, magnetic domain walls have attracted much attention, as they can be easily controlled by external fields as in general magnets, and moreover, host nontrivial electronic states distinct from those in bulks and sample surfaces. Here, we report a new antiferromagnetic topological insulator candidate DyPtBi, which hosts conductive magnetic domain walls controllable by uniaxial stress and magnetic field. We find that the resistivity exhibits abrupt increase upon the magnetic and structural phase transition. Concomitantly, the transverse ultrasonic mode shows remarkable softening of 6 %, indicating that the Dy 4f ferroquadrupolar order plays a vital role in the phase transition. Furthermore, we reveal by neutron experiments that applying compressive uniaxial stress aligns the magnetic domain state, leading to the strong resistivity enhancement of 14 % while eliminating the conductive magnetic domain walls. These findings demonstrate that DyPtBi exhibits topological electronic states entangled with multipolar degrees of freedom, providing a promising route for in situ control of topological properties.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解并控制强自旋-轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）的铱氧化物中磁各向异性（magnetic anisotropy）的调控机制，特别是能否通过应变作为无电荷（charge-neutral）手段操纵磁矩方向。

Method: 采用第一性原理（first-principles）计算实现双轴压缩应变，并构建基于Wannier函数的无拟合参数磁性模型哈密顿量，以岭解压缩下磁各向异性的微观机制，同时考察Hund耦合J/U比值对各向异性的影响。

Conclusion: 压缩应变薄膜位于终止于零温度双临界点的变磁（metamagnetic）相边界上，提供了一种无电荷的磁矩方向调控手柄。

Abstract: The bilayer iridate Sr$_3$Ir$_2$O$_7$ is a $c$-axis collinear antiferromagnet, held there by a giant interlayer pseudodipolar anisotropy, whereas single-layer Sr$_2$IrO$_4$ cants in the $ab$ plane. Hund's exchange sets the scale of the anisotropy and, beyond $J/U\approx0.15$, removes the collinear state altogether. The flop is not a rigid rotation---the ordered moments of the two states cross at $\varepsilon_c$---and it carries a stark fingerprint, in that the giant easy-axis magnon gap collapses to a gapless, Goldstone-like spectrum. Compressively strained films thus sit on a metamagnetic phase boundary ending in a zero-temperature bicritical point, a charge-neutral handle on the moment direction.

</details>


### [81] [Anomalous dynamical energy flows via nonlinear phononics in spin-Peierls chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22517)
*Jakob Dolgner,Dirk Manske,James K. Freericks,Mohsen Yarmohammadi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用多层非线性声子结构（multi-tiered nonlinear phononics architecture）研究强二聚化自旋-佩尔斯（spin-Peierls）链中的非平衡自旋-声子动力学与能量级联，证明最大磁能量转移由动态阻抗匹配条件（dynamical impedance-matching condition）决定，并揭示连续波与脉冲驱动在反向作用（back-action）和量子干涉方面截然不同的效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文的动机在于突破线性选择定则（linear selection rules）的限制——该定则禁止直接激发拉曼活性（Raman-active）的二聚化模式——从而探索通过太赫兹（terahertz）激光驱动红外模式、进而非线性耦合到拉曼晶格位移以调控磁交换的途径，为动态控制磁序提供理论基础。

Method: 采用多层级非线性声子架构，使用太赫兹激光激发与拉曼位移非线性耦合的红外模式；在理论层面采用键算符形式（bond-operator formalism）结合Lindblad主方程的二级累积量展开（second-order cumulant expansion of the Lindblad master equation）描述系统动力学。

Result: 研究发现最大能量向磁自由度转移的速率由动态阻抗匹配条件主导，而非未受扰动的三激子态密度（unperturbed triplon density of states）。连续波（continuous-wave）驱动的持续能量输入会构建高激发密度，严重反向作用（back-action）于晶格，造成主声子过阻尼（overdamping）和功率展宽（power broadening）；相反，短脉冲的时间积分能量很小，响应保持在微扰范围内，并在反向作用累积前终止，从而保留尖锐的类法诺（Fano-like）量子干涉。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了在不猝灭驱动晶格模式的前提下控制动态磁状态的极限条件，为非线性声子驱动的磁调控策略提供了明确的设计边界。

Abstract: We investigate the nonequilibrium spin-phonon dynamics and energy cascades in a strongly dimerized spin-Peierls chain using a multi-tiered nonlinear phononics architecture. To bypass linear selection rules prohibiting the direct excitation of the Raman-active dimerization mode, a terahertz laser drives an infrared mode that nonlinearly couples to the Raman lattice displacement, subsequently modulating the magnetic exchange. Employing a bond-operator formalism with a second-order cumulant expansion of the Lindblad master equation, we show that maximum energy transfer into the magnetic sector is governed by a dynamical impedance-matching condition rather than the unperturbed triplon density of states. We find that the sustained energy input of continuous-wave driving builds high excitation densities that severely back-act on the lattice, overdamping the primary phonon and smearing magnetic features via power broadening. Conversely, the small time-integrated energy of a pulse keeps the response perturbative, terminating before back-action accumulates and preserving sharp Fano-like quantum interferences. These insights establish limits for controlling dynamic magnetic states without quenching the driving lattice modes.

</details>


### [82] [Role of $d$-electron density of states in the quantum size effect \newline of Pt-Ni and Pt-Pd nanoparticles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22743)
*S. Kitagawa,T. Ihara,Y. Kinoshita,K. Ishida,K. Kusada,H. Kitagawa*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过$^{195}$Pt核磁共振（nuclear magnetic resonance）测量，系统研究了双金属Pt$_{1-x}$Pd$_x$和Pt$_{1-x}$Ni$_x$纳米颗粒中的量子尺寸效应（quantum size effect, QSE），发现d电子态密度在QSE表现中起关键作用。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究发现Pt$_{1-x}$Cu$_x$纳米颗粒中量子尺寸效应被抑制，而实验目的旨在厘清不同d电子金属元素（Pd、Ni）对量子尺寸效应表现的影响，以及d电子在量子尺寸效应表现中的本质作用。

Result: Pt$_{1-x}$Pd$_x$纳米颗粒的$1/T_1T$异常可以通过量子尺寸效应解释；而Pt$_{1-x}$Ni$_x$纳米颗粒随Ni含量增加，态密度系统性增大，特征能量尺度$T^*$降低，与Kubo能隙（Kubo gap）δ$_{\mathrm{Kubo}}$一致，并表现出清晰量子能级离散特征。Korringa参数分析提示Ni浓度增加时铁磁关联增强，接近铁磁量子临界（ferromagnetic quantum critical）区间。

Conclusion: 与Pt$_{1-x}$Cu$_x$纳米颗粒中量子尺寸效应被抑制的现象相反，Pt$_{1-x}$Ni$_x$纳米颗粒表现出明显的量子能级离散化，说明d电子态密度在由金属d电子原子构成的纳米颗粒的量子尺寸效应表现中起决定性作用。

Abstract: We investigated the quantum size effect (QSE) in bimetallic Pt$_{1-x}$Pd$_x$ and Pt$_{1-x}$Ni$_x$ nanoparticles, using $^{195}$Pt nuclear magnetic resonance measurements. The temperature and size dependencies of the anomaly in the nuclear spin-lattice relaxation rate divided by temperature $1/T_1T$ in the Pt$_{1-x}$Pd$_x$ nanoparticles suggest similar electron states between Pt and Pd atoms and are well understood by the QSE. The temperature and composition variations of $1/T_1T$ and Knight shift reveal a systematic increase in the density of states and reduction of the characteristic energy scale $T^*$ with increasing Ni content, consistent with the Kubo gap $δ_{\mathrm{Kubo}}$. Furthermore, analysis of the modified Korringa parameter $K(α)$ suggests enhanced ferromagnetic correlations with increasing Ni concentration, approaching a ferromagnetic quantum critical regime. These results provide experimental evidence that $d$-electron density of states plays a crucial role in the manifestation of the QSE in the nanoparticles formed by the metallic $d$-electron atoms.

</details>


### [83] [Interplay of Magnetic Order, Structural Stability, and Orbital Ordering in BaFe2X3 (X = S and Se)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23081)
*Kunihiko Yamauchi,Takuya Aoyama,Kenya Ohgushi*

  <summary>Details</summary>

Both compounds also show resistivity anomalies above the Neel temperature, suggesting that orbital degrees of freedom play an important role in their electronic properties. To clarify the interplay among magnetic order, structural stability, orbital ordering, and transport properties, we performed resistivity measurements and first-principles calculations for BaFe2S3 and BaFe2Se3, systematically comparing candidate magnetic and crystal structures. We find that the magnetic configuration strongly influences the stable crystal structure, orbital ordering, and transport anisotropy, providing a microscopic understanding of the contrasting electronic properties of the two compounds.

### [84] [Quantics tensor cross interpolation for high-order strong-coupling expansions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23160)

TL;DR: 本文证明使用量子张量交叉插值（quantics tensor cross interpolation）的变量分离框架可以显著降低自洽强耦合展开（self-consistent strong-coupling expansion）高阶计算所需的键维数（bond dimension），从而将稳态动力学平均场理论（dynamical mean-field theory, DMFT）计算推进到第四阶，并在自由极限下计算至第六阶以分析渐近区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶强耦合展开中，图拓扑数量随展开阶数呈阶乘增长，且自能和格林函数（Green's function）的评估需要越来越高的维数积分，导致计算代价高昂，阻碍了超越最低阶非交叉近似（non-crossing approximation）的实际应用。

Method: 采用量子张量交叉插值（quantics tensor cross interpolation）于变量分离框架中，替代此前使用的尺度分离（scale-separated）方法，以缓解高阶自能评估中的高维积分问题，并将计算扩展至稳态DMFT第四阶以及自由极限下的第六阶。

Result: 与尺度分离方案相比，新方法显著降低了键维数；成功实现了第四阶稳态DMFT自洽计算，并在自由极限下计算至第六阶，观察到强耦合展开渐近区域（asymptotic regime）的开始，从而可应用外推技术。

Conclusion: 量子张量交叉插值的变量分离框架有效克服了高阶强耦合展开的高维积分瓶颈，为平衡及非平衡系统的输运和谱函数研究提供了更高效的计算途径。

Abstract: Real-time impurity solvers enable the study of transport phenomena and the description of nonequilibrium lattice systems within the framework of dynamical mean-field theory (DMFT). They also provide direct access to the spectral functions of both equilibrium and nonequilibrium systems. A widely used approach is the self-consistent strong-coupling expansion, whose lowest-order implementation corresponds to the non-crossing approximation. Higher-order implementations, however, are computationally demanding because the number of diagram topologies grows factorially with expansion order, while the evaluation of self-energies and Green's functions requires increasingly high-dimensional integrations. Here, we demonstrate that the latter challenge can be mitigated by employing quantics tensor cross interpolation in a variable-separated framework. Compared with the previously used scale-separated approach, the new scheme yields substantially lower bond dimensions and capacitates self-consistent steady-state DMFT calculations up to fourth order. We illustrate its performance with representative results for both equilibrium and photo-doped systems. In addition, we analyze the convergence of the strong-coupling expansion in the challenging noninteracting limit by computing diagrams up to sixth order. At this order, the onset of the asymptotic regime of the strong-coupling expansion becomes apparent, which allows the application of extrapolation techniques.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金属环境通常被认为会通过强介电屏蔽抑制激子，但在金属与绝缘区域共存的复合体系中，其影响可能不同。本文旨在探究逾渗驱动的金属-绝缘体相变附近激子态的行为，以理解金属纳米尺度邻近效应对激子相互作用的影响。

Result: CuCrO2中激子结合能约为489 meV；在Pd替代接近x=0.5时系统发生绝缘体-金属转变，符合三角晶格（triangular lattice）的位点逾渗阈值（site-percolation threshold）。在逾渗前区域内，激子共振红移241 meV，而Tanguy连续谱起点几乎不变，表明激子结合能显著增大。镜像电荷模型显示，孤立的金属区域可通过镜像电荷相互作用增强电子-空穴束缚，而连续金属网络一旦形成则恢复常规屏蔽。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了逾渗驱动的金属-绝缘体相变附近金属与激子的反常共存现象，提示纳米尺度金属邻近效应（nanoscale metallic proximity）可能成为调控激子相互作用的新途径；但模型仍不能排除金属与激子响应源于电子学上不同态并独立演化的替代解释。

Abstract: Metallic environments are generally expected to suppress excitons through strong dielectric screening, yet their influence can differ in composite systems where metallic and insulating regions coexist. Here, we investigate excitonic states in PdxCu1-xCrO2 thin films across a percolation-driven metal-insulator transition. Optical spectroscopy and many-body GW calculations show that CuCrO2 hosts strongly bound excitons with a binding energy of about 489 meV. With increasing Pd substitution, the system approaches an insulator-to-metal transition near x = 0.5, consistent with the site-percolation threshold of a triangular lattice. In the pre-percolation regime, the excitonic resonance redshifts by 241 meV while the Tanguy continuum onset remains nearly unchanged, consistent with a substantial increase in exciton binding energy before metallization. An image-charge-based excitonic hydrogen model shows that isolated metallic regions can enhance electron-hole binding through image-charge interactions, whereas conventional screening is recovered once a continuous metallic network forms. Although this model provides a possible interpretation of the observed excitonic evolution, an alternative scenario in which metallic and excitonic responses originate from electronically distinct states and evolve independently cannot be excluded. These results reveal unusual metallic-excitonic coexistence near a percolation-driven metal-insulator transition and suggest nanoscale metallic proximity as a possible route for modifying excitonic interactions.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解电子-声子耦合（electron-phonon coupling）对二维凝聚态系统中动力学性质的定量影响，特别是在不同物理参数（声子能量、耦合强度、载流子浓度和温度）条件下系统的相变与输运行为，是凝聚态物理中具有基础意义的问题。

Method: 采用数值精确（numerically exact）的行列式量子蒙特卡洛模拟方法对二维正方晶格Holstein哈密顿量进行计算；通过系统扫描声子能量、电子-声子耦合强度、载流子浓度和温度等参数，计算单粒子谱函数与光导率，并辅以等时关联测量（equal-time correlation measurements）及由时间位移关联测量重构的代理量（proxy quantities）对结果进行交叉验证。

Result: 研究表明，随着载流子浓度从稀薄值调节至半填充（half-filling），系统经历了从披覆金属相到（双）极化子液/绝缘体，再至电荷密度波（charge-density-wave）绝缘体的演化过程；相关等时关联测量与时间位移关联重构的代理量均一致支持该相图示。

Conclusion: 该研究系统刻画了二维Holstein模型中电子-声子耦合与载流子浓度驱动的多相演化图景，并公开了涵盖超过16,640组独特参数值的大规模开放数据集，为后续研究与社区验证提供了宝贵资源。

Abstract: We study the dynamical properties of the two-dimensional square lattice Holstein Hamiltonian using numerically exact determinant quantum Monte Carlo simulations. In particular, we report systematic calculations of the model's single-particle spectral function and optical conductivity over a range of phonon energies, electron-phonon coupling strengths, carrier concentrations, and temperatures. In doing so, we map the evolution of the system from a dressed metallic phase to a (bi)polaron liquid/insulator to a charge-density-wave insulator as the carrier concentration is tuned from dilute values to half-filling. We corroborate these results using several equal-time correlation measurements and related proxy quantities reconstructed from time-displaced correlation measurements. This paper is also accompanied by an extensive open data set, covering over 16,640 unique parameter values.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究交变磁性（altermagnetism）与拓扑性（topology）在强关联体系中的共存机制，以及这种共存如何产生非平凡拓扑相，特别是量子自旋霍尔效应和二阶拓扑相。

Method: 采用无约束Hartree-Fock理论（unrestricted Hartree-Fock theory）计算交变磁序和拓扑序参数，并通过精确对角化（exact diagonalization）进行交叉验证；分析Lieb晶格上扩展Hubbard模型在电子填充2和4时的基态性质。

Result: 在相图的大片区域中，交变磁序与拓扑序共存，并伴随量子自旋霍尔效应；反转对称（inversion symmetry）破缺项可介导具有角模的二阶拓扑相。

Conclusion: Lieb晶格上的相互作用可以同时驱动交变磁有序和拓扑非平凡性，为在单一体系中实现磁序与拓扑的协同效应提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We study an extended Hubbard model on Lieb lattice at electron filling 2 and 4, and demonstrate that the interactions lead to the simultaneous emergence of altermagnetic order and a topological gap. Using unrestricted Hartree-Fock theory, we evaluate both the altermagnetic and topological order parameters and show that they co-exist with an associated quantum spin Hall effect in a large region of the phase diagram, which we further support using exact diagonalization. Moreover, we demonstrate that inversion-symmetry breaking terms can also mediate second-order topological phases with associated corner modes.

</details>

Method: 采用动量分辨电子能量损失谱（momentum-resolved electron energy-loss spectroscopy, M-EELS）结合单晶X射线衍射和弹性M-EELS，在近公度到公度CDW相变区间系统测量了1T-TaS₂的低能电荷激发谱。

Abstract: We use momentum-resolved electron energy-loss spectroscopy (M-EELS) to study the low-energy charge excitations of 1$T$-TaS$_2$ across the nearly commensurate-to-commensurate charge-density-wave (CDW) transition. Single-crystal x-ray diffraction and elastic M-EELS measurements confirm the expected rotation of the CDW wave vector upon entering the commensurate phase. In the nearly commensurate phase, the low-energy M-EELS spectra reveal an acoustic phonon branch and two optical phonon features whose energies and dispersions are broadly consistent with previous calculations and inelastic x-ray measurements. Across the transition, the optical phonon energies remain nearly unchanged, while their spectral intensity develops a pronounced temperature dependence near the CDW ordering wave vector. At higher energies, the finite-momentum charge response undergoes a substantial redistribution of spectral weight below the transition, consistent with the opening of an energy gap. These results demonstrate that M-EELS provides simultaneous access to lattice dynamics and finite-momentum valence band charge excitations in 1$T$-TaS$_2$, revealing their evolution across the commensurate CDW transition.

</details>


### [89] [Flux-induced Aharonov-Bohm Oscillation in the Tunneling Spectroscopy of Kitaev Spin Liquids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23561)
*Wen-Han Kao,Elio J. König*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文证明在Kitaev自旋液体中，孤立的π通量会在电中性马约拉纳连续谱上留下类似阿哈罗诺夫-博姆（Aharonov-Bohm）效应的本征干涉图案，并表明利用扫描隧道显微镜结合非弹性电子隧穿谱（STM-IETS）可在局域动态自旋响应中直接探测该振荡，从而无需操控单个任意子即可诊断费米子-通量互统计。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别涌现的通量激发（flux excitations）及其与巡游马约拉纳费米子（itinerant Majorana fermions）之间互统计（mutual statistics）的签名，是Kitaev自旋液体（Kitaev spin liquids）研究中的核心挑战。

Method: 采用大尺度精确对角化（exact diagonalization）求解Kitaev蜂窝模型，并辅以互补的低能连续介质理论（low-energy continuum theory），结合空间分辨的STM-IETS技术分析局域动态自旋响应的振荡行为。

Result: 发现隔离的π通量在电荷中性的马约拉纳连续谱上产生本征的类Aharonov-Bohm干涉图案；局域动态自旋响应随偏压和针尖-通量间距呈现径向振荡，反映了马约拉纳轨迹环绕通量时累积的π相位；精确对角化与连续介质理论结果在振荡行为上一致。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一种有限能量、空间分辨的隧穿签名来探测预先存在的通量，并作为费米子-通量互统计的诊断手段，无需操控Kitaev自旋液体中的单个任意子（anyon）。

Abstract: Identifying emergent flux excitations and signatures of their mutual statistics with itinerant Majorana fermions remains a central challenge in Kitaev spin liquids. We show that an isolated $π$ flux imprints an intrinsic Aharonov-Bohm-like interference pattern on the charge-neutral Majorana continuum. Spatially resolved scanning tunneling microscopy with inelastic electron tunneling spectroscopy (STM-IETS) probes this oscillation in the local dynamical spin response as a function of both bias voltage and tip-flux separation, reflecting the $π$ phase acquired by Majorana trajectories encircling the flux. We show that large-scale exact diagonalization of the Kitaev honeycomb model and a complementary low-energy continuum theory yield consistent radial oscillations. These results provide a finite-energy, spatially resolved tunneling signature of a preexisting flux and a diagnostic of fermion-flux mutual statistics without requiring manipulation of individual anyons in the Kitaev spin liquid.

</details>

### [90] [Importance-Reweighted Fock-Space Variational Monte Carlo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22925)
*Zheng Che*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出重要性重加权Fock空间变分蒙特卡洛（IR-FS-VMC）方法，通过辅助分布、哈密顿量引导的提议分布和自归一化重要性重加权，在不改变变分目标的前提下重新设计蒙特卡洛评估过程，显著提升了对电子结构哈密顿量Fock空间变分蒙特卡洛估计的稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于从头算电子哈密顿量（ab initio electronic Hamiltonians），Born分布在系统间差异显著，即使马尔可夫链混合良好也可能产生高方差估计量，导致Fock空间变分蒙特卡洛（FS-VMC）方法在实际电子结构计算中缺乏稳健性。

Method: IR-FS-VMC保持变分目标不变，但重新设计其蒙特卡洛评估：引入辅助分布和哈密顿量引导（Hamiltonian-guided）的提议分布（proposal distribution）定义马尔可夫链，同时用评估马尔可夫核（evaluation Markov kernel）定义可解析处理的评估分布，再通过自归一化重要性重加权（self-normalized importance reweighting）恢复能量、梯度和随机重构（stochastic reconfiguration, SR）矩阵中的Born分布期望。

Result: 在平衡态H2O和Fe2S2上的对照实验表明，该方法能将马尔可夫链接受率、局域能量涨落与优化行为分离开来。在单一生产协议下，H2O解离、36位点氢晶格、Fe2S2和Fe4S4活性空间等算例在不同Born分布和电子关联区间中均获得准确的变分能量。

Conclusion: 研究表明，IR-FS-VMC生产协议能够在所研究的电子结构区间内支持稳健的FS-VMC优化，为该类方法在电子结构计算中的实际应用提供了可靠基础。

Abstract: Fock-space variational Monte Carlo (FS-VMC) evaluates variational quantities by stochastic sampling over discrete many-body configurations. For ab initio electronic Hamiltonians, Born distributions can differ markedly between systems, and a Markov chain that mixes well need not yield low-variance estimators. We introduce importance-reweighted FS-VMC (IR-FS-VMC), which leaves the variational objective unchanged while redesigning its Monte Carlo evaluation. An auxiliary distribution and Hamiltonian-guided proposal define the Markov chain, while an evaluation Markov kernel defines an analytically tractable evaluation distribution. Self-normalized importance reweighting then recovers the Born-distribution expectations entering the energy, gradient, and stochastic reconfiguration (SR) matrix. Controlled comparisons on equilibrium H2O and Fe2S2 separate Markov-chain acceptance from local-energy fluctuations and optimization behavior. Using one production protocol, calculations for H2O dissociation, 36-site hydrogen lattices, and Fe2S2 and Fe4S4 active spaces yield accurate variational energies across different Born distributions and electronic-correlation regimes. Together, these results show that the production protocol can support robust FS-VMC optimization across the electronic-structure regimes studied here.

</details>


### [91] [Generalizing Abell-Tersoff bond-order potential with explicit high-order many-body correlations for robust extrapolation of potential energy surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22933)
*Ikuma Kohata*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于广义Abell–Tersoff键序势（bond-order potential）的半参数（semiparametric）机器学习原子间势（interatomic potential），通过在函数形式中引入物理启发的约束和显式高阶多体关联，在保持与传统MLIP相当的插值精度的同时，显著提升了向未见构型（包括高压高温条件）的外推能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习原子间势能实现准确高效的原子尺度模拟，但其在远超出训练域（out-of-distribution）的构型上的可靠性仍是重大挑战。现有纯数据驱动模型在训练域之外的外推表现往往不可预测。

Method: 方法是对Abell–Tersoff键序势进行推广，构造一种半参数原子间势，其函数形式包含化学信息（chemically informed）的显式高阶多体关联（high-order many-body correlations）。模型在多个数据集上进行训练与评估，包括硅、碳、水及小分子体系。

Result: 模型在插值精度上与现有MLIP模型相当，但在外推到未见构型方面表现更优，特别是高压（high-pressure）和高温（high-temperature）条件下的构型。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，在函数形式中融入物理启发的约束（physically motivated constraints）能够改善原子间势的外推行为，为机器学习原子间势中可用于控制外推的归纳偏置（inductive biases）提供了见解。

Abstract: Machine-learning interatomic potentials enable accurate and efficient atomistic simulations, but their reliability for out-of-distribution configurations far beyond the training domain remains a significant challenge. Here, to tackle this challenge, we introduce a semiparametric interatomic potential based on a generalization of the Abell--Tersoff bond-order potential, with a chemically informed functional form and explicit high-order many-body correlations. The model is trained and evaluated on various datasets, including silicon, carbon, water, and small molecules, achieving interpolation accuracy comparable to that of existing MLIP models while demonstrating better extrapolation to unseen configurations, including those under high-pressure and high-temperature conditions. These results demonstrate that physically motivated constraints incorporated into the functional form can improve the extrapolation behavior of interatomic potentials, providing insights into the inductive biases that can be used to control extrapolation in machine-learning interatomic potentials.

</details>


### [92] [Environmental Control Extends Beyond Quantum Dephasing in Exciton Energy Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23423)
*Junhua Zhou,Tianrui Chen,Dehao Yuan,Enhu He,Vandana Tiwari,Maxim Gelin,Francoise Remacle,R. J. Dwayne Miller,Fulu Zheng,Ajay Jha,Hong-Guang Duan*

TL;DR: 本研究利用温度依赖二维电子光谱（temperature-dependent 2DES）在10–296 K范围内研究了光合天线蛋白别藻蓝蛋白（allophycocyanin, APC）中的能量转移，发现主导的β→α转移步骤呈现非单调温度依赖：转移时间在30–40 K附近出现最小值（约200 fs）。通过层次运动方程（hierarchical equations of motion）建模，仅当环境光谱密度（spectral density）低频部分随温度发生强非谐演化、而高频部分基本不变时，才能定量复现实验观测，表明输运效率由环境光谱权重的频率分布而非整体波动幅度决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光合生物中激发能转移通常被描述为非相干的、热辅助的跳跃过程，但当电子耦合、振动相互作用和环境涨落处于可比能量尺度时，这种描述不再充分，因此理解环境如何控制输运仍然是一个基本挑战。

Method: 采用温度依赖的二维电子光谱（2DES）在10–296 K范围内测量APC蛋白中的能量转移动力学，并将APC建模为振动耦合的激子二聚体（vibronically coupled excitonic dimer），利用层次运动方程（HEOM）求解其在结构化环境中的动力学演化。

Result: 实验观察到β→α转移时间呈现非单调温度依赖：从10 K时的400 fs缩短至30–40 K附近的约200 fs，随后在296 K回升至400 fs；相比之下，均匀光学退相时间（homogeneous dephasing time）在相同温度范围内单调减小。常规固定浴模型（如Drude-Lorentz和分子间模式谱密度）无法复现该转折，唯有允许环境光谱密度低频部分随温度强非谐演化、高频浴不变时才能定量吻合。

Conclusion: 输运效率并非仅由环境涨落的幅度决定，而是由环境光谱权重在频率空间的分布所控制，这为复杂量子环境中分子输运理论提供了新的实验约束。

Abstract: Excitation-energy transfer underpins the conversion of light into usable energy in photosynthetic organisms and serves as a paradigm for evolutionary optimized transport in open quantum systems. Although this process is often described as incoherent thermally assisted hopping, such descriptions become inadequate when electronic coupling, vibronic interactions and environmental fluctuations occur on comparable energy scales. Determining how the environment controls transport therefore remains a fundamental challenge. Here, we use temperature-dependent 2DES to investigate energy transfer in the photosynthetic antenna protein allophycocyanin over the range 10 - 296 K. The dominant $β\rightarrow α$ transfer step exhibits a pronounced non-monotonic temperature dependence: the transfer time decreases from 400 fs at 10 K to 200 fs near 30- 40 K before increasing again to 400 fs at 296 K. In contrast, the homogeneous optical dephasing time decreases monotonically across the same temperature range. To interpret these observations, we model APC as a vibronically coupled excitonic dimer interacting with a structured environment and solve the dynamics using hierarchical equations of motion. Conventional fixed-bath models, including Drude-Lorentz and explicit intermolecular-mode spectral densities, fail to reproduce the observed turnover. Quantitative agreement is obtained only when the low-frequency sector of the environmental spectral density is allowed to anharmonically evolve strongly with temperature, while the high-frequency bath remains essentially unchanged. More broadly, these findings demonstrate that transport efficiency is controlled not simply by the magnitude of environmental fluctuations, but by the distribution of environmental spectral weight across frequency space, providing new experimental constraints on theories of molecular transport in complex quantum environments.

</details>

### [93] [NestyNet. IV. Laws Chosen by Nothing in Advance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21491)
*Rodrigo Ibata,Wassim Tenachi,Foivos Diakogiannis,Neil Ibata,Anirudh Shankar*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出NestyNet-DE框架，通过两种互补的微分方程（differential equation, DE）发现策略（稀疏库法和算子因子分解法），对强非线性耦合系统进行方程发现，并能从同一数据中恢复方程的Lie点对称几何。该框架在小行星历数据上恢复了约化开普勒层级，并在57个实值ODE及26个复值系统（含薛定谔方程、狄拉克方程和麦克斯韦方程组）上完成基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微分方程发现（DE discovery）在物理学的许多关键场景中会失效，因为物理系统往往包含耦合场、对状态非线性的控制律，且导数需要在场、通道和微分阶数之间保持一致。现有方法多受限于固定库（fixed library）的搜索空间，无法发现库中未预见的组合式定律。

Method: NestyNet-DE采用两种互补的DE搜索策略，均利用分段神经代理（segmented neural surrogates）的解析导数：（1）稀疏库路径，对外层系数为线性；（2）新的算子因子分解路径，直接搜索方程结构而非固定库，可恢复稀疏库方法遗漏的组合式（compositional）定律。框架支持多数据集共享支撑发现、复数和向量定律、从相空间轨迹进行哈密顿量发现，并能从同一数据中提取恢复方程的几何属性——即Lie点对称（Lie point symmetries）。

Result: 该框架应用于308颗主带小行星30年每日星历数据，成功恢复了约化开普勒层级（面积律、平方反比力和约化哈密顿量），其中发现的旋转对称性固定了离心力系数而非通过拟合获得。基准测试涵盖57个实值常微分方程（ODE）和26个复值系统，包括薛定谔方程（Schrödinger equation）、狄拉克方程（Dirac equation）以及麦克斯韦方程组（Maxwell's equations）作为耦合向量偏微分方程（PDE）的案例研究。

Conclusion: 本文的发现既不受固定库的限制，也不止步于方程本身。NestyNet-DE能够组合出任何字典都未曾预见的定律，并实现从数据到无预设的定律再到解释及整合该定律的几何结构的完整闭环。

Abstract: Differential-equation (DE) discovery tends to break down precisely where much of physics begins. Fields are coupled, governing laws are nonlinear in the state, amplitudes, coordinates, or operators of interest, yet derivatives must remain consistent across fields, channels, and differentiation orders. NestyNet-DE addresses this via two complementary DE search strategies, both employing analytic derivatives from segmented neural surrogates: a sparse-library route linear in the outer coefficients, and a new operator-factorized route that searches directly over equation structure rather than a fixed library, recovering compositional laws the sparse route misses. The recovered laws can be strongly nonlinear in states, fields, coordinates, and their couplings. The framework also handles multi-dataset shared-support discovery, complex and vector laws, and Hamiltonian discovery from phase-space trajectories. Beyond a law's form, the same data yield its geometry, the Lie point symmetries of the recovered equation. We apply the pipeline to 30 years of daily ephemerides of $308$ main-belt asteroids and recover the reduced-Kepler hierarchy (areal law, inverse-square force, and reduced Hamiltonian), where a discovered rotational symmetry fixes the centrifugal coefficient rather than fitting it. We also present a benchmark of 57 real-valued ODEs and 26 complex-valued systems, including the Schrödinger and Dirac equations, with Maxwell's equations as a coupled vector-PDE case study. Here, discovery is not shackled to a fixed library, nor does it end at the equation. It composes laws that no dictionary anticipated, and closes the loop from data, to a law chosen by nothing in advance, to the geometry that explains and integrates it.

</details>


### [94] [Variational Bayesian Inference for the Spectral Structure of LISA Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22245)
*Jianan Liu,Avi Vajpeyi,Renate Meyer,Jeung Eun Lee,Patricio Maturana-Russel*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出一种专门化的平均场随机梯度变分贝叶斯（mean-field SGVB）程序，用于长时程多元谱密度估计的快速后验近似，并将其应用于未来空间引力波探测器（如LISA）的噪声谱密度矩阵估计，在显著降低计算成本的同时获得了与哈密顿蒙特卡洛（Hamiltonian Monte Carlo, HMC）一致的估计结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来空间引力波探测器（如LISA）的谱密度矩阵估计面临数据时长长、时延干涉（time-delay interferometry, TDI）通道间仪器噪声相关的双重挑战，传统贝叶斯方法计算成本高昂，亟需可扩展的高效后验近似方法。

Method: 基于分块Whittle似然（blocked Whittle likelihood）方法的已有特征基（eigenbasis）表示，该方法使用Cholesky分解表示逆谱密度矩阵，并用余弦基函数（cosine basis functions）建模频率相关的矩阵元素，对基系数赋予带折扣的正则化马蹄先验（discounted regularized horseshoe prior）。随后采用平均场随机梯度变分贝叶斯（mean-field SGVB）进行后验近似，并与针对同一后验模型的哈密顿蒙特卡洛方法进行对比评估。

Result: 在自回归（autoregressive）和滑动平均（moving average）过程的模拟研究中，SGVB得到的后验中位数谱估计与HMC结果接近且计算成本显著更低。将方法应用于两个一年的、固定延迟、平稳、纯噪声LISA模拟数据后，SGVB谱密度估计在整个LISA分析频带内与HMC及Welch估计一致，同时所需计算量大幅减少。

Conclusion: 结果表明SGVB提供了一种可扩展的贝叶斯方法，适用于长时程多元LISA噪声分析中的谱密度估计，为未来空间引力波探测数据分析提供了高效的计算工具。

Abstract: Estimating spectral density matrices for future space-based gravitational-wave detectors such as LISA is challenging due to the long duration of the data and the correlated instrumental noise across multiple time-delay interferometry channels. In this work, we investigate a specialized mean-field stochastic gradient variational Bayes (SGVB) procedure for fast posterior approximation in long-duration multivariate spectral density estimation. Based on the existing eigenbasis representation of the blocked Whittle likelihood approach, the posterior model applies a Cholesky factorization to represent the inverse spectral density matrix and models the resulting frequency-dependent entries with cosine basis functions, with a discounted regularized horseshoe prior assigned to the basis coefficients. We test mean-field SGVB as a stand-alone posterior approximation by comparing it with Hamiltonian Monte Carlo (HMC) targeting the same posterior model. In simulation studies based on autoregressive and moving average processes, SGVB produces posterior median spectral estimates close to those obtained from HMC at substantially lower computational cost. We then apply the method to two one-year, fixed-delay, stationary, noise-only LISA simulations and show that the SGVB spectral density estimates are consistent with HMC and Welch estimates across the LISA analysis band, while requiring substantially less computation. These results demonstrate that SGVB provides a scalable Bayesian approach to spectral density estimation for long-duration multivariate LISA noise analysis.

</details>

### [95] [How Much Geometry Does Newtonian Dynamics Fix?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21642)
*C S López-Monsalvo*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出从动力学描述出发重构牛顿力学的时空几何，不预先设定固定几何背景，而是通过引入传播子和双线性形式来构造时空。在该重构中，惯性原理作为定理而非独立公设出现，牛顿第二定律被表述为曲线上两个导数之间的相容性关系。作者推导出剩余挠率（torsion）为全反对称三形式，并提出将其收缩视为经典自旋（spin）的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的牛顿力学预设了固定几何的时空背景，惯性原理被视为独立公设而缺少几何层面的推导。作者试图从动力学描述本身出发重建时空几何，使惯性定律、牛顿第二定律等基本命题成为几何结构的推论而非假设，从而揭示经典力学更深层的几何基础。

Method: 作者提出一种重构方法：不预先指定时空几何，而是根据描述需求引入传播子（propagator）和双线性形式（bilinear form）。通过分析惯性运动的尺度不变性及其对传播子挠率的"盲目性"来导出几何约束；将牛顿第二定律视为沿曲线两个导数间的相容性条件；利用弧长参数化下的无力运动限制挠率为全反对称三形式；提出将挠率与速度的收缩作为经典自旋的猜想。

Result: 惯性原理在该框架中以定理形式导出，其几何先兆对应自由落体普适性（universality of free fall）在引力进入前的预兆。牛顿第二定律在任意挠率下成立，且力与质量乘以下降加速度之差为精确项。剩余挠率被限制为全反对称三形式，其与速度的收缩恒消去该速度，使自旋粒子力学的补充条件恒成立。自旋进动速率仅由加速度决定，不依赖任何耦合常数。所有数学结果均有证明，并建立了三个构成性假设和一个物理猜想。

Conclusion: 该重构显示牛顿力学的基本定律可来源于几何结构的相容性要求，而非独立公设。所提出的自旋赋值方案与相对论自旋流体力学（relativistic spin hydrodynamics）的代数结构一致，为已有文献中未见的新归属。该时空形式非退化的要求使其区别于牛顿-嘉当（Newton–Cartan）的伽利略时空，引力在该构造之外。

Abstract: Newtonian mechanics is usually written on a spacetime of fixed geometry. Here we build that geometry, introducing a propagator and a bilinear form only as the description demands. Within this reconstruction the Principle of Inertia emerges as a theorem rather than an independent postulate. Inertial motion is blind to the scale of the form and to the torsion the propagator defines, and that blindness is a geometric precursor of the \emph{universality of free fall}, before gravity enters. Newton's Second Law is a compatibility between two derivatives along a curve. The force exceeds the mass times the lowered acceleration by an exact term, and the law survives arbitrary torsion. Requiring force-free motion to be inertial along curves parametrised by arc length restricts the torsion to be totally antisymmetric. The remaining torsion is a three-form which we postulate the particle carries along its worldline. Its contraction with the velocity annihilates that velocity, so the supplementary condition of spinning-particle mechanics holds identically, and we propose that contraction as a classical spin. The algebra is familiar from relativistic spin hydrodynamics; the assignment, to the best of our knowledge, has not been proposed elsewhere. Since inertial motion resolves neither the mass nor the spin, the predictive content lies in the transport law we postulate, which turns the spin against a parallel-transported frame at a rate fixed by the acceleration alone and by no coupling constant. That rate becomes a laboratory number only once the arc-length parameter is identified with a clock, which we leave open. The dynamics asks non-degeneracy of the form, so this spacetime is not Newton--Cartan's Galilean one, and gravitation lies outside the construction. We prove every mathematical result, make three constitutive postulates and one physical conjecture.

</details>


### [96] [Finite-term recurrences in a generalized Bochner--Krall family](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21802)
*L. M. Anguas,D. Barrios Rolanía,B. Shapiro,M. Tater*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文完整分类了一类形如 \(T=z^j\partial_z^j+z^m\partial_z^\ell\) 的微分算子（其中 \(0\le m<\ell\)，\(1\le j<\ell\)），其首一特征多项式满足有限项递推关系。研究发现该递推存在当且仅当 \(j=1\) 且 \(k\mid\ell\)（其中 \(k=\ell-m\)），并在这种情况下以闭式给出所有递推系数，证明关联差分算子阶数为 \(\ell\)，同时提供显式分解以验证 Horozov–Shapiro–Tater 猜想中的 Type (2) 情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在分类具有有限项递推关系的微分算子族，并验证 Horozov–Shapiro–Tater 关于微分算子阶数与差分算子阶数相等的猜想 1.9 及算子类型的猜想 1.10。

Method: 方法包括：设定算子形式 \(T=z^j\partial_z^j+z^m\partial_z^\ell\) 及其参数条件；通过数学推导建立递推关系存在性的充要条件（\(j=1\) 且 \(k\mid\ell\)）；以闭式确定所有递推系数；通过显式因子分解证明算子的分类归属。

Result: 结果表明：递推关系存在当且仅当 \(j=1\) 且 \(k\mid\ell\)；在此条件下可求得所有递推系数的闭式表达，并证明关联差分算子阶数为 \(\ell\)，与微分算子阶数一致；每个可容许算子均属于 Horozov–Shapiro–Tater 猜想 1.10 中的 Type (2)，从而验证了猜想 1.9 的结论。

Conclusion: 本文完成了对该类微分算子的完整分类，给出了递推存在的精确条件，并证实了 Horozov–Shapiro–Tater 猜想在此条件下成立，即微分阶数与差分阶数相等且算子归属 Type (2)。

Abstract: We classify the differential operators \(T=z^j\partial_z^j+z^m\partial_z^\ell\), where \(0\le m<\ell\) and \(1\le j<\ell\), whose monic eigenpolynomials satisfy a finite-term recurrence relation. Writing \(k=\ell-m\), such a recurrence exists if and only if \(j=1\) and \(k\mid\ell\). In that case we determine all recurrence coefficients in closed form and prove that the associated difference operator has order \(\ell\). We also give an explicit factorization showing that every admissible operator is of Type~(2) in Conjecture~1.10 of Horozov--Shapiro--Tater; the equality of the differential and difference orders is the conclusion predicted by their Conjecture~1.9.

</details>


### [97] [TENSKEL: A Combinatorial Observable Tensor for Structured Measurement and Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21999)
*Yvan Richard*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出 TENSKEL，一种基于离散域张量表示的组合观测框架（combinatorial observable framework），旨在显式刻画测量过程对潜在配置（latent configurations）施加的组织结构，而非提出新的重建算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多成像问题需从部分可观测测量中重建潜在配置，然而测量过程所诱导的观测组织在现有重建算法中很少被显式表示。作者希望通过数学框架显式表达这种由测量过程产生的潜在配置组织形式。

Method: 从二元潜在系综出发，通过逐级聚合（aggregation）与折叠（folding）操作，构建将测量上下文耦合到潜在Pascal组织（latent Pascal organization）的张量层级。每个测量上下文诱导同一潜在系综的可观测划分，张量公式显式表达组合重数、壳结构、简并性和重建几何；通过张量核（tensor kernel）刻画潜在坐标间的相似性，并利用正则化反演（regularized inversion）从可观测测量中重建潜在表示。该二元构造可自然扩展到基于离散单纯形（discrete simplex）的多项式扩展。

Result: 该框架通过与Pascal元胞自动机（Pascal cellular automata）及结构化离散颜色映射（structured discrete color mappings）的联系，分别展示了压缩与多项式实现；结果表明 TENSKEL 为观测诱导的组织提供了组合基础，对计算成像、反问题、结构化传感与量子启发的测量公式具有潜在相关性。

Conclusion: TENSKEL 提供了一个不依赖新算法重建步骤的组合数学表示框架，用于理解潜在配置在观测下的组织方式，可作为推理观测诱导组织的通用组合基础。

Abstract: Many imaging problems seek to reconstruct underlying configurations from partial observable measurements. While reconstruction algorithms operate on these measurements, the observable organization induced by the measurement process is rarely represented explicitly.
  We introduce TENSKEL, a combinatorial observable framework for structured measurement and reconstruction based on tensor representations defined over discrete domains. Starting from a binary latent ensemble, the framework constructs a hierarchy of tensors coupling measurement contexts to a latent Pascal organization through successive aggregation and folding operations. Each measurement context induces an observable partition of the same latent ensemble, and the resulting tensor formulation makes explicit the associated combinatorial multiplicities, shell organization, degeneracies, and induced reconstruction geometry.
  Rather than introducing a new reconstruction algorithm, this framework provides a mathematical representation of how latent configurations become organized under observation. The induced tensor kernel characterizes similarities between latent coordinates through their measurement-context responses, while regularized inversion provides a structured reconstruction of the latent representation from observable measurements. The binary construction further admits a natural multinomial extension to discrete simplex-supported latent representations.
  Connections to Pascal cellular automata and structured discrete color mappings illustrate respectively compressed and multinomial realizations of the framework. More generally, TENSKEL provides a combinatorial basis for reasoning about the organization induced by observation, with potential relevance to computational imaging, inverse problems, structured sensing, and quantum-inspired measurement formulations.

</details>


### [98] [Interacting Lattice Bosons on the Concrete Buchholz Algebra -- All-Temperature Equilibrium States from Functional-Integral Local-Number Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22362)
*Yoshitsugu Sekine*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在Fock空间的具体Buchholz代数上建立了相互作用的Bose–Hubbard动力学。在排斥有限程相互作用和低活度间隙条件下，通过离散泛函积分在所有温度下获得体积一致的指数局部数界，这些界去除了局部矩假设，并导出了有限体积近似和KMS累积态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望在Bose–Hubbard模型（Bose–Hubbard model）的相互作用动力学研究中，消除对局部矩（local moments）假设的依赖，从而在更一般的条件下建立可靠的数学框架。

Method: 作者将相互作用Bose–Hubbard动力学具体化在Fock空间（Fock space）的Buchholz代数（Buchholz algebra）上，并在排斥有限程相互作用与低活度间隙（low-activity gap）条件下，构造了离散泛函积分（discrete functional integral）以获得体积一致的指数局部数界（volume-uniform exponential local-number bounds）。

Result: 所得局部数界在所有温度下均成立，它们移除了局部矩假设，并由此导出了有限体积近似（finite-volume approximation）以及arXiv:2505.13170中的KMS累积态（KMS accumulation states）。

Conclusion: 该工作为Bose–Hubbard模型在一般温度条件下提供了更普适的数学基础，无需额外假设即可建立有限体积近似与KMS态的收敛性。

Abstract: Interacting Bose--Hubbard dynamics is formulated on the concrete Buchholz algebra in Fock space. Under repulsive finite-range interactions and a low-activity gap, a discrete functional integral gives volume-uniform exponential local-number bounds at all temperatures. These bounds remove the local-moment hypothesis and yield finite-volume approximation and KMS accumulation states in arXiv:2505.13170.

</details>


### [99] [On the gauge invariant boundary condition in open XXZ chains: SoV bases, elementary blocks and overlaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22365)
*G. Niccoli,V. Terras*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究开放XXZ自旋链（open XXZ spin chain）在非平行边界场下的一种特殊规范不变边界条件：位置上1的K矩阵经由Vertex-IRF变换后为对角且不依赖于规范参数，而位置N的K矩阵任意。作者证明该模型可通过广义Sklyanin分离变量（SoV）方法在包含两个任意规范参数家族中求解，且SoV基仅依赖于参数差α-β；在未规范极限下可计算关联函数的基本构建块，并推导该模型本征态与另一共享位N边界条件的自旋链本征态之间的重叠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解具有一般边界条件下可积自旋链的可解性和关联函数结构是数学物理的重要课题。本文聚焦于XXZ自旋链中一种特殊但可解的规范不变边界条件，旨在探讨其在Vertex-IRF框架下的可解性以及关联函数与态重叠的可计算性。

Method: 作者在Vertex-IRF框架下利用广义Sklyanin分离变量方法（Separation of Variables, SoV）对传递矩阵进行对角化，构建依赖于两个任意规范参数α和β的SoV基族。通过分析这些基的依赖关系，证明其仅依赖于差α-β；随后考察α-β趋于无穷的未规范（ungauged）极限，并由此计算关联函数的基本构建块；最后利用SoV表示计算两个共享位N边界条件但位1边界条件不同的自旋链本征态之间的重叠。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) SoV基族实际仅依赖α与β之差α-β，而非各自独立；(2) 在α-β趋向无穷的未规范极限下，可显式计算关联函数的所有基本构建块；(3) 可计算本模型本征态与位1处为更一般（非规范不变）边界条件的另一自旋链本征态之间的重叠。

Conclusion: 该特殊规范不变边界条件下的开放XXZ自旋链在Vertex-IRF框架下完全可解，其SoV结构具备简化性质，且关联函数与态重叠均可基于此框架系统计算，为更一般边界条件情形提供了可推广的分析路径。

Abstract: We consider the open XXZ spin chain with unparallel boundary fields, in the special case in which the Vertex-IRF transform of the K-matrix labelling the boundary field at site 1 is diagonal and invariant under the value of the gauge parameters, the other K-matrix being arbitrary. We discuss here several properties related to this special gauge invariant boundary condition. Such a model can be solved in the Vertex-IRF framework with arbitrary gauge parameters: we can diagonalise the transfer matrix by means of generalised Sklyanin's Separation of Variable (SoV) approach in a family of SoV bases that depend on two arbitrary gauge parameters $α$ and $β$, $β$ being the so-called dynamical parameter and $α$ an arbitrary shift of the spectral parameter. We show here that such SoV bases depend actually on $α$ and $β$ through the difference $α-β$ only. Considering the ungauged limit in which $α-β$ tends to infinity, we show how to compute all elementary building blocks for the correlation functions of this model. We finally show how to compute overlaps between eigenstates of this model and eigenstates of a spin chain sharing the same boundary condition at site $N$ and in which the gauge invariant boundary condition at site 1 has been changed to a more general one.

</details>


### [100] [Quantitative Furstenberg Theory for Large Random Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22543)
*Reuben Drogin*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文对块Anderson模型（block Anderson model）中与GOE势块（GOE potential blocks）关联的2W×2W传递矩阵（transfer matrix）进行了经典Lyapunov指数（Lyapunov exponent）理论的定量化研究，证明了两项结果：顶端Lyapunov指数的定量极限定理，以及任意两个Lyapunov指数之间至少有c/W的间隙。作为推论，该一维块Anderson模型的局域化长度（localization length）至多为CW²。证明依赖于Furstenberg型公式和Malliavin微积分（Malliavin calculus）论证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Lyapunov指数理论为随机传递矩阵的指数增长提供了定性描述，但缺乏量化结果。作者旨在将该理论定量化，即给出Lyapunov指数的精确极限行为及指数之间的间隙下界，从而对局域化长度给出上界估计。

Method: 采用两种关键技术：其一，利用Furstenberg型公式来计算Lyapunov指数；其二，在辛群（symplectic group）中运用Malliavin微积分式的论证，证明足够多个传递矩阵的乘积具有足够光滑的密度。此过程的核心里程碑是对一个结构化随机矩阵建立最小奇异值（least singular value）估计。

Result: 证明了顶端Lyapunov指数的定量极限定理，并证明了任意两个Lyapunov指数之间的间隙至少为c/W；由此推论出一维块Anderson模型的局域化长度不超过CW²。

Conclusion: 通过定量化Lyapunov指数理论，获得了该块Anderson模型局域化长度的显式上界CW²，建立了随机传递矩阵乘积光滑密度与结构化随机矩阵最小奇异值估计之间的内在联系。

Abstract: We consider the $2W\times 2W$ transfer matrices associated to the block Anderson model with GOE potential blocks. We make the classical Lyapunov exponent theory quantitative in two ways. First, we prove a quantitative limit theorem for the top Lyapunov exponent. Second, we prove every gap between Lyapunov exponents is at least $c/W$. As a corollary, this implies the localization length of this $1$d block Anderson model is at most $CW^2$. The proof uses Furstenberg type formulas for the Lyapunov exponents, and Malliavin calculus style arguments in the symplectic group to show the product of sufficiently many transfer matrices has a sufficiently smooth density. The main technical input for the latter is a least singular value estimate for a structured random matrix.

</details>


### [101] [Fractality of the Schrödinger Density for Rough Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22547)
*Masoud Ataei*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了对任意有界变差且至少含一个跳跃点的实数据，环面上自由薛定谔演化（free Schrödinger evolution）的密度在几乎所有时刻都是分形的，其上盒维数（upper box dimension）恰好为3/2，并将临界Sobolev质量（critical Sobolev mass）的对数发散率以维纳跳跃统计量（Wiener's jump statistic）的闭式形式给出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Talbot效应（Talbot effect）的可观测量是光栅后的强度（密度），其分形性质此前仅对有理跳跃的阶梯数据（step data）成立，还需证明一般情况；此外，临界质量与色散关系的关联此前未被揭示。

Method: 利用秩演算与derangetropy算子（derangetropy operators）的秩演算（rank calculus）构建理论框架，通过维纳跳跃统计量刻画临界Sobolev质量的对数发散率，并分析密度沿时间迹线（time traces）及Airy流（Airy flow）下的行为差异。

Result: 任意有界变差且至少含一个跳跃点的实数据，其自由薛定谔演化密度在几乎所有时刻均为分形，上盒维数精确为3/2；临界Sobolev质量以维纳跳跃统计量决定的对数速率发散，该速率与跳跃位置和相位无关；时间迹线速率减半而Airy流加倍；在有理时间出现高斯和（Gauss-sum）回波，在几乎所有其他时刻呈现普适分形。

Conclusion: 该理论为每个概率分布赋予了一幅"量子地毯"（quantum carpet），并指出单一实测强度谱的谱增长率可返回光栅的跳跃含量，可直接在光学和物质波干涉测量中检验。

Abstract: The observable of the Talbot effect is the intensity behind the grating: the density of the evolving field, not the field itself. Its fractality was previously known only for step data with rational jumps. We prove the general case. For arbitrary real data of bounded variation with at least one jump, the density of the free Schrödinger evolution on the torus is fractal at almost every time, with graph of upper box dimension exactly three halves. Its critical Sobolev mass diverges logarithmically, at a rate given in closed form by Wiener's jump statistic of the datum and independent of the positions and phases of the jumps. The same rate is halved along time traces and doubled for the Airy flow, so the critical mass probes the dispersion relation. Transported by the rank calculus of derangetropy operators, the theory equips every probability law with a quantum carpet: Gauss-sum revivals on quantile cells at rational times, a universal fractal at almost every other. The spectral growth rate of a single measured intensity profile returns the jump content of the grating, a prediction open to test in optical and matter-wave interferometry.

</details>


### [102] [Correlation inequalities for transversal field models with application to quantum glasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23177)
*Chokri Manai,Simone Warzel*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究包含横场（transversal field）与对相互作用（pair interactions）的一类量子模型，包括量子Curie-Weiss、横场Ising模型，以及基于Sherrington-Kirkpatrick和Edwards-Anderson模型的量子自旋玻璃。主要结果是一个基于高斯卷积估计（Gaussian convolution estimate）的一般关联不等式，该不等式源自Ding-Song-Sun不等式的推广与Brascamp-Lieb凸性技巧的应用。在足够强的横场条件下，该结果对量子自旋玻璃给出一个无复制对称破缺（replica order）的高场区域；结合先前建立的低场复制对称破缺区域，可区分低场与高场两种重叠相态。作为推导出的对数Sobolev不等式（logarithmic Sobolev inequality）的推论，还证明了自重叠与复制重叠（self- and replica-overlap）的集中性，并确定了量子SK模型在高场下压强的渐近行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子自旋玻璃（quantum spin glass）的低场与高场行为呈现不同的有序相态，但现有工具难以在强横场下对关联函数和重叠分布给出定量刻画，缺乏统一的一般性关联不等式。

Method: 提出一种基于高斯卷积估计的一般关联不等式，其证明依赖于对Ding-Song-Sun不等式的扩展，并运用Brascamp-Lieb凸性技巧（Brascamp-Lieb convexity techniques）；同时推导了相应泛函积分（functional integrals）的对数Sobolev不等式。

Result: 在足够强的横场下，量子自旋玻璃模型出现无复制序（replica order）的高场区域；结合已有低场复制对称破缺结果，确立了低场与高场两种截然不同的重叠相态。此外，证明了自重叠与复制重叠的集中性（concentration），并给出了量子SK模型高场压强（pressure）的渐近表达式。

Conclusion: 该研究为量子模型中的关联不等式提供了新的一般性工具，并在量子自旋玻璃中建立了明确的低场-高场相变区分；对SK模型高场压强的确定同时为后续热力学性质研究提供了基础。

Abstract: We study the class of transversal field models with pair interactions, including the quantum Curie-Weiss and the transversal field Ising model, as well as quantum spin glasses based on the Sherrington-Kirkpatrick (SK) and Edwards-Anderson models. Our main result is a general correlation inequality based on a Gaussian convolution estimate, which is derived from an extension of the Ding-Song-Sun inequality and an application of Brascamp-Lieb convexity techniques. The results apply for sufficiently strong transversal fields and, for quantum spin glasses, yield a high-field regime without replica order. Combined with the previously established low-field replica-symmetry-breaking regime, this gives distinct low- and high-field overlap phases. As a corollary of the logarithmic Sobolev inequalities for the underlying functional integrals derived here, we show the concentration of the self- and replica-overlap, and determine the high-field asymptotics of the pressure in the quantum SK model.

</details>


### [103] [Universality of the conformal anomaly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23201)
*Sid Maibach,Eveliina Peltola*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了一维局域实模函子（local real one-dimensional modular functors）的一个普适性质，其可由单一实参数——中心荷（central charge）——完全刻画，这一结果可视为Segal在复情形下"Mumford定理"的实类比，并将盘-盘上循环（cocycle）识别为普适Liouville作用量（即环Loewner能量）。该结果在数学上解释了中心荷在共形场论（CFT）与Schramm-Loewner演化（SLE）中出现的理论根源，并证明了Brownian环测度作为SLE规范限制函数的唯一性猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形场论与随机共形不变性（如SLE）中普遍出现的中心荷参数虽已被广泛认识，但其数学根源缺乏系统的统一解释。作者旨在为实情形的模函子建立类似Segal复情形下"Mumford定理"的普适分类定理，从而在数学上阐明中心荷的出现机制，并证实Brownian环测度唯一性的猜想。

Method: 作者从局域实一维模函子的抽象框架出发，将其视为Segal模空间（Segal moduli spaces）上缝合（sewing）运算的中心扩张（central extension）。在代数层面，这类模函子由定义在曲面缝合对上的实值上循环（real-valued cocycles）所刻画，作者进一步将盘-盘上循环识别为普适Liouville作用量（universal Liouville action），即环Loewner能量（loop Loewner energy）。文中还以实行列式线丛（real determinant line bundle）编码CFT的迹反常与SLE环测度的限制函数作为引导性实例。

Result: 主要结果为一维实模函子的普适分类定理：该类模函子仅由一个实参数即中心荷唯一确定，类比于Segal专著中复情形的"Mumford定理"。作为推论，该结果给出中心荷在CFT和SLE中出现的严格数学解释，并证明了Brownian环测度作为SLE规范限制函数的唯一性猜想。

Conclusion: 该工作为实情形的模函子理论建立了普适的代数结构分类，将CFT中的迹反常（trace anomaly）与SLE中的环测度限制性质统一于同一数学框架之下，揭示了中心荷作为连接共形场论与随机共形几何的核心参数的本质地位。

Abstract: We prove a universal property of local real one-dimensional modular functors, which may be thought of as central extensions of the sewing operation on the (infinite-dimensional) Segal moduli spaces. Such modular functors are characterized by one real parameter: their central charge: Our result is an analogue of ``Mumford's theorem,'' that appeared in Segal's monograph [Seg88, Seg04] in the complex case. Algebraically, the modular functors are characterized by real-valued cocycles on pairs of surfaces under sewing. We identify the disk-disk cocycle as the universal Liouville action, also known as loop Loewner energy.
  The guiding example is the real determinant line bundle encoding the trace anomaly of conformal field theories (CFT) and the restriction function of Schramm-Loewner evolution (SLE) loop measures. Our result thus gives a mathematical explanation for the appearance of the central charge in CFT and SLE, and proves the conjectured uniqueness of Brownian loop measureas the canonical restriction function for SLE.

</details>


### [104] [Elements of Topology, Differential Geometry and General Relativity for Physicists: A Mathematica-based Tutorial Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23266)
*Balasubramanian Ananthanarayan,Souvik Bera,Subhasish Chakrabarty,Souradeep Das,Amitabha Lahiri,Suhas Sheikh,Sarthak Talukdar*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 这是一本以 Mathematica 笔记本为特色的自包含教材，面向物理学领域的学生和研究者，系统介绍拓扑学（topology）、微分几何（differential geometry）和广义相对论（general relativity），强调通过可运行的代码将抽象数学概念具体化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理学家在学习和应用现代几何与拓扑工具时常面临抽象数学表述难以直观理解的问题，作者希望通过 Mathematica 驱动的实操方式弥补这一鸿沟，使读者既能理解理论又能亲手复现所有结果。

Method: 全书分为三部分：第一部分涵盖点集拓扑、拓扑空间、映射连续性、同伦（homotopy）与基本群（fundamental group）；第二部分从零开始发展微分几何，包括流形、切空间、向量场、李括号（Lie bracket）、张量、微分形式（differential forms）、外微分（exterior derivative）、霍奇对偶（Hodge duality）等，并将其应用于麦克斯韦方程和斯托克斯定理，同时以李群、纤维丛、联络和曲率连接几何与规范场论；第三部分运用该框架阐述广义相对论，利用专用 Mathematica 包计算标准时空的曲率张量和场方程。

Result: 本书的核心成果是一套由作者原创编写的大规模 Mathematica 笔记本集合，使读者能够运行、验证和扩展书中所有演示；全书在数学严谨性与动手计算之间取得平衡，使读者能够同时理解理论并自行复现每个结果。

Conclusion: 通过对拓扑、微分几何和广义相对论的系统化 Mathematica 化呈现，本书为物理研究者提供了一条从抽象数学概念到可计算实现的有效学习路径，兼顾理论理解与实际操作能力。

Abstract: This book is a self-contained, tutorial introduction to topology, differential geometry, and general relativity for students and researchers in physics. Its distinguishing feature is a Mathematica-based approach that makes abstract constructions concrete through explicit, runnable notebooks and worked examples. A key contribution is a large collection of original Mathematica notebooks, written by the authors themselves, that let readers run, verify, and extend every demonstration. The first part covers point-set topology, topological spaces, continuity of maps, homotopy, and the fundamental group with applications that highlight the role of topology in modern physics. The second and largest part develops differential geometry from the ground up: manifolds, tangent and dual spaces, vector fields, pullbacks and pushforwards, Lie brackets and Lie algebras, local flows, and the Lie derivative. It then treats tensors, differential forms, the exterior derivative, volume forms, the metric tensor, and Hodge duality, emphasizing coordinate-free formulations and their computational realization. These tools are applied to Maxwell's equations in the language of forms and the generalized Stokes theorem, while Lie groups, fiber bundles, connections, and curvature bridge geometry and gauge-theoretic physics. The final part uses this framework to present general relativity, computing curvature tensors and field equations for standard spacetimes with dedicated Mathematica packages. Throughout, the book balances mathematical rigor with hands-on computation, enabling readers both to understand the theory and to reproduce every result themselves.

</details>


### [105] [Instantons in a Double-Well are Poisson Distributed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23342)
*Jacob Shapiro*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出了半经典薛定谔算子（semiclassical Schrödinger operator）在对称双阱势（double-well potential）下稀薄瞬子图像（dilute instanton picture）的严格实现。通过局部化的Feynman–Kac表示，将热核迹分解为布朗桥在两个阱的收缩邻域之间穿越次数的贡献，证明了特征值劈裂的瞬子展开可以直接从热核迹的因式化中自然导出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 瞬子图像是量子力学和量子场论中用于理解隧穿现象和能级劈裂的经典启发式框架，但在数学上缺乏严格的实现。本文旨在为对称双阱势下Schrödinger算子的半经典极限提供瞬子图像的严格数学基础，并直接从热核迹的分解导出已知的特征值劈裂瞬子展开。

Method: 采用局部化的Feynman–Kac表示，将热核迹按布朗桥（Brownian bridge）在两个阱的收缩邻域之间的穿越次数进行分解。每次穿越的权重由一个跃迁系数（hopping coefficient）$\rho_\lambda$刻画，其定义为在阱边界$\partial\Omega$上的通量积分。在此基础上研究指数长时间尺度$\beta=N/|\rho_\lambda|$上的穿越次数收敛性，并分析$\rho_\lambda$的指数衰减率。

Result: 证明了在时间尺度$\beta=N/|\rho_\lambda|$上，对于每个固定的$N>0$，布朗桥在两个阱之间的穿越次数收敛到均值为$N$的泊松随机变量（Poisson random variable）。同时证明了$-\frac{1}{\lambda}\log|\rho_\lambda|\to S(d,-d)$，并得到特征值劈裂公式$E_1(\lambda)-E_0(\lambda)=2|\rho_\lambda|(1+o(1))$。

Conclusion: 本文以严格的方式建立了双阱势下特征值劈裂的瞬子展开与热核迹因式化之间的直接联系，验证了稀疏穿越的泊松统计图像，为半经典分析的隧穿问题提供了从随机路径角度出发的严格数学框架。

Abstract: We give a rigorous realization of the dilute instanton picture for a semiclassical Schrödinger operator with a symmetric double-well potential on $\mathbb{R}^n$. Using a localized Feynman--Kac representation, we decompose the heat-kernel trace according to the number of passages made by a Brownian bridge between shrinking neighborhoods of the two wells. We identify the weight of one passage with a hopping coefficient $ρ_λ$,
  $\displaystyle ρ_λ=
\int_{\partialΩ} \left( \nabla\overline{\varphi_{λ,0}^Ω}\, \varphi_{λ,0}^{-Ω} -
\overline{\varphi_{λ,0}^Ω}\, \nabla\varphi_{λ,0}^{-Ω} \right)\cdotν. $
  On the exponentially long time scale $β=N/\lvertρ_λ\rvert$, the number of passages converges, for every fixed $N>0$, to a Poisson random variable of mean $N$. We identify $-\frac{1}λ\log\lvertρ_λ\rvert\to S(d,-d)$ and obtain
  $\displaystyle E_1(λ)-E_0(λ) =
2\lvertρ_λ\rvert\left(1+o(1)\right). $
  Thus the familiar instanton expansion of the double-well eigenvalue splitting emerges directly from a factorization of the heat-kernel trace.

</details>


### [106] [Bound States in Exactly Solvable Polaritonic Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23535)
*Sam Johar,Ryogo Katahira,Joseph Kraisler*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一维标量模型，考察有效质量准粒子场与最多含一个激发的二能级原子连续谱耦合问题，证明了在定号空间局域扰动的常数原子密度下束缚态存在的一般性定理，并通过若干精确可解的例子分析束缚态性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索有效质量准粒子与二能级原子连续谱耦合体系中束缚态的存在性和性质，为相关物理系统提供理论保证。

Method: 建立一维标量模型，约束体系最多含有一个激发；通过数学证明给出束缚态存在的一般性定理，并选取多个精确可解（exactly solvable）示例进行具体分析。

Result: 证明了在定号（sign-definite）空间局域扰动作用于常数原子密度时束缚态存在的一般性定理，并在多个精确可解的模型示例中刻画了束缚态的具体特征。

Conclusion: 在指定条件下束缚态的存在具有普遍性保障，精确可解示例进一步验证并展示了束缚态的结构。

Abstract: We study a one-dimensional scalar model for an effective-mass quasiparticle field coupled to a continuum of two-level atoms with at most one excitation present. We prove a general theorem on the existence of bound states under sign-definite spatially localized perturbations of constant atomic density and analyze the bound states for several exactly solvable examples.

</details>

### [107] [All-Optical Control of Interfacial Polarization in MoS$_2$/WSe$_2$ Heterobilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21493)
*Muhammad Sufyan Ramzan,Giancarlo Soavi,Caterina Cocchi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 基于实时含时密度泛函理论（real-time time-dependent density functional theory, rt-TDDFT）的第一性原理框架，预测了通过强超快激光脉冲可在MoS₂/WSe₂异质双层中诱导持久、长寿命的面外极化，并揭示了从微扰到强场区的转变以及层间电荷转移的四倍增强机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用相干辐射对范德华异质结构（van der Waals heterostructures）进行全光学调控，为超快存储和光电子器件提供有前景的路径。作者旨在探索通过超快脉冲在低维异质结构中实现持久极化的可能性，而非依赖外电场或机械手段。

Method: 采用实时含时密度泛函理论这一第一性原理框架进行数值模拟，通过分析高次谐波产生（high-harmonic generation, HHG）谱来区分微扰区与强场区，并系统考察了脉冲强度与晶格应变对极化诱导和层间电荷转移的影响。

Result: 弱场下保持固有的II型能带排列；中等强度激发下，层间电荷转移实现了四倍增强。HHG谱分析揭示了从微扰到强场区的转变，该转变诱导了光致界面极性（photoinduced interfacial polarity）。此外，晶格应变可作为额外调控参数来调整共振条件，同时不影响永久偶极的诱导。

Conclusion: 该研究为在飞秒时间尺度上对低维异质结构中的极性相进行全光学操控提供了理论蓝图，展示了通过激光强度与晶格应变双参数调控实现持久极化的可行性。

Abstract: All-optical tuning of van der Waals heterostructures with coherent radiation offers a promising path toward ultrafast memory and optoelectronic devices. In the first-principles framework of real-time time-dependent density functional theory, we predict the induction of a persistent, long-lived out-of-plane polarization in MoS$_2$/WSe$_2$ heterobilayers, resonantly driven by intense ultrafast pulses. While weak fields preserve the intrinsic type-II band alignment, intermediate intensities trigger a four-fold enhancement of interlayer charge transfer. By analyzing the high-harmonic generation spectrum, we identify a transition from the perturbative to the strong-field regime inducing photoinduced interfacial polarity. We additionally show that lattice strain, ubiquitously present in heterobilayers, can be used as additional knob to adjust the resonant condition without compromising the permanent dipole induction. Our findings provide a theoretical blueprint for the all-optical manipulation of polar phases in low-dimensional heterostructures at the femtosecond scale.

</details>


### [108] [Machine-learned exchange-correlation functionals for molecules, solids, and reactive surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21525)
*Mohamed S. Abdallah,Zhuotao Jin,Boris Kozinsky,Kyle Bystrom*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为CIDER26SS的交换关联泛函框架，它结合机器学习与显式非局域的物理描述符，经过正则化处理以实现广泛的迁移性。该泛函在分子和固态体系上表现平衡且准确，特别在过渡金属表面化学上表现优异，成功解决了CO/Pt谜题，正确预测了Pt(111)表面上CO吸附的结合位点、吸附能、晶格常数和表面能，且即使在训练集中排除所有Pt体相和表面数据时预测仍与实验值吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统密度泛函近似（density functional approximations）在描述非均相催化时存在固有缺陷，阻碍了密度泛函理论（density functional theory，DFT）在该领域的有效应用，因此需要开发更准确的泛函框架。

Method: 将机器学习与显式非局域的物理信息描述符相结合，构建了一个经过正则化处理（regularization）以便广泛迁移的交换关联泛函框架CIDER26SS。该方法同时注重尺寸扩展性（size-extensive）和高计算效率。

Result: CIDER26SS在分子和固态体系上均提供平衡而准确的描述，在过渡金属表面化学上表现出色。它超越了现有常规泛函，成功解决了CO/Pt谜题，准确预测了Pt(111)表面CO吸附的位点、吸附能、Pt晶格常数和表面能；即使训练集完全排除Pt的体相与表面数据，预测仍与实验值一致，甚至在训练域之外的体系上精度也超过半局域近似（semilocal approximations）。

Conclusion: CIDER26SS是一种具有广泛迁移性的非局域交换关联泛函，能显著提升密度泛函理论在异相催化中的预测能力，并超越现有半局域近似的精度，展示了机器学习与物理约束结合构建泛函的潜力。

Abstract: The application of density functional theory to heterogeneous catalysis is hindered by the shortcomings of conventional density functional approximations. We combine machine learning with explicitly non-local physically informed descriptors and introduce an exchange-correlation functional (CIDER26SS) framework regularized for wide transferability. CIDER26SS is size-extensive, highly efficient, provides a balanced and accurate description of both molecular and solid-state systems, and is specifically well-optimized for transition metal surface chemistry. Surpassing existing conventional functionals, CIDER26SS resolves the CO/Pt puzzle, identifying the correct binding site for CO adsorption on the Pt(111) surface, along with an accurate adsorption energy, Pt lattice constant, and surface energy. Predictions agree well with the experimental values, even when all bulk and surface data for Pt are excluded from the training set. Remarkably, CIDER26SS exceeds the accuracy of semilocal approximations even for systems far outside the training domain.

</details>


### [109] [Automated Analysis to Reveal Grain Boundary Phase Microstructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21540)
*R. Daniel Moore,Ian S. Winter,Robert E. Rudd,Fadi Abdeljawad,Timofey Frolov*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自动化分析晶界（grain boundary, GB）微观结构的方法，能够识别不同的界面相（interfacial phase）以及分隔这些相的位错线缺陷（dislocation line defect）。该方法可绘制平面重合位置点阵（coincidence site lattice, CSL）晶界的界面微结构图谱，定量表征晶界相的空间分布、各相的超额性质（excess property）以及相结（phase junction）的Burgers含量，并通过三个代表性案例验证了其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶界可像块体材料一样呈现多种不同的界面相并经历一级相变，当这些相共存时，其空间排列和相结构成晶界微观结构，表现为超额性质和位错含量的变化。然而，现有自动化方法缺乏识别单个晶界相、解析各相超额性质以及度量相结Burgers含量的能力，导致对晶界微观结构的定量表征受到限制。

Method: 开发了一种自动化界面微结构图谱（interfacial microstructure mapping）工具，面向平面重合位置点阵晶界。该方法通过识别晶界相的空间分布、量化各相特定的超额性质，并估计晶界相结的Burgers含量，实现对晶界微观结构的全面定量表征。

Result: 通过三个代表性案例验证了方法有效性：(i) 扩散限制晶界相变过程中的质量传输定量分析；(ii) 识别由空位环和间隙原子环形成的结构上不可区分的相；(iii) 表征含相核（phase nucleus）的晶界微观结构。

Conclusion: 该框架可推广至晶界演化过程的定量研究，包括旋节分解（spinodal decomposition）和粗化（coarsening），对晶界变形、蠕变（creep）和迁移等行为具有直接的启示意义。

Abstract: We develop a method for analyzing grain boundary (GB) microstructures that identifies distinct interfacial phases and the dislocation line defects separating them. Similar to bulk materials, GBs can adopt multiple distinct interfacial phases and undergo first-order phase transitions. When these phases coexist, their spatial arrangement and phase junctions constitute a GB microstructure, characterized by variations in excess properties and line defects with associated dislocation content. Despite this intrinsic heterogeneity, our ability to quantitatively characterize GB microstructures remains limited, as it requires identification of individual GB phases, phase-resolved excess properties, and the Burgers content of phase junctions, capabilities not available in existing automated methods. Here, we present an automated tool that performs interfacial microstructure mapping for planar coincidence site lattice GBs. The method identifies the spatial distribution of GB phases, quantifies phase-specific excess properties, and estimates the Burgers content of GB phase junctions. We demonstrate the approach using three representative cases: (i) quantification of mass transport during diffusion-limited GB phase transformations; (ii) identification of structurally indistinguishable phases formed by vacancy and interstitial loops; and (iii) characterization of GB microstructures containing phase nuclei. More broadly, this framework enables quantitative studies of GB evolution processes, including spinodal decomposition and coarsening with direct implications for GB deformation, creep, and migration.

</details>


### [110] [Investigating Sulfur Vacancy Passivation in Monolayer MoS2 FETs via Optically Coupled Low-Frequency Electrical Noise Spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21604)
*Shashwata Chattopadhyay,Soumyadip Hazra,Sraboni Dey,Kritika Sharu,Renjith Nadarajan,Arijit Kayal,Joy Mitra*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究通过辛硫醇（octane thiol）钝化单层MoS2场效应晶体管中的硫空位缺陷，结合光耦合低频噪声谱（optically coupled low-frequency noise spectroscopy）等多项表征手段，将空位浓度从7.5%降至5%，使Hooge参数下降两个数量级以上，并揭示暗态下载流子迁移率涨落与光照下载流子数涨落两种不同的噪声机制，确立了光耦合低频噪声谱作为量化缺陷钝化的灵敏、低成本无损工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学气相沉积生长的过渡金属硫族化合物（TMDC）单层薄膜性能受天然硫空位（sulphur vacancies）严重限制，这些空位引入带隙中间态（mid-gap states）、降低载流子迁移率并提升电噪声，亟需有效的缺陷钝化策略及对应的量化评估手段。

Method: 实验上结合X射线光电子能谱（XPS）、光致发光（photoluminescence）、拉曼散射（Raman scattering）与电输运及光耦合低频噪声谱对MoS2场效应晶体管进行表征；理论上辅以密度泛函理论（DFT）计算验证空位态抑制效果。辛硫醇钝化处理后系统考察硫空位浓度、沟道电阻、开关比、场效应迁移率及不同光照条件下的噪声行为。

Result: 硫醇处理将硫空位浓度从7.5%降至5%，沟道电阻提高35倍，恢复栅极开关特性（开关比10^4），场效应迁移率从1提升至5 cm²/Vs；Hooge参数在钝化后下降超过两个数量级。栅压依赖噪声表明暗态下以载流子迁移率涨落（mobility fluctuation）为主，光照则转向载流子数涨落（number fluctuation）主导，反映光生载流子经产生-复合俘获与残余空位态相互作用。DFT计算显示空位诱导带隙中间态抑制超过50%，本征带隙部分恢复。

Conclusion: 光耦合低频噪声谱能够提供栅压测量无法区分的机制性见解，可作为量化TMDC器件缺陷钝化效果的高灵敏度、低成本、非破坏性工具，为二维材料器件工程提供新的检测与评估手段。

Abstract: Transition metal dichalcogenide monolayers are promising materials for electronic and photonic applications, yet the performance of chemical vapour deposition grown films is severely limited by native sulphur vacancies that introduce mid-gap trap states, degrade carrier mobility, and elevate electrical noise. Here we investigate octane thiol passivation of sulphur vacancies in monolayer MoS2 field effect transistors, combining x-ray photoelectron spectroscopy, photoluminescence, and Raman scattering with electrical transport and optically coupled low-frequency noise spectroscopy. Thiol treatment reduces the sulphur vacancy concentration from 7.5% to 5%, which increases the channel resistance 35-fold while restoring gate switching with an on/off ratio of 10^4 and improving field-effect mobility from 1 to 5 cm^2/Vs. Low frequency noise spectroscopy directly quantifies the defect suppression: the Hooge parameter drops by more than two orders of magnitude after passivation. Gate-dependent noise confirms carrier mobility fluctuation as the dominant dark noise mechanism, while optical excitation drives a crossover to carrier number fluctuation dominated noise, reflecting preferential interaction of photogenerated carriers with residual vacancy states via generation-recombination trapping, a mechanistic distinction inaccessible to gate- bias measurements alone. Density functional theory calculations corroborate these findings, showing suppression of vacancy-induced mid-gap states by more than 50% and partial restoration of the intrinsic bandgap. These results establish optically coupled low-frequency noise spectroscopy as a sensitive, low-cost, and non-destructive tool for quantifying defect passivation in TMDC-based devices.

</details>


### [111] [Vibrational, structural, and chemical fingerprints of ion diffusion in crystalline solids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21624)
*Gavin Winter,Juno Nam,Rafael Gómez-Bombarelli*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种数据驱动方法，利用短时分子动力学（molecular dynamics, MD）轨迹中提取的热力学与结构特征来预测固态电解质中移动离子的自扩散系数（self-diffusivity）$D^*$，以机器学习神经网络模型替代昂贵的长时间扩散模拟，预测收敛的 $\log_{10} D^*$ 的均方绝对误差为0.398，Spearman秩相关系数为0.844。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从MD模拟中预测移动离子自扩散系数对筛选固态电解质至关重要，但直接模拟离子扩散需要极长的轨迹才能收敛，尤其是使用高精度机器学习原子间势（machine learning interatomic potentials, MLIPs）时计算成本极高。扩散作为缓慢的涌现过程需要长轨迹，而热力学性质（焓、振动熵、超额构型熵）收敛快速且编码了自由能景观信息，本文旨在利用这一联系构建低成本预测框架。

Method: 作者训练了一个简单神经网络，输入特征来自短时MD轨迹：振动指纹（振动态密度，vibrational density of states, VDOS）和结构指纹（径向分布函数，radial distribution function, RDF），并以MLIP嵌入中编码的化学信息为条件，实现从上述特征直接预测收敛后的 $\log_{10} D^*$。

Result: 该模型能够预测通常需要显著更长MD模拟才能获得的收敛自扩散系数，预测的 $\log_{10} D^*$（cm²/s）的均方绝对误差（mean absolute error, MAE）为0.398，Spearman秩相关系数（Spearman's rank correlation）$\rho$ 为0.844。

Conclusion: 基于短MD轨迹上快速收敛的热力学与结构特征，结合神经网络可以高效且准确地预测离子自扩散系数，为固态电解质高通量筛选提供了一条低成本路径。

Abstract: Predicting mobile-ion self-diffusivity $D^*$ from molecular dynamics (MD) simulations is essential for identifying promising solid-state electrolytes, but directly simulating ion diffusion is computationally expensive, particularly with high-accuracy machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs). Diffusion is a slow, emergent process that requires long trajectories to converge. Thermodynamic properties, by contrast, converge much faster: the enthalpy $h$, vibrational entropy $s_{vib}$, and 2-body, excess configurational entropy $s^{ex}_{2,config}$ can be extracted from comparatively short MD trajectories, and they encode rich information about the free energy landscape from which transport properties like self-diffusivity ultimately arise. Intuitive correlations are discussed between these thermodynamic properties and ion diffusion, motivating a data-driven approach to exploit this link. A simple neural network was trained to predict diffusivity from features computed over short MD trajectories: a vibrational fingerprint (the vibrational density of states, VDOS) and a structural fingerprint (the radial distribution function, RDF), conditioned on chemistry information encoded in the MLIP embedding. This combination allows the model to predict the converged $\log_{10} D^*$ (cm$^2$/s) \textemdash\ normally obtained from significantly longer MD simulations \textemdash\ with a mean absolute error of 0.398 and a Spearman's rank correlation $ρ$ of 0.844.

</details>


### [112] [A high-entropy form of $R$Mn$_6$Sn$_6$ with distinct magnetotransport regimes correlated to different magnetic structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21633)
*Kyle W. Fruhling,Jonathan Gaudet,William D. Ratcliff,Jonathan S. White,Siddharth Nandanwar,Noah J. Fau,Gregory T. McCandless,Enrique O. González Delgado,Julia Y. Chan,Oksana Zaharko,Michael A. Susner,Fazel Tafti*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究报道了一种由Tb、Dy、Ho、Er、Tm和Lu组成的稀土元素混合的kagome RMn₆Sn₆高熵化合物，该化合物在380 K以下依次经历顺磁态到易面铁磁态（FiM）、易轴态以及倾斜态的三个相变，并展示出宽温度范围的各向异性转变和非单调磁阻行为，中子散射数据表明这些现象与由稀土相互作用竞争引起的非公度调制自旋结构相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: kagome RMn₆Sn₆材料家族因其多种磁有序和各向异性而备受关注，理论研究表明稀土元素（R）决定了化合物中磁各向异性的方向和磁有序的类型，因此研究高熵形式的RMn₆Sn₆可以探索多种稀土元素共存对单晶中磁态的影响。

Method: 本研究选择了Tb、Dy、Ho、Er、Tm和Lu六种稀土元素的混合，制备高熵RMn₆Sn₆单晶，并通过磁性测量和中子散射（neutron scattering）技术表征其相变行为、磁各向异性转变与自旋结构。

Result: 该化合物在T_C=380 K以下从顺磁态转变为易面铁磁态，在T_SR1=207 K转变为易轴态，在T_SR2=79 K以下进入倾斜铁磁基态。该混合在270 K至170 K区间展示了从易面到易轴各向异性的宽转变，并呈现非单调磁阻（magnetoresistance）行为；中子散射数据表明两现象与稀土相互作用竞争引起的非公度调制自旋贡献相关。

Conclusion: 该稀土混合表现出的磁阻行为及其与自旋结构的关联，凸显了通过稀土工程（rare-earth engineering）调控磁性的潜力。

Abstract: The kagome $R$Mn$_6$Sn$_6$ material family has attracted significant attention as high-temperature metallic magnets with a host of different magnetic orderings and anisotropy. Theoretical studies point to the rare-earth ($R$) as the determining factor for both the direction of magnetic anisotropy and the type of magnetic ordering in a given compound. This motivates studying high-entropy forms of $R$Mn$_6$Sn$_6$ to examine how the interplay of several rare-earth elements leads to different magnetic states in a single crystal. Here, we present a rare-earth mix of Tb, Dy, Ho, Er, Tm, and Lu that produces phase transitions from a paramagnet to an easy-plane ferrimagnet (FiM) below $T_{\text{C}}$ = 380 K, then to a FiM easy-axis state at $T_{\text{SR1}}$ = 207 K, to a canted FiM ground state below $T_{\text{SR2}}$ = 79 K. This behavior is consistent with previously reported high-entropy $R$Mn$_6$Sn$_6$ compounds; however, uniquely, the rare-earth mix studied here exhibits a broad transition from easy-plane to easy-axis anisotropy from 270 K to 170 K, and reveals a nonmonotonic magnetoresistance. Using neutron scattering data, we found that both observations correlate with an incommensurate modulated contribution to the spin state due to competing rare-earth interactions. This magnetoresistive behavior and the correlated spin structures underscore the potential for rare-earth engineering of magnetism.

</details>


### [113] [First-Principles Atomistic Structure and Dynamics of Polyethylene During High-Pressure Radical Polymerization via Machine Learning Force Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21741)
*Bharatha K. Gunawardana,Teresa Shah,Bicha Azizova,Deepa Ranabhat,Yizhi Song,Akshath Shastri,Srinjoy Ghose,Thomas E. Gartner,Hsin-Yu Ko*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
