TL;DR: 本研究将深度学习势（deep potential, DP）机器学习力场与基于SeA高通量框架的一性原理范德华（vdW）修正混合密度泛函理论（DFT）相结合，构建了聚乙烯（PE）的全原子机器学习力场，并用于模拟超临界乙烯溶剂中PE齐聚物和聚合物的结构、动力学行为及分子量标度关系，在保持化学真实性的同时克服了传统经验势在复杂反应体系中的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚乙烯（polyethylene, PE）是最常用的合成聚合物之一，但其原子级分辨率下的微观结构实验测定仅限于高度结晶体系。经验原子间势在批量性质和集体性质上表现良好，但在复杂体系（如反应过程）中缺乏化学真实性，亟需一种兼具计算效率和化学精度的模拟方法。

Method: 采用深度学习势（deep potential, DP）机器学习力场提供计算效率，并借助SeA高通量框架实现一性原理范德华（van der Waals, vdW）修正的混合密度泛函理论（hybrid density functional theory, DFT）以提供化学真实性，二者结合构建PE机器学习力场。随后在超临界自由基聚合条件下，系统模拟不同链长的PE齐聚物和聚合物在乙烯溶剂中的结构与动力学。

Result: PE齐聚物中自由基局部溶剂化环境在链长大于约6个碳原子（n~6）后收敛，表明基于齐聚物训练的力场可外推至显著更长的聚合物链。通过单链结构和动力学的分子量标度分析证实了模型对长链PE的可扩展性，呈现出经典良溶剂（good solvent）行为。该力场在广泛热力学状态点和链长范围内均保持一致的精度和稳定性。

Conclusion: 本研究构建的PE机器学习力场在全原子分辨率下兼具一性原理化学真实性和可扩展性，为基于一性原理的聚合物结构与性质预测铺平了道路。

Abstract: Polyethylene (PE) is one of the most commonly used synthetic polymers. While the synthesis and processing protocols for PE are well established, precise experimental assignment of microscopic structures at atomistic resolution (i.e., the position of each atom) remains largely limited to highly crystalline systems. This gap is often addressed via computer simulations using empirical interatomic potentials, which use approximate but efficient descriptions of interatomic interactions to reach the length and time scales needed to describe macromolecules. These empirical potentials typically perform well for bulk and/or collective properties but face challenges with chemical realism for complex systems, e.g., during reactive processes. In this work, we address this challenge by combining the computational efficiency of a deep potential (DP) machine-learning force field and the chemical realism of first-principles van der Waals (vdW) corrected hybrid density functional theory (DFT) enabled by a SeA high-throughput framework. Using this approach, we study the structure and dynamics of PE oligomers and polymers in an ethylene solvent under common high-pressure (supercritical) radical polymerization conditions. We found that the local solvation environment of radical-containing PE oligomers converges for chain lengths greater than (n~6), suggesting extensibility of our oligomer-trained MLFF to significantly longer polymers. We then confirmed the extensibility of these models to long PE chains by characterizing the molecular weight scaling of single-chain structure and dynamics, which showed classic good solvent behavior. Our PE MLFF retained a consistent level of fidelity and stability across a wide range of thermodynamic state points and chain lengths, at full atomistic resolution, therefore paving the way towards first-principles-based polymer structure and property prediction.

</details>


### [114] [Thermodynamic and Kinetic Tests for Low-Pressure Metal-Flux Growth of Diamond, with an Extension to Cubic Boron Nitride](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21742)
*Rodney S. Ruoff*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文在热力学上石墨更稳定的条件下，从液态Ga-Fe-Ni-Si合金在近1 atm压力下生长金刚石的现象出发，分析了亚稳态（metastable）金刚石生长前沿持续推进所必须满足的条件，提出了基于有限差分转移自由能（finite-difference transfer free energy）和刀口位点（kink）碳原子附着重复事件的理论框架，并给出了区分持续金刚石生长与种子存活等情形的同位素分辨实验方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金刚石可以在石墨在热力学上更稳定的亚稳态条件下从液态金属合金中生长，但这一现象背后的热力学与动力学条件尚不明确。本文旨在回答：在这种亚稳态条件下，金刚石生长前沿要持续推进，必须具备什么条件。为低气压金属助熔剂金刚石生长声称提供可定量检验的判据，并指导实验、热力学建模和原子模拟。

Method: 作者为指定界面事件定义了一个有限差分转移自由能，并将刀口位点处单个碳原子的完整附着识别为控制持续生长的可重复事件。在大尺度极限下消去表面项后，所需碳活度由金刚石体相自由能决定；将碳活度归一化到石墨基准后，1300 K下金刚石平衡阈值约为2.0，而石墨饱和为1。分析进一步区分了热力学驱动力、动力学可达性、实验检测极限与实验后保留的碳，并提出了基于13C富集种子与12C富集外延层的同位素分辨检验方案，同时将该分析方法推广到立方氮化硼（cubic boron nitride），引入独立的硼与氮化学势。

Result: 金刚石生长所需的碳活度由体相自由能决定，在石墨归一化标度下1300 K时金刚石平衡阈值约为2.0，而石墨饱和为1。持续金刚石生长要求动力学选择（kinetic selection）超过石墨阈值，而非翻转体相稳定性。同位素分辨测试可区分持续生长与种子存活、瞬态碳附着或石墨检测缺失；13C富集种子配合天然丰度碳源可验证来源归属的外延生长；对立方氮化硼，10B/11B与15N对比度可区分储库供给与每元素种子损失。

Conclusion: 低气压金属助熔剂金刚石生长声称可以通过本文建立的标准进行定量检验。该框架区分了热力学驱动力、动力学可达性、实验检测极限与实验后保留碳，并延伸到立方氮化硼的生长分析，为实验设计、热力学建模和原子尺度模拟提供了统一的评判基准。

Abstract: Diamond was grown from a liquid Ga-Fe-Ni-Si alloy near 1 atm, although bulk graphite is thermodynamically more stable under those conditions. This Perspective asks what must be true for a diamond growth front to continue advancing in such a metastable regime. I define a finite-difference transfer free energy for a specified interfacial event and identify the completed attachment of one carbon atom at a kink as the repeatable event that governs sustained growth. In the macroscopic limit, surface terms cancel, and the required carbon activity is fixed by the bulk free energy of diamond. On a graphite-normalized scale, the diamond equilibrium threshold is about 2.0 at 1300 K, whereas graphite saturation occurs at unity. Diamond growth therefore requires kinetic selection above the graphite threshold, not a reversal of bulk phase stability. The analysis distinguishes thermodynamic driving force, kinetic accessibility, experimental detection limits, and the carbon retained after an experiment. It gives isotope-resolved tests for distinguishing sustained diamond growth from seed survival, transient carbon attachment, or failure to detect graphite. A $^{13}\mathrm{C}$-enriched diamond seed provides a test of natural-abundance gas or condensed carbon sources: a registered, crystallographically continuous $^{12}\mathrm{C}$-rich layer outside the original seed can establish source-attributed overgrowth without isotopically labeling each feed. The same accounting is extended to cubic boron nitride. Separate boron and nitrogen chemical potentials are required, and $^{10}\mathrm{B}/^{11}\mathrm{B}$ and $^{15}\mathrm{N}$ contrasts could distinguish reservoir supply from seed loss for each element. The resulting criteria make low-pressure metal-flux growth claims quantitatively testable and guide experiments, thermodynamic modeling, and atomistic simulation.

</details>


### [115] [Dynamic Magnetic Pair-Density Function of a One-Dimensional Ferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21759)
*Shin-ichi Shamoto*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文将静态磁对分布函数（mPDF）推广至有限能量转移，推导出动态磁对分布函数（DymPDF），通过一维海森伯铁磁体的解析解与SpinW模拟结果对比，证明其最近邻自旋对关联的能量依赖与模拟高度吻合，并为利用DymPDF研究磁性材料局域自旋动力学奠定了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态磁对分布函数（mPDF）可描述磁性材料中的局域磁结构，但无法捕捉自旋动力学的能量信息。本文旨在将mPDF推广到有限能量转移，建立动态磁对分布函数（DymPDF）的理论框架，以满足对局域磁激发（magnetic excitations）进行实空间、能量分辨分析的需求。

Method: 从磁子色散关系（magnon dispersion）出发，解析推导一维海森伯铁磁体（one-dimensional Heisenberg ferromagnet）的DymPDF解析表达式，并使用SpinW软件进行数值模拟对比。进一步建立包含有限仪器分辨率（finite instrumental resolution）、磁关联长度（magnetic correlation length）和傅里叶截断效应（Fourier-termination effects）的模型，用于复现低能区的实空间DymPDF。

Result: 最近邻自旋对关联的能量依赖与SpinW模拟结果高度一致；DymPDF在最大磁子能量一半处的磁子模跃迁（magnon-mode transition）处发生符号改变；低能区的实空间DymPDF可通过包含仪器分辨率、磁关联长度和傅里叶截断效应的模型精确复现。

Conclusion: 本工作建立了DymPDF分析的理论基础，表明该方法能够用于研究磁性材料中的局域自旋动力学（local spin dynamics），为后续实验数据的解析与解读提供了理论依据。

Abstract: The dynamic magnetic pair-density function (DymPDF) $D_{\rm M}(r, E)$ is derived by extending the static magnetic pair distribution function (mPDF) to finite energy transfer. The analytical DymPDF of a one-dimensional Heisenberg ferromagnet is obtained from the magnon dispersion and compared with simulations performed using {\textsc SpinW}. Excellent agreement is achieved for energy dependence of the nearest-neighbor spin-pair correlation, demonstrating that the DymPDF changes sign at the magnon-mode transition occurring at one-half of the maximum magnon energy. The real-space DymPDF at low energy is also reproduced with a model including finite instrumental resolution, magnetic correlation length, and Fourier-termination effects. These results establish the theoretical foundation of DymPDF analysis for investigating local spin dynamics in magnetic materials.

</details>


### [116] [Nudged Elastic Band Method in the CRYSTAL code. Theory and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21973)
*Andreha Gelli,Silvia Casassa,Albert Rimola,Chiara Ribaldone*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文在CRYSTAL代码中实现了多种爬坡弹性带（nudged elastic band, NEB）算法方案，该代码基于Hartree-Fock和密度泛函理论，利用局域高斯型函数展开波函数以高效计算精确交换项，从而能够在杂化泛函水平下精确表征分子与凝聚相体系的过渡态。通过两个分子反应和一个周期性菱沸石中的质子交换过程进行了基准验证，结果与实验和以往理论数据高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NEB方法是确定化学反应和相变中最小能量路径及过渡态（transition states）的广泛应用算法，但在CRYSTAL这类针对凝聚态体系的从头算（ab initio）量子力学程序中缺乏杂化泛函水平下的NEB实现，限制了复杂周期性体系反应过程的高精度研究。

Method: 在CRYSTAL代码中实现了多种NEB算法方案，利用局域高斯型函数（localized Gaussian-type functions）展开波函数，从而高效评估杂化交换关联泛函（hybrid exchange-correlation functionals）中的精确交换级数；同时回顾了力投影方案、切线估计、优化策略，以及爬坡图像（climbing image）和可变弹簧常数（variable spring constant）等扩展变体。

Result: 通过三个基准测试验证了算法：线性质子转移过程、甲酰胺的酮-烯醇互变异构反应以及周期性的菱沸石（chabazite）沸石中的质子交换过程。所有结果与实验和先前理论数据高度一致，确认了实现的准确性和适用性。

Conclusion: 该工作为未来在扩展周期性体系中使用杂化泛函研究复杂反应过程开辟了可能性，证明了CRYSTAL代码中NEB实现能够在此高精度理论水平下可靠表征过渡态。

Abstract: The nudged elastic band (NEB) method is a widely used algorithm for determining minimum energy paths and transition states in chemical reactions and phase transitions. In this work, we present the implementation of different NEB algorithm schemes in the CRYSTAL code, a quantum mechanical ab initio program for the calculation of electronic properties of condensed matter systems, based on Hartree-Fock and density functional theory. The use of a set of localized Gaussian-type functions to expand the wavefunction permits a very efficient evaluation of the exact exchange series for the hybrid exchange-correlation functionals. Therefore, our implementation allows an accurate characterization of transition states in both molecular and condensed phase systems, at the hybrid functional level of theory. The theoretical framework, including the force projection scheme, tangent estimation, optimization strategies, as well as the extensions of the method with climbing image and variable spring constant variants, is recalled. Then, the NEB algorithm is validated through a series of benchmark tests: two molecular reactions (a collinear proton transfer process and the keto-enol tautomerization in formamide) and a proton exchange process in a periodic chabazite zeolite. Our results are in excellent agreement with experimental and previous theoretical data, confirming the accuracy and applicability of the implementation. This work opens the possibility for future studies of complex reactive processes on extended periodic systems, using hybrid functionals.

</details>


### [117] [Impact of interstitial carbon on local lattice distortions in CoCrFeMnNi high-entropy alloys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22009)
*Alevtina Smekhova,Alexei Kuzmin,G. Mohan Muralikrishna,Edmund Welter,Sergiu Levcenko,Fritz Körmann,Yuji Ikeda,Sergiy V. Divinski*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究利用多边扩展X射线吸收精细结构谱结合反向蒙特卡罗分析，发现等原子面心立方CrMnFeCoNi高熵合金中Cr组分展现出最大的局域晶格畸变，且随间隙碳掺杂单调增加，而Mn、Fe、Co、Ni则表现出较弱且非单调的趋势；第一性原理密度泛函理论和有限温度分子动力学模拟进一步证实碳对Cr周围局域畸变的影响大于其他3d组元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解多晶等原子面心立方高熵合金（high-entropy alloys）CrMnFeCoNi中组分依赖的局域晶格畸变及其受稀散间隙碳掺杂的影响，以揭示碳诱导相变与非单调扩散行为背后的原子尺度机制。

Method: 采用多边扩展X射线吸收精细结构谱（multiple-edge extended X-ray absorption fine structure spectroscopy）结合反向蒙特卡罗（reverse Monte Carlo）分析，系统考察不同退火温度（993 K或1373 K）和不同碳含量（0至0.8 at.%）下的局部畸变；并辅以第一性原理密度泛函理论（density functional theory）和有限温度分子动力学（finite-temperature molecular dynamics）模拟进行验证。

Result: Cr组分在所有条件下均表现出最显著的局域晶格畸变，且其静态无序随碳合金化呈现明显单调增加；Mn、Fe、Co、Ni则表现出较弱且非单调的变化趋势。碳诱导的畸变延伸至多个配位壳层（coordination shells），对Cr吸收体周围局域环境影响显著。模拟结果确认碳优先占据Cr富集的间隙位点，从而对Cr周围畸变的影响大于其他3d组元。

Conclusion: 这些结果为碳掺杂CrMnFeCoNi合金中先前报道的非单调扩散行为提供了原子尺度的关键线索，并对理解组分复杂体系中碳诱导的相变具有重要意义。

Abstract: Here, we explore component-dependent local lattice distortions in polycrystalline, equiatomic, face-centered cubic CrMnFeCoNi high-entropy alloys and their modifications induced by dilute interstitial carbon. Multi-edge extended X-ray absorption fine structure spectroscopy combined with reverse Monte Carlo analysis reveals that the Cr component experiences the most substantial local distortions, independent of the temperature of prolonged annealing treatments (993 K or 1373 K) and the nominal carbon content (0 to 0.8 at.%). The static disorder around Cr atoms was found to increase markedly and monotonically upon carbon alloying, whereas Mn, Fe, Co, and Ni demonstrate weaker and non-monotonic tendencies. The carbon-induced lattice distortions extend over several coordination shells, indicating the pronounced effect of the carbon presence on the local environment around Cr absorbers. First-principles density functional theory and finite-temperature molecular dynamics simulations confirm the greater impact of carbon on the local lattice distortions around Cr than around the other $3d$ constituent elements, based on the previous finding that carbon preferentially occupies Cr-rich interstitial sites. These results provide decisive hints towards the atomistic origin of the non-monotonic diffusion behavior previously reported for carbon-doped CrMnFeCoNi alloys, and are noticeable for understanding the carbon-induced phase transitions in compositionally complex systems.

</details>


### [118] [Cross-Temperature Defect Identification in Atomistic Simulations via Multi-Level Domain Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22074)
*Yating Fang,Jungmin Kim,Qian Qian Zhao,Pallavi Biswas,Joshua M. Gonjon,Ryan B. Sills,Ahmed Aziz Ezzat*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种跨温度域对齐（cross-temperature domain alignment）框架，通过等变去噪器、跨温度对比学习和形貌感知正则化三个层次对齐低温标注与高温无标注域，实现了无需高温标签即可在近熔点温度下高精度识别原子缺陷（包括空位和自间隙原子），并在百万原子分子动力学轨迹和铝双晶界相变中均保持可靠性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高温下热涨落会模糊局部对称性，使得依赖几何启发式或监督分类器的缺陷识别方法失效：可靠标签只存在于低温参考构型中，而最需要稳健分析的高温区域实质上无标注。作者将空间位形识别问题重新表述为一个跨温度域漂移（cross-temperature domain-shift）问题，以实现标签高效的温度鲁棒缺陷检测。

Method: 方法包含三个层次的域对齐：输入层使用等变去噪器（equivariant denoiser）消除热涨落；表示层采用跨温度对比学习（cross-temperature contrastive learning）拉近两域特征分布；预测层引入形貌感知正则化器（morphology-aware regularizer）引导输出趋向物理缺陷结构的紧凑几何。此外，提出无标签评估套件（label-free evaluation suite），沿五个空间与物理维度对预测缺陷结构评分，实现无需高温标注的模型评估与选择。

Result: 在近熔点温度下，框架在面心立方（fcc）、体心立方（bcc）和六方密排（hcp）三种铁体系中对空位和自间隙原子的识别，实现所有间隙原子全部精确定位且每个空位体系零误检。在百万原子、2.5纳秒轨迹上持续保持准确，可分辨单空位跳跃和完整Frenkel对复合；在铝双晶粒边界相变中区分两种成核模式。

Conclusion: 多层次域对齐为大规模分子动力学（molecular dynamics）的高温鲁棒结构分析提供了一条实际可行、标签高效的路径，无需高温真值标签即可实现与Wigner-Seitz基准相当甚至更优的缺陷识别性能。

Abstract: Identifying atomic defects at elevated temperature is difficult because thermal fluctuations blur the local symmetry that both geometric heuristics and supervised classifiers rely on: trustworthy labels exist in low-temperature reference configurations, while the high-temperature regime where robust analysis matters most is effectively unlabeled. We cast this as a cross-temperature domain-shift problem and align the two domains at three levels: an equivariant denoiser at the input level, cross-temperature contrastive learning at the representation level, and a morphology-aware regularizer that steers predictions toward the compact geometry of physical defect structures. Because no atom-wise truth exists at temperature, we further introduce a label-free evaluation suite that scores predicted defect structures along five spatial and physics-based axes, enabling model assessment and selection without high-temperature labels. Near the melting point, the framework identifies vacancies and self-interstitial atoms across face-centered-cubic, body-centered-cubic, and hexagonal-close-packed iron systems with every interstitial localized and zero false detections in every vacancy system against Wigner-Seitz ground truth, with no high-temperature labels used in training. It sustains this fidelity on a million-atom, 2.5 ns trajectory, resolving single vacancy hops and complete Frenkel-pair recombination, and captures grain-boundary phase transformations in aluminum bicrystals, distinguishing two nucleation modes. Multi-level domain alignment thus offers a practical, label-efficient route to temperature-robust structural analysis of large-scale molecular dynamics.

</details>


### [119] [Insight into the molecular dynamics of barocaloric molecular crystals using quasi-elastic neutron scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22091)
*Frederic Rendell-Bhatti,David Boldrin,Markus Appel,Donald A. MacLaren*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于准弹性中子散射（QENS）与非弹性固定窗口扫描（IFWS）的全局拟合方法，用于系统研究压热材料（BC）分子晶体的分子动力学，并通过对新戊二醇（NPG）及其三种衍生物的演示，揭示了羟甲基旋转活化能的变化与氢键网络稳定性密切相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 压热分子晶体（barocaloric molecular crystals）的固态加热/制冷应用依赖其有序-无序相变，但理性设计这类材料需要理解驱动相变熵变的分子动力学的微观机制。现有方法缺乏系统化手段来直接探测此类分子运动。

Method: 采用准弹性中子散射（quasi-elastic neutron scattering, QENS）技术，特别是非弹性固定窗口扫描（inelastic fixed-window scans, IFWSs），结合全局拟合（global fitting）方法，在跨越热力学相变的过程中直接探测分子运动。研究以新戊二醇（neopentyl glycol, NPG）及其三种化学上不同的衍生物为对象，通过固定各样品的尝试频率（attempt frequency）进行跨样品比较。

Result: 通过固定尝试频率，观察到羟甲基（hydroxymethyl）旋转与整体分子旋转在相对活化能和可动分数上存在可测量的差异。其中羟甲基旋转活化能受氢键网络稳定性影响最大，相较于纯NPG，衍生物的活化能变化范围为-3.6%至+11.1%。

Conclusion: 该全局拟合策略能够提取分子旋转中的细微变化，表明准弹性中子散射可作为多功能设计工具，用于指导压热分子晶体的理性设计与优化。

Abstract: Barocaloric (BC) molecular crystals exhibit order-disorder phase transitions that o9er a promising basis for solid-state heating and cooling. However, rational design of these next-generation materials requires microscopic understanding of the molecular dynamics that drive the transitional entropy change. Quasi-elastic neutron scattering (QENS) techniques including inelastic fixed-window scans (IFWSs) enable the direct probing of such molecular motions throughout thermodynamic phase transitions. Here we present a QENS-IFWS global fitting methodology for systematically investigating the molecular dynamics underpinning BC performance in molecular crystals. We demonstrate this methodology on neopentyl glycol (NPG) and three derivatives spanning chemically distinct routes to dynamical perturbation. By fixing attempt frequencies across samples, we observe measurable differences in the relative activation energies and mobile fractions of hydroxymethyl and full-molecular rotations. The largest observed e9ect is on the hydroxymethyl rotation activation energies, which is closely related to hydrogen bond network stability, ranging from -3.6% to +11.1% in the derivatives compared to pure NPG. The capability of this global fitting strategy to extract subtle variations in molecular rotations highlights QENS as a versatile design tool for guiding BC molecular crystal design.

</details>


### [120] [Unified Bonding Entropy Model for Kekulé Graphene Nanoflakes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22206)
*Chang-Chun He,Yu-Jun Zhao,Xiao-Bao Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为“Clar数不变共振空间扩展”的开放壳层稳定化新机制，指出在某些Kekulé石墨烯纳米片（graphene nanoflakes, GNFs）中，电子去配对不增加最大Clar六元环数，但能显著扩大可访问的Clar共振结构数量。通过枚举闭壳层与开放壳层Clar共振结构并结合键合熵模型（bonding entropy model, BEM），作者展示了电子去配对释放配对约束、扩大共振流形并重新分配C–C键占据的过程。BEM预测的未配对电子数目与空间分布与密度泛函理论计算结果高度相关，为筛选开放壳层碳纳米结构提供了一种基于图论的物理透明方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统理论认为Kekulé石墨烯纳米片的开放壳层特性源于电子去配对时Clar芳香π-六元环数目的增加。这一规则仅考虑最大六元环数量，忽略了实现相同Clar计数的共振构型的多重性和空间分布，无法解释所有开放壳层GNFs的稳定的原因，因此需要寻找第二条稳定化路径。

Method: 作者枚举了闭壳层和开放壳层Clar共振结构，提出并验证了“Clar数不变共振空间扩展”机制，并将该分析框架与键合熵模型（BEM）结合，以定量预测未配对电子的数目与空间分布。

Result: BEM预测的未配对电子数量与空间分布与密度泛函理论（density-functional theory）计算的二价自由基特征（diradical character）、局部磁矩、优化的C–C键长及相对能量在广泛的GNFs集合上高度一致。结果表明，电子去配对可以在不增加最大Clar六元环数的情况下，通过扩大共振流形来实现开放壳层稳定化。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一条基于图论且物理上透明的途径，可用于筛选开放壳层碳纳米结构，并设计可调控的分子自旋，而无需依赖最大Clar数目的增加。

Abstract: The open-shell character of Kekulé graphene nanoflakes (GNFs) is conventionally rationalized by the gain of Clar aromatic $π$-sextets upon electron unpairing. While this rule successfully explains many quinoidal diradicaloids, it treats only the maximum number of sextets and neglects the multiplicity and spatial distribution of resonance configurations that realize the same Clar count. Here, we identify a second route to open-shell stabilization in which the maximum Clar-sextet number remains unchanged while the number of accessible Clar resonators increases substantially. We term this mechanism \emph{Clar-number-invariant resonance-space expansion}. By enumerating closed-shell and open-shell Clar resonators and combining this analysis with a bonding entropy model (BEM), we show that electron unpairing can release closed-shell pairing constraints, enlarge the resonance manifold, and redistribute C--C bond occupancies away from localized single- and double-bond limits. The BEM-predicted number and spatial distribution of unpaired electrons correlate strongly with density-functional-theory diradical character, local magnetic moments, optimized C--C bond lengths, and relative energies across a broad set of GNFs. The resulting framework offers a graph-based and physically transparent route for screening open-shell carbon nanostructures and for designing tunable molecular spins without requiring an increase in the maximum Clar number.

</details>


### [121] [Oxygen stoichiometry directs rutile-anatase phase selection through kinetic control of nucleation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22239)
*Han Uk Lee,Hyeon Woo Kim,Ji Min Kim,Dong Won Jeon,Rohan Mishra,Sung Beom Cho*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过增强采样模拟和分子动力学模拟，证明氧的化学计量比（oxygen stoichiometry）可将传统经验合成的变量转化为控制TiO2−x中锐钛矿（anatase）和金红石（rutile）多晶型选择的动力学参数。氧含量通过改变成核势垒景观和核周围弥散中间壳层的Ti-O配位与连通性，驱动壳层基元从锐钛矿型向金红石型演化，最终通过耦合通量模型生成的相对成核速率图与实验观察到的氧依赖合成趋势一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标多晶型的合成仍然更偏经验性而非可预测性，因为结晶往往选择最容易到达的成核路径而非热力学最稳定的相。作者希望将氧的化学计量比这一经验合成变量转化为系统的动力学控制参数，从而为多晶型选择提供可预测的理论框架。

Method: 采用增强采样模拟（enhanced-sampling simulations）研究氧含量对TiO2−x成核势垒景观的影响；利用分子动力学模拟（molecular dynamics simulations）揭示核周围弥散中间壳层的结构特征及其随氧缺陷的演化；构建耦合通量模型（coupled-flux model），将成核势垒竞争与壳层介导的附着/交换过程整合，生成相对成核速率图。

Result: 氧含量改变了成核势垒景观的相对高度，即使金红石在热力学上仍然更有利，也能切换锐钛矿和金红石的可及性。氧缺陷改变了Ti-O配位和连通性，驱动壳层局部基元从类锐钛矿环境向类金红石环境演化。耦合通量模型产生的相对成核速率图与文献报道的氧依赖合成趋势一致。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了由化学计量比控制的中间壳层基元演化作为多晶型选择的动力学起源，为组合耦合结晶（composition-coupled crystallization）中目标相的预测提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Synthesis of a target polymorph remains more empirical than predictive because crystallization often selects the most accessible nucleation pathway rather than the thermodynamically most stable phase. Here, we show that oxygen stoichiometry converts this empirical synthesis variable into a kinetic control parameter for anatase-rutile selection in TiO$_{2-x}$. Enhanced-sampling simulations reveal that oxygen content alters the nucleation-barrier landscape, switching the relative accessibility of anatase and rutile, even while rutile remains thermodynamically favored. Molecular dynamics simulations show the presence of a diffuse intermediate shell around the nucleus, where oxygen deficiency alters Ti-O coordination and connectivity and drives shell-local motif evolution from anatase-like toward rutile-like environments. A coupled-flux model that integrates barrier competition with shell-mediated attachment/exchange yields a relative nucleation-rate map consistent with reported oxygen-dependent synthesis trends. These results establish stoichiometry-controlled intermediate-shell motif evolution as a kinetic origin of polymorph selection and provide a framework for predicting target phases in composition-coupled crystallization.

</details>


### [122] [Machine-learning-guided molecular dynamics simulations of point defect evolution in beta-Ga2O3 during ion implantation and annealing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22282)
*Huawen Li,Mengzhi Yan,Zongwei Xu,Junlei Zhao,Jiale Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 针对β-Ga₂O₃中镓离子注入及退火引发的点缺陷问题，开发了基于相似性匹配与DBSCAN聚类的缺陷识别算法，克服了传统Wigner-Seitz分析方法在高浓度下的局限。研究发现电子阻止效应显著影响离子射程与缺陷浓度估算，1373 K为最佳恢复温度，镓间隙原子占据四面体和八面体位点并驱动β→γ相变，而氧间隙原子的迁移对退火温度敏感并促进氧亚晶格再结晶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: β-Ga₂O₃中镓离子注入和退火会诱导大量点缺陷，而传统Wigner-Seitz（WS）缺陷分析方法在缺陷浓度较高时无法准确区分晶格原子与缺陷原子，亟需开发更稳健的缺陷识别方法。

Method: 开发了一种基于相似性匹配（similarity matching）和DBSCAN聚类（DBSCAN clustering）的缺陷识别算法，用于β-Ga₂O₃缺陷分析；结合SRIM与分子动力学（MD）数据对比研究电子阻止效应，并在五种注量（1至5×10¹⁴ cm⁻²）下进行多尺度分析，利用静水应力（hydrostatic stress）、偏径向分布函数（PRDF）和缺陷浓度等指标追踪缺陷演化。

Result: 算法识别出八种镓间隙原子构型（Gai a至Gai h）；忽略电子阻止效应会高估离子射程和缺陷浓度；1373 K为最优恢复温度；镓间隙原子占据四面体和八面体位点，驱动β→γ相变；注量增加会降低β相恢复并不可逆地增强γ相转变；氧间隙原子（Oi）迁移对退火温度敏感，从而增强氧亚晶格再结晶。

Conclusion: 所提出的缺陷识别算法为高浓度缺陷体系提供了可靠的β-Ga₂O₃分析工具；研究揭示了电子阻止效应、退火温度与注量对缺陷演化和相变的综合影响机制，为β-Ga₂O₃离子注入工艺优化提供了理论依据。

Abstract: In beta-gallium oxide (beta-Ga2O3), Ga-ion implantation and annealing induce abundant point defects. To overcome conventional Wigner-Seitz (WS) defect analysis limitations, a defect identification algorithm based on similarity matching and DBSCAN clustering is developed for beta-Ga2O3. It distinguishes lattice atoms from defects at high concentrations and identifies eight Ga interstitial configurations (Gaia to Gaih). Comparing SRIM and MD data highlights electronic stopping effects: neglecting them overestimates ion range and defect concentration. Across five fluences (1 to 5 x 10^14 cm-2), 1373 K is the optimal recovery temperature. Multiscale analyses using hydrostatic stress, PRDF, and defect concentration reveal defect evolution. Ga interstitials (Gai) occupy tetrahedral and octahedral sites, driving a defect-mediated phase transition from beta- to gamma-Ga2O3. Increasing fluences reduce beta-phase recovery and increase gamma-phase transformation irreversibly. Oxygen interstitial (Oi) migration is sensitive to annealing temperature, which enhances O-sublattice recrystallization.

</details>


### [123] [Diagnosing and narrowing the simulation-to-real gap in powder X-ray diffraction with a wet-dry agentic loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22400)
*Shaoguang Wang,Weiyu Guo,Ben Fei,Xiaohong Shao,Zhihui Wang,Wanli Ouyang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文介绍Xtalyst，一个基于代理协调（agent-orchestrated）的深度学习系统，用于粉末X射线衍射（PXRD）分析，包含物相鉴定、精修和校准性质预测模块。其核心发现是仿真到真实数据（simulation-to-real）的差距本质上是结构性的，而非加性噪声问题：纠正小规模峰位漂移可使中位检索相关性翻倍以上，而合成降噪对真实谱无提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粉末X射线衍射（PXRD）是晶体物质的标准表征手段，但随着实验室自动化采集的推进，谱图分析成为限制通量的瓶颈环节。现有深度学习分析器在模拟谱图上表现优异，但在实测谱图上性能显著退化，这种仿真到真实（simulation-to-real）差距的根源尚不清晰，阻碍了自动化分析的可靠部署。

Method: 论文通过消融实验揭示仿真-真实差距的结构性特征：对比合成降噪与峰位漂移校正对真实谱图检索相关性的不同影响，并引入真实谱微调（real-spectrum fine-tuning）、峰对齐重排序（peak-aligned reranking）和重校准（recalibration）等策略。在此基础上，Xtalyst将这些策略整合为代理协调（agent-orchestrated）系统，覆盖物相鉴定、精修与校准性质预测三大模块。

Result: 在冻结的留出测试集（n=534）上，各模块在两种划分上均复现了开发阶段的主要发现，包括合成锚点欠覆盖（synthetic-anchor under-coverage）现象及其在两池间的量级差异；留出精修能够收敛并保持对称性但未达到轮廓质量拟合水平；在衍射仪实测中，湿-干推荐-重扫-重分析（wet-dry recommend-rescan-reanalyze）循环使盲测硅标准从失败翻转为门控通过（gated PASS），并改变了多金属合金中次要相的解析结果。

Conclusion: 仿真到真实的PXRD分析差距是结构性的而非加性噪声，峰位漂移校正是关键瓶颈所在；通过真实谱微调、峰对齐重排序与重校准的组合策略，配合代理编排的闭环工作流，可以有效弥合该差距并提升实测谱图分析的可靠性。

Abstract: Powder X-ray diffraction (PXRD) is the routine probe of crystalline matter, yet its analysis is the rate-limiting step as laboratories automate acquisition. Deep-learning analyzers excel on simulated patterns and degrade on measured ones. This simulation-to-real gap is structural, not additive: synthetic denoising gives no measurable lift on real spectra, whereas correcting a small peak-position drift more than doubles median retrieval correlation. Real-spectrum fine-tuning, peak-aligned reranking, and recalibration narrow what remains and restore the coverage synthetic anchors lose. Xtalyst integrates these in an agent-orchestrated system spanning phase identification, refinement, and calibrated property prediction. On a frozen held-out partition (n=534) each module measured on both splits reproduces its development finding -- including the synthetic-anchor under-coverage, whose magnitude differs between the two pools -- while held-out refinement converges and preserves symmetry without reaching profile-quality fits, and on a diffractometer its wet-dry recommend-rescan-reanalyze loop flips a blinded silicon standard to a gated PASS and changes which minor phase is resolved on a multi-metal alloy.

</details>


### [124] [Flexo-Strain Engineering of Phonons and Ferrons in Thin Films of Van der Waals Ferrielectrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22441)
*Anna N. Morozovska,Eugene A. Eliseev,Yujie Zhu,Mykola Yelisieiev,Hanna V. Shevliakova,Yulian M. Vysochanskii,Venkatraman Gopalan,Long-Qing Chen,Jia-Mian Hu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文基于朗道-金兹堡-德文希尔（Landau-Ginzburg-Devonshire）理论，研究了范德瓦尔斯铁电薄膜（如CuInP2S6）中挠曲电耦合（flexoelectric coupling）与失配应变（mismatch strain）对软光学声子、声学声子和铁子（ferrons）色散关系的影响，发现应变可使声学声子频率在非零波矢处趋于零，预示着挠曲应变诱导的非公度极性相的出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体相范德瓦尔斯铁电材料中，挠曲电耦合对极化涨落和弹性应变的涨落具有显著影响，可改变软光学声子与声学声子以及铁子的色散规律。由于尺寸、梯度和应变效应决定薄膜的相图与极化行为，作者合理推测挠曲耦合和失配应变也应强烈影响薄膜中声子与铁子的色散特性。

Method: 采用朗道-金兹堡-德文希尔（Landau-Ginzburg-Devonshire）唯象理论方法，对范德瓦尔斯铁电薄膜CuInP2S6中由晶格常数失配产生的弹性应变如何影响软声子与铁子的色散关系进行解析推导与分析。

Result: 研究发现软声子与铁子的色散强烈依赖于弹性应变的符号和大小。声学声子和铁子的频率在非零波矢k>k_cr处趋于零，其中临界波矢k_cr由失配应变、挠曲电耦合强度与温度共同决定。随着拉伸应变的增加，声学声子频率归零，表明可能出现由挠曲应变效应诱导的空间调制非公度极性相。

Conclusion: 该文解析推导的结果为范德瓦尔斯铁电薄膜中软声子与铁子色散的挠曲应变工程（flexo-strain engineering）开辟了途径，为通过应变调控实现非公度极性相的主动设计提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The influence of the flexoelectric coupling on the fluctuations of electric polarization and elastic strains can lead to the principal changes of the dispersion law of soft optical and acoustic phonons and ferrons in a bulk van der Waals ferrielectrics. Since the size, gradient and strain effects determine phase diagrams and polarization behavior in thin films, it is reasonable to assume that the flexocoupling and mismatch strains should have a strong influence on the dispersion of phonons and ferrons in thin films of van der Waals ferroelectrics. Using the Landau-Ginzburg-Devonshire approach, in this work we reveal that the dispersion of soft phonons and ferrons is strongly dependent on the sign and magnitude of elastic strains, which originate from the lattice constants mismatch in thin strained films of van der Waals ferrielectric CuInP2S6. In particular, the frequency of acoustic phonons and ferrons tends to zero at nonzero wavevectors k>k_cr, where the critical value of the wavevector k_cr is determined by the mismatch strain, flexoelectric coupling strength and temperature. Zeroing of the acoustic phonon frequency, that appears with increase of tensile strains, indicates a possible emergence of a spatially modulated incommensurate polar phase induced by the flexo-strain effects. Analytical results, derived in this work, open the way for flexo-strain engineering of soft phonon and ferron dispersion in thin films of van der Waals ferrielectrics.

</details>


### [125] [A test drive for exchange-correlation functionals on noncollinear magnets: Mn$_3$Ir, Mn$_3$Ge, NiS$_2$, and YMnO$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22464)
*Marie-Therese Huebsch,Martijn Marsman,Jacques K. Desmarais,Stefano Pittalis,Fabien Tran*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理计算，系统比较了两种非共线自旋流密度泛函理论（SCDFT）泛函与四种局域共线自旋密度泛函理论（SDFT）扩展泛函在多个非共线d电子体系中的表现。结果表明，在所有测试泛函中，仅SCDFT泛函能恢复NiS₂实验基态的自旋织构（spin texture），且计算成本与标准半局域近似相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非共线磁体的第一性原理描述具有挑战性，而真正非共线的交换关联近似的实际行为在很大程度上未经检验。SCDFT因其局域U(1)×SU(2)规范不变性这一精确条件，为构建非共线近似提供了比SDFT更严格的物理约束框架。

Method: 使用维也纳从头算模拟软件包（VASP），将两种近期发展的非共线SCDFT泛函（NCMSCAN和LFNCBR89-NCCS）与四种常用泛函（PZ、PW92、PBE、SCAN）的局域共线SDFT扩展进行对比，测试对象为若干非共线d电子体系。

Result: 在所有受测泛函中，仅SCDFT泛函能够恢复NiS₂实验基态的自旋织构，且计算成本与标准半局域近似相当。但两种SCDFT泛函在平衡体积、带隙（band gap）和局域磁矩（on-site magnetic moment）方面存在差异。

Conclusion: 该项系统性调查表明，SCDFT在捕捉非共线物理方面具有实用性，所测试的SCDFT近似能够有效描述非共线磁序，优于传统SDFT框架下的局域共线扩展。

Abstract: A first-principles description of noncollinear magnets is challenging, and the practical behavior of genuinely noncollinear exchange-correlation approximations is largely untested. Spin-current density-functional theory (SCDFT) admits noncollinear approximations constrained by local U(1)$\times$SU(2) gauge invariance, an exact condition unavailable in the more restrictive framework of spin-DFT (SDFT). Using the Vienna ab initio simulation package (VASP), we compare two recently developed noncollinear SCDFT functionals, NCMSCAN and LFNCBR89-NCCS, against locally collinear SDFT extensions of four common functionals (PZ, PW92, PBE, and SCAN) for several noncollinear $d$-electron systems. The key finding is that among all tested functionals, only the SCDFT functionals recover the spin texture of the experimental ground state of NiS$_2$, and they do so at a cost comparable to standard semi-local approximations. Yet the two SCDFT functionals differ in equilibrium volumes, band gaps, and on-site magnetic moments. Overall, this survey illustrates the usefulness of SCDFT in capturing noncollinear physics with the tested approximations.

</details>


### [126] [High-throughput identification of ferromagnetic Kagome candidates in the AT6X4 and AT6X5 families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22666)
*Shiya Chen,Vladimir Antropov,Yang Sun*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过高通量密度泛函理论（density-functional theory, DFT）计算，系统研究了层状kagome化合物AT6X4和AT6X5家族的热力学稳定性、共线磁基态和电子结构。筛选出78种取代成分，复现了已知Fe基Ge化合物的稳定性与反铁磁特征，并鉴定出六个具有稳健铁磁性的新候选材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层状kagome材料因其几何阻挫导致的丰富磁性与电子态而备受关注，但AT6X4和AT6X5家族的磁性基态与稳定成分尚未被系统探索。作者旨在通过高通量计算筛选填补这一空白，寻找可实现铁磁性和kagome衍生电子态的新材料平台。

Method: 以实验报道的结构类型为模板，对AT6X4和AT6X5两个家族各筛选了78种取代成分，进行高通量密度泛函理论计算。研究涵盖热力学稳定性分析、共线自旋构型下的磁性基态判定，以及交换作用分析，并进一步考察代表性铁磁成员的电子能带结构。

Result: 计算成功复现了已知Fe基Ge化合物的稳定性与反铁磁性，并识别出六个具有稳健铁磁性的新稳定候选材料。共线自旋计算显示清晰的化学依赖趋势：稳定的Fe基Ge化合物主要采用AFM2基态，而Mn基Ge化合物一致倾向铁磁序。交换分析表明磁性相空间由竞争的层间相互作用主导，与AT6X6 kagome磁体的机制一致。铁磁成员在K点附近保留kagome衍生的色散能带特征，其中AT6X5相表现出更强的能带折叠与杂化。

Conclusion: AT6X4和AT6X5被确立为有望实现铁磁性和kagome衍生电子态的新型层状kagome材料家族，为后续实验合成与物性研究提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We present a systematic high-throughput density-functional theory study of the thermodynamic stability, collinear magnetic ground states, and electronic structures of layered kagome compounds in the AT6X4 and AT6X5 families. Using the experimentally reported structure types as templates, we screened 78 substitutional compositions in each family. Our calculations reproduce the stability and antiferromagnetic character of the known Fe-based Ge compounds and identify six additional stable candidates with robust ferromagnetism. Within collinear spin configurations, we find a clear chemistry-dependent trend: stable Fe-based Ge compounds predominantly adopt AFM2 ground states, whereas stable Mn-based Ge compounds consistently favor ferromagnetic order. Exchange analysis further shows that the magnetic phase space is governed by competing interlayer interactions, consistent with the mechanism established for AT6X6 kagome magnets. Representative ferromagnetic members from the two structural families also retain kagome-derived dispersive band features near K, although the AT6X5 phase exhibits stronger band folding and hybridization. Overall, these results establish AT6X4 and AT6X5 as promising layered kagome families for realizing ferromagnetism and kagome-derived electronic states.

</details>


### [127] [Chiral Phonons and Giant Anisotropic Photoresponse in Quasi-1D van der Waals Semiconductor ZrSnS3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22700)
*Zahir Muhammad,Shashi B. Mishra,Gayatri,Grzegorz Krasucki,Wajid Ali,Katarzyna Olkowska-Pucko,Obaid Iqbal,Zia Ur Rehman,Aziz Ur Rahman,Maciej R. Molas,Lin Xiaoyang,Weisheng Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究通过角度分辨和偏振依赖的拉曼光谱（Raman spectroscopy）、密度泛函理论（density functional theory）计算以及光电器件制造，系统研究了低维范德华半导体ZrSnS₃中各向异性晶格动力学、声子极化特征及其光电响应，揭示了极化、晶格振动与电荷载流子之间的耦合机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低维范德华半导体具有降低的对称性，为探索各向异性物理性质提供了独特平台。准一维家族MXQ₃具有显著的结构各向异性，其之字形原子链影响如双折射（birefringence）等光学现象，因此需要深入研究其晶格动力学和各向异性响应。

Method: 采用角度分辨和偏振依赖的拉曼光谱研究ZrSnS₃各向异性晶格动力学；利用变温测量揭示非谐声子（anharmonic phonon）行为；结合密度泛函理论计算验证实验拉曼光谱；通过第一性原理分析研究角动量类声子纹理（angular-momentum-like phonon textures）在Γ点附近模式杂化与避免交叉区域的涌现；并基于薄ZrSnS₃纳米线制造光电器件进行光电性能测试。

Result: 温度相关测量揭示非谐声子行为，表明强声子-声子耦合；密度泛函理论计算与实验拉曼光谱良好一致。观察到圆偏振光下的螺旋度依赖性强度以及低、高频模式间声子强度反转，这是由Zr/Sn链极化控制的手性声子（chiral phonon）特征。第一性原理分析表明，角动量类声子纹理可在Γ点附近模式杂化和避免交叉区域涌现。器件在520 nm激光（1 mW/cm²）激发下表现出50 mA/W的光响应度，并具有显著的功率可标度各向异性光电响应和明确的优先极化方向。

Conclusion: 这些结果阐明了ZrSnS₃中极化、晶格振动与电荷载流子之间的耦合机制，为极化敏感光电子学（polarization-sensitive optoelectronics）和定向量子输运（directional quantum transport）应用提供了有前景的材料。

Abstract: Low-dimensional van der Waals semiconductors with reduced symmetry provide a unique platform for exploring anisotropic physical properties. The quasi-one-dimensional family MXQ$_3$ (M = Hf, Zr; X = Sn; Q = S, Se) exhibits notable structural anisotropy, where zigzag atomic chains influence optical phenomena such as birefringence. This study investigates anisotropic lattice dynamics in ZrSnS$_3$ using angle- and polarization-dependent Raman spectroscopy. Temperature-dependent measurements reveal anharmonic phonon behavior, indicating strong phonon-phonon coupling. Density functional theory calculations show good agreement with the experimentally observed Raman spectra, validating the microscopic description of the lattice dynamics. We also observe a helicity-dependent intensity and a reversal in phonon intensity between lower- and higher-frequency modes under circularly polarized light, which is characteristic of chiral phonons governed by the polarization of the Zr/Sn chains. Our first-principles analysis further shows that angular-momentum-like phonon textures can emerge away from the $Γ$-point near mode-hybridization and avoided-crossing regions, providing microscopic insight into the observed helicity-dependent Raman signatures. Furthermore, we fabricate an optoelectronic device from a thin ZrSnS$_3$ nanowire, demonstrating a photoresponsivity of 50~mA/W under 520~nm laser excitation (1~mW/cm$^2$). The device exhibits a pronounced, power-scalable anisotropic photoresponse with a clear preferred polarization direction. These results highlight the coupling mechanisms between polarization, lattice vibrations, and charge carriers in ZrSnS$_3$, establishing it as a promising material for polarization-sensitive optoelectronics and directional quantum transport.

</details>


### [128] [A Novel Decoupling Method for Investigating Distinct Domain Evolution in Ferroelectric Film](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22755)
*Keying Sun,Jiajun Qiu,Hao Li,Zongwei Shang,Lang Zeng,Ming Li,Lining Zhang,Runsheng Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新型畴去耦（domain decoupling）方法，用于在HfO2基铁电器件中分别定量追踪不同铁电畴群体的开关行为，超越传统电荷极化表征技术仅能给出平均响应的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统极化表征技术只能提供铁电薄膜的平均响应，无法解析畴相关的可靠性机制（如印记和疲劳），限制了HfO2基铁电器件的可靠性研究。

Method: 提出三畴模型（three-domain model），将畴群体划分为向上不可开关畴、向下不可开关畴和可开关畴（Pd）；结合互补的开关电流（switching-current）测量，选择性提取并重构不同畴群体的响应，实现对电循环过程中各畴群体数量的定量追踪。

Result: 该方法能够定量追踪电循环过程中各个畴群体随时间的动态演化，并以电循环后的时间依赖演化分析作为演示验证了方法的可行性。

Conclusion: 该办法为研究铁电器件中的印记（imprint）、疲劳（fatigue）及其他可靠性相关现象提供了新的分析途径。

Abstract: Conventional polarization characterization techniques provide only the averaged response of ferroelectric films, limiting the investigation of domain-dependent reliability mechanisms in HfO2-based ferroelectrics. In this work, a new domain decoupling method is proposed to separately analyze ferroelectric domains with distinct switching behaviors. A three-domain model consisting of upward non-switchable domains, downward non-switchable domains, and switchable domains (Pd) is first introduced to describe heterogeneous domain populations during electrical cycling. By combining complementary switching-current measurements, the responses of different domain populations can be selectively extracted and reconstructed. The proposed method enables quantitative tracking of individual domain populations during cycling. As a demonstration, the method is further applied to analyze the time-dependent evolution of domain populations after electrical cycling. This approach provides a new route for investigating imprint, fatigue, and other reliability-related phenomena in ferroelectric devices.

</details>


### [129] [Mechanistic Phase-Field Modelling of Woven-Domain Formation in Ferroelectric Material](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22776)
*P G Kubendran Amos*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文发展了一个无量纲相场（phase-field）框架，用于研究K TN:Li（铌酸钾钽锂）块体材料在缓慢冷却通过铁电相变（ferroelectric transition）时自发形成三维编织铁电畴织物（woven ferroelectric domain fabric）的物理途径。二维大区域模型揭示了依赖于冷却速率的受阻交叉前驱体的形成，三维扩展模型则在所探索的无量纲参数范围内证实了慢冷条件下相交畴壁族之间深度次序的几何分离与交换，从而产生真正的编织拓扑结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期实验报道了K TN:Li在充分缓慢冷却通过铁电相变时自发形成三维编织铁电畴织物，但缺乏对其形成机制的理论理解。本文旨在通过相场模拟，考察一种物理上合理的路径来解释这一奇特织构的产生条件与演化过程。

Method: 本文建立了一个无量纲相场框架。首先构建大区域二维模型，耦合一级铁电热力学（first-order ferroelectric thermodynamics）、静电与弹性相互作用、成分调制（compositional modulation）和有效电荷屏蔽（effective charge screening），以解析冷却速率依赖的受阻交叉前驱体形成。随后扩展到三维模型，引入立方梯度各向异性（cubic gradient anisotropy）、挠曲电耦合（flexoelectric coupling）和应变梯度正则化（strain-gradient regularization），检验平面前驱体能否发展为真正的编织拓扑。

Result: 慢冷条件下会产生持续存在的电荷相关交叉结构，而快速冷却路径则不会。三维模拟中，慢冷可产生相交畴壁族之间深度次序的几何分离与交换，快速冷却则不能；这一区别在独立多次模拟中可重复，并对时间步长细化及计算域尺寸变化保持稳健。模拟还表明慢冷为拓扑选择提供了动力学窗口，全局电荷弛豫可与幸存交叉处强局域电荷集中共存。

Conclusion: 模拟结果支持慢冷为编织拓扑的形成提供动力学窗口这一假说。但作者明确承认模型未针对K TN:Li的材料特定系数进行标定，且无法再现实验观测到的低温下编织态的消失现象，因此模型仍具有定性与探索性特征。

Abstract: A recent experimental study reports the spontaneous formation of a three-dimensional woven ferroelectric domain fabric in bulk KTN:Li during sufficiently slow cooling through the ferroelectric transition. The present work develops a nondimensional phase-field framework to examine a physically plausible route by which such a state can emerge. A large-area two-dimensional model first resolves the cooling-rate-dependent formation of a frustrated crossing precursor by combining first-order ferroelectric thermodynamics, electrostatic and elastic interactions, compositional modulation, and effective charge screening. Slow cooling produces a persistent population of charge-associated crossings, whereas the corresponding fast-cooling pathway does not. A three-dimensional extension then incorporates cubic gradient anisotropy, flexoelectric coupling, and strain-gradient regularization to examine whether the planar precursor can develop a genuine woven topology. Within the explored nondimensional parameter regime, slow cooling produces a directly resolved geometrical separation and exchange of depth ordering between the intersecting wall families, whereas the corresponding fast-cooling pathway does not. This distinction is reproduced across independent realisations and remains robust to timestep refinement and changes in computational domain size. The simulations suggest that slow cooling provides a kinetic window for topological selection and that global charge relaxation can coexist with strong local charge concentration at surviving crossings. The model is not calibrated to material-specific KTN:Li coefficients and does not reproduce the experimentally observed low-temperature disappearance of the woven state.

</details>


### [130] [A Topological Magnonic Black-White Hole Crystal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22783)
*D. Galvez-Poblete,R. M. Otxoa,A. S. Nunez,S. Allende*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文揭示了反铁磁自旋波（antiferromagnetic spin waves）的低能动力学可映射为有质量的Klein-Gordon标量场，并利用非均匀自旋流形成黑洞和白洞视界（horizon）。通过周期性排列亚磁声（submagnonic）与超磁声（supermagnonic）区域，构建了非互易磁子晶体（non-reciprocal magnonic crystal），实现了能带拓扑转变和混合磁子-反磁子边缘态，从而将类比视界物理（analogue-horizon physics）与拓扑磁子学（topological magnonics）直接联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在将引力类比系统中的视界物理拓展至凝聚态平台，具体而言，希望利用固态系统实现黑洞和白洞视界，并进一步探讨视界物理与拓扑量子输运之间的深层联系，为电学可调谐的拓扑磁子学提供理论基础。

Method: 文章证明非均匀自旋流可产生有效磁子流，从而形成黑洞/白洞视界；视界对腔表现出共振输运和超辐射样放大（superradiant-like amplification），其机制涉及磁子-反磁子混合（magnon-antimagnon mixing）。进一步，对周期性亚磁声与超磁声区域进行排列，使体带隙（bulk gap）闭合与重开，引发由Zak相位变化表征的拓扑相变，并产生混合磁子-反磁子边缘态。

Result: 研究发现：视界对腔存在共振输运和超辐射样放大效应；周期性排列的超磁声区域可开启负范数（negative-norm）传播通道，驱动拓扑相变，体现在Zak相位改变与混合边缘态的出现，从而确立了类比视界物理与拓扑磁子学之间的直接联系。

Conclusion: 该研究为电学可调控的拓扑磁子带结构提供了新途径，表明类比视界物理可以在固态自旋系统中实现，并为拓扑磁子学与模拟引力物理的交叉研究开辟了新方向。

Abstract: The low-energy dynamics of antiferromagnetic spin waves map onto a massive Klein-Gordon scalar field, providing a solid-state platform for analogue-horizon physics. We show that an inhomogeneous spin current generates an effective magnon flow capable of forming black- and white-hole horizons. Horizon-pair cavities exhibit resonant transport and superradiant-like amplification mediated by magnon-antimagnon mixing. Our central result is that a periodic arrangement of submagnonic and supermagnonic regions forms a non-reciprocal magnonic crystal in which entering the supermagnonic regime makes negative-norm propagating channels available and enables the bulk gap to close and reopen. This drives a topological transition characterized by a change in the Zak phase and the emergence of a hybrid magnon-antimagnon edge state. These results establish a direct connection between analogue-horizon physics and topological magnonics, providing an electrically tunable route for controlling magnonic band topology.

</details>


### [131] [Extreme mixing enables broadband high-temperature electromagnetic absorption in high-entropy perovskites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22792)
*Ziyu Feng,Fangchao Gu,Lei Zhuang,Wu Wang,Hulei Yu,Yanhui Chu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究通过极端混合策略，在高熵钙钛矿中实现了高达9.3 GHz的超宽带有效吸收带宽，且可在600摄氏度下工作，超越了此前报道的氧化物吸波材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发具有宽频宽、高温电磁吸收性能的氧化物吸波材料是该领域的长期目标，但现有氧化物的吸收性能一直不够理想。

Method: 采用激光驱动的可控合成技术，成功将多达13种阳离子元素（其中8种占据A位、5种占据B位）掺入高熵钙钛矿，实现极端混合（extreme mixing）。

Result: 极端混合最大程度地促进了原子尺度界面的形成，显著增强了原子级界面极化损耗，从而实现了9.3 GHz的超宽有效吸收带宽且工作温度可达600摄氏度；然而在700摄氏度时，由于电导率大幅升高导致阻抗失配（impedance mismatch），有效吸收带宽下降至4.6 GHz。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一种新的极端混合设计范式，为实现具有前所未有的高温电磁吸收性能的高熵氧化物吸波材料提供了途径。

Abstract: Developing oxide absorbers with broadband high-temperature electromagnetic absorption performance has long been desirable in the electromagnetic absorption field, yet their absorption performance has remained unsatisfactory. Here, through an extreme mixing strategy, we successfully develop an ultrabroad effective absorption bandwidth (EAB) of 9.3 GHz in high-entropy perovskites up to 600 degree celsius, surpassing the previously reported oxide absorbers. Specifically, we achieve the extreme mixing in high-entropy perovskites by the successful incorporation of up to 13 cation elements, with 8 and 5 cations occupying the A- and B-sites, respectively, using a laser-driven controllable synthesis technique. Such extreme mixing maximally promotes the formation of atomic-scale interfaces to markedly amplify atomic-level interfacial polarization loss for the effective dissipation of electromagnetic wave energy, thereby giving rise to a remarkably broad EAB of 9.3 GHz in high-entropy perovskites up to 600 degree celsius. Owing to the profoundly increased electrical conductivity that causes impedance mismatch at elevated temperatures, however, their EAB deteriorates to 4.6 GHz at 700 degree celsius. This work establishes a new extreme mixing design paradigm for enabling high-entropy oxide absorbers with unprecedented high-temperature EM absorption performance.

</details>


### [132] [Observation of current-induced orbital quadrupole accumulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22838)
*Geun-Hee Lee,Yubin Ji,Yongho Park,Changmin An,San Ko,Hye-Won Ko,Jinseob Lim,Jung Hyun Oh,Farzad Mahfouzi,Byong-Guk Park,Kab-Jin Kim,Mark D. Stiles,Kyoung-Whan Kim,Paul M. Haney,Kyung-Jin Lee*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用偏振分辨Kerr显微术（polarization-resolved Kerr microscopy）在Ti和Pt金属表面观测到电流诱导的轨道四极矩（orbital quadrupole）积累，并通过分离光学电导率中的对称与反对称分量，证明四极光响应在两种金属中均超过偶极响应，揭示了不依赖自旋-轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）的电荷-轨道转换新通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋电子学（spintronics）和轨道电子学（orbitronics）传统上依赖电流诱导的自旋与轨道角动量（即磁性偶极子）积累，但电子轨道本质上携带超越偶极的多极矩，其中秩-2轨道四极矩是主导项。此前电流诱导的轨道极化现象尚未被系统地视为多极性质，作者旨在探索这一被忽视的物理通道。

Method: 采用偏振分辨Kerr显微术观测Ti和Pt金属表面电流诱导的轨道四极矩积累；通过分离非对角光学电导率（off-diagonal optical conductivity）的对称与反对称分量，将时间反演偶（time-reversal-even）的四极响应从传统的时间反演奇（time-reversal-odd）的磁偶极响应中区分出来；并结合第一性原理（first-principles）计算分析所测响应。

Result: 在Ti和Pt两种自旋-轨道耦合强度显著不同的金属中，观测到电流诱导的轨道四极矩积累，且四极光响应均超过偶极响应。第一性原理分析表明，两种金属中的四极积累量级相当，尽管其自旋-轨道耦合强度差异悬殊，该结果与一种此前未被识别的、不依赖自旋-轨道耦合的电荷-轨道转换通道一致。

Conclusion: 该研究确立电流诱导的轨道极化本质上是多极性的，将电流诱导现象从偶极领域拓展至多极领域，并为电学调控轨道有序相（orbital-ordered phases）开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Spintronics and orbitronics rely on current-induced accumulations of magnetic dipoles: spin and orbital angular momentum. However, electronic orbitals inherently carry multipoles beyond the dipole, with the rank-2 orbital quadrupole as the leading term. Here we use polarization-resolved Kerr microscopy to observe current-induced orbital-quadrupole accumulation at the surfaces of Ti and Pt, metals with markedly different spin--orbit-coupling strengths. By separating the symmetric and antisymmetric components of the off-diagonal optical conductivity, we isolate the time-reversal-even quadrupolar response from the conventional time-reversal-odd magnetic-dipolar one, and find that the quadrupolar optical response exceeds the dipolar one in both metals. First-principles analysis of the measured responses indicates that the quadrupole accumulations are of the same order of magnitude in the two metals despite their widely different spin--orbit-coupling strengths, consistent with a previously unidentified channel of charge-to-orbital conversion that does not require spin--orbit coupling. Our findings establish that current-induced orbital polarization is fundamentally multipolar, expanding current-induced phenomena from the dipolar to the multipolar regime and opening a route to electrical control of orbital-ordered phases.

</details>


### [133] [Composition- and Ordering-Dependent Evolution of Simulated Kikuchi Patterns of Au-Ni Alloys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22931)
*Camila A. Teixeira,Lukas Berners,Sandra Korte-Kerzel,Ulrich Kerzel*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过模拟二元Au-Ni体系的菊池花样（Kikuchi patterns），系统研究晶格参数、化学组成、部分位点占据和有序化对花样特征的影响。研究发现归一化强度分布和能带宽度是区分菊池花样的关键描述量，可为未来基于表示学习的机器学习索引方法提供基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解菊池花样如何随细微材料变化而改变，对于开发面向结构和化学复杂体系（如具有亚晶格有序或偏析缺陷相的体系）的基于机器学习（ML）的索引方法至关重要。

Method: 利用EMsoft仿真模拟二元Au-Ni体系的菊池花样，覆盖从纯镍到纯金的全部成分范围，并纳入假想的L1₂ Au₃Ni有序结构；仿真参数通过与实验花样对比进行优化；采用归一化互相关（normalized cross correlation）和差异图（difference maps）等方法分析花样差异。

Result: 归一化互相关对花样间的细微差异灵敏度有限；随金含量增加，平均强度和显著反射数量系统性上升，但99%金到纯金样品间平均强度出现四倍异常增长，揭示了EMsoft中部分位点占据模拟的局限性；20%金样品在{111}和{200}能带上表现出增强的归一化强度；化学组成对平均强度和强反射数量起主导作用；L1₂有序化增加了强反射数量和平均强度，并重新分配了归一化强度分布。

Conclusion: 归一化强度分布和能带宽度是区分菊池花样的关键描述量，可作为未来基于表示学习的索引方法中的有效特征。

Abstract: Understanding how Kikuchi patterns behave with subtle material variations is essential for developing machine learning (ML) based indexing methods for structurally and chemically complex cases, such as phases with potential sub-lattice order or (meta)stable defects with segregation (defect phases). Simulated Kikuchi patterns of the binary Au-Ni system were systematically analysed to investigate the effects of lattice parameter, chemical composition, partial site occupancy and ordering. The full compositional range from pure nickel to pure gold was considered, including a hypothetical ordered L$1_2$ Au$_3$Ni structure. Simulation parameters were optimised by comparison with experimental patterns. Normalized cross correlation showed limited sensibility to subtle differences between patterns. EMsoft simulation results revealed a systematic increase in mean intensity and more reflections contribute significantly as gold content increases. A surprising four-fold increase in mean intensity from 99 % gold to the pure gold sample highlighted limitations of partial site occupancy simulation by EMsoft. Difference maps showed enhanced normalised intensity along the {111} and {200} bands for the sample with 20 % gold compared to higher gold compositions. By isolating lattice parameter and chemical composition effects, chemical composition contributes predominantly to the mean intensity and strong reflections count, although the normalised intensity distribution was affected by both. Introducing L$1_2$ ordering increased the number of strong reflections and mean intensity, and redistributed normalised intensity along {111} and {200} bands and selected zone axis. Normalised intensity distribution and band width, are key descriptions to distinguish the Kikuchi patterns, which can be incorporated in future representation learning based indexing methods.

</details>


### [134] [Discovery of New Zintl Films and Nanowires Grown by Topotaxy Conversion of III-V Semiconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22989)
*Man Suk Song,Lothar Houben,Jean Souza,Edanel Fishbein,Moshe Haim,Ambikesh Gupta,Yufei Zhao,Anna-Eden Kossoy,Binghai Yan,Haim Beidenkopf,Hadas Shtrikman*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文报道了将拓扑取向互交换（topotaxial mutual-exchange）生长方法从InAs扩展到更广泛的III-V族半导体及三维衬底，成功制备了Eu5Ga2As6和Eu5Al2As6薄膜及Eu5Ga2As6纳米线，揭示了Pnma对称性新Zintl框架、复杂磁性相图及热电应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Zintl相因多样的结构、磁性、热电、拓扑和光学性质而受到广泛关注，但此前仅有利用InAs纳米线通过拓扑转换合成的Eu3In2As4和Eu5In2As6纳米线。作者希望验证该方法是否可推广至更广泛的III-V族半导体体系、三维衬底薄膜及不同晶体结构的纳米线。

Method: 采用分子束外延（molecular beam epitaxy, MBE）结合拓扑取向互交换方法，将GaAs和AlAs衬底及GaAs纳米线（纤锌矿和闪锌矿结构）分别转换为Eu5Ga2As6和Eu5Al2As6薄膜及Eu5Ga2As6纳米线；利用显微学和衍射技术表征结构，并通过从头计算（ab initio calculations）预测电子带隙。

Result: 成功生长出两种新化合物Eu5Ga2As6和Eu5Al2As6薄膜及Eu5Ga2As6纳米线；虽然化学计量与Eu5In2As6相同，但呈现单一Pnma对称群而非Pbam；两类Zintl材料均为半导体，带隙分别为0.79 eV和0.90 eV；磁性测量揭示了三次磁转变，包含两种不同反铁磁序及自旋翻转转变。

Conclusion: 拓扑取向互交换方法可普适应用于多种III-V族半导体，为直接从相关III-V族材料中发现和集成磁性Zintl薄膜与纳米线提供了外延兼容的路线；Pnma较低对称性和复杂结构有望抑制晶格热导率，在热电、自旋电子学和探测器技术方面具有应用前景。

Abstract: Zintl phases draw broad interest for their diverse structural, magnetic, thermoelectric, topological and optical properties. Recently, Zintl Eu$_3$In$_2$As$_4$ and Eu$_5$In$_2$As$_6$ nanowires with axion magneto-topology have been synthesized by molecular beam epitaxy via topotactic conversion of InAs wurtzite and zincblende nanowires. Here we extend this methodology, demonstrating that topotaxial mutual-exchange growth applies not only to a broader set of III-V semiconductors beyond InAs but also to three-dimensional substrates whose surfaces are converted into Zintl thin films, as well as to nanowires. We report the growth of two new compounds: Eu$_5$Ga$_2$As$_6$ thin films converted from GaAs substrates, and Eu$_5$Al$_2$As$_6$ thin films from AlAs films. We also convert GaAs nanowires of both wurtzite and zincblende structures into Eu$_5$Ga$_2$As$_6$ nanowires. Though the stoichiometry is the same as in the previously reported Eu$_5$In$_2$As$_6$ case, microscopy and diffraction reveal a single-phase Pnma symmetry group rather than Pbam, highlighting symmetry-guided topotactic pathways to new Zintl frameworks. The compounds host an intricate magnetic phase diagram with three magnetic transitions, including two distinct antiferromagnetic orders and a canted antiferromagnetic phase that evolves into another antiferromagnetic phase under applied field through a spin-flop transition. Ab initio calculations predict that both Zintls are semiconductors with gaps of 0.79 eV in Eu$_5$Ga$_2$As$_6$ and 0.90 eV in Eu$_5$Al$_2$As$_6$. The lower symmetry and increased structural complexity suggest suppressed lattice thermal conductivity, pointing to thermoelectric potential alongside prospects in spintronics and detector technologies. These results give an epitaxy-compatible route for discovering and integrating magnetic Zintl thin films and nanowires directly from relevant III-V semiconductors.

</details>


### [135] [Displacement-field-driven reconstruction of low energy transport in few-layer PtSe2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23003)
*Xiao Liu,Yaroslav Zhumagulov,Yuang Jie,Ahmet Enes Bozcali,Johan Felisaz,Qi Zhang,Oldvrich Cicvarek,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Zdeněk Sofer,Oleg V. Yazyev,Ahmet Avsar*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过双栅控半导体PtSe₂的输运实验与Wannier输运计算，揭示了位移场（displacement field）如何通过诱导能带重叠和价带色散重构来调控低带隙半导体的电子结构，证明有限密度输运是探测位移场驱动电子结构重构的灵敏手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在层状半导体中，垂直位移场产生层间电势差，与层间杂化竞争，同时改变带隙和输运相关的有限密度电子态。解析这一相互作用需要材料处于半导体到半金属转变（semiconductor-to-semimetal transition）附近，以便中等电场能强烈重塑低能电子结构。

Method: 采用双栅控半导体PtSe₂进行位移场驱动的输运测量，并辅以固定弛豫时间（fixed-relaxation-time）的Wannier输运计算，分别评估位移场对带隙和载流子输运的影响。

Result: 六层PtSe₂位于半导-半金属交叉边缘，仅存在小的残余输运带隙；弱位移场即可迅速抑制电荷中性点附近的残余带隙，驱动系统进入能带重叠（band-overlap）区域。同时，重空穴掺杂区电导降低，表明位移场不仅改变带隙，还修正导电价带态。Wannier输运计算复现了这两种响应，归因于场致能带重叠和价带色散重构。

Conclusion: 有限密度输运可作为位移场驱动电子结构重构的灵敏探针，并将电学调控能力从传统带隙工程扩展至能带色散重构层面。

Abstract: In layered semiconductors, a perpendicular displacement field generates an interlayer potential difference that competes with interlayer hybridization, modifying both the band gap and the finite-density electronic states that carry current. Resolving this interplay requires a material lying close to the semiconductor-to-semimetal transition, where moderate electric fields can strongly reshape the low-energy electronic structure. Here, we investigate displacement-field-driven transport in dual-gated semiconducting PtSe2, whose pronounced thickness-dependent electronic structure provides access to this low-band-gap regime. Unlike thinner layers, the displacement-field response is strong in six-layer PtSe2, which lies at the verge of the semiconductor-to-semimetal crossover with only a small residual transport gap. Even weak displacement fields rapidly suppress this residual gap near charge neutrality, driving the system toward a band-overlap regime. At the same time, the conductivity decreases in the heavily hole-doped regime, demonstrating that the displacement field modifies not only the gap but also the conducting valence-band states. Fixed-relaxation-time Wannier transport calculations reproduce both responses, showing that they originate from field-induced band overlap together with reconstruction of the valence-band dispersion. These results establish finite-density transport as a sensitive probe of displacement-field-driven electronic structure reconstruction and extend electrical control beyond conventional band-gap engineering.

</details>


### [136] [X-ray driven displacive excitation of coherent phonons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23080)
*Oleg Dogadov,Remi Claude,Christoph Emeis,Giovanni Batignani,Giuseppe Fumero,Roberto Costantini,Agata Azzolin,Sabine Rockenstein,Paolo Cattaneo,Rebeca Gomez Castillo,Matteo Manzi,Angelo Giglia,Dario De Angelis,Ettore Paltanin,Zeinab Ebrahimpour,Marija Krstulovic,Gabor Kurdi,Miltcho Danailov,Luca Giannessi,Riccardo Mincigrucci,Emiliano Principi,Alberto Crepaldi,Giulio Cerullo,Giovanni De Ninno,Claudio Masciovecchio,Laura Foglia,Tullio Scopigno,Fabrizio Carbone,Francesca Calegari,Fabio Caruso,Michele Puppin,Oliviero Cannelli*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用自由电子激光脉冲在碲（tellurium）的N4,5-边（core resonance）激发相干晶格动力学，证明在宽能量密度范围内振荡幅度符合扩展至芯共振的相干声子位移激发（displacive excitation of coherent phonons, DECP）框架，且驱动效率为可见光泵浦的两倍；结合从头计算揭示了多带贡献竞争机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 调制电子-声子耦合可控制结构位移并调节材料功能，但价带到导带的跃迁对驱动力只能提供有限的杠杆作用，因此需要探索更有效的调控途径。

Method: 利用调谐至Te N4,5-edge的自由电子激光脉冲激发三角碲中的相干晶格动力学，测量宽能量密度范围内的振荡幅度行为，并通过从头计算（ab initio calculations）分解驱动力中的多带贡献。

Result: 振荡幅度在宽能量密度范围内遵循扩展至芯共振的相干声子位移激发框架，其驱动效率为可见光泵浦的两倍；从头计算表明力由竞争中且随载流子弛豫而转移的多带贡献组成，这些贡献在光激发下不可及。

Conclusion: 可调谐的极紫外和X射线脉冲开辟了新的调控区间，其中位移响应由依赖于能带的晶格耦合决定，而不仅仅由光载流子的数量和温度决定。

Abstract: Modulating electron-phonon coupling offers a route to control structural displacements and tune material functionality. Valence-to-conduction band transitions, however, provide limited leverage over the driving force. Here, we demonstrate coherent lattice dynamics in trigonal tellurium using free-electron laser pulses tuned to the Te N4,5-edge. Over a broad fluence range, the oscillation amplitude obeys the displacive excitation of coherent phonons framework, extended to core resonance with twice the driving efficiency of a visible pump. Ab initio calculations decompose the force into competing multiband contributions, inaccessible to optical excitation, whose balance shifts as carriers relax. Tunable extreme-ultraviolet and X-ray pulses thus open a regime in which the displacive response is set by band-dependent coupling to the lattice, not by the number and temperature of the photocarriers alone.

</details>


### [137] [Z1 oscillations and charge state in electronic stopping power from first principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23112)
*Noor Ul Ain,Emilio Artacho*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文利用实时含时密度泛函理论（real-time time-dependent density-functional theory）从第一性原理计算了O和Mg弹丸在体相Al中的电子阻止本领（electronic stopping power），研究了随弹丸原子序数Z1变化的摩擦系数振荡及其对方位方向、临界速度的敏感性，并通过Hirshfeld与Voronoi电荷分析及实空间电子密度图揭示了芯电子逐层剥落（undressing）与电子拖尾效应等微观物理图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低弹丸速度下，摩擦系数随原子序数Z1呈振荡行为，源于芯电子随弹丸运动对电荷的屏蔽效应；该振荡随速度增加而消失。作者希望从第一性原理理解这一振荡的物理机制，并考察其对传播方向的敏感性，以深化对能量转移率、有效电荷等基本过程的认识。

Method: 采用实时含时密度泛函理论计算电子阻止本领，针对O（对应Z1振荡最大处）与Mg（对应最小处）两种弹丸穿越体相Al的多种轨迹；利用Hirshfeld和Voronoi两种电荷划分方案独立量化弹丸电荷态，同时提出基于阻止本领自身的有效电荷定义，并结合实空间电子密度图分析芯电子剥落和密度形变。

Result: Z1振荡对传播方向高度敏感，尽管Al在多数用途中近乎理想金属；振荡消失的临界速度在探测轨迹间变动于低于0.1 a.u.至超过1 a.u.之间。Hirshfeld与Voronoi分析结果高度一致，并与基于阻止本领的有效定义相符；密度图揭示芯电子剥落部分源于电子在弹丸后方形成拖尾，暗示除净电荷外还需考虑多极形变的唯象描述。

Conclusion: 弹丸有效电荷随速度增大而逐渐剥落，符合预期；但实空间电子密度图呈现更丰富的图像，芯电子的剥落部分由电子拖尾造成，可在净电荷之外引入多极形变的唯象修正。密度图还提供了对芯电子耗散机制的见解。

Abstract: The energy transfer rate from a projectile nucleus to the electrons of the matter it traverses depends on the charge of that projectile, Q= e Z1. At low projectile velocities the friction coefficient is known to oscillate with atomic number Z1, since core electrons travel with the projectile screening its charge. The effective charge increases with velocity and oscillations disappear. That effect is studied here calculating electronic stopping power from first principles for O and Mg projectiles shooting through bulk Al, using real-time time-dependent density-functional theory. Both projectiles represent maximum and minimum of the first Z1 oscillation, respectively. The oscillation is found to be very sensitive to the direction of propagation, in spite of Al being quite an ideal metal for many purposes. The critical velocity for the oscillation disappearance ranges between below 0.1 a.u. and beyond 1 a.u. for the explored trajectories. The charge state is independently quantified with Hirshfeld and Voronoi analyses, offering remarkably consistent results in spite of their very different partition methods, as well as with an effective definition based on the stopping power itself. They display a gradual undressing of the projectile's core electrons with increasing velocity in qualitative accordance with expectations. However, electron density plots in real space present a richer picture in which the undressing is partly due to the electrons trailing behind the projectile, suggesting possible phenomenological descriptions correcting for the deformation of the density in terms of multipoles beyond the net charge. The plots also offer insights into dissipation by core electrons.

</details>


### [138] [Probing the transport properties of Cantor-Wu alloys by means of femtosecond and nanosecond laser ablation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23116)
*David Redka,Maximilian Spellauge,Rosemary Babu,Christopher D. Woodgate,Hubert Ebert,Ján Minár,Daniel J. Förster,Heinz P. Huber*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究通过实验测量与第一性原理计算，系统研究了五类Cantor-Wu等原子合金在飞秒和纳秒脉冲下的单脉冲激光烧蚀阈值（single-pulse laser ablation threshold），发现阈值与电子热导率、电子-声子耦合及磁相变存在定量关联，从而将烧蚀阈值确立为探测复杂合金电子输运性质及其磁相依赖性的灵敏无接触探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对成分复杂合金（compositionally complex alloys）中电子输运行为与激光烧蚀阈值之间的关联缺乏系统理解，特别是飞秒与纳秒时间尺度下烧蚀阈值差异的物理机制尚不明确，反射率变化无法解释实验观测到的阈值趋势，亟需建立烧蚀阈值与微观电子性质之间的定量联系。

Method: 对FeNi、CoNi、CrFeNi、CrCoNi和CrMnFeCoNi五类等原子Cantor-Wu合金，在同一批样品上同时开展合金合成、飞秒/纳秒脉冲烧蚀实验以及第一性原理（first-principles）计算，计算内容包括电子结构、电子-声子耦合（electron-phonon coupling）和电子热导率（electronic thermal conductivity），并使用双温模型（two-temperature model）对阈值进行标度分析。

Result: 吸收的飞秒阈值从FeNi到含Cr合金系统性下降最多达36%，该趋势无法由反射率变化解释；双温模型结合自旋无序相（spin-disordered phase）的电子热导率与电子-声子耦合参数可复现实测阈值层级。纳秒阈值则反映沿加热路径平均的热平衡电导率。CoNi作为表观离群值，其室温输运对两种脉冲的阈值预测均过高约两倍，但通过考虑铁磁序（ferromagnetic order）丧失导致电导率塌缩后得到定量解释。

Conclusion: 单脉冲烧蚀阈值可作为成分复杂合金中电子输运性质及其磁相依赖性的灵敏、无接触式探针（contact-free probe），为连接激光材料加工参数与微观电子结构性质提供了新的定量途径。

Abstract: Single-pulse laser ablation thresholds of selected equiatomic Cantor-Wu alloys - FeNi, CoNi, CrFeNi, CrCoNi, and CrMnFeCoNi - are measured for femtosecond and nanosecond pulse durations and interpreted through first-principles calculations of the electronic structure, the electron-phonon coupling, and the electronic thermal conductivity. Alloy synthesis, ablation experiments, and theory are performed consistently on the same set of samples. The absorbed femtosecond thresholds decrease systematically by up to 36 % from FeNi to the Cr-containing alloys, a trend that reflectance variations cannot explain. Two-temperature-model scaling of the thresholds with the electronic thermal conductivity and the electron-phonon coupling, with all parameters taken from the spin-disordered phase, reproduces the measured hierarchy. The nanosecond thresholds instead probe the thermal equilibrium conductivity averaged along the heating path. The apparent outlier of CoNi, whose room-temperature transport over-predicts its thresholds by up to a factor of two for both pulse durations, is resolved quantitatively by the collapse of its conductivity upon loss of ferromagnetic order. Single-pulse ablation thresholds thereby emerge as sensitive, contact-free probes of electronic transport and of its magnetic-phase dependence in compositionally complex alloys.

</details>


### [139] [Multiscale Quasiparticle Electronic Structure and Excitonic Properties of CdSe Nanoclusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23130)
*Surender Kumar,Martin Thümmler,Alexander Croy,Stefanie Gräfe,Caterina Cocchi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过GW/BSE计算系统研究了化学计量比Cd_nSe_n纳米团簇（n=3,6,13,33）中量子限域效应导致的准粒子自能修正（Δ_QP）与激子束缚能（E_b）之间的尺寸依赖竞争，并验证了一个从第一性原理导出的可扩展原子级紧束缚（tight-binding, TB）框架。研究揭示了文献中1-2 eV光谱蓝移实为单粒子GW收敛伪影，且随着团簇体积增大，Δ_QP与E_b的完美抵消逐渐瓦解。通过剪切修正（scissor-corrected）的DFT参数化TB模型可精确复现限域诱导的准粒子带隙和光学起始能量标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子限域如何调控纳米半导体中的多体效应（many-body effects）对于可靠预测其光学性质至关重要，但现有的GW/BSE方法在大尺寸纳米结构中计算代价过高，亟需一种兼具物理准确性和计算可扩展性的多尺度建模策略。

Method: 采用GW/BSE方法对n=3,6,13,33的一系列化学计量比Cd_Se纳米团簇进行系统计算，并通过空间逆参与率（inverse participation ratio）分析电子结构特征。在此基础上，构建一个由第一性原理参数化、经剪刀修正的原子级紧束缚模型，并对比其与GW/BSE结果的预测一致性。

Result: 研究发现文献中报道的1-2 eV光谱蓝移源于单粒子GW收敛伪影而非电子-空穴核的缺陷；Δ_QP与E_b的近乎完美抵消随团簇体积增大而瓦解，原因是介电屏蔽快速出现使E_b衰减快于Δ_QP，导致与平均场预测产生显著偏差。空间逆参与率分析表明光学压制源自局域价带轨道与离域导带态之间的严重空间失配。剪刀修正的DFT参数化TB模型能准确复现准粒子带隙与光学起点的限域标度行为。

Conclusion: 该工作为将有效多体效应核嵌入计算高效模型提供了定量的多尺度路线图，使包含数千原子的实际半导体纳米结构的光学性质预测成为可能。

Abstract: Quantum confinement in stoichiometric $\mathrm{Cd}_n\mathrm{Se}_n$ nanoclusters dramatically attenuates electronic screening, driving a delicate, size-dependent competition between quasiparticle self-energy corrections ($Δ_{\mathrm{QP}}$) and exciton binding energies ($E_b$). Here, we present a $GW$/BSE study across a representative size series ($n = 3, 6, 13, 33$) and leverage it to validate a scalable atomistic tight-binding (TB) framework derived from first principles. Our results demonstrate that 1-2 eV spectral blueshifts previously reported in the literature arise from single-particle $GW$ convergence artifacts rather than deficiencies in the electron--hole kernels. We show that the near-perfect cancellation between $Δ_{\mathrm{QP}}$ and $E_b$ breaks down as cluster volume increases, driven by the rapid onset of dielectric screening attenuating $E_b$ faster than $Δ_{\mathrm{QP}}$ and leading to a pronounced divergence from mean-field predictions. Spatial inverse participation ratio analysis of the electronic structure reveals that optical suppression of fundamental pre-peaks stems from a severe spatial mismatch between localized valence orbitals and delocalized conduction states. Finally, we demonstrate that the confinement-induced scaling of the quasiparticle gap and the optical onset is accurately reproduced by a scissor-corrected, DFT-parameterized TB model. As such, this work provides a quantitative multiscale roadmap for embedding effective many-body effects kernels into computationally efficient models, enabling reliable optical predictions for realistic semiconducting nanostructures containing up to thousands of atoms.

</details>


### [140] [Impact of Carbon Contamination on the Low-Temperature Electric Conduction of the Spark-Plasma Sintered Barium Titanate Ceramics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23151)
*Oleksandr S. Pylypchuk,Taisiia O. Kuzmenko,Denis O. Stetsenko,Oleksyi V. Bereznykov,Serhii E. Ivanchenko,Ihor M. Danylenko,Anna N. Morozovska,Vladimir N. Poroshin,Victor V. Vainberg*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究通过放电等离子烧结（spark plasma sintering）制备了不同碳含量的BaTiO3样品，在77-408 K温度范围内及最高20 kV/cm强电场下研究了其介电和导电性质。结果表明，含碳样品在低频下表现出巨大的有效介电常数（在393 K时可达10^6量级），低温范围内仍保持10^4-10^5的高值，且强依赖于碳含量；77-200 K范围内的电导符合Mott跳迁导电（hopping conduction）定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索放电等离子烧结过程中引入碳污染对BaTiO3陶瓷介电和导电性质的影响，以理解碳含量如何调制材料在宽温度范围内的电学行为。

Method: 对放电等离子烧结制备的含不同碳含量BaTiO3样品，在77-408 K温度范围内于非加热小电场下测量介电与导电性质，并在77-200 K范围内施加最高20 kV/cm强电场进行测试，系统分析碳含量、温度和外加电场强度对有效介电常数和电导的影响。

Result: 含碳样品在低频下表现出巨大有效介电常数，393 K时高达10^6量级；低温范围内仍为10^4-10^5且强依赖于碳含量。77-200 K电导遵循Mott跳迁导电定律。电场强度对电导和有效介电常数的影响也被系统研究。

Conclusion: 实验结果可以用跳迁导电理论以及极化过程与导电过程由不同组分贡献的假设来统一解释，表明碳含量是控制BaTiO3介电与导电性能的关键因素。

Abstract: The dielectric and electric conduction properties of BaTiO3 samples fabricated by the spark plasma sintering with additional contamination by different content of carbon have been investigated in the temperature range from 77 through 408 K in small non-heating electric fields and in the range of 77 - 200K under strong electric fields up to 20 kV/cm. The effective dielectric permittivity of the samples with additional carbon achieves colossal values at low frequency, up to several units per 106 at 393 K. In the low temperature range, it remains very high magnitude, 10^4 -10^5 at low frequency and is strongly dependent on the carbon content. The electric conduction in the range of 77 - 200 K bears the hopping conduction and obeys the Mott law. We also studied electric conduction and effective dielectric permittivity vs electric field strength in the range of 77 - 200 K. The results are explained within the frames of hopping conduction theory and suggestion on different constituents in polarization processes and electric conduction.

</details>


### [141] [First-Principles Spin-Lattice Coupling from Downfolded Electron-Phonon Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23157)
*Xu He,Álvaro Adrián Carrasco Álvarez,Gian-Marco Rignanese,Eric Bousquet,Samuel Poncé,Matthieu J. Verstraete*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于第一性原理微扰论的自旋-声子耦合参数计算方法，通过降维（downfolding）电子-声子耦合矩阵，在瓦尼尔表示（Wannier representation）中结合局域自旋旋转与晶格微扰，无需构建位移磁超胞即可获得磁交换参数对原子位移的实空间导数，并在SrMnO₃上验证了方法的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋-声子耦合对理解磁性材料的磁振子弛豫、热输运和相变等物理过程至关重要，但传统方法通常需要构建位移磁超胞，计算成本高且提取参数繁琐。作者旨在发展一种无需超胞计算、可直接集成到标准工作流中的第一性原理方法。

Method: 方法的核心思路是：首先利用瓦尼尔表示描述电子哈密顿量和电子-声子耦合（EPC）矩阵，利用磁矩和原子位移的局域性；将自旋系统映射为经典海森堡哈密顿量（classical Heisenberg Hamiltonian），通过格林函数形式将局域自旋旋转作为微扰处理；通过电子-声子耦合参数将晶格微扰引入紧束缚哈密顿量，并与自旋旋转微扰相结合，从而获得磁交换参数的实空间导数，无需进行位移磁超胞计算。

Result: 在SrMnO₃上对该方法进行了实例验证，结果表明该方法能够成功计算自旋-声子耦合参数，并且可以直接集成到标准的计算工作流中。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种高效、可集成化的第一性原理自旋-声子耦合计算方案，避免了传统超胞方法的开销，为大规模磁性材料中磁振子-声子相互作用研究提供了新的工具。

Abstract: We present a method to calculate spin-phonon coupling parameters from first-principles perturbation theory by downfolding the electron-phonon coupling (EPC). We exploit the localized nature of magnetic moments and atomic displacements by working in the Wannier representation of the electronic Hamiltonian and the EPC matrix. The spin system is mapped to a classical Heisenberg Hamiltonian, whose parameters are obtained by treating local spin rotations as a perturbation within a Green's-function formalism. The spin and phonon perturbations are connected through the EPC parameters, which enter as lattice-induced perturbations to the tight-binding Hamiltonian. By combining these lattice perturbations with local spin rotations, we obtain real-space derivatives of magnetic exchange parameters without performing displaced magnetic supercell calculations. We illustrate the method on SrMnO$_3$ and show that it can be integrated directly into standard workflows.

</details>


### [142] [The Achilles tendon enthesis rebuilds its mineralization front on reloading but retains a nanoscale imprint of unloading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23210)
*M. L. Stammer,C. Camy,M. Frewein,I. Silva Barreto,C. Genovesio,M. Eckermann,A. Karimbana,K. Iliopoulos,R. Ranjan,N. Wittig,T. Fovet,T. Brioche,A. Chopard,M. Burghammer,S. Brasselet,H. Birkedal,M. Pithioux,S. Roffino,T. A. Grünewald*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究结合多模态二维/三维X射线成像（multimodal 2D/3D X-ray imaging）与非线性光学显微镜（nonlinear optical microscopy），系统揭示了小鼠跟腱止点（enthesis）在卸载与再加载条件下矿物化前沿的纳米尺度稳定机制，证明止点是一个受力学调控的分级界面，其基质介导的边界控制约束矿物形成，并可在纳米结构上留下力学历史的印记。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 止点作为肌腱与骨骼之间传递载荷的渐变纤维软骨界面，其矿物化前沿的纳米尺度稳定机制尚不清楚，理解卸载与再加载条件下这一界面的结构变化对于阐明力学调控下的矿物形成机制至关重要。

Method: 将多模态二维/三维X射线成像与非线性光学显微镜相结合，在小鼠跟腱止点上系统绘制卸载与再加载条件下的结构、结晶性（crystalline）以及细胞外基质（extracellular matrix）组织学特征。

Result: 卸载导致潮标（tidemark）相关的双光子荧光（2PF）峰值降低，并伴随弥散性矿化侵入先前未矿化的纤维软骨区域；卸载相关矿物表现出增大的表观微晶尺寸、增大的c轴晶格参数、降低的结晶织构以及减弱的胶原有序梯度。再加载后2PF峰值恢复，但新潮标在距原边界约20微米处形成，在矿物嵌镶结构中留下持久的纳米尺度印记。

Conclusion: 研究确立了止点作为力学调控的分级界面，其中基质介导的边界控制约束矿物形成，且力学历史被印记于纳米结构之中。

Abstract: The enthesis is a graded fibrocartilaginous interface that transfers load between tendon and bone, yet the nanoscale mechanisms stabilizing its mineralization front remain unclear. Here, we combine multimodal 2D/3D X-ray imaging with nonlinear optical microscopy to map structural, crystalline and extracellular matrix organization across the murine Achilles tendon enthesis under unloading and reloading. Unloading reduces the tidemark-associated two-photon fluorescence (2PF) peak and is accompanied by diffuse mineralization into previously unmineralized fibrocartilage. This unloading-associated mineral exhibits increased apparent crystallite size, an enlarged c-axis lattice parameter, reduced crystalline texture and a diminished collagen order gradient, consistent with an altered mineralization environment. Upon reloading, the 2PF peak recovers, but a new tidemark forms ~20 um from the original boundary, creating a zone with a persistent nanoscale imprint in the mineral tessellation. These findings establish the enthesis as a mechanically governed graded interface in which matrix-mediated boundary control constrains mineral formation and in which a record of mechanical history is imprinted into the nanostructure.

</details>


### [143] [Predicted High $n$-Type $zT$ and Ultralow Lattice Thermal Conductivity in A$_2$AgIrCl$_6$ (A = Cs, Rb)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23212)
*Neeraj Kulhari,Krishna Swaroop Sharma,Sung Gu Kang,K. C. Bhamu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过第一性原理计算系统表征了立方Cs₂AgIrCl₆和Rb₂AgIrCl₆双钙钛矿材料的结构稳定性、电子结构与热电输运性质，预测两者在800 K下n型zT峰值分别可达2.81和2.36，源于轻谷简并电子、中等掺杂与低晶格热导率的协同作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一种实用的热电（thermoelectric）器件必须在不阻碍电荷输运的前提下抑制热流。作者选取立方Cs₂AgIrCl₆和Rb₂AgIrCl₆双钙钛矿材料，考察二者逼近这一理想平衡（即"声子玻璃-电子晶体"）的程度，从而探索高性能热电材料候选体系。

Method: 采用第一性原理（first-principles）计算方法，涵盖结构稳定性、化学键合、弹性响应、晶格动力学及散射分辨的载流子输运计算；利用HSE06泛函结合自旋-轨道耦合（spin–orbit coupling）计算电子能带结构；采用修正的Debye–Callaway模型评估晶格热导率，并利用AMSET代码处理声学形变势、电离杂质和极性光学声子散射。

Result: 两种材料均满足立方弹性稳定性判据且声子谱无虚频；Rb替代Cs主要产生化学压力，使晶格收缩1.34%并增强Ag–Cl和Ir–Cl键。HSE06+自旋-轨道耦合给出直接X点带隙分别为1.597 eV（Cs体系）和1.637 eV（Rb体系）；电子有效质量轻（0.43–0.57 m₀），空穴质量重（2.10–4.68 m₀）。300 K晶格热导率为0.346与0.428 W m⁻¹ K⁻¹，800 K下降至0.118与0.150 W m⁻¹ K⁻¹；在800 K、载流子浓度约6×10¹⁹ cm⁻³处，n型zT峰值分别达2.81和2.36。

Conclusion: 优异的热电响应源于轻电子、谷简并（valley degeneracy）、中等掺杂水平与弱晶格热输运的协同作用，而非单一优越系数所致。预测值可作为实验可检验的目标，但前提是在高温下保持立方相并实现可控的电子掺杂。

Abstract: A useful thermoelectric device must impede heat flow without impeding charge transport. Here, we examine how closely cubic Cs$_2$AgIrCl$_6$ and Rb$_2$AgIrCl$_6$ approach this balance using first-principles calculations of structural stability, chemical bonding, elastic response, lattice dynamics, and scattering-resolved carrier transport. Both materials satisfy the cubic elastic-stability criteria, and neither harmonic phonon spectrum contains an imaginary mode. Replacing Cs with Rb mainly exerts chemical pressure: the lattice contracts by 1.34\% and the Ag--Cl and Ir--Cl bonds strengthen, whereas the band-edge topology changes little. HSE06 calculations including spin--orbit coupling yield direct X-point gaps of 1.597 and $1.637\,\mathrm{eV}$ for Cs$_2$AgIrCl$_6$ and Rb$_2$AgIrCl$_6$, respectively. The three symmetry-equivalent X valleys have light electron masses of $0.43$--$0.57\,m_0$, whereas the hole masses span $2.10$--$4.68\,m_0$. For Cs$_2$AgIrCl$_6$ and Rb$_2$AgIrCl$_6$, respectively, the modified Debye--Callaway model gives lattice thermal conductivities of 0.346 and $0.428\,\mathrm{W\,m^{-1}\,K^{-1}}$ at 300 K, decreasing to 0.118 and $0.150\,\mathrm{W\,m^{-1}\,K^{-1}}$ at 800 K. Treating acoustic-deformation-potential, ionized-impurity, and polar-optical-phonon scattering with AMSET gives peak $n$-type $zT$ values of 2.81 and 2.36 at 800 K near $6\times10^{19}\,\mathrm{cm^{-3}}$. This response arises from the convergence of light, valley-degenerate electrons, intermediate doping, and weak lattice heat transport rather than from a single exceptional coefficient. The predicted values are experimentally testable targets, contingent on retaining the cubic phases and controlled electron doping at elevated temperatures.

</details>


### [144] [FDTBX - Computational Tools for Simulation of X-ray Fiber Diffraction Patterns from Atomic Coordinates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23245)
*Pawel Sikorski*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文介绍了 fdtbx，一个基于 Python 的开放可扩展工具箱，用于直接从原子坐标模拟 X 射线纤维衍射（fiber diffraction）图样。该工具箱构建于 cctbx 之上，支持多核执行，整合了有限晶粒尺寸、取向无序和准晶格无序等主要物理展宽机制，并提供解析 Ewald 投影算法以生成可与实验图像直接比较的模拟图样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纤维衍射是解析部分有序、非晶态体系中分子结构的少数实验技术之一，但衍射图样复杂，包含准晶序（paracrystalline order）、堆积缺陷和宽重叠反射，解释严重依赖基于模型的精修。现有工具缺乏透明度、可复现性和并行扩展能力，阻碍了模型驱动的数据分析。

Method: fdtbx 基于 cctbx 库构建，从 PDB 模型直接计算结构因子，并通过每反射壳层求积（shell-quadrature）方案在任意查询点评估卷积，而非固定网格，以灵活权衡精度与速度。算法实现了三类物理展宽机制：有限晶粒尺寸、取向无序（含 Gauss、Lorentz 和 Voigt 角峰形状）以及 Hosemann 表述下的第二类准晶格无序；解析 Ewald 投影例程配合 Lorentz 校正将采样反射映射至平面探测器。

Result: 工具箱在纤维素 I-alpha/I-beta、alpha-chitin 和纤维素三乙酸酯上进行了演示，并给出了实用的模拟工作流。结果表明 fdtbx 能够生成可直接与实测图像比较的真实感衍射图样，同时保持代码的透明性与并行可扩展性。

Conclusion: fdtbx 通过提供透明、文档完善且可并行的纤维衍射专门算法实现，降低了可复现的基于模型分析的准入门槛，既可作为研究工具，也可作为教学资源使用。

Abstract: Fiber diffraction is one of the few experimental techniques capable of resolving molecular structure in partially ordered, non-crystalline systems such as fibrous proteins, biopolymers, and synthetic polymers. Because fiber diffraction patterns are complex, exhibiting paracrystalline order, packing defects, and broad, overlapping reflections, their interpretation relies on model-based refinement, in which theoretical patterns simulated from candidate atomic models are iteratively compared with experiment. We present fdtbx, a modern, open, and extensible Python toolbox for simulating X-ray fiber diffraction patterns directly from atomic coordinates. Built on the library cctbx and structured for readability and multicore execution, fdtbx computes structure factors from a PDB model and constructs realistic reciprocal-space reflection profiles that incorporate the principal physical broadening mechanisms of fiber diffraction: finite crystallite size, orientational disorder (with Gaussian, Lorentzian, and Voigt angular peak shapes), and paracrystalline (second-kind) lattice disorder in Hosemann's formulation.
