A per-reflection shell-quadrature scheme evaluates the required convolutions at arbitrary query points rather than on a fixed grid, giving direct control over the trade-off between accuracy and speed, and an analytic Ewald-projection routine maps each sampled reflection, with an appropriate Lorentz correction, onto a flat detector to produce a simulated pattern for direct comparison with measured images. We illustrate the toolbox on cellulose I-alpha/I-beta, alpha-chitin, and cellulose triacetate, and outline a practical simulation workflow. By providing a transparent, well-documented, and parallelizable implementation of the specialized algorithms of fiber diffraction, fdtbx lowers the barrier to reproducible model-based analysis and serves as both a research and a teaching resource.

</details>


### [145] [Absence of lateral domain wall mobility in Zn1-xMgxO thin films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23305)
*Jack Eckstein,Kyle P. Kelley,William Prudnick,Jon-Paul Maria,Stephen Jesse,Neus Domingo,Rama K. Vasudevan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过扫描振子显微镜（scanning oscillator microscopy）和点脉冲成像（point pulse-imaging）方法，直接测绘了Zn1-xMgxO中的局部开关事件和畴壁响应，发现其极化反转（polarization reversal）主要通过垂直延伸的柱状细丝（columnar filaments）成核（nucleation）实现，而非如钙钛矿铁电体那样由移动畴壁传播主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典钙钛矿氧化物（perovskite oxides）与新兴纤锌矿（wurtzite）铁电体的极化反转机理存在根本差异，而此前对纤锌矿体系开关机制的理解尚不清晰，需要直接的实验证据来阐明其动力学路径。

Method: 采用扫描振子显微镜结合点脉冲成像方法，直接绘制局部开关事件及畴壁（domain wall）响应，对Zn1-xMgxO中极化反转的微观过程进行空间分辨测量。

Result: 实验表明，Zn1-xMgxO中的畴壁横向迁移率可忽略不计（小于10纳米），极化反转主要通过垂直延伸的柱状细丝成核进行，细丝横向尺寸与晶粒尺度相当；该成核控制开关行为与钙钛矿铁电体的生长介导（growth-mediated）动力学形成鲜明对比。

Conclusion: 成核主导的细丝状反转（nucleation-dominated filamentary reversal）被确认为Zn1-xMgxO的定义性开关机制，解释其突发性、空间局域化的开关行为，并为未来研究不同晶粒尺寸下的关联长度（correlation lengths）和成核过程提出了进一步方向。

Abstract: Polarization reversal in ferroelectrics arises from the coupled processes of domain nucleation and subsequent growth, yet the governing mechanisms differ fundamentally between classical perovskite oxides and emerging wurtzite ferroelectrics. While switching in perovskites is typically governed by mobile domain walls whose field-driven propagation dominates macroscopic kinetics, here we show that polarization reversal in wurtzite Zn1-xMgxO proceeds through a qualitatively different pathway. Using scanning oscillator microscopy, in combination with point pulse-imaging methods, we directly map local switching events and domain wall responses, revealing that domain walls in Zn1-xMgxO exhibit negligible lateral mobility (sub 10nm) and that polarization reversal proceeds predominantly through the nucleation of vertically extended columnar filaments with a lateral size on the order of the grains. This nucleation-controlled switching contrasts sharply with the growth-mediated dynamics characteristic of perovskite ferroelectrics and explains the abrupt, spatially localized switching behavior observed in wurtzite systems. These results establish nucleation-dominated filamentary reversal as a defining switching mechanism in Zn1-xMgxO and point towards the need for further studies to understand correlation lengths and nucleation processes across a range of grain sizes.

</details>


### [146] [Determination of the ferrimagnetic structure of monoclinic TbFe$_{2}$D$_{4.2}$ deuteride and its evolution versus temperature and high magnetic field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23346)
*Valerie Paul-Boncour,Olivier Isnard*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究通过多种磁性测量手段（含高脉冲磁场）与中子衍射实验，系统研究了单斜晶系TbFe₂D₄.₂的磁性质。该化合物具有亚铁磁（ferrimagnetic）基态，在4.2 K表现出显著磁各向异性，并在升温和施加磁场过程中呈现出两个变磁转变（metamagnetic transitions）及一次伴随晶胞收缩的巡游电子变磁行为（itinerant electron metamagnetic behavior）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究TbFe₂D₄.₂作为金属间氢化物（intermetallic hydride）的磁行为，理解稀土（Tb）与过渡金属（Fe）子晶格之间磁矩的耦合机制及其随温度和外加磁场的变化规律，并与YFe₂D₄.₂进行对比，以阐明影响转变温度（transition temperature）的关键因素。

Method: 将多种磁性测量手段相结合，包括涉及高脉冲磁场（high pulsed magnetic field）的测量，并与随温度和施加磁场变化的中子衍射（neutron diffraction）实验进行联合分析。

Result: TbFe₂D₄.₂呈亚铁磁基态，Tb磁矩为8.2(2) μB，Fe磁矩为2.1(2) μB。4.2 K下在50 T仍未达到饱和，表明存在与Tb磁矩相关的大磁各向异性（large magnetic anisotropy）。在3 T和29 T处观察到两个变磁转变。升温过程中Tb磁矩逐渐减小而Fe磁矩保持恒定至150 K；在160 K时两者均急剧下降，伴随0.38%的晶胞体积收缩，归因于Fe子晶格的巡游电子变磁行为。该转变温度比YFe₂D₄.₂高76 K，原因是晶胞体积增加以及Fe与Tb磁矩间的交换相互作用（exchange interactions）。220 K以上该氘化物变为顺磁性（paramagnetic）。

Conclusion: TbFe₂D₄.₂呈现出复杂的温度-磁场依赖的磁性演化，涵盖亚铁磁基态、场致变磁转变及160 K处的巡游电子变磁转变；与YFe₂D₄.₂的对比表明，晶胞体积与Tb-Fe交换耦合是提高转变温度的关键因素。

Abstract: The magnetic properties of monoclinic TbFe$_{2}$D$_{4.2}$ have been investigated by combining different magnetic measurements, including those involving a high pulsed magnetic field, with neutron diffraction experiments versus temperature and applied field. TbFe$_{2}$D$_{4.2}$ displays a ferrimagnetic ground state with M$_{Tb}$ = 8.2(2) $μ$$_{B}$ and M$_{Fe}$ = 2.1(2) $μ$$_{B}$. At 4.2 K a large magnetic anisotropy related to the Tb moments is observed, as saturation is not reached at 50 T. In addition, two metamagnetic transitions are observed for fields of 3 and 29 T. Upon heating, the Tb moment decreases progressively whereas the Fe moment remains constant up to 150 K. At 160 K both Tb and Fe moments sharply decrease, and a 0.38% contraction of the cell volume is observed due to an itinerant electron metamagnetic behavior of the Fe sublattice. This transition temperature is 76 K higher than that of YFe$_{2}$D$_{4.2}$ due to an increase in cell volume, as well as exchange interactions between the Fe and Tb moments. The deuteride becomes paramagnetic above 220 K.

</details>


### [147] [Giant Surface-driven Nonlinear Hall Effect in BiTeCl at Room Temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23368)
*Zhihua Liu,Ziheng Wang,Yongbo Lv,Hanru Feng,Zhiwei Zhang,Bo Zhang,Feng Liu,Guohua Wang,Shengwei Jiang,Hao Chu,Hui Li,Dong Qian*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文报道了在Rashba型极性半导体BiTeCl中于室温下观测到巨型表面驱动二阶非线性霍尔效应（nonlinear Hall effect, NLHE），其300 K时的二阶非线性霍尔磁化率为1.68 μV·mV⁻¹，比此前最佳表面主导系统大80倍，并将极性晶体上下表面不对称性确立为发现更强NLHE材料的普适设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性霍尔效应（NLHE）可在时间反演对称但反转对称破缺的体系中产生霍尔响应，具有射频整流、能量收集和太赫兹探测等应用前景，而器件小型化是核心追求。表面固有的反转对称破缺可解除对体相对称性的严格限制，因此探索表面驱动的强非线性响应具有重要意义。

Method: 实验中在Rashba型极性半导体BiTeCl表面观测了室温下的二阶非线性霍尔效应，并测定了其二阶非线性霍尔磁化率；同时进行了对称性分析与标度分析（scaling analysis），用以判定散射机制（skew-scattering与side-jump）的贡献。

Result: BiTeCl在300 K下的二阶非线性霍尔磁化率达到1.68 μV·mV⁻¹，为此前最佳表面主导体系的80倍。该巨大响应源于极性晶体结构与丰富表面态的协同作用：极性堆垛使上下表面不等价，非线性响应来自单一表面而不被另一表面抵消。对称性与标度分析表明散射（skew-scattering）和侧跳（side-jump）两种机制均有贡献。

Conclusion: 研究确认BiTeCl是应用NLHE的有前景平台，并提出极性晶体相反表面之间的不对称性可作为设计更大非线性霍尔响应的表面驱动材料的一般性原则。

Abstract: The nonlinear Hall effect (NLHE) provides a pathway to generate a Hall response in time-reversal-symmetric yet inversion-symmetry-broken systems. NLHE can rectify an alternating current into a transverse direct voltage, making it attractive for radio-frequency rectification, energy harvesting, and terahertz detection, applications for which device miniaturization remains a central pursuit. In this context, the inherent inversion symmetry breaking at surfaces is particularly appealing: because symmetry is necessarily broken at the surface of any crystal, irrespective of whether its bulk is centrosymmetric, surface-driven nonlinear responses lift the stringent constraint on bulk symmetry and open a route toward compact device architectures. Here we report the observation of a giant, surface-driven second-order nonlinear Hall effect in the Rashba-type polar semiconductor BiTeCl at room temperature. The determined second-order nonlinear Hall susceptibility at 300 K reaches 1.68 $μ$mV$^{-1}$, which is 80 times larger than that of the best previously reported surface-dominated systems. We attribute this giant response to the synergistic interplay between BiTeCl's polar crystal structure and its rich surface states: the polar stacking renders the top and bottom surfaces inequivalent, so that the nonlinear response originates from a single surface without compensation from the other. Symmetry and scaling analyses suggest that both skew-scattering and side-jump mechanisms contribute to the observed effect. Our findings not only identify BiTeCl as a promising platform for future applications utilizing the NLHE, but also establish the asymmetry between the opposite surfaces of a polar crystal as a general design principle for discovering surface-driven materials with larger nonlinear Hall responses.

</details>


### [148] [FUCrIMODo: structure recovery from atomistic descriptors via multi-stage genetic algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23381)
*Louis Boehm,Martin Kuban,Claudia Draxl*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通用、基于相似度驱动的采样方法，通过新颖的分阶段优化策略，直接从描述符（descriptor）恢复原子类型、原子位置和晶胞形状，无需任何先验结构知识，并以平均SOAP描述符验证了其能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动的材料发现依赖机器学习模型，而将这些描述符反演为原子结构是生成式材料设计流程的关键环节。现有反演方法要么仅适用于特定可逆描述符，要么需要与目标结构相似的候选结构，限制了在化学和构型空间中探索新区域的可能性。

Method: 本文提出一种通用且基于相似度驱动的采样方法，采用新颖的分阶段优化（stage-wise optimization）策略，直接从描述符中恢复原子类型、原子位置和晶胞形状。该方法仅需描述符特征及其参数作为输入，不依赖任何先验结构信息。

Result: 该方法的能力通过平均平滑重叠原子位置（SOAP）描述符进行了演示验证，证明了其在不借助候选结构的情况下从描述符反演周期性原子结构（periodic systems）的可行性。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法为描述符反演提供了一种通用且可推广的解决方案，有望扩展生成式材料设计在未知化学和构型空间中的探索能力。

Abstract: Data-driven approaches to materials discovery rely on numerical representations of atomic structures as input for machine learning models. Inverting these descriptors - recovering atomic structures from their representations - is essential for most generative material design pipelines, yet it remains challenging, particularly for periodic systems. Existing inversion methods are either tailored to specific invertible descriptors or require candidate structures with similar atomic arrangements and compositions, limiting the exploration of novel regions in chemical and configurational space. Here, we propose a generalizable, similarity-driven sampling approach, powered by a novel stage-wise optimization strategy, to recover atom types, atomic positions, and unit cell shapes directly from a descriptor. Our approach requires only descriptor features and parameters as input without any prior structural knowledge. The capability of our method is demonstrated by the averaged Smooth Overlap of Atomic Positions (SOAP) descriptor.

</details>


### [149] [The principle of detailed balance between electrons and phonons in presence of excitonic effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23403)
*Alberto Guandalini,Giovanni Caldarelli,Francesco Mauri,Francesco Macheda*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文从多体（many-body）框架出发，推导出包含激子效应（excitonic effects）且同时保证电子与声子散射过程热力学细致平衡（detailed balance）的电子-声子耦合形式，并应用于石墨烯中电子与声子线宽的计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电子-声子耦合顶点通常采用局域近似，难以同时保证电子与声子自能计算的细致平衡。作者旨在构建一个统一的非局域顶点，在包含激子效应的同时使电子与声子两方面的散射过程满足热力学细致平衡。

Method: 从微观电子-原子核哈密顿量出发，围绕Born-Oppenheimer平衡几何展开，构建电子与声子传播子的有效作用量（effective action）。由该有效作用量推导电子与声子自能，二者均以包含激子效应的非局域顶点G^s表示，推广了通常的局域相互作用顶点g^s。在屏蔽交换（screened-exchange）近似下并取G^s的静态极限，两自能均化为含相同G^s的Fermi黄金规则（Fermi golden rule）表达式。

Result: 在石墨烯应用中，分析了屏蔽交换能带结构导致的散射相空间（scattering phase space）缩减与激子效应对顶点增强之间的竞争，发现顶点增强可补偿并超越相空间缩减效应，使电子与声子线宽均得到提升。

Conclusion: 该共同顶点构造（common-vertex construction）能够在包含激子效应的同时保证细致平衡，为电子-声子耦合的可靠计算提供了新途径，且顶点增强效应在石墨烯中可超越相空间缩减的影响。

Abstract: Based on a many-body formulation, we derive an electron-phonon coupling including excitonic effects that preserves thermodynamic detailed balance between electronic and phononic scattering processes. We start from the microscopic electron-nucleus Hamiltonian, expand around the Born-Oppenheimer equilibrium geometry, and construct an effective action for the electronic and phononic propagators. From the same effective action, we derive both electronic and phononic self-energies in terms of a nonlocal vertex $\mathcal G^{\textrm{s}}$ including excitonic effects, which generalizes the usual local interaction vertex $g^{\textrm{s}}$. When electrons and phonons are well-defined quasiparticles in the screened-exchange approximation and $\mathcal G^{\textrm{s}}$ is taken in its static limit, both self-energies reduce to Fermi-golden-rule expressions containing the same $\mathcal G^{\textrm{s}}$, thereby ensuring detailed balance. As an application, we compute electronic and phononic linewidths in graphene and illustrate this common-vertex construction. We analyze the competition between the reduced scattering phase space induced by the screened-exchange band structure and the enhancement of the electron-phonon vertex due to excitonic effects, finding that the vertex enhancement can compensate for and overcome the phase-space reduction in both electronic and phononic linewidths.

</details>


### [150] [Mapping metallic d-wave altermagnetism across the TiNiSi structural family](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23409)
*Zhen Zhang,Subhadip Pradhan,Kirill D. Belashchenko,Vladimir Antropov*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理筛选方法，在TiNiSi结构家族中识别出16种金属d波交变磁体（d-wave altermagnet），其中6种新预测候选材料表现出显著的旋劈裂响应和有限的反常霍尔电导（anomalous Hall conductivity），为高效电荷-自旋转换提供了化学多样化的材料平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交变磁体作为可切换的垂直极化自旋电流源具有广阔前景，但具有可观旋劈裂响应的金属d波候选材料仍然稀缺，亟需系统的方法来发现新的候选材料。

Method: 该研究基于经验磁性基序（ferromagnetically ordered zigzag chains with antiferromagnetic interchain coupling）识别方法，结合第一性原理（first-principles）对280种三元体系进行热力学稳定性和磁性基态筛选，并对候选材料进行非共线（noncollinear）计算验证。

Result: 在280种三元体系中识别出16种金属d波交变磁体，包含4种已知成员（WFeB、NbMnP、TaMnP、NbMnAs）和12种新预测，其中ScMnP、TaMnAs、ScMnAs、MoMnAs、MoMnSi和WMnSi最具前景。所有候选材料的共线交变磁构型均为基态，且六种主要候选均具有非零旋劈裂角和有限反常霍尔电导。

Conclusion: TiNiSi型金属d波交变磁体构成了化学上多功能的电荷-自旋转换平台，其经验磁性基序为指导进一步发现候选材料提供了有效路径。

Abstract: The prospect of using altermagnets as switchable sources of perpendicularly polarized spin currents has intensified the search for candidate materials, yet metallic d-wave systems with sizable spin-splitter responses remain scarce. Here, we identify an empirical magnetic motif that supports metallic d-wave altermagnetism in the TiNiSi structural family: ferromagnetically ordered zigzag chains with antiferromagnetic interchain coupling in a relatively low-symmetry crystal environment. The TiNiSi structure type combines this motif with broad chemical flexibility and competing magnetic ground states. By combining first-principles screening of thermodynamic stability and magnetic ground states across 280 ternary systems, we identify 16 metallic d-wave altermagnets. This set recovers four experimentally known members---WFeB, NbMnP, TaMnP, and NbMnAs---and yields 12 new predictions, of which ScMnP, TaMnAs, ScMnAs, MoMnAs, MoMnSi, and WMnSi are the most promising. Noncollinear calculations indicate that the collinear altermagnetic configuration is the ground state across them. Each of the six leading new candidates has a nonzero spin-splitter angle and a finite anomalous Hall conductivity. Notably, ScMnP and TaMnAs exhibit strong spin-splitter responses despite modest altermagnetic band splitting. These results establish TiNiSi-type metallic d-wave altermagnets as a chemically versatile platform for efficient charge-to-spin conversion and provide an empirical magnetic-motif-guided route to identifying further candidates.

</details>


### [151] [Resistive Switching and Neuromorphic Computing in Metal/Nb:SrTiO$_3$: Mechanisms, Interface Physics, and Charge Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23430)
*Christopher Broyles,Elizabeth Krenkel,Frank Barrows,Sundar Kunwar,Aiping Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文综述了基于铌掺杂钛酸锶（Nb-doped SrTiO₃, Nb:STO）的金属/氧化物异质结中电阻开关（resistive switching, RS）机制的研究进展，提出一个统一框架——以金属/Nb:STO界面处外部界面层（extrinsic interfacial layer）的形成与演化为核心，整合电荷俘获、氧空位迁移、隧穿等多种看似竞争的机制，并为可靠的氧化物忆阻器件设计提供准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Nb:STO基忆阻器件因其免形成（forming-free）操作、大开关比和渐进式电导调制特性，在信息处理和新型计算领域具有持续吸引力，但经过二十余年研究，电阻开关的物理起源仍存在争议。现有多种竞争性机制（电荷俘获与去俘获、氧空位迁移、隧穿、界面氧化还原反应、导电细丝形成）均无法独立完整解释实验现象，亟需一个统一的理论框架。

Method: 本文采用综述（review）研究方法，系统梳理并对比金属/Nb:STO肖特基结中报道的多种电阻开关机制，分析这些看似竞争的解释之间的关联，并引入以外部界面层形成与演化为核心的综合框架。在该框架下，考察界面质量与缺陷介导过程（质子掺入、氧空位动力学、隧穿）对肖特基势垒调制与开关行为的影响，同时总结制备条件、测量协议和老化对界面形成及开关特性的影响。

Result: 研究表明，许多看似相互竞争的实验观察可以在统一的框架内得到理解，该框架聚焦于金属/Nb:STO界面处外部界面层的形成和演化。综述进一步确立了M/Nb:STO异质结的整合图像，并提出了通过界面与缺陷工程（interface and defect engineering）在M/Nb:STO和M/oxide/Nb:STO体系中设计可靠氧化物忆阻器件的设计原则。

Conclusion: 本文建立了关于金属/Nb:STO异质结电阻开关机制的整合性理解，表明外部界面层的形成与演化是统一解释多种实验现象的关键，并为面向实际应用的氧化物忆阻器件提供了基于界面与缺陷工程的可靠设计准则。

Abstract: Resistive switching (RS) in Nb-doped SrTiO$_3$ (Nb:STO) based memristive devices has attracted sustained interest in information processing and novel computing because of its forming-free operation, large on/off ratio, and gradual conductance modulation. Metal/Nb:STO Schottky junctions have emerged as a prototypical system for understanding RS mechanisms. Despite more than two decades of research, the physical origin of RS remains controversial, with proposed mechanisms including charge trapping and detrapping, oxygen vacancy migration, tunneling, interfacial redox reactions, and conductive filament formation. In this review, we examine these seemingly competing mechanisms and show that many experimental observations can be understood within a unified framework centered on the formation and evolution of an extrinsic interfacial layer at the metal/Nb:STO interface. We discuss how interface quality and defect-mediated processes, including proton incorporation, oxygen vacancy dynamics, and tunneling, govern Schottky barrier modulation and RS behavior. We further summarize how fabrication conditions, measurement protocols, and aging influence the interface formation and switching characteristics. This review establishes an integrated picture of M/Nb:STO heterojunctions and provides design principles for reliable oxide memristive devices through interface and defect engineering in M/Nb:STO and M/oxide/Nb:STO systems.

</details>


### [152] [Defect-Mediated Nucleation and Dynamics across the Phase Transition in the Excitonic Insulator Candidate Ta2NiSe5](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23438)
*Guilherme Rodrigues-Fontenele,Gabriel Fontenele,Ângelo Malachias,Rogério Magalhães-Paniago*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究利用变温扫描隧道显微镜与扫描隧道谱（scanning tunneling microscopy/spectroscopy）在实空间解析了准一维材料Ta2NiSe5在TC≈326 K处的结构-电子相变过程，确认了该马氏体式（martensitic-like）相变通过单斜相与正交相的空间共存及畴界演化来实现，而非均匀连续转变；初始区中正交相沿垂直于Ta-Ni-Ta链方向各向异性成核，而点缺陷和台阶边缘则充当局部成核中心，促进转变并抑制固有各向异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ta2NiSe5的相变虽通常被视为二级相变，但此前被认为可能具有马氏体特性，即转变可能通过空间相共存和畴界而非连续演化进行；然而该场景缺乏直接的实空间证据，本文旨在通过表面敏感测量直接观测并解析这一过程。

Method: 采用变温扫描隧道显微镜（variable-temperature STM）和扫描隧道谱（STS），在原始区域以及孤立点缺陷和台阶边缘附近，对单斜相与正交相的光谱特征进行空间分辨。

Result: 加热通过TC时，两种相以空间分离的区域长时间共存，由明确界定的畴界分隔，转变通过局域成核与生长进行而非空间均匀演化。在原始区域，正交相沿垂直于Ta-Ni-Ta链的方向各向异性成核；而点缺陷和台阶边缘作为局域成核中心促进转变并抑制各向异性。

Conclusion: 结果提供了直接实空间可视化证据，表明表面特有的结构和电子变化与局域无序共同调节了Ta2NiSe5中马氏体式相变的演化路径。

Abstract: Ta2NiSe5 is a quasi-one-dimensional material that exhibits a structural and electronic phase transition from a low-temperature monoclinic (semiconductor) to a high-temperature orthorhombic (semimetal) phase at approximately TC = 326 K. Here, we used variable-temperature scanning tunneling microscopy and spectroscopy to resolve the phase transition spatially, identifying the distinct spectroscopic signatures of the monoclinic and orthorhombic phases in pristine regions and near isolated point-defects and step edges. Although the phase transition of Ta2NiSe5 is generally regarded as second-order, it has previously been described as exhibiting martensitic-like characteristics. This implies that the transformation may proceed via spatial phase coexistence and domain boundaries rather than through a continuous evolution. Our surface-sensitive measurements confirm this scenario, retrieving the coexistence and evolution of monoclinic and orthorhombic domains in real space. Upon heating through TC, we find that the two phases coexist as spatially segregated regions over extended timescales, separated by well-defined boundaries that evolve via localized nucleation and growth, rather than a spatially uniform transformation. In pristine regions, the orthorhombic phase nucleates anisotropically, perpendicular to the Ta-Ni-Ta chains, whereas point-defects and step edges act as local nucleation centers that promote the transition and suppress this intrinsic anisotropy. These results provide direct real-space visualization of how surface-specific structural and electronic variations, together with local disorder, modify the martensitic-like phase transition in Ta2NiSe5.

</details>


### [153] [Emergent Noncollinearity and Near-Degenerate Magnetic Superlattices in AT6X6 Kagome Metals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23485)
*Weiyi Xia,Wei-Shen Tee,Peter Minch,Feng Zhang,Cai-Zhuang Wang,Vladimir Antropov*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文对若干具有较低磁基态稳定性的铁磁性AT6X6 Kagome化合物进行了电子结构和磁性基态研究，发现包括复杂自旋螺旋态和磁性长程超结构在内的异常磁有序，其中MgFe6Ga6存在竞争的自旋螺旋与长周期反铁磁结构，TiFe6Ga6具有双自旋螺旋基态。磁性表现为局域性，绝热能量分布揭示非海森堡长程相互作用（含强双二次项），并在TiMn6Ge6中发现磁隧穿条件。研究结果表明此类近简并磁超结构可自然形成，适合自旋电子学开关应用及中子衍射实验研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AT6X6 Kagome化合物是一类已观测到具有拓扑特征的量子磁性的热门体系，且其易于化学取代的特性提供了调控性质的机会。作者旨在探索磁基态稳定性较低的此类化合物，寻找异常磁有序结构及其在自旋电子学中的应用潜力。

Method: 采用电子结构计算与磁性基态研究方法，对多种AT6X6化合物进行系统性分析。通过绝热能量分布（adiabatic energy profile）考察磁相互作用性质，并确定各体系的磁基态构型。

Result: LiFe6Ga6和TiMn6Ge6保持共线反铁磁（collinear AFM）基态并具有低能FM/AFM层序列；MgFe6Ga6呈现竞争的自旋螺旋与长周期反铁磁结构；TiFe6Ga6具有双自旋螺旋基态。所有体系的磁性表现为局域磁性，存在非海森堡长程相互作用，其中双二次（biquadratic）项显著；TiMn6Ge6中发现了磁隧穿（magnetic tunneling）条件。

Conclusion: 在此类铁磁Kagome体系中，除传统磁拓扑特征外，可自然形成近简并的磁超结构（magnetic superstructure），适合自旋电子学开关（spintronic switching）应用。这些体系整体上是中子衍射和自旋电子学实验研究的重要候选平台。

Abstract: Ferromagnetic AT6X6 Kagome compounds are a popular class of systems in which quantum magnetism with topological features has been observed. These systems allow easy chemical substitution, creating an opportunity to fine-tune their properties. In this paper, we present electronic-structure and magnetic ground-state studies of several AT6X6 compounds with relatively low magnetic-ground-state stability. We find unusual magnetic orderings, including complex spin-spiral states and the formation of magnetic long-range superstructures. While LiFe6Ga6 and TiMn6Ge6 retain collinear AFM ground states with low-energy FM/AFM layer sequences, competing spin-spiral and long-period antiferromagnetic structures in MgFe6Ga6 and a double-spin-spiral ground state in TiFe6Ga6 were determined. Magnetism in all these systems appears local, with adiabatic energy profiles suggesting non-Heisenberg long-range interactions, including a strong biquadratic term. In TiMn6Ge6, we found the conditions for magnetic tunneling. Our results show that, in addition to traditional magnetic topological features in such FM Kagome systems, near-degenerate magnetic superstructures suitable for spintronic switching applications can form naturally. Overall, these systems represent a potentially rich playground for neutron diffraction and spintronics experimental studies.

</details>


### [154] [Large Jahn-Teller shifts and splittings observed in halide perovskite CsPbBr3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23510)
*Maryam Sajedi,Maxim Krivenkov,Dmitry Marchenko,Saleem Ayaz Khan,Andrei Varykhalov,Jaime Sánchez-Barriga,Daniel M. Többens,Thomas Unold,Ján Minár,Oliver Rader*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在CsPbBr3卤化物钙钛矿中首次同时观测到杨-泰勒效应（Jahn-Teller effect）的两个关键特征：正交相复带（replica bands）的出现以及数百毫电子伏（meV）量级的能带窄化、能级劈裂和简并解除，并通过温度依赖的X射线衍射（X-ray diffraction）将这些效应直接关联到PbBr6八面体的倾斜与旋转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一阶和二阶杨-泰勒效应在塑造结构和电子性质方面具有基础性作用，但此前在固体角分辨光电子能谱（angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy）中几乎未有观测；卤化物和氧化物钙钛矿中偶尔报道的复带结构缺少伴随的能移或劈裂，无法确认其源自杨-泰勒效应。

Method: 采用角分辨光电子能谱对CsPbBr3进行温度依赖测量，识别冷却过程中出现的正交相复带；结合温度依赖的X射线衍射获取结构输入参数，开展能带结构计算，将电子结构变化与PbBr6八面体的倾斜和旋转直接联系起来。

Result: 在CsPbBr3中发现额外的价带极大值（valence band maximum）位于Γ点，且在室温下即可分辨；沿Γ-M方向出现数百meV量级的能带窄化、Γ点处能级劈裂以及不等价M点间简并解除（degeneracy lifting）。这些效应均与PbBr6八面体的倾转直接关联。

Conclusion: 实验结果揭示了卤化物钙钛矿中存在强电子-晶格相互作用（electron-lattice interaction），这一发现为理解极性输运、动态无序和激子俘获等长期未解问题提供了关键基础。

Abstract: Despite the fundamental role of Jahn-Teller effects of first and second order in shaping structural and electronic properties, there is hardly any observation in angle-resolved photoemission of solids. In oxide and halide perovskites, band structure replicas have occasionally been reported as fingerprints of Jahn-Teller effects, but no accompanying energy shifts or splittings that would allow a conclusion about their origin. In CsPbBr3, we uncover both key signatures: Upon cooling, orthorhombic replica bands emerge which had eluded earlier studies. This includes an extra valence band maximum at Γ which can even be distinguished at room temperature. Most importantly, band narrowing along Γ-M, a splitting at Γ, and the lifting of degeneracy between nonequivalent M points, all of several 100 meV, become apparent. Temperature-dependent x-ray diffraction, used as input for band structure calculations, links these effects directly to tilts and rotations of the PbBr6 octahedra. Our results uncover a strong electron-lattice interaction which is at the heart of so-far unresolved questions concerning polaronic transport, dynamic disorder, and exciton trapping in halide perovskites.

</details>

### [155] [General Stirling-Ramanujan Constants are exponential periods and applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22113)
*Mounir Hajli,Ricardo Perez-Marco*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了广义Stirling-Ramanujan常数（General Stirling-Ramanujan constants）及其扭变形式均为指数周期（exponential periods），并建立了它们与Hurwitz zeta函数导数、Bendersky Gamma函数、球面上拉普拉斯算子zeta正则化行列式以及L函数导数之间的深刻联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知Stirling-Ramanujan常数$S_n$（即发散级数$\sum_{k\geq 1} k^n \log k$的Ramanujan和）在基域$\mathbb{Q}(t,e^{-t})$上是指数周期。作者希望将该结果推广到更广泛的一类常数，以揭示这类特殊值在数论与几何中的深层统一结构。

Method: 作者引入了广义Stirling-Ramanujan常数$S(P,Q)$，将其定义为级数$\sum_{k\geq 1} P(k)\log Q(k)$的Ramanujan和。利用积分表示与指数周期理论，他们证明了这些常数在基域$\mathbb{K}_P((\omega))(t,e^{-t},(e^{-\omega t}))$上是指数周期。此外，他们通过周期性函数$\chi$引入扭变Ramanujan求和并定义了广义扭变Stirling-Ramanujan常数$S_\chi(P,Q)$，推导出相应的积分公式。

Result: 证明了Hurwitz zeta函数在负整数处的导数$\zeta'_H(-n,w)$是基域$\mathbb{Q}(t,e^{-t},w,e^{-wt})$上的指数周期；Bendersky Gamma函数$\hat{\Gamma}_n$在$\Re w>0$处的对数值同样是指数周期；球面上拉普拉斯算子的zeta正则化行列式的对数在基域$\mathbb{Q}(t,e^{-t})$上是指数周期；$\zeta(2n+1)/\pi^{2n}$也是该基域上的指数周期。对于$L$函数的导数$L'_\chi(-n)$，证明了其可用$S_\chi(P,Q)$表示且是基域$\mathbb{Q}(\chi)(t,e^{-t})$上的指数周期。

Conclusion: 该研究将Stirling-Ramanujan常数的指数周期性质系统性地推广到广义情形及扭变情形，建立了这些常数与zeta函数、L函数、Gamma函数和几何对象（如拉普拉斯行列式）之间的统一框架，表明这些看似不同的数学对象共享相同的超越性结构。

Abstract: For $n\geq 0$, Stirling-Ramanujan constants $S_n$ are the Ramanujan summation of the divergent series $\sum_{k\geq 1} k^n\log k$. These constants are exponential periods over the base field $\mathbb{Q}(t,e^{-t})$. We generalize this result to a broad class of General Stirling-Ramanujan constants. Given polynomials $P$ and $Q$, with $\Re Q(s)>0$ for $\Re s >0$, the constant $S(P,Q)$ is the Ramanujan summation of the series $\sum_{k\geq 1} P(k)\log Q(k)$. They are exponential periods over the base field $\mathbb{K}_P((ω)) (t,e^{-t}, (e^{-ωt}))$ where $\mathbb{K}_P$ is the field of definition of $P$ and $(ω)$ are the zeros of $Q$. These constants are related to the derivatives $ζ'_H(-n,w)$ of Hurwitz zeta function at negative integers, which we prove are exponential periods over the base field $\mathbb{Q}\left(t,e^{-t}, w,e^{-wt}\right)$. They can be expressed using Bendersky Gamma functions $\hat Γ_n$ and the values $\log \hat Γ_n(w)$ for $\Re w >0$ are exponential periods over the base field $\mathbb{Q}\left(t,e^{-t}, w,e^{-wt}\right)$. The logarithms of the zeta-regularized determinants of the Laplacian on spheres are exponential periods in the base field $\mathbb Q (t,e^{-t})$. The values $ζ(2n+1)/π^{2n}$ are exponential periods in the base field $\mathbb Q(t,e^{-t})$. For a periodic function $χ:\mathbb{Z}\to \mathbb{C}$, we extend Ramanujan summation to the twisted series $\sum_{k\geq 1} χ(k) P(k)\log Q(k)$ and define General Twisted Stirling-Ramanujan constants $S_χ(P,Q)$. We derive integral formulas proving that they are exponential periods over $\mathbb{Q}(χ)(t,e^{-t})$. For $n\geq 0$, the derivative of the $L$-function, $L_χ'(-n)$ can be expressed in terms of these constants and is an exponential period over the base field $\mathbb{Q}(χ)(t,e^{-t})$.

</details>

### [156] [A surviving pink spinel records an early aluminous melt on the ureilite parent body](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21711)
*Yaozhu Li,Phil J. A. McCausland,Roberta L. Flemming,Noriko T. Kita,Carsten Detlefs*

Main category: astro-ph.EP

TL;DR: 本文通过对多碎屑橄榄古陨石（polymict ureilite）EET 87720中富铝尖晶石（aluminous spinel）的矿物学、同位素和显微结构分析，揭示其是来自橄榄陨石母体（ureilite parent body）的原生晶体，记录了早期富铝熔体结晶产物，为行星早期分异过程提供了新证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 橄榄陨石（ureilites）被解释为分异母体的碎片，但其成因和演化仍存在争论，因为其既表现出织构平衡（textural equilibrium）又具有化学原始组成（chemically primitive compositions）。需要新的矿物学和同位素证据来厘清这些陨石的原生性与演化历史。

Method: 对多碎屑橄榄陨石EET 87720样品进行了矿物学、原位三重氧同位素（in situ triple oxygen isotope）分析以及三维暗场X射线显微镜（3D dark-field X-ray microscopy）观测，以表征尖晶石和共生镁橄榄石的成分、同位素组成及变形微结构。

Result: 样品含有异常富镁的橄榄石（Mg#高达98.7）和贫钙辉石（Wo低至1.0），以及罕见的粗粒粉色富铝尖晶石（Al2O3含量56.4-58.7 wt%，Cr2O3含量11.3-11.8 wt%）。尖晶石和橄榄石的氧同位素沿约1斜率的碳质球粒无水矿物（CCAM）线分布，与整体橄榄陨石一致；碎屑遵循橄榄陨石Fe丢失/加入趋势，并具有近恒定的球粒陨石Mn/Mg比值。三维暗场X射线显微镜揭示了尖晶石中层级变形微结构，包括分布晶格曲率、局部滑移带状边界和相干镶嵌畴边界。尖晶石与橄榄石之间的Al分配得出结晶温度1318±43 K。

Conclusion: 富铝尖晶石可能从局部富铝、贫钙且在低氧逸度（low oxygen fugacity）条件下形成的熔体中结晶，结晶温度与热升高（thermally elevated）的母体一致。该尖晶石保留了早期富铝熔体稀有的结晶产物，而这类熔体在橄榄陨石记录中大多已消失，为早期行星分异提供了重要档案。

Abstract: Ureilites are ultramafic achondrites interpreted as fragments of a differentiated parent body, yet their origin and evolution remain debated because textural equilibrium coexists with chemically primitive compositions. Here we report mineralogical, isotopic, and microstructural observations from polymict ureilite Elephant Moraine (EET) 87720. The sample contains unusually magnesian olivine (Mg# up to 98.7), Ca-poor pyroxene (Wo as low as 1.0), and rare coarse-grained pink aluminous spinel containing 56.4-58.7 wt% Al2O3 and 11.3-11.8 wt% Cr2O3. In situ triple oxygen isotope measurements of spinel and associated forsteritic olivine plot along the ~1-slope Carbonaceous Chondrite Anhydrous Mineral (CCAM) line, consistent with bulk ureilites. The clasts also follow the ureilitic Fe-loss/addition trend, with near-constant chondritic Mn/Mg ratios. These observations demonstrate that the clasts are indigenous to the ureilite parent body and extend the known ureilite oxygen isotope range to delta18O ~9.7 per mil. Three-dimensional dark-field X-ray microscopy reveals a hierarchical deformation microstructure in the spinel, comprising distributed lattice curvature, localized slip-band-like boundaries, and coherent mosaic-domain boundaries, indicating multiscale accommodation of shock-induced crystal-plastic deformation. We propose that the aluminous spinel crystallized from a locally Al-rich, Ca-poor melt under low oxygen fugacity. Al partitioning between coexisting spinel and olivine yields a crystallization temperature of 1318 +/- 43 K, consistent with a thermally elevated parent body. The spinel may therefore preserve a rare crystallization product of an early aluminous melt that has largely disappeared from the ureilite record, providing an archive of early planetary differentiation.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.other'></div>

# cond-mat.other [[Back]](#toc)

### [157] [Theory of the Spinon-Mediated Witness Spin Glass in Herbertsmithite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21514)
*Mitikorn Wood-Thanan,Felix Flicker*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个仅含单一自由参数（witness-kagome自旋耦合）的理论模型，完整解释了Herbertsmithite中磁性杂质作为量子自旋液体'见证者'（witness）的所有实验现象，并预测Z2与U(1)两种候选量子自旋液体在260 mK以下的中子散射强度存在定性差异，为区分二者提供了决定性检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Herbertsmithite是典型的候选量子自旋液体（quantum spin liquid, QSL）材料，但磁杂质的存在阻碍了对长程纠缠基态和去禁闭分数化自旋子（spinon）激发的确认。近期杂质的角色被重新概念化为QSL的'见证者'，本文旨在对这一想法给出完整的理论描述。

Method: 构建一个仅含单一自由参数——见证者与kagome晶格自旋之间耦合——的理论模型，通过该模型对自旋玻璃形成、磁噪声的频率与温度依赖、直流磁化率尖峰以及2 K下的静态中子散射结构因子进行全面拟合与预测，并比较Z2和U(1)两种候选QSL的理论结果。

Result: 模型成功重现全部实验数据，包括见证者间的自旋玻璃形成、磁噪声的频率与温度依赖、DC磁化率随温度的尖峰及2 K下的静态中子散射结构因子。两种候选QSL（Z2和U(1)）与数据吻合程度相似，但模型预测二者在260 mK以下的中子散射强度存在定性差异，并在高见证者浓度下分别转变为不同的长程有序见证者态。

Conclusion: 该理论模型为理解Herbertsmithite中磁性杂质在QSL中的作用提供了统一框架，并给出了区分Z2与U(1)两种QSL候选类型的决定性实验判据，以及在温度-见证者浓度相图上的具体预测。

Abstract: Herbertsmithite is a prototypical candidate quantum spin liquid (QSL), believed to feature a long-range entangled ground state and deconfined fractionalised spinon excitations. Confirmation of these properties has been hindered by the presence of magnetic impurities (spin-1/2 Cu2+ spins substituted onto non-magnetic Zn2+ sites). Recently these impurities were reconceptualised as 'witnesses' of the QSL, inheriting long-range interactions and entanglement mediated by the QSL spinons, leading to spin glass formation amongst witnesses below 260 mK. Here we present a full theoretical account of this idea. Despite having only one free parameter (the witness-kagome spin coupling), our model captures the full range of experimental data, including: the formation of a spin glass amongst witnesses; the frequency and temperature dependence of the magnetic noise; a sharp peak in the DC magnetic susceptibility as a function of temperature; and the static neutron scattering structure factor at 2 K. Both candidate QSLs (Z2 and U(1)) give similar agreement with all data; however, our model predicts a qualitative difference between the Z2 and U(1) neutron scattering intensities below 260 mK, providing a long-sought definitive test to distinguish the two cases. We also predict phase diagrams as a function of temperature and witness concentration, finding a phase transition to different long-range ordered witness states for Z2 and U(1) at high concentration.

</details>


### [158] [Nonreciprocal Control of the Goos--Hänchen Shift via the Barnett Effect in Cavity Magnomechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21795)
*Shah Fahad,Gao Xianlong*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本研究提出了在混合腔磁机械系统（hybrid cavity magnomechanical system）中实现可调谐非互易古斯-汉欣位移（nonreciprocal Goos-Hänchen shift, GHS）的理论方案。该系统由嵌入微波腔中的旋转钇铁石榴石（yttrium iron garnet）球体构成，通过磁偶极和磁致伸缩相互作用分别实现磁子-光子与磁子-声子耦合。利用巴尼特效应（Barnett effect）实现磁子频率的旋转诱导位移，并通过反向偏置磁场实现GHS的非互易响应，为磁可重构微波光子器件提供新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有古斯-汉欣位移的调控机制多为互易性（reciprocal）的，缺乏在微波频段实现非互易光束位移的有效方案。本研究旨在利用腔磁机械系统中的旋转效应与多体相互作用，实现可调谐且方向非对称的GHS，以满足微波光子器件对方向选择性调控的需求。

Method: 采用理论分析方法，构建包含旋转钇铁石榴石球体与微波腔的混合腔磁机械系统模型；利用巴尼特效应引入磁子频率的旋转相关位移，通过改变偏置磁场方向调控位移符号；分析输出探针光谱的FANO共振（Fano resonance）特征及其对应的GHS响应，并考察磁子-光子与磁子-声子耦合强度及腔长对GHS的影响。

Result: 输出探针光谱呈现FANO共振特征，相应的GHS对相反磁场方向表现出非对称响应，从而实现非互易光束位移。磁子-光子与磁子-声子相互作用对GHS产生相反方向的影响，而腔长提供了额外的可调谐自由度。

Conclusion: 该方案为磁可重构（magnetically reconfigurable）微波光子器件和巴尼特诱导有效场的高灵敏探测提供了可行的理论路径，推动了非互易控制技术在微波频段的应用发展。

Abstract: We propose a theoretical scheme for realizing a tunable nonreciprocal Goos-Hänchen shift (GHS) in a hybrid cavity magnomechanical system. The setup consists of a rotating yttrium iron garnet sphere embedded in a microwave cavity, with magnetic-dipole and magnetostrictive interactions mediating magnon-photon and magnon-phonon couplings, respectively. Owing to the Barnett effect, the magnon frequency acquires a rotation-induced shift whose sign can be reversed by changing the direction of the bias magnetic field. We show that the output probe spectrum exhibits a Fano resonance, while the associated GHS responds asymmetrically to opposite field directions, providing a controllable mechanism for nonreciprocal beam shifts. The magnon-photon and magnon-phonon interactions are found to affect the GHS in opposite ways, while the cavity length offers an additional degree of tunability. These results provide a route toward magnetically reconfigurable microwave photonic devices and sensitive detection of Barnett-induced effective fields.

</details>

### [159] [Coexistence of Magnon-Induced Optical Vortex and Gaussian Beam Scattering Assisted by Rotational Umklapp Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21788)
*Ryusuke Hisatomi,Kotaro Taga,Hisakazu Matsuki,Shutaro Karube,Yoichi Shiota,Teruo Ono*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文实验证实，在特定铁磁晶体取向下，高斯光束散射与光学涡旋散射可以共存，其机理是晶体晶格通过旋转Umklapp过程提供了角动量补偿，并预测光聚焦程度提高时两种散射过程的相对效率会发生反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于磁振子与光子间角动量守恒的理论预期认为，在布里渊光散射中，当聚焦光平行于外磁场传播并与铁磁均匀磁振子相互作用时，只允许光学涡旋散射发生。这一预期与实验观察之间的矛盾需要被解释，揭示了晶格对称性对光-磁振子角动量转移的新调控作用。

Method: 通过实验测量布里渊光散射（Brillouin light scattering）信号，在特定铁磁晶体取向下观测高斯光束散射与光学涡旋散射的共存现象；结合磁光耦合（magneto-optical coupling）与光学自旋轨道相互作用（optical spin-orbit interaction）的理论框架，并引入旋转Umklapp过程解释缺失的角动量来源。

Result: 实验首次证实，在特定铁磁晶体取向下，高斯光束散射与光学涡旋散射能够共存，推翻了仅基于磁振子-光子角动量守恒的传统预期。结果表明晶格通过旋转Umklapp过程为高斯光束散射提供了缺失的角动量；此外，理论预测当光聚焦程度增强时，两种散射过程的相对效率将发生反转。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了晶格对称性在调控结构光与磁振子之间角动量转移中的核心作用，为光磁子学（optomagnonics）研究开辟了新前沿，并为未来通过调节光聚焦条件主动操控散射通道提供理论依据。

Abstract: The exploitation of crystal-lattice symmetries to engineer angular momentum transfer between structured light and magnons marks a novel frontier for optomagnonic research. In Brillouin light scattering, when focused light propagates parallel to an external magnetic field and interacts with ferromagnetic uniform magnons, only optical-vortex scattering is expected to be permitted. Due to the combined effects of magneto-optical coupling and optical spin-orbit interaction, the transfer of magnon spin angular momentum to photon orbital angular momentum allows for this distinctive scattering phenomenon. Here, we experimentally demonstrate that, for a specific ferromagnetic crystal orientation, Gaussian-beam scattering coexists with the optical-vortex scattering, contrary to conventional expectations based on angular momentum conservation between magnons and photons. We show that the crystal lattice, via the rotational Umklapp process, provides the missing angular momentum required for the Gaussian-beam scattering. Furthermore, we predict that as the degree of light focusing increases, the relative efficiencies of the Gaussian-beam and optical-vortex scattering processes reverse.

</details>


### [160] [Critical Topological Photonics in Synthetic Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21791)
*Mingyuan Chen,Yuwei Jing,Franco Nori,Xue-Jia Yu*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文在合成频率光子平台上提出了一类拓扑与临界性共存的拓扑态，通过一维合成格点中的中带简并（midgap degeneracy）识别临界拓扑光子态，并发现了由拓扑强制产生的多临界点，进一步推广到二维体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为拓扑态与临界性互不相容——前者需要有限谱隙，后者要求谱隙闭合；拓扑光子学因此几乎只关注有隙相。作者旨在打破这一限制，探索拓扑与临界性共存的可能性。

Method: 在实验上可达的合成频率光子平台中构建一维合成格点，通过单粒子纠缠谱（single-particle entanglement spectrum）中的中带简并识别临界拓扑光子态，并进一步将框架推广至二维。

Result: 发现了由拓扑强制产生的多临界点（multicritical point），该临界点能够重组相邻临界态的拓扑结构；该框架成功延伸至二维体系。

Conclusion: 该工作为临界拓扑光子学（critical topological photonics）提供了实验上可及的路径，并可能启发临界拓扑探测（critical topological sensing）等新型应用。

Abstract: Topological states and criticality have long been regarded as incompatible ingredients: the former requires a finite spectral gap, whereas the latter demands its closure. Guided by this view, topological photonics has focused almost exclusively on gapped phases, treating gap-closing transitions as mere phase boundaries. In this work, we propose a class of topological states in which topology coexists with criticality in experimentally accessible synthetic-frequency photonic platforms. In a one-dimensional (1D) synthetic lattice, we identify such critical topological photonic states through midgap degeneracies in the single-particle entanglement spectrum, and uncover a topology-enforced multicritical point that reorganizes the topology of neighboring critical states. We further extend this framework to two dimensions (2D). Our work provides an experimentally accessible route to critical topological photonics, and may also inspire novel applications such as critical topological sensing.

</details>


### [161] [Topological space-time waves in complex channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22848)
*Renwei Zou,Kelsey Everts,Andrew Forbes,Yungui Ma*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文通过在微波频段利用对称性破缺的超表面（symmetry-broken metasurface）的共振响应，一步生成拓扑时空光束（topological space-time beams），并直接观察其在自由空间到复杂随机介质等多种信道中的动力学，证明了拓扑结构在自由度被打乱时的保持性，进而实现首次无串扰的时空波传输，为时空波物理及其实际应用提供了新工具和路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时空结构化光（structured light）蕴含丰富的基础物理现象并催生新奇的波形式，但其产生受限于复杂的光学系统，且在任意信道中直接观测其演化十分困难——色散与衍射的相互作用、缺乏合适的探测工具进一步阻碍了研究进展。

Method: 利用对称性破缺的谐振超表面，通过其共振响应将空间（spatial）、时间（temporal）和偏振（polarisation）三个自由度混合于单个微波场中，一步生成拓扑时空光束；由于工作于微波频段，可在从自由空间到复杂随机介质等任意信道中直接观测其动力学。

Result: 在微波频段直接观测了拓扑时空波在各种信道中的演化，发现其拓扑得以保持而基础自由度被打乱；并进一步利用该控制能力，实现了首次无串扰（crosstalk-free）的时空波传输。

Conclusion: 该工作推进了时空波物理的研究，提出了时空波产生与控制的新工具，并为时空波在真实场景中的应用（例如通过噪声信道的稳健通信）提供了可行的路线图。

Abstract: Structured light in space and time has become a powerful playground in which to explore the fundamental physics of wave systems, while simultaneously introducing new exotic forms of light, from spatio-temporal vortices to toroidal pulses of light. Yet their creation remains restricted by complex optical systems while directly observing their evolution in arbitrary channels remains elusive, complicated by the interplay of dispersion and diffraction and exacerbated by the lack of suitable detection tools. Here we create topological space-time beams in a single step by a resonant response of a symmetry-broken metasurface, mixing spatial, temporal and polarisation degrees of freedom in a single microwave field. Our realisation in the microwave regime allows us to directly observe their dynamics in arbitrary channels, from free-space to complex random media, showing the preservation of topology while the foundational degrees of freedom are scrambled. We use our control to show how to unscramble the space-time properties for the first crosstalk-free transmission of space-time waves. Our work advances the physics of space-time waves, introduces a new toolkit for their creation and control, and offers an exciting roadmap to their exploitation in real-world scenarios, e.g., for robust communications through noisy channels.

</details>


### [162] [Self-consistent modelling of all-optical switching and magneto-optic readout in an integrated Si$_3$N$_4$/GdFeCo waveguide](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22969)
*Muhammad Arief Mulyana,Budi Adiperdana,Ayi Bahtiar*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文针对全光开关磁性薄膜后的导波行为进行了首次计算。研究团队将三维色散有限时域差分求解器与吸收光功、四温模型和随机双子格朗道-利夫希茨-布洛赫动力学等耦合，对一个集成Si3N4/GdFeCo波导元件的传输、反射、吸收和偏振响应进行了完整仿真，发现磁化仅通过反对称介电张量元素影响体系，且通过偏振测量和波瓣平衡可实现磁化状态的高灵敏度区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管全光开关已在氮化硅波导上通过电学读出得以验证，但光束穿过全光开关磁性薄膜后所携带的信息此前从未被计算。本文旨在填补这一空白，建立从光吸收到磁化动力学再到读出信号的完整计算链路。

Method: 研究耦合了三类工具：三维色散有限时域差分（finite-difference time-domain）求解器用于电磁场计算；四温模型描述热动力学；随机的双子格朗道-利夫希茨-布洛赫（Landau-Lifshitz-Bloch）动力学结合涨落耗散一致的朗之万（Langevin）场描述磁化演化，并将磁化反馈至回旋介电张量供第二束导波读出。

Result: 传输、反射和吸收对磁化不敏感（饱和态、退磁态和多畴态之间差异至多0.02），因为磁化通过反对称介电张量元素以二阶进入吸收功；而奇磁偏振计在法拉第（Faraday）旋转和科尔（Kerr）旋转上分别实现一个和两个数量级的分辨。导波几何带来-11.77°的几何基座，参考退磁膜可将反射通道恢复至0.063°。校准脉冲可从任一侧饱和态翻转0.859的磁化分数，切换分数与单格点阈值下的导模吸收图互补累积分布吻合至8×10⁻⁴精度；从零磁化出发时，分支选择交由朗之万场决定，16个种子下占据率为0.502±0.004。

Conclusion: 该工作完成了集成Si3N4/GdFeCo全光开关元件的光-磁-光完整计算链，揭示了导波几何下磁化读出的优势（偏振及波瓣平衡双通道）与代价（几何基座），并证明未完全翻转的磁化差异源于光子而非磁性因素。

Abstract: What a guided beam carries after passing through an all-optically switched magnetic film has not been computed, although such switching has been demonstrated on a silicon nitride waveguide with electrical readout. We close the chain for an integrated Si$_3$N$_4$/GdFeCo element, an 8 nm film on a 60 $μ$m waveguide, coupling a three-dimensional dispersive finite-difference time-domain solver to absorbed optical work, a four-temperature model, and stochastic two-sublattice Landau-Lifshitz-Bloch dynamics with a fluctuation-dissipation-consistent Langevin field, the magnetization feeding back into the gyrotropic permittivity that a second guided shot reads. Transmission, reflection and absorption are magnetization-blind, closing to 0.974-0.975 and shifting by at most 0.02 between saturated, demagnetized and multidomain films, because magnetization enters the dielectric tensor only through an antisymmetric element on which absorbed power depends at second order; the odd-in-magnetization polarimetry separates them by an order of magnitude in Faraday rotation and two in Kerr. The guided geometry levies a cost free-space measurement does not: a $-11.77^\circ$ geometric pedestal on the reflected channel, exceeding the $8.24^\circ$ rotation it hides. Referencing against a demagnetized film returns it to $0.063^\circ$. The transmitted mode carries a second signature in its lobe balance, needing no polarimetry. Calibrated pulses toggle 0.859 of the film from either saturated state across a $2.4\times$ fluence window, and the shortfall from unity is photonic rather than magnetic: the switched fraction is the complementary cumulative distribution of the guided-mode absorption map at the single-cell threshold, holding to $8\times10^{-4}$ at five full-wave anchors. From exactly zero magnetization, branch selection passes to the Langevin field alone, at occupancy $0.502 \pm 0.004$ over 16 seeds.

</details>

### [163] [Ricci-flat string algebroids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22455)
*Agnaldo A. da Silva,Henrique N. Sá Earp,Bernardo S. Vieira*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究由4-形式（4-forms）定义的一类几何结构，统一涵盖带有挠率的近似埃尔米特（almost Hermitian）、G₂、Spin(7)以及近似四元数/超埃尔米特（almost quaternion/hyper Hermitian）几何。在该统一框架下，底层4-形式自然确定了一个弦代数胚（string algebroid），并由此导出规范场论（gauge-theoretic）结构、瞬子（instanton）概念以及广义里奇平坦（generalized Ricci-flat）度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立一个统一框架，将多种带有挠率的重要几何结构（如近似埃尔米特、G₂、Spin(7)等）纳入同一理论体系，并揭示它们与广义几何（generalized geometry）及弦论（heterotic string theory）中规范场论结构的深层联系。

Method: 以4-形式作为统一的基础几何数据进行建模，由其推导出弦代数胚的几何数据；在此基础上定义规范场论结构中的瞬子概念，并证明这些结构可归结为广义里奇平坦度量；最后通过文献中的主要实例以及若干显式构造进行验证。

Result: 证明了由4-形式确定的几何数据可以诱导广义里奇平坦度量，建立了瞬子的典范定义，并给出文献中主要例子的统一描述以及多个新的显式构造。

Conclusion: 4-形式提供了一个统一的几何框架，将多种类型的带挠率几何与广义几何和异质弦论中的规范场论结构联系起来，为相关领域的研究提供了新的统一视角。

Abstract: We investigate geometric structures defined by 4-forms, encompassing important classes of geometric structures with torsion including almost Hermitian, $\mathrm{G}_2$, $\mathrm{Spin}(7)$, and almost quaternion/hyper Hermitian. Within this unified framework, the underlying 4-form naturally determines geometric data that define a string algebroid over the manifold. In particular, it gives rise to a canonical notion of instanton, thereby linking these geometries to gauge-theoretic structures in generalized geometry and heterotic string theory. We further prove that these data induce generalized Ricci-flat metrics. Finally, we examine the main examples in the literature and construct several explicit ones.

</details>


<div id='hep-ex'></div>

# hep-ex [[Back]](#toc)

### [164] [Gaussian-derivative expansion of detector resolution functions via convolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21908)
*Zan Ren*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的傅里叶空间（Fourier-space）框架，将可写为高斯函数与核卷积（Gaussian convolved with a kernel）的探测器分辨率函数展开为高斯导数（Gaussian derivatives）级数，并建立了容许性判据（admissibility criterion），对多种实际使用的核函数进行了可容许性分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于探测器的光谱学和高能物理中，窄共振的观测线型会受到探测器有限分辨率的畸变。高斯函数虽为标准的一阶近似，但实际分辨率函数以非平凡方式偏离高斯形式，缺乏统一的框架来描述和比较这类非高斯参数化。

Method: 提出统一傅里叶空间框架，将分辨率函数表示为高斯与核的卷积f=G*h，并展开为高斯导数级数。Voigt分布作为原型，机制可推广至傅里叶变换在原点解析且为指数型的任意核函数。建立容许性判据，并将多种实际使用的核函数分类为直接、微扰或渐近可容许。

Result: 对实际使用的多种核函数——包括涂抹的Hypatia（smeared Hypatia）、Cruijff分布、拉普拉斯分布（Laplacian distribution）以及广义双曲核（generalised hyperbolic core）——逐一进行了容许性分类，分别判定为直接可容许、微扰可容许或渐近可容许。

Conclusion: 该框架为非高斯分辨率参数化提供了一个围绕高斯的统一比较语言和构造工具，有助于在探测器光谱学和高能物理领域标准化非高斯线型建模。

Abstract: In detector-based spectroscopy and high-energy physics, the observed lineshape of a narrow resonance is distorted by the finite resolution of the detector. While the Gaussian is the standard first approximation, realistic resolution functions deviate from it in nontrivial ways. We present a unified Fourier-space framework in which a resolution function that can be written as a Gaussian convolved with a kernel, $f=G*h$, is expanded into a series of Gaussian derivatives. The Voigtian distribution serves as the prototype, and the mechanism extends to arbitrary kernels whose Fourier transforms are analytic at the origin and of exponential type. We establish an admissibility criterion, and then examine several kernels used in practice--- smeared Hypatia, Cruijff, the Laplacian distribution, and the generalised hyperbolic core---classifying each as directly, perturbatively, or asymptotically admissible. The framework thus provides a common language for comparing and constructing non-Gaussian resolution parametrisations around the Gaussian.

</details>


### [165] [Scouting Super Resolution: from HLT-Level Reconstruction to Offline-like Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22262)
*Maurizio Pierini*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文展示了利用回归模型（regression）基于目标物理对象及其周围粒子云（particle cloud）训练，可以将CMS实验Data Scouting流的在线（在线）重建分辨率压缩到离线（offline）数据的典型不确定带内，使scouting数据可在实际应用中使用标准离线标定程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Data Scouting技术虽能使CMS实验记录的统计数据量约为标准物理流（physics stream）的10倍，但其在线重建分辨率较差，且需要推导专用的在线到离线修正（online-to-offline corrections），这成为限制scouting数据使用的主要瓶颈。

Method: 在CMS Run-1 Open Data上，将同一事件分别通过HLT（High-Level Trigger）在线重建和离线重建进行处理，构成配对数据集。在此基础上训练一个统一的回归模型，对电子（electron）和喷注（jet）对象施加修正，该模型不针对特定对象调整架构。

Result: 相同的网络架构无需修改即可应用于电子与喷注，在运动学、隔离度、能量构成等多种量上将残余偏差压低1至2个数量级。按p_T微分测量，修正后的对象落入CMS提供的离线标定不确定度（uncertainty band）范围内，而未修正的HLT对象则位于该范围之外。

Conclusion: 经过修正的scouting数据对象可采用标准的中心化标定流程处理，使得scouting数据在实用层面与离线数据高度接近，可显著扩展实验的数据采集能力。

Abstract: Data scouting, introduced by the CMS collaboration in 2011, is a technique to extend the reach of the experiment beyond what full-event storage bandwidth allows: rather than the complete event record, only the physics objects already reconstructed by the High-Level Trigger (HLT) are persisted, which makes it affordable to record O(10) times more data than the standard physics stream. The price for this rate increase is in experimental resolution: online reconstruction has typically a lower resolution and a worse response, mostly due to shortcuts taken to speed up the reconstruction and to the use of suboptimal calibration constants. For this reason, scouting data has mostly been used when the resolution loss is negligible in absolute terms (muons) or with respect to the offline resolution (jets). In any case, the need to derive specific online-to-offline corrections has been a limiting factor. We show that a regression trained on a given object and the surrounding particle cloud can bring the gap between online and offline resolution within the typical uncertainty band of the offline data. This procedure could be applied to the scouting monitoring stream, the subset of scouting data also reconstructed offline. We demonstrate this on CMS Run-1 Open Data, reprocessed through paired HLT and offline passes over the same events. One architecture, applied unchanged to electrons and jets, suppresses residual biases by one to two orders of magnitude on various quantities (kinematics, isolation, energy composition) and achieves a resolution improvement. Measured differentially in p_T, the corrected objects fall inside the offline calibration uncertainties CMS quotes for this dataset, where the uncorrected HLT objects lie outside them: a corrected object can be treated with the standard, centrally provided calibration, bringing scouting data much closer to offline data for practical purposes.

</details>


### [166] [Differentiable Parametric Simulation and Reconstruction Models in Parnassus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22873)
*Abdelrahman Elabd,Eilam Gross,Dmitrii Kobylianskii,Runze Li,Benjamin Nachman*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将粒子物理探测器模拟中的参数化模型（如Delphes类）变得完全可微的方法，使得其参数可以通过梯度下降自动拟合到目标探测样本，从而统一了Parnassus框架中深度生成网络与手写参数模型之间的不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Parnassus框架中的快速探测器模拟和重建模型存在两种构建方式：基于深度生成网络自动拟合，以及基于手工构造的参数化处方（如Delphes）。这两种方式之间存在不对称性，本文旨在消除这一不对称，使参数化模型也能自动拟合。

Method: 作者将参数化模型完全可微化，使其参数可以通过梯度下降（gradient descent）方式拟合到目标样本。为验证可行性，将参数化模型拟合到其自身已知配置生成的样本上，恢复生成参数并刻画参数间的退化（degeneracy）关系。

Result: 通过自洽性验证，成功恢复了生成参数并表征了参数退化关系，同时完成了对CMS全模拟（full simulation）的首次拟合。

Conclusion: 所得模型具有可解释性、计算开销低的优点，可在完全基于Python且支持GPU的Parnassus标准流水线中运行。

Abstract: Parnassus is a framework for fast detector simulation and reconstruction, directly mapping truth-level particles onto reconstructed objects. Such models can be built from deep generative networks trained on paired samples, which are fit automatically to a target detector, or from parametric prescriptions of the kind used by Delphes, which are constructed by hand. We remove this asymmetry by making the parametric models fully differentiable so that their parameters can be fit to a target sample by gradient descent. We demonstrate closure by fitting a parametric model to samples from a known configuration of itself, recovering the generating parameters and characterizing the degeneracies among them, and we present a first fit to CMS full simulation. The resulting models are interpretable, inexpensive, and run in the standard Parnassus pipeline which is fully Python based and GPU enabled.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文利用Belle II实验在SuperKEKB非对称能量e⁺e⁻对撞机上采集的428 fb⁻¹数据样本，搜索轻子味破坏衰变τ±→μ±γ。通过多变量分类器压低标准模型本底，采用扩展极大似然拟合提取信号，未观察到显著超出预期本底的信号，使用CL_s方法设定分支比为9.5×10⁻⁸（90%置信度）和12.2×10⁻⁸（95%置信度）的上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻子味破坏（lepton-flavor-violation）衰变τ±→μ±γ在标准模型（Standard Model）中受到强烈压制，其观测将是超越标准模型新物理的明确信号，因此对该衰变过程的搜索是检验新物理的重要途径。

Method: 使用Belle II实验记录的总积分为428 fb⁻¹的数据样本，采用多变量分类器（multivariate classifier）压制来自标准模型过程的本底，并通过扩展极大似然拟合（extended maximum-likelihood fit）进行信号提取。

Result: 未观察到显著超出预期本底的信号，利用CL_s技术设定分支比（branching fraction）上限为B(τ±→μ±γ) < 9.5×10⁻⁸（90%置信度）和12.2×10⁻⁸（95%置信度）。

Conclusion: 当前数据样本未发现τ±→μ±γ轻子味破坏信号，所得分支比上限为未来更高统计量实验搜索提供了参考基准，并进一步缩小了新物理参数空间的可能范围。

Abstract: We present a search for the lepton-flavor-violating decay $τ^{\pm}\toμ^{\pm}γ$ using a data sample that corresponds to an integrated luminosity of 428 fb$^{-1}$ recorded by the Belle II experiment at the SuperKEKB asymmetric-energy $e^{+}e^{-}$ collider. We employ a multivariate classifier to suppress the backgrounds from the Standard Model processes, and the signal extraction is performed using an extended maximum-likelihood fit. Since no significant excess over the expected background is observed, we set an upper limit on the branching fraction $\mathcal{B}(τ^{\pm}\toμ^{\pm}γ) < 9.5$ $ (12.2)\times10^{-8}$ at the 90\% (95\%) confidence level, using the CL${_s}$ technique.

</details>

### [168] [Flippered hyperbolic surfaces and renormalized volumes of their moduli spaces I](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22544)
*Yi Huang,Ivan Telpukhovskiy*

Main category: math.GT

TL;DR: 本文引入一类新的双曲曲面——"翻翅曲面"（flippered surfaces），推广了皇冠双曲曲面（用于开弦的世界面），系统建立了其Teichmüller理论与模空间理论，构造了广义Weil-Petersson体积形式与Chekhov作用量，证明了广义Mirzakhani体积的有限性，并建立了三种几何递归公式（瓶颈切割、盘切除、皇冠提取），还给出了基本多边形与环带情形的积分表示和椭圆积分/多重对数显式公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广皇冠双曲曲面（crowned hyperbolic surfaces）以覆盖更广泛的双曲曲面类别，并建立相应的Teichmüller与模空间理论；进一步将Mirzakhani体积理论推广到这一新类别，为开弦世界面提供更一般的数学框架。

Method: 定义翻翅曲面这一新类别；发展其Teichmüller空间与模空间理论；构造广义Weil-Petersson体积形式和Chekhov作用量；通过瓶颈切割（neck chopping）、盘切除（disk excision）和皇冠提取（crown extraction）三种几何递归公式，将复杂曲面的体积归结为拓扑更简单曲面的体积；对基本多边形和环带情形利用锥形Legendre函数（conical Legendre functions）推导积分表示及椭圆积分与多重对数公式。

Result: 证明了广义Mirzakhani体积的有限性；建立了三种几何递归公式；推导出基本多边形和环带情形的积分表示及椭圆积分、多重对数显式公式；描述了体积Taylor系数的算术结构，证明适当特化情形为Kontsevich-Zagier周期；在虚边界长度2π√-1处证明了推广Do-Norbury弦方程与伸缩子型方程的恒等式。

Conclusion: 翻翅曲面构成了一个自然的双曲曲面类别，其广义Mirzakhani体积理论完备且可计算，兼具深厚的算术结构与物理意义，尤其是与弦方程（string equations）和伸缩子方程（dilaton-type equations）的联系表明该理论在开弦世界面的模空间几何中具有广泛应用前景。

Abstract: We introduce a natural class of hyperbolic surfaces called flippered surfaces that generalize crowned hyperbolic surfaces (i.e.: worldsheets for open strings). We develop their Teichmüller and moduli-space theory, construct generalized Weil-Petersson volume forms and Chekhov's action, and prove that the resulting generalized Mirzakhani volumes are finite. We establish three geometric recursion formulae-neck chopping, disk excision, and crown extraction-which express these volumes in terms of those of topologically simpler surfaces. For the fundamental polygonal and annular cases, we derive integral representations involving conical Legendre functions, as well as explicit formulae in terms of elliptic integrals and polylogarithms. We further describe the arithmetic structure of the Taylor coefficients of these volumes, show that suitable specializations are Kontsevich-Zagier periods, and prove identities at the imaginary boundary length $2π\sqrt{-1}$ that generalize the Do-Norbury paraphrasing of string and dilaton-type equations.

</details>


<div id='hep-th'></div>

# hep-th [[Back]](#toc)

### [169] [String stars, small black holes, and the supersymmetric index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21511)
*Erez Y. Urbach*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文讨论了角速度趋近于超对称指标βΩ = 2πi时黑洞-弦转变（black hole-string transition）的若干方面，发现高温Atick-Witten有效描述在该极限下失效，并在杂化弦理论和II型弦理论中分别发现了新的高温描述与弦星（string star）解，这些解在指标处退耦，且带荷弦星在荷电黑洞与自由弦相之间起插值作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在理解当角速度βΩ趋近超对称指标2πi时黑洞-弦转变的本质，因为在该极限下标准的Atick-Witten高温有效理论失效，需探索新的描述框架；同时，带荷情形下平滑转变与弦性BPS两电荷黑洞之间的潜在矛盾也构成了重要动机。

Method: 作者分别在杂化弦（Heterotic）和II型弦理论中考察βΩ→2πi极限下的高温描述：前者采用带磁场轮廓的Higgs化SU(2)_L × U(1)_R超引力，后者则考虑绕弦（winding string）变轻并在T对偶（T-dual）图像中开启新维度；在此基础上寻找与Horowitz-Polchinski解类似的弦星解，并分析其与荷电黑洞及自由弦相的关系。

Result: 在杂化弦中，Hagedorn不稳定性表现为背景磁场诱导的Ambjorn-Olesen W玻色子凝聚；在II型弦中，轻绕弦塔开启了一维新时空维度。两种情形下均找到弦星解，它们与Horowitz-Polchinski解类似，但在指标βΩ = 2πi处退耦。带荷弦星似乎在荷电黑洞与自由弦相之间进行插值，且在指标处两种带荷相均退耦。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，平滑的黑洞-弦转变与弦性BPS两电荷黑洞的存在之间存在张力，这意味着在该极限附近弦理论的整体相结构可能比先前认识更为复杂。

Abstract: We discuss aspects of the black hole-string transition as the angular velocity approaches the supersymmetric index $βΩ= 2πi$. In this limit, the high-temperature Atick-Witten effective description breaks down. For Heterotic string theory, a new high-temperature description emerges near the index in terms of a Higgsed $SU(2)_L\times U(1)_R$ supergravity with a magnetic field profile. The Hagedorn instability manifests in the theory as Ambjorn-Olesen W boson condensation, due to the background magnetic field. For type II, a tower of winding strings becomes light near the index, which opens up a new dimension in the T-dual picture. In both cases, a string star solution is found, similar to the Horowitz-Polchinski solution, but decouples at the index $βΩ= 2πi$.
  Considering charged solutions, the string star appears to interpolate between charged black holes and the free string phase. At the index, both the charged black hole and the charged string star phases decouple. This suggests a tension between a smooth black hole-string transition and the existence of a stringy BPS two-charge black hole.

</details>


### [170] [On flat space limits of $AdS_4$ black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21518)
*Pedro Vicente Marto,Shradha Ramakrishnan,Stefan Vandoren*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过研究规范化的 N=2 超引力中渐近 AdS4 黑洞的平直时空极限，利用全息重整化（holographic renormalisation）分析壳上作用量的极限行为，明确了该极限下对配分函数（partition function）有贡献的态，并揭示了大黑洞导致的热力学体积发散、小黑洞与平直时空对应体配分函数的半经典一致性，以及 BPS 与近平直时空黑洞之间的映射关系。最终在 M 理论中通过 BPS Kerr-Newman-AdS4 黑洞得到平直时空带电 BPS 解，并在对偶侧通过对 ABJM 配分函数取 Carrollian-like 极限重现了正确的平直时空带电 BPS 黑洞熵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解平直时空全息原理（flat space holography）是研究动机的核心——通过分析渐近 AdS4 黑洞的平直时空极限，探讨如何从 AdS/CFT 对应关系出发建立起平直时空中的全息框架。

Method: 使用全息重整化方法仔细分析规范化的 N=2 超引力中各类黑洞壳上作用量（on-shell action）的平直时空极限，并显式确定此极限下对配分函数有贡献的态；同时对 BPS Kerr-Newman-AdS4 黑洞取合适的平直极限，并在对偶 ABJM 配分函数上施加 Carrollian-like 极限以作交叉验证。

Result: 研究发现大黑洞导致配分函数出现可理解为热力学体积（thermodynamic volume）发散的奇异行为；半经典下小黑洞精确重现其平直时空对应体的配分函数。同时澄清了带电与转动情况下 BPS、(近)极值与非极值黑洞在 AdS 侧和平直时空侧的映射关系。在 M 理论中获得了平直时空的带电 BPS 解，并通过 ABJM 配分函数的 Carrollian-like 极限在大 N 下正确重现该黑洞的熵。

Conclusion: 结论表明，通过对 AdS 黑洞取平直时空极限并结合全息重整化，可以系统地提取平直时空中的全息信息；ABJM 配分函数的适当极限能够在对偶侧重现平直时空带电 BPS 黑洞的正确熵，为平直时空全息原理提供了具体实例与可行路径。

Abstract: With a view towards understanding flat space holography, we study the flat space limit of asymptotically $AdS_4$ black holes in gauged $\mathcal{N}=2$ supergravity. Using holographic renormalisation, we carefully analyse the flat space limit of the on-shell action of various black holes in this setting and make explicit the states that contribute to the partition function in this limit. We find several features: large black holes lead to a divergence in the partition function which can be understood as a thermodynamic volume divergence. Meanwhile, semiclassically, small black holes reproduce the partition function of their flat space counterparts, and we clarify the mapping between BPS, (near-)extremal or non-extremal black holes on both sides for charged and rotating solutions. Finally, by studying a suitable flat limit of the BPS Kerr-Newman-$AdS_4$ black hole in M-theory, we obtain a charged BPS solution in flat space. On the dual side, we determine a Carrollian-like limit on the known ABJM partition function on $S^1\times S^2$ at large $N$, and show that it reproduces the correct entropy of the BPS charged black hole in asymptotically flat space.

</details>


### [171] [What Neural Network Field Theory Can and Cannot Realise on a Computer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21523)
*Thomas R. Harvey*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个针对神经网络场论（neural network field theory）的不可行性定理（no-go theorem），证明在标准网络架构假设下，有限宽度系综的量子场论（QFT）诠释违反反射正性（reflection positivity），而有效场论（EFT）诠释则缺乏尺度分离来规避该违反；两个极限版本中仅一个可完全模拟，另一个只能部分模拟


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络场论的目标是将量子或有效场论放入计算机中，以网络系综本身作为理论。作者希望系统性地检验这一目标对可计算的正则函数类能推进到什么程度。

Method: 通过构造一个不可行性定理（no-go theorem），其假设适用于标准网络架构，并据此将神经网络场论划分为四个版本：有限宽度系综及其无限宽度极限分别与量子场论（QFT）和有效场论（EFT）目标的组合。

Result: 有限宽度系综的QFT诠释违反反射正性；EFT诠释无法建立尺度分离将正性违反排除在有效域之外。无限宽度极限的两个版本中，一个可完全模拟，另一个仅能计算涂抹关联函数（smeared correlators）且误差可控。在受控数值计算层面，QFT与EFT版本不可区分。一个维度可规避障碍，但在余弦网络中反射正性在任意有限宽度下仍然失败。

Conclusion: 存在两条可能的逃逸途径：放弃每点有限方差或放弃精确旋转不变性。作者对这两种可能性进行了讨论。

Abstract: One aim of neural network field theory is to put a quantum or effective field theory on a computer, with the network ensemble itself as the theory. We ask how far that aim can be pushed for a function class regular enough to be computed with. Our main result is a no-go theorem with assumptions that hold for standard network architectures. We use it to separate four versions of neural network field theory, according to whether the defining object is the finite width ensemble or its infinite width limit, and whether the target we want to compute is a quantum or an effective field theory. Neither finite width interpretation is straightforwardly consistent. For finite width ensembles with finite variance at each point, the QFT interpretation fails reflection positivity, while the EFT interpretation establishes no scale separation by which the positivity violation can be placed outside its domain of validity. Of the two limit versions, one can be simulated in full and the other only in part, as only its smeared correlators are computable with a controlled error. As such, at the level of a controlled numerical computation, the QFT and EFT versions cannot be distinguished. One dimension escapes the obstruction, yet reflection positivity is shown to still fail there at every finite width for the cosine network. Two escapes from the theorem remain, giving up either finite variance at a point or exact rotation invariance, and we discuss both of these possibilities.

</details>


### [172] [Neural Network Field Theory at Finite Width](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21588)
*Christian Ferko,Aaron Mutchler*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在温和假设下证明了任何量子力学模型或量子场论都可表示为具有可数无限随机参数的神经网络系综，并研究了有限参数（如有限宽度前馈网络）情形下NN-QM和NN-FT模型的性质，发现此类模型一般必然违反传统欧几里得量子场论的某些性质（如反射正性或团簇分解）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子模型与神经网络表示之间的联系，并理解有限参数神经网络框架下量子场论基本性质的命运，为量子场论的形式化提供新的表征途径。

Method: 在一般性假设下建立量子力学/量子场论与神经网络系综的表示等价性，然后通过分析有限宽度（N<∞）前馈网络的代表性模型，考察反射正性（reflection positivity）、团簇分解（cluster decomposition）等欧几里得QFT的经典性质在有限N下的保持情况，并从多个互补视角进行验证。

Result: 证明在有限参数（有限N）情况下，此类神经网络量子模型一般性地必然违反传统欧几里得量子场论的至少一个关键性质，如反射正性或团簇分解；并明确了哪些性质可以在有限N下保留、哪些不能保留的边界。

Conclusion: 有限参数的神经网络量子模型无法同时满足所有常规欧几里得量子场论性质，其与完整量子场论的差别在有限N下是本质性的；这为理解神经网络的量子表征能力设定了理论边界。

Abstract: Under mild assumptions, any quantum mechanical (QM) model or quantum field theory (QFT) admits a representation in terms of an ensemble of neural networks with countably many random parameters. We investigate the features of NN-QM and NN-FT models with finitely many parameters, such as a feedforward network of width $N < \infty$. We find that, generically, such models must violate one of the properties of conventional Euclidean QFTs, such as reflection positivity or cluster decomposition. We present several complementary ways of understanding which features can and cannot be preserved at finite $N$, both in QM and in QFT.

</details>


### [173] [One-Loop Quantum Correction to the Kink Mass in a Lorentz-Violating Scalar Field Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21613)
*Reza Moazzemi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在$(1+1)$维$λφ^4$扭结（kink）模型中引入不可约的类空洛伦兹对称性破缺（Lorentz-violating）相互作用项$-\frac12 cφ^2(u\!\cdot\!\partialφ)^2$，完成一圈重整化并计算到$c$一阶的扭结单圈质量修正，发现洛伦兹破缺贡献为数值负值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有洛伦兹不变$λφ^4$理论中的扭结质量修正是已知的，但非最小洛伦兹破缺算符对拓扑孤子质量的影响尚未被系统研究。选用的算符是这类模型中最低阶的单标量算符，其效应无法通过坐标或场的线性重定义消除，因而值得研究。

Method: 选取洛伦兹破缺算符$-\frac12 cφ^2(u\cdot\partialφ)^2$（$u^μ=(0,1)$），对底层量子场论执行单圈重整化，引入修正的质量反项（mass counterterm）和非零的场强反项（field-strength counterterm）。随后求解修正后的稳定性（stability）问题，获取束缚态与连续谱模式，并利用真空扣除零点能（vacuum-subtracted zero-point energy）与反项哈密顿量计算至$c$一阶的扭结质量。

Result: 与二维洛伦兹不变$λφ^4$理论不同，洛伦兹破缺理论在此阶同时需要修正质量反项和非零场强反项；修正后的束缚态与连续谱模式均发生改变，但连续谱相移在所选边界条件下至$O(c)$不变；最终通过真空扣除零点能与反项哈密顿量得到$c$一阶的单圈扭结质量，洛伦兹破缺贡献在所用减除方案中为数值负值。

Conclusion: 该洛伦兹破缺相互作用对扭结质量产生可计算的单圈修正，且洛伦兹破缺贡献为负，表明此类非最小耦合可能软化扭结的量子质量修正。研究还表明该模型中场重正化（field renormalization）在单圈阶不可忽略。

Abstract: We study the radiative correction to the mass of the $(1+1)$-dimensional $λφ^4$ kink in the presence of an irreducible, spacelike Lorentz-violating interaction. The Lorentz-violating operator is chosen as $-\frac12 cφ^2(u\!\cdot\!\partialφ)^2$ with $u^μ=(0,1)$, which is the lowest-order single-scalar operator considered in this class of models that produces effects that cannot be removed by a linear redefinition of coordinates or the field. We perform the one-loop renormalization of the underlying quantum field theory. In contrast with Lorentz-invariant $λφ^4$ theory in two dimensions, the Lorentz-violating theory requires, at this order, both a modified mass counterterm and a nonzero field-strength counterterm. The corrected stability problem contains modified bound and continuum modes, while the continuum phase shift remains unchanged at $O(c)$ with the boundary-condition prescription adopted here. Finally, using the vacuum-subtracted zero-point energy and the counterterm Hamiltonian, we obtain the one-loop kink mass through first order in $c$. We give the finite coefficient in the subtraction prescription implemented in the accompanying thesis calculation, for which the Lorentz-violating contribution is numerically negative.

</details>


### [174] [Heterotic string couplings at order $α'^3$ in NS-NS sector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21798)
*Alireza Pahlavan,Mehdi Ameri,Mohammad R. Garousi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用标准T-对偶（T-duality）程序，从最小基（由872个偶宇称和477个奇宇称耦合组成）出发，推导了杂化弦理论（heterotic string theory）在NS-NS扇区八阶导数（eight-derivative）水平下的经典有效作用量，并通过场重定义将其化为规范形式，验证了纯引力项与Yang-Mills项可统一表示为(Tr(R²−F²))²的形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本研究旨在通过T-对偶不变量约束，确定杂化弦理论在八阶导数阶次的有效作用量系数，从而填补该阶次理论推导的空白，并验证已知的S-矩阵结果与统一耦合形式。

Method: 从八阶导数阶次包含872个偶宇称和477个奇偶称耦合的最小基出发，在圆上执行维度约化（dimensional reduction），并施加T-对偶变换（即Buscher规则辅以高阶导数修正）的不变性约束。该不变量唯一固定了部分耦合以匹配II型理论（相差一个整体因子），其余耦合则由α′阶系数决定，并分别采用Metsaev-Tseytlin和Meissner两种方案。随后通过场重定义（field redefinitions）将作用量化为规范形式，使弦场（dilaton）仅通过整体因子e^{−2Φ}出现。

Result: 在Meissner方案中，纯引力扇区精确复现了已知的S-矩阵（S-matrix）结果。在两种方案下，纯引力项均可表达为双重迹(Tr(R²))²的形式；当与相应的Yang-Mills项(Tr(F²))²合并后，得到统一形式(Tr(R²−F²))²，与文献预期相符。

Conclusion: T-对偶不变量约束有效确定了杂化弦理论八阶导数作用量的结构，且纯引力与Yang-Mills耦合的统一形式(Tr(R²−F²))²得到确认，该结果为该阶次理论提供了自洽且与S-矩阵结果一致的规范作用量表达。

Abstract: We utilize the standard T-duality procedure to derive the classical effective action of heterotic string theory at the eight-derivative order within the NS-NS sector, which comprises the metric, the \(B\)-field, and the dilaton. Starting from the minimal basis at this order, consisting of 872 even-parity and 477 odd-parity couplings, we perform a dimensional reduction on a circle and impose invariance under T-duality transformations, specifically the Buscher rules supplemented by higher-derivative corrections. This invariance uniquely fixes a subset of the couplings to match those of type II theory up to an overall factor, while the remaining couplings are determined in terms of the coefficients at order \(α'\). For these latter couplings, we adopt both the Metsaev-Tseytlin and Meissner schemes. Subsequently, through field redefinitions, we recast the action into a canonical form in which the dilaton appears solely through the overall factor \(e^{-2Φ}\). In the Meissner scheme, the pure gravity sector precisely reproduces the known S-matrix results. In both schemes, the pure gravity terms can be expressed as the double trace \((\Tr(R^2))^2\), and when combined with the corresponding Yang-Mills terms \((\Tr(F^2))^2\), they take the unified form \((\Tr(R^2-F^2))^2\), as anticipated in the literature.

</details>


### [175] [Characteristic-cone running from on-shell residues: Identifiability on the physical EFT quotient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21865)
*Renat Gafarov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个数据完备性检验框架，用于从平空间壳重整化群（renormalization group）残差中提取低能特征锥（characteristic cone）径流。在四维宇称偶合的爱因斯坦-标量-麦克斯韦有效场论（effective field theory）中，作者发现发布的对偶螺旋矢量-标量残差可确定一个对角锥贡献，但无法在声明的可容许系数域内确定完整的特征响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从重整化群残差中提取低能特征锥径流是一个有待验证数据完备性的问题。

Method: 将Wilson方向放置在物理EFT商空间上，用无迹响应表示背景传播，通过有限算子注册表构造秩二不完备性检验，并结合Schur约化与麦克斯韦Ward恒等式进行分析。

Result: 得到β_μC_{γφ}^{(2)}=-55/(96π^2)以及约化后β_μ(r_T-r_γ)=-55/(48π^2M_P^4)；独立F^4方向在磁背景上分裂光子极化，CFF沿主Weyl轴产生解析分裂；标量-光子接触项原始混合非零但一阶物理非对角项为零。

Conclusion: 残差仅能固定一个对角锥贡献，无法在声明的可容许系数域内确定项完备的特征响应，表明有效场论耦合空间的数据覆盖存在不完备性。

Abstract: We formulate a data-completeness test for extracting low-energy characteristic-cone running from flat-space on-shell renormalization-group residues. In four-dimensional, parity-even Einstein--scalar--Maxwell effective field theory through four derivatives and four external fields, Wilson directions are first placed on the physical EFT quotient and background propagation is represented by a traceless response on the full massless root sector. The response is identifiable from chosen amplitude coordinates precisely when the amplitude kernel lies in the response kernel. For the published opposite-helicity vector--scalar residue we find $β_μC_{γφ}^{(2)}=-55/(96π^2)$ and, after an order-reduced frame translation, the contribution $β_μ(r_T-r_γ)=-55/(48π^2M_{\rm P}^4)$. A finite operator registry gives explicit rank-two incompleteness tests: independent $F^4$ directions split photon polarizations on a magnetic background, while $\mathcal CFF$ produces an analytic splitting along a principal Weyl axis on a Petrov-I Einstein--scalar background. For the isolated scalar--photon contact, direct Schur reduction finds nonzero raw mixing but no first-order physical off-diagonal entry, by the Maxwell Ward identity. Thus the residue fixes one diagonal cone contribution but does not determine the term-complete characteristic response over the declared admissible coefficient domain.

</details>


### [176] [Perturbative Reconstruction of Self-Adjoint Generators from Bosonic Canonical Commutation Relations: Application to the Null-Surface Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21947)
*C. N. Kozameh*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文针对微扰玻色子出射映射（outgoing map）证明了一个构造性可积性定理：在多项式玻色子正则对易关系（CCR）代数中，逐阶保持CCR足以递归重构一个形式自伴生成元，其指数映射通过伴随共轭实现出射映射。将该构造应用于零曲面表述（NSF）中的引力子散射，在平坦解空间扇区内，证明了出射映射在(δa_3)阶保持Ashtekar辐射CCR，重构了自伴生成元(δT_1)和(δT_2)，并给出了形式微扰酉实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文研究的是出射映射的逆伴随问题（inverse adjoint problem），目标是确定在何种条件下可以从保持CCR的出射映射重构其生成元，从而获得微扰酉实现。这一工作为引力子散射中的量子化问题提供了结构性保证。

Method: 在多项式玻色子CCR代数框架内，通过逐阶归纳构造形式自伴生成元：在每一阶，先从出射系数中减去由先前生成元决定的贝克–坎贝尔–豪斯多夫（Baker–Campbell–Hausdorff, BCH）贡献，剩余的同次CCR条件显式确定下一阶生成元。随后将该构造应用于零曲面表述中平坦解空间扇区的引力子散射。

Result: 在NSF框架中，出射映射在(δa_3)阶（对应(ε²)阶）保持Ashtekar辐射CCR。标量CCR扇区唯一选取对称排序（symmetric ordering, c=1）的非平凡相互作用核；线性收缩取消了([δa_2,δa_2^\dagger])的双重收缩贡献。定理重构了自伴生成元(δT_1)和(δT_2)，将直接(2→2)贡献组织为不可约圈（irreducible-loop）、单粒子可约（one-particle-reducible）和因子化（factorized）三类扇区。

Conclusion: 该定理为出射映射的微扰酉实现提供了构造性框架，并且NSF引力子散射应用表明，CCR保持条件可以唯一确定相互作用核的排序选择，同时自然分离出散射贡献的不同图结构类别。

Abstract: We study the inverse adjoint problem for a perturbative bosonic outgoing map. Within the polynomial bosonic canonical-commutation-relation (CCR) algebra, we prove a constructive integrability theorem: order-by-order preservation of the CCRs is sufficient to reconstruct recursively a formal self-adjoint generator whose exponential implements the outgoing map by adjoint conjugation. At each order, the Baker--Campbell--Hausdorff contribution determined by previously reconstructed generators is subtracted from the outgoing coefficient, and the remaining homogeneous CCR conditions determine the next generator explicitly.
  We apply the construction to graviton scattering in the null-surface formulation (NSF), specialized to the flat solution-space sector with Minkowski metric, null generators, affine parameter, and cone measure. The NSF outgoing map preserves the Ashtekar radiative CCRs through (δa_3), corresponding to order (\varepsilon^2). At this order, the scalar CCR sector uniquely selects the symmetric ordering (c=1) for the nontrivial interacting kernel. The associated linear contraction, generated by the same cubic kernel, cancels the double-contraction contribution from ([δa_2,δa_2^\dagger]). The theorem then reconstructs the self-adjoint generators (δT_1) and (δT_2), providing a formal perturbative unitary implementation. The resulting canonical organization also separates the direct (2\to2) contribution into irreducible-loop, one-particle-reducible, and factorized sectors.

</details>


### [177] [Holographic SU(3) color superconductivity at finite baryon chemical potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22013)
*Xin Zhao,Zhang-Yu Nie,Ya-Peng Hu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个全息（holographic）SU(3)色超导（color superconductivity, CSC）模型，通过夸克对凝聚（diquark condensate）在有限重子化学势下自发破缺全局色对称性。研究发现重子化学势增强了S1+iS2相的临界温度并抑制p波凝聚，使得平衡S+相随化学势升高占据整个色超导区域，为致密QCD和中子星内部物理提供了新的全息描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 色超导是致密QCD（dense QCD）中的核心物理现象，但已有的全息模型对SU(3)色对称性破缺的系统性处理尚不完善。本文旨在构建一个非阿贝尔全息框架，系统地研究有限重子化学势下SU(3)色超导各通道之间的竞争关系及其相图结构。

Method: 采用全息（holographic）方法构建SU(3)色超导模型，在有限重子化学势（baryon chemical potential, μ_b）下通过夸克对凝聚自发破缺全局色对称性。先对μ_b=0时所有SU(3)通道进行系统稳定性分析以复现已知的SU(2) s+p竞争，再在有限μ_b下将s波序参量推广为S1+iS2结构，并考虑背景反作用（backreaction）效应。

Result: 在μ_b=0时，系统稳定性分析在所有SU(3)通道中恢复了已知的SU(2) s+p波竞争。有限μ_b将s波序推广为S1+iS2结构；μ_b增强S1+iS2相的临界温度，同时抑制p波凝聚，且在包含反作用时p波在超过某μ_b阈值后消失。这一相反趋势重塑相图：随μ_b增大，平衡S+相（S1=S2）占据整个色超导区域。

Conclusion: 该非阿贝尔全息框架为全局SU(3)对称性破缺提供了具体的全息描述，有助于理解致密QCD中的色超导相，并可能为中子星内部（neutron-star interiors）的物理提供新的见解。

Abstract: We present a holographic model of SU(3) color superconductivity (CSC) where a global color symmetry is spontaneously broken by a diquark condensate at finite baryon chemical potential. At $μ_b=0$, a systematic stability analysis over all SU(3) channels recovers the known SU(2) s+p competition. Based on that, the finite $μ_b$ further generalizes the s-wave order to an S1+iS2 structure. We find that $μ_b$ enhances the critical temperature of the S1+iS2 phase while suppressing the p-wave -- the latter vanishes beyond a certain $μ_b$ when backreaction is included. This opposite trend reshapes the phase diagram: with increasing $μ_b$, the balanced S+ phase (S1=S2) takes over the entire color-superconducting region. Our non-Abelian framework provides a concrete holographic description of global SU(3) symmetry breaking, relevant for understanding color-superconducting phases in dense QCD and may offer new insights into the physics of neutron-star interiors.

</details>


### [178] [Thermal propagator of the bosons and fermions fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22469)
*M. A. A. Ahmed,H. Zainuddin,N. M. Shah*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在热场论（thermal field theory, TFT）框架下，通过引入任意参数σ，在实时形式（real-time formalism, RTF）中构造了标量传播子（scalar propagator）和费米子传播子（fermion propagator），以研究有限温度、无化学势条件下粒子在动量空间和混合空间中的运动规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热场论描述热力学环境中大量相互作用粒子的系综，这与经典统计力学（classical statistical mechanics）表面上相似，因此作者希望探究两者之间的深层差异，并建立有限温度下粒子传播的完整描述。

Method: 作者采用路径积分（path integral）方法，在实时形式（RTF）中引入一个任意的参数σ（an arbitrary parameter σ），据此系统地构造了标量场和费米子场的传播子。这些传播子使得粒子在动量空间和混合空间中的传播行为得以明确刻画。

Result: 文章成功构建了两个传播子，从而揭示了在有限温度、没有化学势（chemical potential）的情况下，粒子如何从一点运动到另一点——既在动量空间中有效，也在混合空间中成立。

Conclusion: 所构造的热传播子为理解热环境中粒子的传播机制提供了清晰的路径积分描述，并为进一步研究热场论与经典统计力学的区别奠定了基础。

Abstract: In a thermodynamic environment, thermal field theory (TFT) describes a large ensemble of interacting particles. This may appear to be the same as in classical statistical mechanics. Therefore, we study the scalar propagator and fermion propagator by considering an arbitrary parameter $σ$ that allows for a path integral description in real-time formalism (RTF). We constructed those propagators which allowed us to know how particles moved from one point to another point in momentum space as well as in mixed space, at finite temperature without a chemical potential.

</details>


### [179] [A second rotational Killing field on gauged D=5 vector multiplet horizons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22509)
*Usman Kayani*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了D=5超引力耦合矢量多重态的超对称近水平几何（near-horizon geometries），在水平截面规范旋转Killing矢量非零的分支上，不假设任何旋转对称性，证明了存在第二个独立旋转Killing场，并据此区分了两种子分支：模量恒定与模量变化的情况，两者均可在紧致水平截面上实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在超越此前工作中对旋转对称性的假设，研究无旋转对称性假设下超对称近水平几何的完整分类，并试图从超对称性和紧致性出发推导出已知的局部几何结果，而非依赖额外的ansatz。

Method: 在紧致连通无边界水平截面$\mathcal{S}$上，利用规范Killing矢量$\tilde V$与第二旋转Killing场之间的线性无关性，通过第一阶系统（first-order system）的闭包条件将正交于$P$的Killing方程转化为沿$P$平移的平坦秩二系统，并以$U=\|η_-\|^2(h_YN-h_NY)$显式求解。同时通过$K=Q_{IJ}C^IC^J$的符号分析区分两种子分支，并借助实例（$U(1)^3$黑洞与非静态Kunduri-Lucietti族紧致水平）验证各子分支的非空性。

Result: 当$K>0$时，$P=Z+W$在每个临界点处消失，出现两个均可实现的子分支：第一子分支中$P\equiv0$、模量恒定，由齐次性推出等距性，恢复Kunduri-Lucietti局部几何；第二子分支中$P\not\equiv0$、模量真实变化，由平坦秩二系统显式解出。还证明了$K$处处为零或处处为正，排除了隐藏的Killing张量对称性，并留出了$S^1\times S^2$黑环窗口未被排除。

Conclusion: 本文证明在紧致水平截面上，超对称性本身足以生成第二旋转Killing场的等距性，无需预先假设旋转对称性或额外ansatz；两种子分支在紧致情形下均非空，$S^1\times S^2$黑环窗口仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: We study supersymmetric near-horizon geometries of gauged D=5 supergravity coupled to vector multiplets, on the branch where the canonical rotational Killing vector $\tilde V$ of the horizon section $\mathcal{S}$ is non-vanishing. No rotational symmetry is assumed. On a compact, connected $\mathcal{S}$ without boundary, a second rotational Killing field, independent of $\tilde V$, always exists on the open set where $Z$ and $W$ are linearly independent, and extends to an isometry of all of $\mathcal{S}$, unconditionally if the moduli are constant and under a checkable extension hypothesis otherwise.
  With $K=Q_{IJ}C^IC^J$ vanishing exactly in the minimal theory, $K\equiv0$ recovers the known result of Grover, Gutowski, Papadopoulos and Sabra. When $K>0$, $P=Z+W$ vanishes at every critical point of $\|η_-\|^2$, and two sub-branches occur, both realised.
