On the first, $P\equiv0$, the moduli are constant and the isometry follows by homogeneity, recovering Kunduri and Lucietti local geometries as a consequence of supersymmetry and compactness rather than an ansatz. On the second, $P\not\equiv0$, the moduli genuinely vary; closure of the first-order system ($dP=0$) turns the Killing equation for a vector orthogonal to $P$ into a flat rank-two system transported along $P$, solved explicitly by $U=\|η_-\|^2(h_YN-h_NY)$.
  Both sub-branches are non-empty on compact $\mathcal{S}$: known $U(1)^3$ black holes populate the first, and an explicit compact horizon from the non-static Kunduri-Lucietti family, with $P$ vanishing at two points, populates the second and verifies the extension hypothesis numerically. We also prove $K$ is identically zero or everywhere positive, exclude a hidden Killing-tensor symmetry, and leave the $S^1\times S^2$ black-ring window $\tfrac13Φ^2<χ^2K\leq\tfrac43Φ^2$ unexcluded on either sub-branch.

</details>


### [180] [Genus-Two Contributions to Late-Time Correlators in JT Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22609)
*Masayoshi Sato*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文分析Jackiw–Teitelboim引力中晚期（late-time）二点关联函数的亏格二（genus-two, g=2）贡献，该贡献与两个婴儿宇宙（baby universe）相关。通过将问题化为涉及Weil–Petersson体积V₀,₃的模空间（moduli space）积分，并借助Kontsevich图分解和no-shortcut条件及条带近似（strip approximation），作者将积分重组为有限个几何构型的贡献之和，并显式计算了两个婴儿宇宙发射对应的条带几何贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作旨在将此前单婴儿宇宙（g=1）情形的分析推广到更复杂的高亏格几何，理解包含多个虫洞（wormhole）的晚期二点关联函数结构，从而为研究Jackiw–Teitelboim引力中的高阶效应提供分析工具。

Method: 作者将两个婴儿宇宙的发射与吸收表述为一个振幅，将问题化为涉及Weil–Petersson体积V₀,₃的模空间积分；随后将V₀,₃分解为Kontsevich图贡献，并将no-shortcut条件推广到亏格二条带几何；结合条带近似，将模空间积分重组为有限项几何构型的贡献，并逐一显式计算条带几何贡献。

Result: 作者显式求得了与两个婴儿宇宙发射相对应的条带几何贡献，并据此确定了其对亏格二晚期二点关联函数的贡献。

Conclusion: 结果表明，条带近似结合no-shortcut条件为研究包含多个虫洞的高亏格几何提供了一个有效的解析框架。

Abstract: In this work, we analyze the late-time two-point correlation function in Jackiw--Teitelboim gravity, focusing on the genus-two ($g=2$) contribution associated with two baby universes. Extending the analysis of the single-baby-universe ($g=1$) case, we formulate an amplitude describing the emission and absorption of two baby universes during the evolution of the Hartle--Hawking state. This formulation reduces the problem to a moduli-space integral involving the Weil--Petersson volume $V_{0,3}$. To evaluate this integral, we decompose $V_{0,3}$ into contributions from Kontsevich graphs and extend the no-shortcut condition to the relevant genus-two strip geometries. Combined with the strip approximation, this decomposition reorganizes the moduli-space integral into a finite sum of contributions associated with distinct geometric configurations. We then explicitly evaluate the strip-geometry contributions corresponding to the emission of two baby universes and determine their contribution to the genus-two late-time two-point correlation function. Our results suggest that the strip approximation, together with the no-shortcut condition, provides a useful analytic framework for studying higher-genus geometries containing multiple wormholes.

</details>


### [181] [Higher-spin charges and the $L_Λ w_{1 + \infty}$ algebra in (A)dS$_4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22625)
*Lorenzo Di Giacomo,Maitá Micol,Daniele Pranzetti,Ana-Maria Raclariu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有引力通量边界条件的(3+1)维渐近局域(反)德西特时空，构造了作为宇宙学常数Λ微扰解的高阶自旋荷的无穷塔，证明这些荷在受限引力相空间上实现Λ形变的$w_{1+\infty}$代数（$L_\Lambda w_{1+\infty}$），并将平坦时空的高阶自旋对称性推广到渐近(A)dS时空。该工作确立了$L_\Lambda w_{1+\infty}$对称性的体引力起源，并将天球（celestial）体-界字典扩展到渐近(A)dS$_4$引力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究在渐近平坦时空中建立了高阶自旋荷与$w_{1+\infty}$代数的联系，但尚不清楚这种高阶自旋对称结构能否推广到具有宇宙学常数（即(反)德西特背景）的时空。本文将这一框架扩展到渐近(A)dS$_4$时空，以闭合体-界对应关系中关于渐近对称性这一重要环节。

Method: 作者在允许引力通量的边界条件下，对爱因斯坦方程进行渐近展开，得到一系列演化方程层级，并以宇宙学常数Λ为微扰参数构造无穷高阶自旋荷。随后证明这些荷在受限引力相空间上规范实现$L_\Lambda w_{1+\infty}$代数。此外，直接用时空数据构建共形软引力子（conformally soft gravitons）的弯曲时空对应物，考察其作为(A)dS$_4$等度规代数的$\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathbb{R})$子代数主元（primary）的性质，并计算其与二次高阶自旋荷的泊松括号。

Result: 高阶自旋荷规范实现Λ形变的$w_{1+\infty}$代数；共形软引力子的弯曲时空对应物是$\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathbb{R})$子代数的主元；这些主元与二次高阶自旋荷的泊松括号复现了Taylor和Zhu提出的Λ形变天球算子积展开（OPA）。

Conclusion: 本文确立了$L_\Lambda w_{1+\infty}$对称性的体（bulk）引力起源，并将天球体-界字典推广至渐近(A)dS$_4$引力，为理解含宇宙学常数背景下的渐近对称性结构提供了新的理论基础。

Abstract: We consider $(3+1)$-dimensional asymptotically locally (anti-)de Sitter ((A)dS$_4$) spacetimes with boundary conditions allowing for gravitational flux. We construct an infinite tower of higher-spin charges as perturbative solutions in the cosmological constant $Λ$ to a hierarchy of evolution equations resulting from an asymptotic expansion of the Einstein equations. We show that these charges canonically realize the $Λ$-deformed $w_{1+\infty}$ algebra ($L_Λ w_{1+\infty}$) on a restricted gravitational phase space, thereby extending the higher-spin symmetry structure of asymptotically flat spacetimes to asymptotically (A)dS spacetimes. We further construct the curved-space counterparts of conformally soft gravitons directly in terms of spacetime data. We show that they are primaries with respect to an $\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathbb{R})$ subalgebra of the (A)dS$_4$ isometry algebra and that their Poisson brackets with the quadratic higher-spin charges reproduce the $Λ$-deformed celestial operator product expansion proposed by Taylor and Zhu. Our results establish the bulk gravitational origin of the $L_Λw_{1+\infty}$ symmetry and extend the celestial bulk-boundary dictionary to asymptotically (A)dS$_4$ gravity.

</details>


### [182] [Towards Perturbative Unimodular Poincaré Gauge Theories with Propagating Torsion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22669)
*Arthur F. Vieira,Antonio D. Pereira,Reinhard Alkofer*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了庞加莱引力规范理论（Poincaré gauge theories of gravity）的有效场论扩展与其单模（unimodular）对应理论之间在单圈（one-loop）层面的关系。在爱因斯坦表示中，考虑两类代表性二次扭率（torsion）子扇区后，作者发现微分同胚不变理论与其正单模理论在最大对称背景下产生相同的单圈行列式，且在平坦背景下加入扭率后两者的有效作用量仍由同一物理行列式控制，表明单模限制不影响局部单圈扭率动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单模引力和含传播扭率的庞加莱规范理论各自是引力有效场论的重要扩展，但二者在量子层面（尤其是一圈修正中扭率动力学如何被单模约束影响）的关系尚未被系统研究。作者旨在厘清单模限制是否会在局部单圈层面上改变扭率传播行为。

Method: 采用爱因斯坦表示，分别研究完全反对称（轴向，axial）扭率和钩反对称（hook-antisymmetric）扭率的矢量分量这两个代表性二次扭率子扇区。在两个类别背景下计算单圈行列式：一是背景扭率为零的最大对称度规，二是具有均匀轴向或矢量扭率的平坦背景，后者存在度规——扭率混合效应。

Result: 在最大对称度规背景（背景扭率为零）下，微分同胚不变理论与真单模理论在两个子扇区中产生完全相同的单圈行列式与局部对数发散。在具有均匀轴向或矢量扭率的平坦背景下，即便存在度规——扭率混合，单模规范形式与真单模表述中依赖于扭率的单圈有效作用量仍由相同的物理行列式控制。

Conclusion: 对于所考察的作用量与背景而言，单模约束不会改变局部单圈扭率动力学。作者同时指出了该等价性的适用范围限制，并发现在一般的扭率弯曲背景下需要进一步发展才能确认此等价关系是否仍然成立。

Abstract: We study the one-loop relation between an effective-field-theory extension of Poincaré gauge theories of gravity with propagating torsion and its unimodular counterpart. In the Einstein representation, we consider two representative quadratic torsion subsectors, namely totally antisymmetric (axial) torsion and the vector component of hook-antisymmetric torsion. On maximally symmetric metric backgrounds with vanishing background torsion, the diffeomorphism-invariant and genuine unimodular theories yield identical one-loop determinants in both sectors, reproducing the same local logarithmic divergences. We further examine flat backgrounds with homogeneous axial or vector torsion, where metric-torsion mixing is present. In each case, the torsion-dependent one-loop effective action in unimodular gauge and in the genuine unimodular formulation is controlled by the same physical determinant. Thus, for the actions and backgrounds considered, the unimodular constraint does not modify local one-loop torsion dynamics. We also delineate the limitations of this equivalence and the extensions needed for generic torsionful curved backgrounds.

</details>


### [183] [Bouncing shellworld embedded in charged AdS spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22855)
*Karma P. Sherpa,Rishi Pokhrel,Indra K. P. Chettri,Tanay K. Dey*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究五维荷电反德西特（AdS）体时空中的壳世界（shellworld）暗泡（dark bubble）场景，发现球面膜宇宙在不同膜宇宙学常数取值下呈现无奇点反弹（nonsingular bounce）和循环（cyclic）或永恒膨胀的演化行为，反弹由体时空的电荷参数诱导，且可克服柯西视界（Cauchy horizon）不稳定性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准膜世界（braneworld）模型中存在柯西视界不稳定性的问题，且宇宙学演化中的奇点问题也亟待解决。作者试图通过引入五维荷电AdS体时空中的壳世界暗泡场景，利用体时空电荷参数诱导膜宇宙的无奇点反弹，从而同时解决奇点规避与视界不稳定两大问题。

Method: 作者在五维荷电AdS体时空中构造球形膜嵌入壳世界暗泡场景，通过设定不同大小的膜宇宙学常数（brane cosmological constant）研究膜宇宙的演化行为；同时对线性标量扰动（linear scalar perturbations）进行分析以考察反弹过程中的正则性，并进一步在体中引入均匀分布的长弦（long strings）以检验场景的稳健性。

Result: 当膜宇宙学常数为零或很小值时，壳世界宇宙表现出无奇点的反弹与循环性质，反弹由体时空电荷参数诱发；当膜宇宙学常数较大时，壳世界经历无奇点反弹后永恒膨胀。在某些条件下膜在体视界之外反弹，从而从几何上解决标准膜世界模型中的柯西视界不稳定性。线性标量扰动的测试标量场模式在反弹过程中保持正则且不发散。在体中加入均匀分布长弦后，反弹依然维持且视界不稳定性仍被避免。

Conclusion: 壳世界暗泡场景在荷电AdS体时空中能够提供无奇点的宇宙学演化，其反弹机制依赖于体时空的电荷参数，并能有效规避柯西视界不稳定性；即使引入体中的长弦物质，这一结论依然稳健成立。

Abstract: We investigate the cosmological evolution of a spherical brane within the shellworld (dark bubble) scenario embedded in a five-dimensional charged AdS bulk spacetime. For zero or small value of the brane cosmological constant, the shellworld universe has a nonsingular bouncing and cyclic nature with the bounce induced by the charge parameter of the bulk spacetime. On large value of the brane cosmological constant, the shellworld undergoes a nonsingular bounce and expands eternally. Under certain conditions, the brane bounces outside the bulk horizons, leading to a geometrical resolution of the Cauchy horizon instability present in standard braneworld models. Furthermore, analysis of linear scalar perturbations reveals that test scalar field modes remain regular and non-divergent across the bounce. Finally, we incorporate uniformly distributed long strings in the bulk and demonstrate that the bounce still persists while avoiding the horizon instability.

</details>


### [184] [Conformal and Noncommutative Gravity from Gauge Symmetry Breaking and SO(2,16) Unification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23220)
*Stelios Stefas,George Zoupanos*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文回顾了将引力视为规范理论（gauge theory）的经典构想，综述了共形引力（Conformal Gravity）和非交换（模糊）引力（Noncommutative (Fuzzy) Gravity）的规范构造，并提出将这两种引力的规范表述与SO(10)大统一理论（Grand Unified Theory）的内部相互作用统一起来，其关键依据在于弯曲流形切空间对称群的维度不必与时空维度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者受到将引力视为规范理论的古老思想启发，旨在探索引力与内部规范相互作用统一的可能性。

Method: 作者回顾并综述了共形引力和非交换（模糊）引力的规范理论构造，并基于切空间对称群维度可独立于时空维度这一关键观察，提出将这两种引力规范表述与SO(10)大统一理论相统一的框架。

Result: 提出了一个统一方案，将引力（通过共形引力和非交换模糊引力两种规范表述）与SO(10)大统一理论的内部相互作用整合到一个统一框架中。

Conclusion: 切空间对称群维度独立于时空维度的观察为引力与大统一理论的统一提供了关键理论基础，从而使规范的引力表述与SO(10)内部相互作用得以在统一框架中共存。

Abstract: Motivated by the old idea of treating gravity as a gauge theory, we review the gauge-theoretic construction of Conformal Gravity and of Noncommutative (Fuzzy) Gravity. We then outline how these gauge formulations of gravity can be unified with internal interactions described by an SO(10) Grand Unified Theory, based on the critical observation that the dimension of the tangent-space symmetry group of a curved manifold need not coincide with the spacetime dimension.

</details>


### [185] [Analytical Cosmological Collider at Strong Mixing in Laplace Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23243)
*Nathan Belrhali,Arthur Poisson,Sébastien Renaux-Petel*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文表明，当曲率扰动与一个质量场之间存在过强而无法微扰处理的混合时，原初非高斯性（primordial non-Gaussianities）达到最大。作者证明这一在多重场暴胀（multifield inflation）中自然出现的强混合机制，可通过他们此前提出的拉普拉斯空间（Laplace-space）表示解析求解，所得三点函数（bispectrum）与独立数值结果完全一致，且强混合下的宇宙学对撞机（cosmological collider）信号比普遍认知更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原初非高斯性在曲率扰动与质量场强耦合时达到最大，但该强混合区域无法用微扰论处理，因此缺乏解析理解。此前研究中宇宙学对撞机信号在强混合下的幅度被低估，需要更精确的理论工具来刻画这一观测上重要的区域。

Method: 作者利用此前引入的拉普拉斯空间表示，在该表示中每个模式成为由单一核函数（kernel）加权的平面波叠加，从而将强混合机制下的三点函数转化为快速收敛的基本函数级数，实现解析求解。

Result: 解析结果与独立数值模拟完全吻合。发现强混合下宇宙学对撞机信号的幅度比普遍认知更大，仅受到通常玻尔兹曼抑制（Boltzmann suppression）的一半；信号幅度还随相互作用中曲率扰动时间导数数目增加而增大，据此识别出最有希望的观测目标。

Conclusion: 拉普拉斯空间表示为强混合条件下的原初非高斯性提供了精确的解析框架，修正了对宇宙学对撞机信号的预期，并指出具有更多曲率扰动时间导数的相互作用是最具观测前景的目标。

Abstract: Primordial non-Gaussianities are at their largest when the curvature perturbation is coupled to a massive field by a mixing too strong to be treated perturbatively. We show that this regime, naturally reached in multifield inflation, is solved analytically by the Laplace-space representation we recently introduced, in which every mode becomes a superposition of plane waves weighted by a single kernel. The bispectrum then follows as a rapidly convergent series of elementary functions, in perfect agreement with independent numerical results. We find that the cosmological collider signal at strong mixing is larger than widely believed, with only half of the usual Boltzmann suppression. Its amplitude further grows with the number of time derivatives of the curvature perturbation in the interaction, singling out the most promising observational targets.

</details>


### [186] [The ETH matrix model for DSSYK: non-perturbative corrections and intersection theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23298)
*Eleonora Alfinito,Matteo Beccaria*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究DSSYK模型（double-scaled SYK model）在固定属数（fixed genus）下的非扰动修正。作者证明，在$\lambda\to0$极限下，线性于$\widetilde q=e^{-4\pi^2/\lambda}$的带头阶（leading order）修正完全由$q$-形变Weil–Petersson体积（$q$-deformed Weil–Petersson volumes）捕获，并可化为稳定曲线模空间$\overline{\mathcal M}_{g,n}$上$\kappa$-类交点数（intersection numbers）的有限和。作者对所有已知闭式体积的$(g,n)$组合制表，并扩展到无闭式可用的$(3,1),(3,2),(4,1)$情形，同时给出$O(\widetilde q^2)$阶的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DSSYK模型与正弦-双龙引力（sine-dilaton gravity）的对偶关系目前仅在盘（disk）和虫洞（wormhole）拓扑上得到证实。本文旨在理解固定属数下更高边界数的关联函数（correlators）中由$\widetilde q$控制的非扰动修正的结构，并寻找其与模空间上几何不变量的联系。

Method: 利用DSSYK模型关联函数中$N_{g,n}$离散体积的多项式结构，它生成自Eisenstein级数$E_2,E_4,E_6$生成的拟模形式（quasimodular forms）环。通过$S$-对偶（$S$-duality）获取$\lambda\to0$下线性于$\widetilde q$的精确闭式，将其映射到$q$-形变Weil–Petersson体积的展开，并建立其与$\kappa$-类交点数之间的联系，从而无需重复拓扑递推（topological recursion）即可计算修正项。

Result: 作者证明线性$\widetilde q$项由$q$-形变Weil–Petersson体积完全捕获，并可化为$\kappa$-类交点数的有限和。论文对所有已知闭式的$(g,n)$情形制表，并将结果扩展到$(3,1),(3,2),(4,1)$（这些情形无闭式可用），同时给出了这些情形下$O(\widetilde q^2)$阶的完整结果。

Conclusion: $\widetilde q$所控制的非扰动标度不仅主导盘级（disk-level）贡献，同样支配固定属数下的多边界振幅，表明$S$-对偶结构在更高拓扑上持续发挥作用。该构造不限于带头阶，可系统扩展到$\widetilde q$的高阶。

Abstract: At leading order in the genus expansion the ETH matrix model for DSSYK reproduces its correlators by construction, while its higher-genus corrections are conjectured to capture higher-topology contributions in the dual sine-dilaton gravity -- a correspondence established so far only for the disk and the wormhole. At fixed genus the correlators are built from discrete volumes $N_{g,n}$, polynomial in $q$-deformed zeta values $ζ_q(2k)$ with $q=e^{-λ}$, $λ$ being the DSSYK coupling. These lie in the ring of quasimodular forms generated by the Eisenstein series $E_2,E_4,E_6$, whose $S$-duality yields an exact closed form for the leading non-perturbative correction as $λ\to0$, controlled by $\widetilde q=e^{-4π^2/λ}$. Known at disk level, this scale is shown here to govern the fixed-genus, higher-boundary amplitudes as well. We show that the term linear in $\widetilde q$, at leading order in $λ$, is captured entirely by the $q$-deformed Weil--Petersson volumes, and reduces to a finite sum of intersection numbers of $κ$-classes on the moduli space $\overline{\mathcal M}_{g,n}$ of stable curves, computable without repeating the topological recursion that produced the $N_{g,n}$. We tabulate it for every $(g,n)$ whose $q$-deformed volume is known in closed form, and extend it to $(3,1),(3,2),(4,1)$, where none is available. The construction is not restricted to leading order: we work out $O(\widetilde q^2)$ for the same cases.

</details>


### [187] [Perturbative Anomaly Inflow on Orbifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23326)
*Hao Y. Zhang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究有效理论在轨道奇点固定点上支持的微扰反常流入（perturbative anomaly inflow），利用Donnelly等变APS定理计算不动点密度，并将其度(d+2)分量识别为d维有效理论的反常多项式。将该构造应用于由N个M5膜探测横动量C²/Zₖ奇点的六维A型轨道瞬子（orbi-instanton）理论，发现非恒等ALE不动点类的度八分量与MOTZ中猜想的完整Kac标号依赖余项一致，从而复现了未加味张量支反常多项式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望为支持在内部几何轨道奇点固定点上的有效理论建立系统的反常流入框架，并验证此框架在M理论弧控几何（如M5膜与余维M9壁）中给出的反常多项式与已知猜想完全一致，从而为M理论的角流入（corner inflow）解释提供完整图景。

Method: 采用Donnelly的等变APS（equivariant APS）定理，提取固定点密度并识别其度(d+2)分量为异常多项式。对六维A型轨道瞬子理论，考虑平坦E₈联络由ρ:Zₖ→E₈指定，在M9壁（Hořava–Witten壁）上计算非恒等ALE固定点类。

Result: 非恒等ALE固定点类的度八分量与MOTZ猜想中依赖Kac标号的完整余项一致，包含SU(2)_R曲率和切丛曲率贡献。结合已知的M5/Hořava–Witten项与ALE/Hořava–Witten项，完整复现了未加味张量支反常多项式，并将构造推广至包含味对称（flavor symmetry）作为E₈的中心化子（centralizer）的情形。

Conclusion: 该局部等式表明存在完整的M理论角流入解释；等变APS构造可系统覆盖轨道奇点固定点上的反常流入，并自然延伸至味对称性。

Abstract: We study perturbative anomaly inflow for effective theories supported on orbifold fixed loci of the internal geometry. Donnelly's equivariant APS theorem gives a fixed-point density, whose degree-\((d+2)\) component is identified as the anomaly polynomial of the \(d\)-dimensional effective theory. We apply the construction to six-dimensional A-type orbi-instanton theories engineered by \(N\) M5-branes probing a transverse \(\mathbb{C}^2/\mathbb{Z}_k\) singularity at an end-of-the-world M9-brane. For a flat \(E_8\) connection specified by \(ρ:\mathbb{Z}_k\to E_8\), we compute the non-identity ALE fixed-point class on the M9 wall. Its degree-eight component agrees with the complete Kac-label-dependent remainder conjectured in \cite{MOTZ}, including the \(SU(2)_R\) and tangent-bundle curvatures. Together with the known M5/Hořava--Witten and ALE/Hořava--Witten terms, this reproduces the unflavored tensor-branch anomaly polynomial. This local equality further suggests a complete M-theory corner-inflow interpretation. The same prescription also be extended to include flavor symmetries as the centralizer of \(E_8\).

</details>


### [188] [Landau theory, effective temperature, and tricritical phenomena in a holographic nonequilibrium steady state](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23378)
*Motohiro Kanazawa,Masataka Matsumoto,Shin Nakamura*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过D3/D7全息模型研究了非平衡稳态中电流驱动的三相临界点（tricritical point, TCP）处的临界指数（γ, ν），发现其行为依赖于趋近TCP的路径，仅在有效温度与热浴温度之比T*/T固定时与Landau理论一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究非平衡稳态下电流驱动的三相临界点处手征对称性破缺相关的临界现象，并检验传统Landau理论在该非平衡临界点处的适用性。

Method: 采用D3/D7全息模型（holographic model）描述非平衡稳态，计算对称性破缺相中临界指数（γ, ν），并沿不同路径趋近TCP进行比较分析。

Result: 在对称性破缺相中，γ和ν均可能偏离Landau理论预测值，且依赖于趋近TCP的路径；但当以T*/T比值固定趋近TCP时，临界指数与Landau理论预测一致。

Conclusion: 电流驱动的TCP临界现象沿T*/T固定线可由Landau理论描述，而沿任意路径则需要Landau理论的非平凡扩展才能全面刻画。

Abstract: We investigate the critical exponents $(γ,ν)$ at the current-driven tricritical point (TCP) associated with chiral symmetry breaking in a nonequilibrium steady state described by the D3/D7 model. In the symmetry-broken phase, we find that, unlike in the conventional Landau theory, both $γ$ and $ν$ can take values different from those predicted by the Landau theory and depend on the path along which the TCP is approached in the phase diagram. However, when the TCP is approached with the ratio of the effective temperature to the heat bath temperature, $T_{*}/T$, held fixed, the critical exponents agree with those of the Landau theory. These results suggest that the critical phenomena at the current-driven TCP may be described by the Landau theory along the $T_{*}/T$-fixed line, whereas a nontrivial extension of the Landau theory is required for a more general description of the critical phenomena along arbitrary paths.

</details>


### [189] [Energy of toroidal M2 brane in flat 11d background](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23517)
*Arkady A. Tseytlin,Zihan Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本论文计算了平坦11维空间中M2膜（membrane）缠绕在二维环面（2-torus）上的二圈（two-loop）能量修正，结果表明在逆张力展开（inverse-tension expansion）下该能量无对数紫外发散。费米子周期边界条件下量子修正为零，符合BPS态（BPS state）特性；反周期边界条件下能量为修正贝塞尔函数（modified Bessel function）的无穷求和。还计算了玻色膜在R^2×S^1上的三圈修正，包含ζ(9)项，并通过全圈气泡图重求和得到膜能量的三次方程，与弦理论中的平方根表达式形成对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超膜作用量（supermembrane action）是非线性的，先验上不可重整化（non-renormalizable），但其中某些量可能不含对数紫外发散（log UV divergences）从而可无歧义地定义。作者旨在探索这一可能性，检验超膜理论在更高圈阶的可定义性，并评估其对11维理论在反周期圆上等价于type 0A弦理论强耦合极限这一猜想的影响。

Method: 作者在平坦11维空间中，对缠绕在二维环面上的M2膜进行逆张力展开至二圈阶计算其能量。对于玻色膜在R^2×S^1上的情况，计算三圈修正并推广至全圈气泡图（bubble graph）贡献并通过重求和（resummation）获得能量满足的方程。同时对比Nambu/G S弦理论中类似重求和给出的平方根表达式。

Result: 二圈结果不含对数发散，且预期更高圈阶亦然。费米子双周期时量子修正抵消，与缠绕M2膜态的BPS性质一致；费米子在一或两个圆上反周期时，能量为修正贝塞尔函数的无穷和。三圈修正除1圈ζ(3)系数的幂次外还包含新项ζ(9)。全圈气泡图求和给出膜能量的三次方程；弦论情形重得精确平方根表达式E=√((2πR T1)^2+m0^2)。超膜气泡图贡献对任意半径R11均不消失。

Conclusion: 若超膜情形中其他非平凡图（non-trivial diagrams）的贡献不从根本上改变结论，则气泡图部分能量不为零这一点将不利于11维理论在反周期圆上的强耦合极限等同于type 0A弦理论这一猜想。

Abstract: The supermembrane action is non-linear and a priori non-renormalizable. Still, some quantities in this theory may be free of log UV divergences and thus defined unambiguously. To explore this possibility we compute the energy of an M2 brane wrapped on a 2-torus in flat 11d space to two loops in the inverse-tension expansion. The result is free of logarithmic divergences and the same should hold also at higher loop orders. For fermions periodic around both circles the quantum corrections cancel, consistent with the BPS nature of the wrapped M2 brane state. For fermions antiperiodic around one or both circles the energy is a non-trivial function of the radii given by infinite sums of modified Bessel functions. We also compute 3-loop correction to the energy of the bosonic membrane on $\mathbb R^2\times S^1$. In contrast to the string case, it contains, besides powers of the 1-loop $ζ(3)$ coefficient, a new term proportional to $ζ(9)$. Summing contributions of all-loop bubble graphs leads to a simple cubic equation for the membrane energy. The analogous resummation in the Nambu or GS string t case reproduces the familiar exact square-root expression ${\mathcal E}=\sqrt{(2πR\,T_1)^2+m_0^2}$. We find that the bubble-graph part of the membrane energy does not vanish for any value of the radius $R_{11}$. If contributions of other non-trivial diagrams in the supermembrane case do not qualitatively change this conclusion, this would disfavour the conjectured relation between 11d theory on an antiperiodic circle and the strong-coupling limit of type 0A string theory.

</details>


### [190] [Renormalization, cutoff, and gluing for a quartic model with boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23069)
*A. V. Ivanov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本论文研究了一个定义在带边界的紧致连通黎曼流形上的三维四次模型的重整化问题，证明除了标准质量参数平移外还需考虑涉及奇异系数的边界贡献，并在标准的重整化理论框架内扩展经典作用量，且流形粘合一致性要求导致边界上算子的重整化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在带边界的流形上，经典的重整化理论框架可能不足以处理边界产生的奇异贡献，因此需要研究这些边界效应并扩展理论框架。

Method: 在带边界的紧致连通黎曼流形上研究三维四次模型的重整化，通过分析质量参数的标准平移之外所需的额外项，并利用流形粘合（gluing）的一致性条件来确定边界算子的重整化形式。

Result: 发现除了标准的质量参数平移外，还必须考虑涉及奇异系数的边界贡献；流形粘合的一致性要求导致需要在边界上引入算子的重整化。

Conclusion: 在带边界流形上，需在标准重整化理论框架内扩展经典作用量，并通过粘合一致性条件将重整化扩展到边界上的算子。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the renormalization of a three-dimensional quartic model on a compact connected Riemannian manifold with a boundary. We show that, in addition to the standard shift of the mass parameter, it is necessary to take into account boundary contributions involving singular coefficients. This indicates the need to extend the classical action within the framework of standard renormalization theory. The requirements of consistency regarding the gluing of manifolds lead to renormalization of an operator on the boundary.

</details>


<div id='quant-ph'></div>

# quant-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [191] [Complex-Time Parametrization of Evanescent Tunneling and Transmission Delay in non-Hermitian Scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21435)
*Iulia-Maria Daia*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过引入复时间参数化（complex-time parametrization）框架，使用复时间坐标τ = t + iκ，解决了量子隧穿（quantum tunneling）过程中经典四动量变为虚数的悖论，证明隧穿过程严格受光速约束并保持因果隔离，且该模型与开放非厄米系统的微波散射实验存在现象学类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子隧穿是体现量子动力学非经典本质的基本概念，其核心悖论在于穿越势垒时经典四动量（4-momentum）需被强制变为虚数，这一矛盾亟待理论解释。

Method: 引入复时间坐标τ = t + iκ，其中实轴(t)代表标准热力学时间，虚轴(iκ)关联空间消逝（spatial evanescence）；通过评估扩展的复时间框架，并利用有效复时间动量范数从类时到类空态的连续几何符号翻转来解决虚动量异常。

Result: 该复时间框架产生因果光锥（causal lightcone）的双曲截面，证明隧穿过程严格受光速限制并保持因果隔离；同时，该理论模型与使用开放非厄米系统（non-Hermitian systems）的微波散射实验具有物理现象学类比，将复透射时间延迟与空间波衰减联系起来。

Conclusion: 所提出的复时间参数化框架为量子隧穿的虚动量悖论提供了几何化的统一解决方案，并可通过非厄米开放系统的微波散射实验获得物理上的经验验证。

Abstract: The quantum tunneling process is a fundamental concept that presents the unconventional nature of quantum dynamics. The main anomaly of this effect revolves around the choice of imposing the classical 4-momentum to become imaginary during the barrier traversal. This paper addresses this paradox by introducing a complex-time parametrization, which uses a complex temporal coordinate, $τ= t + iκ$. Here, the real axis $(t)$ represents standard thermodynamic time, while the imaginary axis $(iκ)$ is correlated to spatial evanescence. We demonstrate that evaluating this extended complex-time framework yields a hyperbolic section of the causal lightcone, proving that the traversal is strictly bounded by the speed of light and preserves causal disconnection. Applied to quantum tunneling, we resolve the anomaly of imaginary momentum through a continuous geometric signature flip of the effective complex-time momentum norm from a timelike to a spacelike state. This theoretical model finds a physically phenomenological analogy in microwave scattering experiments using open, non-Hermitian systems, which link complex transmission time delay to spatial wave decay.

</details>


### [192] [The Pauli Probability Spectrum Carries the Pure-State Quantum Fisher Metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21437)
*E. A. Ramirez Trino,M. A. Rajabpour*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明纯量子态的泡利期望值平方分布具有经典Fisher信息矩阵恰好等于量子Fisher信息矩阵两倍的信息几何结构，并揭示该结构可通过制备态与其复共轭配对进行Bell测量直接读取，建立了稳定子Rényi统计、量子态几何与固定多参数读出之间的直接联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳定子Rényi熵（Stabilizer Rényi entropies）将纯量子态中泡利期望值平方的分布压缩为非稳定子性（nonstabilizerness）的标量度量，但压缩前的分布本身是否存在更深层的信息几何结构尚不清楚。本文旨在揭示该分布的内在几何性质及其可观测性。

Method: 作者分析了N量子比特任意光滑纯态族中泡利期望值平方分布的经典Fisher信息矩阵，证明其在每个正则点上恰好等于该态的量子Fisher信息矩阵的两倍。进一步，通过将态与其复共轭态配对并对相应量子比特进行成对Bell测量（Bell measurements）来直接采样该分布，并以向量化密度算子（vectorized density operator）的视角在任意厄米Hilbert–Schmidt算子基下推广了该结果；对混合态情形则推导了缺失Fisher信息量的正半定精确表达式。

Result: 纯态情形下，经典Fisher信息矩阵与量子Fisher信息矩阵之间满足精确的两倍关系，且可用固定读出（readout）方案饱和量子Fisher信息矩阵，不依赖于待估参数数目或具体的纯态传感模型。混合态情形下，Bell概率不再等于归一化的泡利期望值平方，同一固定读出通常不再最优，作者给出了缺失Fisher信息量的精确正半定表达式并识别了Bell采样不可见的态变化分量。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了泡利分布压缩前具有简洁的信息几何结构，其普适性超越了泡利代数（Pauli algebra）本身，源于态与其复共轭配对与向量化密度算子的同一性。该结果建立了稳定子Rényi统计、量子态几何与固定多参数读出之间的直接连接。

Abstract: Stabilizer Rényi entropies compress the distribution of squared Pauli expectation values of a pure quantum state into scalar measures of nonstabilizerness. Here we show that, before this compression, the distribution has a remarkably simple information-geometric structure. For any smooth pure-state family of $N$ qubits, its classical Fisher information matrix is exactly twice the quantum Fisher information matrix of the state at every regular point. This information can be accessed directly: by preparing the state together with its complex conjugate and performing pairwise Bell measurements between corresponding qubits, one samples the same Pauli distribution and saturates the quantum Fisher information matrix of the conjugate pair. The readout is fixed and does not depend on the number of estimated parameters or on the particular pure-state sensing model. We show that the origin of this result is more general than Pauli algebra: conjugation identifies the doubled pure state with the vectorized density operator, whose coordinates are real in any Hermitian Hilbert--Schmidt operator basis. The situation changes for mixed states, for which the Bell probabilities no longer coincide with normalized squared Pauli expectation values and the same fixed readout is generally not optimal. We derive an exact positive-semidefinite expression for the missing Fisher information, identifying the components of the state variation that are invisible to Bell sampling. Our results reveal a direct connection between stabilizer Rényi statistics, quantum-state geometry, and fixed multiparameter readout.

</details>


### [193] [Finite-Resolution Limits on Quantum Uniqueness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22001)
*Seramika Ariwahjoedi,Sameer Ahmad Mir,Mir Faizal*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明Stone-von Neumann定理所依赖的强连续性假设无法通过任何有限分辨率的Weyl关系询问来验证。对任意正则Weyl表示、任意正常态和任意有限有界协议观测量，均可构造聚合物（polymer）表示态在任意给定精度下复现相同期望值，从而表明非正则聚合物运动学未被有限Weyl数据排除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Stone-von Neumann定理确保正则量子化的唯一性依赖于Weyl平移群的强连续性假设，但这一假设在物理操作层面是否可以验证尚未明确。该问题对量子引力动机的量子化方案具有直接相关性，因为后者常采用非正则表示。

Method: 引入"协议代数"（protocol algebra）概念，由有限生成的Weyl平移子群生成，对应有限个相空间位移和有界期望值的探测协议。利用聚合物表示作为经典的非正则表示范例，证明对任意正则Weyl表示、任意正常态及任意有限有界观测量族，聚合物态可在任意指定精度下复制相同的期望值。

Result: 证明了有限协议无法在操作上确证正则性假设：正则表示与聚合物表示在任意有限观测量集合上不可区分。该结果不声称正则与聚合物量子化之间存在物理等价性，而是识别出需要额外输入（如哈密顿动力学、能量正则性、半经典性或连续极限）才能在表示间进行区分或选择。

Conclusion: 有限Weyl数据本身不足以排除非正则聚合物运动学，正则性假设的验证需要超出有限探测框架的额外物理输入。该结论对量子引力启发的量子化方案中采用非正则表示提供了操作层面的理论依据。

Abstract: The Stone-von Neumann theorem makes canonical quantization unique only after one imposes strong continuity of the Weyl translation groups. We show that this continuity assumption cannot be certified by any finite-resolution interrogation of the Weyl relations. A finite protocol probes only finitely many phase-space displacements and bounded expectation values, thereby determining a protocol algebra \(C_S\) generated by a finitely generated subgroup of Weyl translations, but not the regularity of an extension to the full Weyl algebra. Using the polymer representation as a canonical non-regular representation, we prove that for every regular Weyl representation, every normal state, and every finite family of bounded protocol observables, a polymer state reproduces the same expectation values to arbitrary prescribed accuracy. Thus finite agreement with Schrödinger quantum mechanics does not operationally certify the regularity hypothesis entering uniqueness. The result does not assert physical equivalence between regular and polymer quantizations, it identifies where additional input, such as Hamiltonian dynamics, energy regularity, semiclassicality, or a continuum limit, is required to distinguish or select representations. In this precise sense, non-regular polymer kinematics are not excluded by finite Weyl data alone, a point of direct relevance to quantum-gravity motivated quantizations.

</details>


### [194] [Time-Resolved Edge Flux on the SU(3) Triangle: A Cooperative-Decay Graph Framework with Exact Bateman Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22664)
*Gombojav O Ariunbold,Vaidyanathan Sivaraman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出将协作衰变（cooperative emission）图上的每一条跃迁边都提升为随时间变化的边流量（edge flux），并证明节点上的流量守恒恰好等价于泡利主方程（Pauli master equation）。在存在深度函数（depth function）且每条边深度递减的条件下，总协作速率等于平均图深度的下降速度，其能量加权形式给出辐射强度 $I(t)=-d\langle E\rangle/dt$，适用于任意能级间隔。该排序对生成元进行三角化，从而从态退出速率导出弛豫谱，并在任意有限有向无环图（directed acyclic graph）上给出每个布居数的广义巴特曼闭合形式（generalized Bateman closed form）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，三能级系综的协作发射仅通过辐射强度来诊断，无法说明每个时刻哪些通道承载光子。作者的动机是超越这种仅依赖强度的诊断方式，建立一种能够分辨通道间流量分配的更细致的理论框架。

Method: 作者将协作衰变图中每条跃迁边转化为时间依赖的边流量，并证明节点流量守恒是泡利主方程的精确重新表述。通过引入深度函数假设每条边深度递减1，将总协作速率与平均图深度的下降速度关联，并利用能量加权形式得到辐射强度公式。该排序使生成元三角化，从而从态退出速率推导弛豫谱，并给出任意有限有向无环图上的广义Bateman闭合解。

Result: 作者得到辐射强度为 $I(t)=-d\langle E\rangle/dt$（适用于任意能级间隔），并通过生成元的三角化从态退出速率导出弛豫谱。此外，在任意有限有向无环图上，所有布居数均可表示为广义Bateman形式的闭合解，其中退出速率的简并性被吸收进多项式乘指数的递归结构中。

Conclusion: 该工作为协作衰变提供了一种基于图论与流量守恒的全新诊断框架，超越了仅依赖辐射强度的传统方法，并给出了适用于任意有限DAG的解析闭合解。

Abstract: Cooperative emission from three-level ensembles is conventionally diagnosed through the radiated intensity alone, which cannot say which channels carry the photons at each instant. We promote every transition of the cooperative-decay graph into a time-dependent edge flux and show that node-wise conservation of this flux is an exact restatement of the Pauli master equation. Suppose the graph carries a depth function that decreases by one along every edge. Then the total cooperative rate is the descent speed of the mean graph depth, and its energy-weighted form gives the radiated intensity as $I(t)=-\dd\langle E\rangle/\dd t$ for arbitrary level spacings. The same ordering triangularizes the generator. This delivers the relaxation spectrum from the state exit rates, together with a generalized Bateman closed form for every population on an arbitrary finite directed acyclic graph, in which all exit-rate degeneracies are absorbed into a polynomial-times-exponential recursion.

</details>


### [195] [Geometric Phases of a Driven Qubit: Comparing Berry and Uhlmann Holonomies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22919)
*Hyeonseok Yang,Changsuk Noh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究开放量子系统中Uhlmann相位与Berry相位之间的关系，通过严格求解含均匀带隙锥形驱动的量子比特Lindblad方程，证明了在联合绝热与低温极限下动态Uhlmann相位收敛于Berry相位，并进一步考察了能隙闭合情形下这种对应关系的幸存方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Berry相位源自纯量子态的绝热动力学，而Uhlmann相位通常以运动学方式描述混态路径，二者之间的物理对应关系缺乏严格的理论基础。作者旨在通过开放系统的实际动力学来建立并验证Uhlmann-Berry对应。

Method: 针对受到均匀带隙锥形驱动（uniformly gapped conical drive）的量子比特（qubit），作者在闭式形式下获得了平衡Gibbs循环的Uhlmann联络（Uhlmann connection）与和乐（holonomy）。随后在旋转坐标系中精确求解相应的Lindblad方程，并分析其稳态性质与有限驱动修正。

Result: 证明Lindblad方程的稳态形成非平衡极限环并滞后于瞬时Gibbs循环；在慢驱动与低温联合极限下动态Uhlmann相位趋近平衡Uhlmann相位并最终收敛于Berry相位。发现赤道循环上Uhlmann相位随温度变化发生不连续的π跳变。在横向能隙闭合情形下，给出纯态路径的开放性、闭合的歧义性及测地线闭合的几何选取规则。

Conclusion: Uhlmann-Berry对应在开放系统的物理动力学中获得严格基础，并且在能隙闭合处通过测地线选择规则（geodesic selection rule）得以幸存——Uhlmann相位在低温极限下从Berry相位族中挑选出由偏置场速度与加速度确定的特定成员，而Gibbs路径通过最大混态平滑闭合，Uhlmann和乐无需正则化即可保持唯一且连续。

Abstract: The Berry phase arises naturally from the adiabatic dynamics of a pure quantum state, whereas the Uhlmann phase is usually formulated kinematically for a prescribed path of mixed states. For a qubit subject to a uniformly gapped conical drive, we obtain the Uhlmann connection and holonomy in closed form for the equilibrium Gibbs cycle. We then solve the corresponding Lindblad equation exactly in the rotating frame and show that its steady state forms a nonequilibrium limit cycle that lags the instantaneous Gibbs cycle. As the driving becomes slow, the Uhlmann phase approaches the equilibrium Uhlmann phase, which cooling then reduces to the Berry phase: in the joint adiabatic and low-temperature limit, the dynamical Uhlmann phase therefore converges to the Berry phase. This grounds the Uhlmann--Berry correspondence in the physical dynamics of an open system. We also characterize the finite-driving corrections, together with the discontinuous $π$ jump that the Uhlmann phase undergoes on the equatorial cycle as the temperature is varied. Finally, we examine an isolated transversal gap closing, where this zero-temperature correspondence can break down: the pure-state path becomes open, its closure is ambiguous, and gap-opening regularizations close it along a geodesic set by the direction of the bias field, splitting the Berry phase into a one-parameter family of values. The Gibbs path instead closes smoothly through the maximally mixed state, so the Uhlmann holonomy requires no regularization and remains unique and continuous at every finite temperature. In the low-temperature limit the Uhlmann phase selects a single member of the Berry family---the geodesic closure in the osculating plane, fixed by the velocity and acceleration of the driving field. The Uhlmann--Berry correspondence of the gapped regime thus survives the gap closing as a geodesic selection rule.

</details>


### [196] [What do position and time mean in the quantum wavefunction?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23032)
*Mustafa Bakr,Zichi Zhang,Margot Stakenborg*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个统一的量子力学教学框架，围绕波函数 ψ(x,t) 中隐藏的两个映射（希尔伯特空间中的时间演化与谱表示），系统澄清了位置表示、广义本征矢、时间算子等概念之间的数学角色与记法差异，并提供了可复用的教学路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ψ(x,t) 是量子力学中最基础也最容易被过度解读的记法。学生常因 x 和 t 以同一函数参数出现而混淆二者的数学地位，也会错误类比 ψ(x)=⟨x|ψ⟩ 而怀疑是否应引入广义右矢 |t⟩ 来定义 ψ(t)。此外，是否不存在普适的时间算子往往被简单归结为 Pauli 论证，而涉及的结构分散于课程不同部分，亟需一个统一的梳理框架。

Method: 作者围绕 ψ(x,t) 中隐藏的两个映射展开：一是时间演化在希尔伯特空间（Hilbert space）中沿轨迹选择态，二是谱表示将此态映射到所选可观测量结果的振幅上。文中在不引入广义本征矢的前提下构造位置表示，将 Dirac 的 |x⟩ 记法视为受控的连续谱简写，并通过有限格点极限说明 delta 归一化在何处进入。作者还从概念上区分背景坐标、平移参数、可观测量、谱标签与物理记录，并通过 Stone 定理类比、预定时间位置测量与到达时间测量的对比、Pauli 论证强形式所排除的内容，以及一个可精确求解的边界案例（其中存在自伴时间算子）来阐明问题。最后用自旋、circuit-QED（circuit quantum electrodynamics）和光钟（optical-clock）等实验案例加以检验。

Result: 论文给出了一个教学上自洽的框架：位置的谱表示无需借助广义本征矢即可形式化；|x⟩ 记法仅在连续谱简写意义上成立；Pauli 论证的强形式所排除的对象得到清晰界定；并展示了一个存在正则自伴（self-adjoint）时间算子的可精确求解边界情形。多个实验案例验证了该框架的适用性。

Conclusion: 该工作的目标并非提出新的时间解释，而是提供一个可复用的教学框架，帮助学生区分数学角色与记法约定，从而避免对量子力学中时间与位置概念的系统性误读。

Abstract: The notation $ψ(x,t)$ is among the first pieces of quantum mechanics that students learn. It is also among the easiest to over-interpret. Because $x$ and $t$ occur as arguments of the same function, students may ask whether they have the same mathematical status. They may also ask whether $ψ(t)$ should require a generalized bra $\bra{t}$ in the same way that $ψ(x)=\braket{x}ψ$ is often written. A related question is whether the absence of a universal time operator follows simply from Pauli's argument. These questions mix several structures that are usually introduced in different parts of the curriculum. We present a unified pedagogical treatment built around two maps hidden in $ψ(x,t)$. Time evolution selects a state along a trajectory in Hilbert space. A spectral representation then maps that state to amplitudes labelled by outcomes of a chosen observable. We formulate the position representation without generalized eigenkets. We recover Dirac's $\ket{x}$ notation as a controlled continuum shorthand and use a finite-grid limit to show where delta normalization enters. We distinguish background coordinates, translation parameters, observables, spectral labels, and physical records. We also clarify the Stone-theorem analogy, compare prescribed-time position measurements with arrival-time measurements, state what the strong form of Pauli's argument excludes, and exhibit an exactly solvable boundary case in which a canonical self-adjoint time observable exists. Spin, circuit-QED, and optical-clock examples provide experimentally grounded checks. The aim is not a new interpretation of time. It is a reusable teaching framework for separating mathematical role from notation.

</details>


### [197] [Quantum observables as Fréchet sensitivity kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23191)
*Rafael Abreu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于变分-伴随（variational-adjoint）框架的量子力学诠释，将正向波函数与伴随波函数之间的相互作用定义为系统的广义灵敏核（sensitivity kernel），并表明玻恩概率密度（Born probability density）是该相互作用密度在伴随波函数取正向波函数复共轭时的一个特例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子力学将玻恩概率密度视为波函数模的平方，缺乏将其置于更一般的观测敏感性框架中的统一视角。作者试图通过变分-伴随方法为量子力学观测提供统一且更具普适性的数学基础。

Method: 采用变分-伴随方法，定义正向波函数与伴随波函数之间的Fréchet相互作用密度作为系统灵敏核。当伴随波函数选择为正向波函数的复共轭时，该密度退化为实数非负的玻恩概率密度，说明概率是该Fréchet相互作用密度的一种特殊正定形式。

Result: 除概率外，该框架还产出与动量、能量和自旋等其他量子可观测量相关联的Fréchet灵敏核，表明玻恩概率密度属于更广泛的量子可观测量灵敏核类别。该诠释还与时间对称量子力学诠释建立联系，并具有量子控制和量子计量方面的潜在应用前景。

Conclusion: 变分-伴随诠释为量子力学观测提供统一框架，将概率密度置于更广泛的灵敏度分析体系中，并为量子控制与量子计量等实际应用提供新的理论工具。

Abstract: We present a variational--adjoint interpretation of quantum mechanics in which the interaction between forward and adjoint wavefunctions defines a general sensitivity kernel of the system. This Fréchet interaction density quantifies how perturbations in the wavefunction (or system parameters) influence a chosen observable. The familiar Born probability density appears as a special case when the adjoint wavefunction is chosen as the complex conjugate of the forward wavefunction, for which the interaction density becomes real and non-negative. Within this framework, probability is a particular positive-definite form of sensitivity described by the Fréchet interaction density.
  In general, the variational--adjoint interpretation also produces Fréchet sensitivity kernels associated with other quantum observables, including momentum, energy, and spin. This suggests that the Born probability density belongs to a broader class of Fréchet sensitivity kernels associated with quantum observables. The proposed interpretation also provides a connection with time-symmetric interpretations of quantum mechanics and possible future applications in quantum control and quantum metrology.

</details>


### [198] [`It from Bit': is there a second law of quantum complexity?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22977)
*S. Davatolhagh,A. Sheykhi,M. H. Zarei*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从最小作用量原理（principle of least action）出发，将其诠释为与普里高金（Prigogine）最小熵产生原理相一致的“最小熵增长定律”，并基于准静态信息量子化规则（quasistatic information quantization rule）探讨了量子复杂性第二定律（second law of quantum complexity）的可推导性。研究结果表明，在热寂（heat-death）之后，量子态复杂性按 C(t)=Cmax·exp(−1/t) 演化，随时间增长并饱和于正比于平衡熵指数函数的 Cmax；而在非平衡情形下，量子复杂性可随时间递减，渐近趋向由远离平衡程度决定的最小值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在从更深层次上理解最小作用量原理与熵增长之间的关系，并验证量子复杂性第二定律是否可由既有信息量子化规则推导得出，从而为量子复杂性在热力学极端情境下的演化行为提供理论依据。

Method: 采用理论推导方法，将最小作用量原理重新诠释为与普里高金最小熵产生原理相容的最小熵增长定律，并利用准静态信息量子化规则（准静态信息量子化规则，源自 Proc. R. Soc. A 480: 20240024）对量子复杂性第二定律进行演绎推导，分别分析热平衡后与非平衡条件下量子复杂性的时间演化方程。

Result: 推导得出量子复杂性第二定律可从信息量子化规则中导出：在热寂之后，量子态复杂性随时间呈 C(t)=Cmax·exp(−1/t) 增长，饱和值 Cmax 为平衡熵 Smax 的指数函数；在非平衡条件下，量子复杂性可随时间下降，渐近趋近于由系统偏离平衡程度所决定的最小值。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，量子复杂性第二定律并非独立假设，而是可从信息量子化规则等基本原理中推导出的必然结果，其在平衡与非平衡情境下分别呈现单调增长与可能降低的演化特征，为理解量子复杂性与热力学定律之间的深层联系提供了新的视角。

Abstract: At a deeper level the principle of least action is interpreted as the law of least entropy increase consistent with Prigogine's principle of minimum entropy production, and the implications of quasistatic information quantization rule (Proc. R. Soc. A 480: 20240024), are explored for the conjectured second law of quantum complexity. It is thus shown that the conjectured second law of complexity is derivable from the information quantization rule such that long after heat-death the quantum state complexity evolves as $C(t)=C_{\rm max}\exp(-1/t)$, increasing with time to a saturation value $C_{\rm max}$ that is exponential in the equilibrium entropy $S_{\rm max}$. For the out-of-equilibrium circumstances, however, the quantum complexity can decrease with the time, asymptotically tending to a minimum determined by the distance from equilibrium.

</details>


### [199] [Collective photon echoes in the Tavis-Cummings model: distribution independence, two detuning regimes, and the Dicke ladder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21442)
*Michael Tavis*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于Tavis-Cummings模型在少光子区域（$\bar n<N$）的精确解，研究了$N$个基态二能级分子与无损耗腔共享弱场时的集体光子回波现象。数值计算证实了回波时间$τ_E=4π\sqrt{N+Δ}/g$在共振条件下的形式，发现回波时间独立于初始光子分布（相干、热、压缩等），但回波振幅对初始态敏感。文章还分析了失谐对动力学结构的组织作用，并通过Lindblad模拟和全希尔伯特空间无序模拟验证了在现有五比特超导器件上可观测性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解多体量子系统与光场相互作用中的集体效应（collective effects），特别是基态系统与弱场相互作用时出现的非平凡吸收——光子回波（photon echo）现象。该研究旨在从精确解出发验证回波时间的理论形式，并探索其在现代量子硬件上的实验可行性。

Method: 作者从Tavis-Cummings模型（Tavis-Cummings model）在少光子区间（$\bar n<N$）的精确解析解出发，对$N=5$到$400$的体系进行数值计算，验证回波时间的理论表达式。研究比较了不同初始光子分布（相干、热、压缩及振荡压缩）和不同初始Dicke态下的回波行为，并采用Lindblad主方程模拟腔衰减和退相位，以及全希尔伯特空间无序模拟来评估超导量子器件的可行性。

Result: 数值结果证实回波时间为$τ_E=4π\sqrt{N+Δ}/g$（排除了备选形式$4π\sqrt{N-\bar n+Δ}/g$），且回波时间独立于初始光子分布，但回波振幅在固定平均值下变化达数十个百分点（含压缩相位导致1.8倍差异）。失谐将动力学分为两个干净区域，色散支回波时间约为共振时的一半。对于任意初始Dicke态，发射在$m\simeq-N/2+\bar n$处取代吸收，仅在基态出现单分量回波。可行性分析表明在现有五比特超导硬件上$N\sim5$-$20$可观测到首批回波，对比度约为$e^{-κτ_E/2}$。

Conclusion: 该工作从精确模型解出发，确立了集体光子回波时间的确切形式及其对初始光子分布的不变性，同时揭示了回波振幅对初始态的敏感性。理论分析表明这一效应在现有超导量子硬件上具备实验观测可行性，为研究多体量子系统中集体量子光学现象提供了理论框架与实验路径。

Abstract: An ensemble of $N$ two-level molecules prepared in its ground state and sharing a lossless cavity with a weak field does not simply absorb: the intensity collapses and then recurs, in a train of collective photon echoes. Working from the exact solution of the Tavis-Cummings model in the few-photon regime $\bar n<N$, we confirm the echo time $τ_E=4π\sqrt{N+Δ}/g$ numerically at resonance from $N=5$ to $400$, the small-$N$ end discriminating this form from the alternative $4π\sqrt{N-\bar n+Δ}/g$ in its favor. The initial state requires no preparation, being the ground state. The echo time is independent of the initial photon distribution: coherent, thermal, squeezed and oscillatory-squeezed distributions spanning variances from 2 to 34 all recur together, as does a controlled pair with identical mean and variance differing only in the shape of $ρ_{nn}$. The echo amplitude is not: it varies at the tens-of-percent level at fixed mean, including a factor of 1.8 with the squeezing phase at fixed squeezing strength. Detuning organizes the dynamics into two clean regimes separated by a fragmented crossover, the dispersive-branch echo time approaching one-half the resonant one, and sufficient detuning removes the dependence on the initial Dicke state. For arbitrary initial Dicke state, emission replaces absorption at $m\simeq-N/2+\bar n$, and the echo envelope acquires one component per step up the ladder: a single-component echo occurs only at the ground state. A feasibility analysis against a five-qubit superconducting device, with Lindblad simulations of cavity decay and dephasing and full-Hilbert-space disorder simulations, shows the first echoes observable at $N\sim5$-$20$ on existing hardware: the echo survives the dominant loss channel with contrast $\sim e^{-κτ_E/2}$, photons being shielded from cavity decay while resident in the emitters.

</details>


### [200] [Anomalous Local Heat Capacity and Bipartite Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21508)
*Jake Xuereb,A. de Oliveira Junior*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究相互作用的量子系统中局部热容（local heat capacity）异常与量子纠缠（entanglement）之间的联系，推导出基于局部能量与相互作用能量涨落的可分离性界（separability bound），并通过自旋链和耦合谐振子两个例子验证。最后给出互信息（mutual information）与热容非加性之间的信息论公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自引力系统（self-gravitating systems）和强相互作用量子系统已知具有负或异常的比热容，但纠缠如何影响相互作用系统对温度变化的热力学响应尚不清楚。作者旨在从本质上理解局部热容异常何时发生，并探索其与纠缠的关联。

Method: 分析相互作用量子系统的局部热容，给出异常情形出现的解析条件；推导基于局部能量与相互作用能量涨落（fluctuations）的可分离性判据；通过最近邻自旋-1/2链和两个耦合量子谐振子两个具体例子加以验证；最后引入信息论公式将互信息、非热性（athermality）与热容非加性联系起来。

Result: 发现局部热容异常与纠缠之间存在联系，并给出基于局部及相互作用能量涨落的模型无关（model-independent）纠缠检测界。两个实例验证了理论结果，且得到了连接互信息与热容非加性的信息论表达式。

Conclusion: 本工作为量子系统热容与量子关联之间的关系提供了深刻见解，所推导的可分离性界可作为热力学性的纠缠检测工具，适用于任意相互作用量子系统。

Abstract: The heat capacity of a system quantifies how it energetically responds to changes in temperature at equilibrium. Whilst this quantity is positive and even additive for non-interacting systems, self-gravitating systems such as stars or subsystems of strongly interacting quantum systems are known to have negative or anomalous specific heat capacities. In this work, we investigate how the presence of entanglement at equilibrium can influence how an interacting system responds energetically to changes in temperature. We examine the local heat capacity of interacting quantum systems providing an analytical understanding for when anomalies occur. Most interestingly, we find a connection between local heat capacity anomalies and entanglement by deriving a separability bound based on the fluctuations of local and interaction energies. We illustrate our results with two examples (i) a nearest neighbour spin-1/2 chain and (ii) two coupled quantum harmonic oscillators. Lastly, we provide an information-theoretic formula connecting mutual information and athermality to the non-additivity of the heat capacity. Our results provide model-independent thermodynamic entanglement detection bounds and insight into the relationship between quantum correlations and the heat capacity of quantum systems.

</details>


### [201] [Many-body ergodicity breaking from wavefunction snapshots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21524)
*Riccardo Andreoni,Devendra Singh Bhakuni,Marcello Dalmonte,Lev Vidmar,Miroslav Hopjan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过分析非平衡波函数快照的二进制固有维数（binary intrinsic dimension, BID）和基于汉明距离（Hamming distance）构建的波函数网络的拓扑特性，研究了多体遍历性破缺转变（many-body ergodicity-breaking transition）的临界特征。研究发现临界点具有广泛但次极大的BID，以及具有高度连接枢纽的无标度（scale-free）网络连接，这些特征可作为判断遍历性、临界和非遍历性状态的实验可观测探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代量子实验可通过投影测量探测多体波函数，但一个根本性问题是测量分布的内在结构能否揭示超出预定义可观测量的量子动力学。本文旨在探究在多体遍历性破缺转变处，测量快照的固有结构是否能够提供关于量子动力学的信息。

Method: 采用两种互补特征分析非平衡波函数快照：（1）测量快照的二进制固有维数（BID），用于刻画快照空间的内在维度；（2）基于汉明距离构建波函数网络，并分析其拓扑性质，包括连接度和枢纽结构。

Result: 临界点表现出广泛但次极大的BID，以及具有高度连接枢纽的无标度网络连接。这些信号能够区分遍历性、临界和非遍历性三个区域，为从投影测量快照探测遍历性破缺提供了实验上可行的探针。

Conclusion: 波函数快照的内在结构和网络拓扑可以作为判断多体遍历性破缺状态的有效实验探针，为超越预定义可观测量的量子动力学表征开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Modern quantum experiments can probe many-body wavefunctions through projective measurements, providing snapshots of individual many-body configurations. A fundamental question is whether the intrinsic structure of their measurement distributions can reveal quantum dynamics beyond predefined observables. Here, we address this question at the many-body ergodicity-breaking transition using two complementary characteristics of nonequilibrium wavefunction snapshots: their binary intrinsic dimension (BID) and the topology of wavefunction networks constructed from Hamming distances. We show that the critical point is characterized by an extensive but submaximal BID, and scale-free network connectivity with highly connected hubs. These signatures distinguish the ergodic, critical, and nonergodic regimes, and provide experimentally accessible probes of ergodicity breaking from projective measurement snapshots.

</details>


### [202] [Scaling vs entanglement in measurement-induced phase transition for non-integrable systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21578)
*Paranjoy Chaki,Protyush Nandi,Subinay Dasgupta,Ujjwal Sen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了确定性全局测量引发的测量诱导相变（measurement-induced phase transition）在非可积模型中的行为，发现此前在可积横向场伊辛模型（TFIM）中观测到的相变在非可积变体中仍然存在，但相变点随系统大小的标度行为强烈依赖于初始态的选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前观测到的确定性全局测量诱导的相变仅限于可积横向场伊辛模型，其在非可积系统中的普适性和鲁棒性尚不明确，需要考察可积性破缺（integrability breaking）对这类测量诱导相变的影响。

Method: 作者选取横场伊辛模型加纵向场（longitudinal field）以及轴向次近邻伊辛（ANNNI）模型作为非可积变体，计算幸存概率（survival probability）和二分纠缠熵（bipartite entanglement entropy），对极化的乘积初态和GHZ态两种初始态进行有限尺寸标度（finite-size scaling）分析。

Result: 两种非可积模型均呈现出面积律（area-law）与体积律（volume-law）纠缠相之间的相变。对于极化的乘积初态，相变点在两个非可积模型中都遵循与可积TFIM一致的逆平方根标度；而对于GHZ初态，非可积模型中的相变点偏离该标度且趋近零的速度显著慢于可积TFIM。

Conclusion: 确定性测量诱导的相变在非可积体系中依然稳健存在，表明其对可积性破缺不敏感；但初态对相变点的标度行为具有决定性影响，说明初始态的微观结构在测量诱导相变中扮演关键角色。

Abstract: We find that the measurement-induced phase transition generated by deterministic global measurements, previously observed in the integrable transverse-field Ising model (TFIM), persists in non-integrable variants of the same. To address this question, we consider the TFIM with longitudinal field and the axial next-nearest-neighbor Ising (ANNNI) model. We show that both the survival probability and the bipartite entanglement entropy consistently capture a transition between area-law and volume-law entangled phases for two distinct initial states: a product state with all spins polarized along the transverse direction and a Greenberger-Horne-Zeilinger (GHZ) state. Finite-size scaling reveals a pronounced initial state dependence: for the polarized product state, the transition point follows an inverse-square-root scaling with system size in both non-integrable models, consistent with the integrable TFIM, whereas for the GHZ initial state, it deviates from this scaling and approaches zero considerably more slowly in the non-integrable models than in the integrable TFIM. These results establish the robustness of measurement-induced transitions under deterministic measurements against integrability breaking while highlighting the crucial role of the initial state in governing their scaling behavior.

</details>


### [203] [Experimental Investigation of Tunable-Order Hilbert-Space Ergodicity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21959)
*Zou-Wei Pan,Wenquan Liu,Xing Rong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并证明了一族m-tone驱动可在量子比特上实现k阶Hilbert空间遍历性（k-HSE），并利用金刚石中的氮-空位中心实验验证了3-tone驱动可实现3-HSE，发现高阶统计量依赖初始状态，揭示了HSE层级中间区域的独特物理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hilbert空间遍历性（HSE）为研究驱动量子系统中的热化提供了新框架，但有限阶HSE动力学因构造此类驱动的困难而几乎未被探索，本文旨在填补这一中间层级（intermediate regime）的研究空白。

Method: 首先从理论上提出并证明一族m-tone驱动在量子比特上实现k-HSE（k=2m-3），驱动参数以O(k)代价确定；随后利用金刚石中的单个氮-空位中心（NV center）进行实验验证，通过3-tone驱动展示3-HSE的实现，并进行进一步的理论分析探讨初始态依赖的高阶统计特性。

Result: 实验证明3-tone驱动可实现3-HSE，且四阶统计量依赖于初始态；进一步理论分析表明这种初始态依赖性在各类驱动中具有普遍性，表明即使动力学是遍历的，仍可从高阶统计量中恢复初始态信息。

Conclusion: 本工作拓展了量子遍历性（quantum ergodicity）的研究范围，揭示了HSE层级中的中间区域蕴含的新颖物理，展示了遍历动力学与信息可恢复性之间复杂而微妙的关系。

Abstract: Hilbert-space ergodicity (HSE) provides a new framework for studying thermalization in driven quantum systems, complementing the eigenstate thermalization hypothesis, which is restricted to static systems. This ergodicity is hierarchical: by quantifying how randomly the dynamics explores the Hilbert space, one obtains a family of levels termed $k$-HSE. While HSE has been observed at the lowest and highest levels, finite-order HSE dynamics remains largely unexplored due to the difficulty of constructing such drives. Here, we explore this intermediate regime and uncover its distinctive physics. We first propose and prove that a family of $m$-tone drives on qubits realizes $k$-HSE up to $k = 2m{-}3$, with drive parameters determined at $O(k)$ cost. Using a single nitrogen-vacancy center in diamond, we verify this design by showing that a 3-tone drive realizes 3-HSE, with fourth-order statistics depending on the initial state. Further in-depth theoretical analysis shows that this initial-state dependence is generic across drives, demonstrating the possibility of recovering the initial state from higher-order statistics even when the dynamics is ergodic. Our work broadens the study of quantum ergodicity and reveals intriguing physics within its hierarchy.

</details>


### [204] [Bloch states of an infinite one-dimensional lattice under transverse electric field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22199)
*Anand Aruna Kumar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个有效一维周期原子链在横向弱静电场下的理论模型。将本征晶格用倒空间中谐波加权的周期赝势表示，横向电场产生的弹性和极化响应用周期性悬链线（catenary）轮廓近似。通过Bloch约化将两者统一到倒空间哈密顿量中，在单胞层面得到含余弦和双曲余弦调制的Mathieu方程，进而在第一布里渊区约化后获得场依赖的能带结构和Bragg带隙层次。该模型揭示了横向电场重组低维周期系统Bloch谱的简单机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究低维周期系统在外加电场作用下的能带结构与Bloch谱变化机制，特别是横向电场对一维周期晶格电子态的调制作用。现有模型对电场影响周期系统能谱的微观机制解释不够清晰，需要构建一个可解析处理的简化模型。

Method: 将一维原子链的本征晶格在倒空间中表示为谐波加权的周期赝势（periodic pseudopotential），横向电场贡献用周期性悬链线轮廓近似。利用Bloch约化（Bloch reduction）将两者统一于倒空间哈密顿量，在单胞层面推导出含余弦与双曲余弦调制的Mathieu方程，并通过第一布里渊区（first Brillouin zone）约化获得能带与Bragg带隙。

Result: 本征晶格产生的相互作用按|r-s|⁻¹衰减，悬链线贡献则是系数正比于(1+π²(r-2)⁻¹)、渐近按|r-s|⁻²衰减的对称Toeplitz修正。悬链线贡献的完整非零倒空间权重绝对收敛且求和为|V_c|/e。模型得到场依赖的能带能量及相应的Bragg带隙层次。

Conclusion: 该模型揭示了一种由横向电场重组低维周期系统Bloch谱的简单机制：电场通过悬链线势对晶格势能产生长程修正，在倒空间中形成Toeplitz型耦合，进而调制能带结构与带隙层次。

Abstract: We consider an effectively one-dimensional periodic atomic chain subjected to a weak static electric field transverse to the chain axis. The intrinsic lattice is represented in reciprocal space by a harmonically weighted periodic pseudopotential, while the transverse field produces a weak elastic and polarizable response whose minimum-energy distribution is approximated by a periodically repeated catenary profile. Bloch reduction places both contributions in a common reciprocal-space Hamiltonian. The intrinsic lattice produces a \ (|r-s|^ {-1} \) hierarchy, whereas the catenary contribution is a symmetric Toeplitz correction with coefficients proportional to \((1+π^2(r-2) ^ {-1} \) and therefore asymptotically to \ (|r-s|^ {-2} \). The complete nonzero reciprocal weight of the catenary contribution is absolutely convergent and sums to \ (|V_ {\rm c} |/e \). At the unit-cell level, the lowest intrinsic harmonic together with the catenary term gives a Mathieu equation containing both cosine and hyperbolic-cosine modulations. A first-Brillouin-zone reduction then yields the field-dependent band energies and the corresponding hierarchy of Bragg gaps. The model isolates a simple mechanism by which a transverse electric field can reorganize the Bloch spectrum of a low-dimensional periodic system.
  Keywords: Bloch theory; transverse electric field; catenary potential; Mathieu equation; Brillouin zone; band structure.

</details>


### [205] [Laughlin quasihole geometry from a single snapshot ensemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22384)
*Kaushlendra Kumar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明，对于晶格Laughlin准空穴（Laughlin quasihole），只需在一个普通参考位置测量粒子占据数分布（occupation distribution），结合已知的解析准空穴因子（quasihole factor），即可完全确定其复Gram核（Gram kernel），进而重构出有限距离重叠、Bargmann相位、量子度规（quantum metric）和Berry曲率等量子几何信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子几何通常需要遍历多个参数空间点才能完整刻画，但直接测量代价高昂且缺乏单点测量的理论依据。本文探讨的核心问题是：能否仅凭单一基下的概率测量就确定复杂的量子几何结构？

Method: 利用单点占据数定律（occupation law）与解析准空穴因子共同约束完整的复Gram核；通过有限快照系综（snapshot ensemble）估计该核而无需制备族中的其他成员；采用精确枚举（exact enumeration）方式验证Nielsen-Cirac-Sierra态的几何量重构，并引入Rényi-2散度（Rényi-2 divergence）刻画重构的统计范围。

Result: 精确枚举展示了对度规和几何相位（geometric phase）的有限次测量（finite-shot）重构均可行；重构的统计误差由Rényi-2散度界定；在非简并点上，占据数读出还达到了局部单拷贝多参数信息界（single-copy multiparameter information bound）。此外，该解析结构将族限制在维数最多随粒子数线性增长的确切投影子空间内。

Conclusion: 在晶格Laughlin准空穴体系中，单一基测量足以确定完整的量子几何，表明由单一测量基出发的量子几何重构在特定多体系统中是严格可行的，并提供了统计效率与信息论下界保障。

Abstract: Can a probability distribution measured in one basis determine complex quantum geometry? The answer is affirmative for a lattice Laughlin quasihole. The exact occupation law at one generic reference position, together with the known analytic quasihole factor, fixes the complete complex Gram kernel. A finite snapshot ensemble estimates this kernel without preparing another member of the family, and thereby determines finite-distance overlaps, Bargmann phases, the quantum metric, and the Berry curvature. The same analytic structure confines the family to an exact projective subspace whose dimension grows at most linearly with particle number. Exact enumeration of a Nielsen-Cirac-Sierra state demonstrates finite-shot reconstruction of both the metric and a geometric phase. Moreover, a Rényi-2 divergence sets the statistical range of the reconstruction while, at nondegenerate points, occupation readout also attains the local single-copy multiparameter information bound.

</details>


### [206] [Deterministic Preparation of Arbitrary Spin Eigenfunctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22892)
*Wenxuan Tao,Jianan Wang,Fen Zuo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对具有守恒总自旋的量子态（自旋本征函数）的确定性制备问题，通过推广Bärtschi和Eidenbenz用于Dicke态制备的算法，成功实现了任意自旋本征函数的确定性量子制备，并作为副产品开发了相应的经典重构算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋本征函数（spin eigenfunctions）对量子化学和量子多体物理研究至关重要，其中Dicke态虽已有多种高效量子制备算法，但任意自旋本征函数的确定性制备此前尚不明确。

Method: 推广Bärtschi和Eidenbenz的Dicke态制备算法，利用分支路径（branching paths）和二叉自旋树（binary spin trees）对任意自旋本征函数进行表征与制备；同时开发对应的经典算法以重构这些自旋态。

Result: 成功实现了任意自旋本征函数的确定性量子制备，并额外获得了能够重构所有此类自旋态的经典算法。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了任意自旋本征函数确定性量子制备方法缺失的空白，为量子化学与量子多体物理中的自旋态研究提供了更通用的工具。

Abstract: Quantum states with conserved total spins, or spin eigenfunctions, are important for studying quantum chemistry and quantum manybody physics problems. A typical class of spin eigenfunctions are Dicke states, which attain maximal spins. While we already have many efficient quantum algorithms to prepare Dicke states, it is not yet clear if we could do so for arbitrary spin eigenfunctions deterministically. Generalizing Bärtschi and Eidenbenz's elegant algorithms for Dicke state preparation, we successfully prepare arbitrary spin eigenfunctions characterized by branching paths and binary spin trees. As a byproduct, we also develop the corresponding classical algorithms to reconstruct all these spin states.

</details>


### [207] [Universal equilibrium magic in quantum many-body systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22939)
*Soumyadeep Sarma,Tobias Haug,John Preskill,Wai-Keong Mok*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了混沌多体系统在平衡态下的"魔法"（magic，即非稳定化资源）具有普适性，可由热Scrooge系综（thermal Scrooge ensemble）统一描述，从而将魔法确立为混沌多体系统的一种热力学性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统热化理论（thermalization）描述孤立多体系统在平衡态下的局域性质，而魔法（magic）作为实现通用量子计算所需的全量子资源（stabilizer Rényi entropies，SRE），编码在许多体波函数的全局结构中。此前人们对平衡态下魔法的全局性质及其普适性缺乏理解。

Method: 作者提出混沌多体系统平衡纯态（包括长时间演化态和能量本征态）的魔法可由热Scrooge系综捕获，即在给定吉布斯态（Gibbs state）下信息量最小的纯态系综。在无其他守恒量的系统中，平衡态魔法被证明仅依赖于温度。作者结合解析论证与大规模数值模拟（extensive numerical simulations）验证这一普适预言，并分析无限温度与有限温度下的SRE行为。

Result: 在无限温度下，SRE由泡利谱（Pauli spectrum）的Haar型波动决定；在有限温度下，能量守恒引入由热泡利谱控制的体积律（volume-law）热力学修正。此外，高温混沌多体系统展现出不可被有限深度局域量子电路消除的长程魔法与纠缠（long-range magic and entanglement）。

Conclusion: 该研究将魔法确立为混沌多体系统的一种热力学性质（thermodynamic property），仅由温度决定而与初始状态和微观细节无关，并提出Scrooge系综可能为量子多体资源提供统一描述框架。

Abstract: Thermalization conventionally describes local properties of isolated many-body systems at equilibrium. Magic, or nonstabilizerness $\unicode{x2013}$ the resource enabling universal quantum computation $\unicode{x2013}$ is by contrast encoded in the global structure of the many-body wavefunction. We show that, despite its global nature, the magic of equilibrium pure states of chaotic many-body systems, including late-time evolved states and energy eigenstates, is universal: it is captured by the thermal Scrooge ensemble, the minimally informative ensemble of pure states consistent with the Gibbs state at the same effective temperature. Therefore, for systems with no conserved quantities other than the total energy, equilibrium magic is a function of temperature alone, independent of the initial state and other microscopic features of the equilibrium state. This yields concrete universal predictions for the stabilizer Rényi entropies (SREs). At infinite temperature, the SRE is set by Haar-like fluctuations of the Pauli spectrum, while at finite temperature energy conservation induces a volume-law thermodynamic correction controlled by the thermal Pauli spectrum. We support these predictions with analytical arguments and extensive numerical simulations. We further show that chaotic many-body systems at high temperatures possess long-range magic and entanglement that cannot be removed by finite-depth local quantum circuits. Our results establish magic as a thermodynamic property of chaotic many-body systems and suggest that Scrooge ensembles may provide a unified framework for quantum many-body resources.

</details>


### [208] [Quantum Monte Carlo in the Age of Many-Body Quantum Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23231)
*Yi-Ming Ding,Bin-Bin Mao,Zheng Yan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了将量子蒙特卡洛（QMC）方法扩展到多体量子信息领域的最新进展，聚焦于自旋-1/2系统，统一阐述了纠缠熵、纠缠谱、Rényi负性、稳定子熵及退相干现象等非线性观测量的提取方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信息为多体物理引入了新的信息论视角和诊断工具，但传统QMC仅关注线性可观测量，需要将其扩展以提取非线性信息论观测量。

Method: 以自旋-1/2系统为主要场景，提出一个统一视角来概述从QMC中提取非线性诊断量的方法，包括纠缠熵与纠缠谱、混合态纠缠的Rényi负性、量子魔力的稳定子熵，以及虚时演化与退相干相互作用、强-弱自发对称破缺等退相干驱动现象。

Result: 系统梳理了QMC在量子信息观测量提取方面的方法进展，并将讨论推广至可用于qudit和玻色子系统的一般框架。

Conclusion: QMC已成功超越传统线性观测量的测量范围，成为研究强关联多体系统中量子信息特性的核心数值工具，尤其在更高维度系统中具有广阔前景。

Abstract: Quantum Monte Carlo (QMC) methods are among the central numerical tools for studying strongly correlated quantum many-body systems, particularly in higher dimensions. As quantum information has introduced new information-theoretic perspectives and diagnostics into many-body physics, QMC methods have accordingly been extended beyond the measurement of conventional linear observables. This review summarizes recent progress in adapting QMC to many-body quantum-information, focusing on qubit or spin-$1/2$ systems as a concrete setting while keeping the discussion broadly applicable to qudit and bosonic systems. We present a unified perspective on the extraction of nonlinear diagnostics, including entanglement entropies and entanglement spectra, Rényi negativities for mixed-state entanglement, stabilizer entropies for quantum magic, and decoherence-driven phenomena such as the interplay between imaginary-time evolution and decoherence and strong-to-weak spontaneous symmetry breaking.

</details>


### [209] [Quantum-Geometric Length Scale for Long-distance Squeezing in Bosonic Bogoliubov Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23380)
*Sonu Verma,Sangmo Cheon,Myung-Joong Hwang,Moon Jip Park*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明参量配对（parametric pairing）可在玻色子晶格中产生由紧致Bogoliubov生成元张成的完全平坦的Bogoliubov能带，并揭示规范Bogoliubov模的量子几何（quantum geometry）作为空间扩展量子资源的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模压缩是连续变量（continuous-variable）量子技术的关键资源，但其在玻色子晶格中的空间范围通常受限于色散传播（dispersive propagation），因此需要寻找能超越有限支撑、实现远距离扩展压缩和反常关联的新机制。

Method: 通过参量配对构造紧致Bogoliubov生成元（compact Bogoliubov generators），使混合湮灭与产生算符的生成元产生平移方向的非对易重叠；为满足规范玻色子对易关系，构造空间扩展的规范Bogoliubov模，并用挤压扇区辛量子度量（squeezing-sector symplectic quantum metric）量化其随动量的压缩变换。

Result: 解析连续的规范Bogoliubov模的复动量奇点同时决定反常关联衰减长度和量子度量动量空间宽度，构成内禀量子几何长度尺度（quantum-geometric length scale）；该奇点可在保持精确平坦性下连续调谐。偏离精确平坦时，长距离关联通过多个衰减通道持续存在；缺陷局域模与二聚化边界模提供额外探测手段；弱阻尼极限下可从频率滤波双端口关联中提取同一几何长度尺度。

Conclusion: 规范Bogoliubov模的量子几何是空间扩展量子资源的一个重要物理机制，它源于紧致Bogoliubov生成元的构造，为平坦能带体系中的量子资源设计提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: Multimode squeezing is a key resource for continuous-variable quantum technologies, but its spatial range in bosonic lattices is usually tied to dispersive propagation. Here we show that parametric pairing can create an exactly flat Bogoliubov band spanned by compact Bogoliubov generators, while producing phase-sensitive anomalous correlations and sub-vacuum collective-mode squeezing that extend far beyond their finite support. Each compact generator mixes annihilation and creation operators, thereby encoding the Bogoliubov squeezing structure, and neighboring translated generators can have nonzero commutator overlap. Enforcing canonical bosonic commutation relations therefore requires spatially extended linear combinations of these translated compact generators, which define the canonical Bogoliubov modes. The squeezing transformation of these modes varies with momentum and is quantified by the squeezing-sector symplectic quantum metric. A complex-momentum singularity of the analytically continued canonical Bogoliubov modes sets both the anomalous-correlation decay length and the momentum-space width of this metric, defining an intrinsic quantum-geometric length scale. This singularity can be tuned continuously while preserving exact flatness, thereby controlling the spatial range of correlations and squeezing. Away from exact flatness, long-distance correlations persist through multiple decay channels, while modes localized by defects and dimerized boundaries provide complementary probes of the underlying quantum geometry. In the weak-damping limit, the same quantum-geometric length scale can be extracted from frequency-filtered two-port correlations. Our results establish the quantum geometry of canonical Bogoliubov modes as a mechanism for spatially extended quantum resources arising from compact Bogoliubov generators.

</details>


### [210] [Dissipation-induced Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev physics in many-body cavity quantum electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23557)
*Pietro Pacchioni,Filippo Ferrari,Vincenzo Savona,Matteo Seclì*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明腔量子电动力学（cavity quantum electrodynamics, cavity QED）器件能够实现耗散型Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev（SYK）物理，这是开放多体系统中量子混沌（quantum chaos）的一个典型设定。通过单模腔中的原子自发辐射和多模腔中的光子泄漏两条互补途径，系统最终都收敛到相同的非厄米随机矩阵普适类（non-Hermitian random-matrix universality），并在单原子分辨密度上留下长寿命预热记忆向快速热化转变的动力学指纹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SYK模型是研究开放多体系统量子混沌的典型框架，但如何在物理器件中实现耗散型SYK物理仍缺乏明确方案。作者希望通过腔QED构建可实现这一目标的具体平台，并探究不同封闭系统极限（可积与混沌）在引入耗散后是否收敛到相同的普适行为。

Method: 考虑超冷费米子（ultracold fermions）在腔中具有无序、全对全（all-to-all）的腔介导相互作用，提出两种实现耗散型SYK物理的互补途径：单模腔中的原子自发辐射（atomic spontaneous emission）与多模腔中的光子泄漏（photon leakage）。通过调节Lamb-Dicke参数和腔模间距分别控制两种情形下的耗散秩（dissipative rank）增长。

Result: 两种实现路径尽管分别起源于可积和混沌的封闭系统极限，却收敛到相同的非厄米随机矩阵普适类。在单模腔情形下，耗散本身可从可积哈密顿量中创造出量子混沌。该混沌在单原子分辨密度中留下可观测的动力学指纹，表现为从长寿命预热记忆（prethermal memory）到快速热化的交叉转变。

Conclusion: 腔QED器件为实验实现耗散型SYK物理提供了切实可行的物理平台，且揭示了不同封闭系统起源在耗散作用下向统一普适类收敛的深层机制。通过可调的耗散秩增长控制混沌涌现，为量子混沌和开放多体系统的实验研究开辟了新途径。

Abstract: We show that cavity quantum electrodynamics (QED) devices can realize dissipative Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev (SYK) physics, a paradigmatic setting for quantum chaos in open many-body systems. Ultracold fermions with disordered, all-to-all cavity-mediated interactions provide two complementary routes: atomic spontaneous emission in a single-mode cavity and photon leakage from a multimode cavity. Strikingly, both converge to the same non-Hermitian random-matrix universality despite originating from integrable and chaotic closed-system limits, respectively. In the single-mode case, dissipation therefore creates quantum chaos from an integrable Hamiltonian. We trace this convergence to a tunable growth in dissipative rank, controlled, respectively, by the Lamb-Dicke parameter and the cavity-mode spacing. The resulting chaos leaves a dynamical fingerprint: a crossover from long-lived prethermal memory to rapid thermalization, visible in single-atom-resolved densities.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.supr-con'></div>

# cond-mat.supr-con [[Back]](#toc)

### [211] [Electrostatic lens of a charged vortex](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21490)
*Iogann Tolbatov,Luca Salasnich*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文研究了圆柱对称涡旋存在下带电超流体（charged superfluid）的局域静电响应，推导了标量势近轴展开式的常数、二次和四次项系数，并将四次项中的状态方程贡献分离出来，建立了涡旋芯热力学与局域静电轮廓之间的解析联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带电超流体中涡旋存在时的局域静电响应问题此前缺乏系统分析，尤其是当粒子密度和热力学压缩率随径向坐标变化时，需要建立更一般性的理论框架。

Method: 从仅含相位项的Popov作用量（phase-only Popov action）出发获得静电电荷响应，引入局域密度延伸（local-density extension），并推导标量势的规则近轴展开式（regular near-axis expansion）；常数项、二次项和四次项系数分别用局域屏蔽响应（local screening response）和涡旋密度轮廓的前导非均匀贡献显式表达。

Result: 对于三维幺正费米状态方程（unitary Fermi equation of state），压缩率的密度依赖性在四次项系数中产生确定的热力学贡献；分解式将该状态方程贡献与同阶出现的常数屏蔽项和电荷源项分离，得到紧凑的解析表达式。

Conclusion: 该结果提供了涡旋芯热力学（vortex-core thermodynamics）与带电超流体局域静电轮廓之间的紧凑解析联系，为理解涡旋芯附近的静电响应提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We study the local electrostatic response of a charged superfluid in the presence of a cylindrically symmetric vortex, allowing both the particle density and the thermodynamic compressibility to vary with the radial coordinate. Starting from the electrostatic charge response obtained from the phase-only Popov action, we introduce a local-density extension and derive the regular near-axis expansion of the scalar potential. The constant, quadratic, and quartic coefficients are obtained explicitly in terms of the local screening response and of the leading nonuniform contribution to the vortex density profile. For a three-dimensional unitary Fermi equation of state, the density dependence of the compressibility generates a definite thermodynamic contribution to the quartic coefficient. The resulting decomposition separates this equation-of-state contribution from the ordinary constant-screening and charge-source terms that occur at the same order. This provides a compact analytical connection between vortex-core thermodynamics and the local electrostatic profile of a charged superfluid.

</details>


### [212] [Loop currents in Haldane's model and in time-reversal-breaking superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22083)
*Azzam Alzahrani,Victor M. Yakovenko*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文对Haldane模型和时间反演对称破缺超导体中的回路电流（loop currents）光学表现进行了理论研究。在Haldane模型中，将回路电流期望值与频率依赖的交流霍尔电导（ac Hall conductivity）光学光谱权重相关联；在手征超导体中，揭示了与Dirac带间激发相关的锐利光学吸收峰，其频率为Fermi能级与Dirac点能量差的两倍，且光学光谱权重直接反映超导配对诱导的回路电流强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回路电流是时间反演对称破缺系统中重要的量子现象，但缺乏直接的实验探测手段。本文旨在建立回路电流与可测量的光学响应（尤其是交流霍尔电导）之间的定量联系，为实验探测回路电流的存在与大小提供理论依据。

Method: 对Haldane模型，依据模型参数计算回路电流的期望值，并将其与频率依赖的交流霍尔电导的光学光谱权重（integrated optical spectral weight）建立解析关系。在手征超导体（chiral superconductor）方面，基于Brydon等人（2019）的蜂巢格子模型进行分析，通过交流霍尔电导的光学响应研究由时间反演对称破缺超导激活的Dirac带激发及其对应的吸收峰特征。

Result: 在Haldane模型中，回路电流的期望值与交流霍尔电导的积分光谱权重直接关联，使后者可作为回路电流存在与否及大小的探测指标。在手征超导体中，交流霍尔电导中出现一个锐利的光学吸收峰，源于下、上Dirac带间的激发（由时间反演对称破缺超导激活），该峰频率为Fermi能级与Dirac点能量差的两倍，其光学光谱权重与手征超导配对在元胞内诱导的回路电流幅度成正比。

Conclusion: 两种系统中均建立了回路电流与光学可观测量的定量对应关系，表明交流霍尔电导的光学测量可作为探测时间反演对称破缺系统中稳态回路电流的有效实验手段，为这一现象的后续实验验证提供了理论框架。

Abstract: We present a theoretical study of optical manifestations of loop currents in Haldane's model and in time-reversal-breaking superconductors. For Haldane's model, we calculate the expectation value of loop currents in terms of model parameters and relate it with the integrated optical spectral weight for the frequency-dependent ac Hall conductivity. Thus, experimental measurements of the latter can provide information about the presence and magnitude of steady loop currents in the system. Then we elaborate on loop currents in a chiral superconductor on the honeycomb lattice, studied earlier by Brydon et al. (2019). We demonstrate that a sharp optical absorption peak in the ac Hall conductivity originates from excitations between the lower and upper Dirac bands, activated by the time-reversal-breaking superconductivity. The frequency of the peak is twice the energy difference between the Fermi level and the Dirac point. The optical spectral weight of the peak is directly related to the magnitude of loop currents induced in the unit cells by the chiral superconducting pairing, in similarity to Haldane's model.

</details>


### [213] [Structural disorder and critical voltage scaling in Al/AlOx/Al Josephson junction arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22654)
*Amanuel M. Berhane,Susan Coppersmith,Emma Mitchell,Timothy Duty*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文实验研究了一维铝基约瑟夫森结（Josephson junction, JJ）阵列中，制造引入的结构缺陷对临界电压标度行为的影响，发现铝蒸发速率改变引起的形貌和电阻变化不改变标度行为，而纳米间隙和氧化处理则会系统性地改变标度系数，为量子相滑（QPS）器件设计提供了指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一维约瑟夫森结阵列的绝缘态由集体电荷动力学和无序诱导的钉扎效应主导，在直流偏置下产生有限的临界电压。理解制造过程中的结构缺陷如何影响这一标度行为，对量子相滑（Quantum Phase Slip, QPS）器件的设计至关重要。

Method: 通过可控改变铝蒸发速率引起颗粒形貌和室温结电阻的显著变化，并人为引入纳米间隙及原位氧化处理，系统对比各类制造缺陷对归一化临界电压标度行为的影响。

Result: 铝蒸发速率变化虽带来显著的颗粒形貌与结电阻改变，但归一化临界电压标度行为保持不变；而纳米间隙的引入和原位后氧化处理则会系统性地改变标度系数，但保持标度定律的函数形式不变。

Conclusion: 集体输运行为对特定类型的制造缺陷敏感，确立了哪些制造缺陷影响Al/AlOx/Al约瑟夫森结阵列的集体输运，为未来量子相滑器件的制造提供了实践指导。

Abstract: The insulating state of one-dimensional Josephson junction (JJ) arrays is governed by collective charge dynamics and disorder-induced pinning, resulting in a finite critical voltage under dc bias. Here, we investigate the influence of fabrication-induced structural defects on the critical-voltage scaling of small-capacitance Aluminium-Aluminium oxide-Aluminium (Al/AlOx/Al) JJ arrays. Controlled variation of the aluminium evaporation rate produces pronounced changes in grain morphology and room-temperature junction resistance. Despite these substantial structural modifications, the normalised critical-voltage scaling is preserved, demonstrating that the collective transport behaviour is remarkably robust against this class of fabrication-induced defects. In contrast, the deliberate introduction of nanoscale gaps into the junctions introduces additional junction-to-junction structural variations that systematically modify the normalised scaling behaviour. Likewise, in situ postfabrication oxidation alters the scaling coefficient while preserving the functional form of the scaling law, indicating that the collective transport is sensitive to specific classes of structural modifications. These results establish which fabrication-induced structural defects influence the collective transport in insulating Al/AlOx/Al Josephson junction arrays, providing new insight into the role of fabrication-induced structural disorder and practical guidance for the design of future Quantum Phase Slip (QPS) devices.

</details>


### [214] [Weak coupling theory of nickel-based 327 superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22759)
*Miao Jianjian,Chen Weiqiang*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了弱耦合（weak-coupling）理论方法在理解加压镍酸盐双层体系 La₃Ni₂O₇ 涌现超导电性方面的应用，重点回顾了随机相位近似（RPA）、涨落交换近似（FLEX）和泛函重整化群（FRG）三种理论框架，并分析了基于双轨道 Huygens 模型的配对相互作用与失稳行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: La₃Ni₂O₇ 在压力下被发现具有高温超导电性，成为继铜氧化物和铁基超导体之后非常规超导领域的新范型平台，亟需系统的理论综述来梳理弱耦合方法在该体系中的应用与进展。

Method: 论文采用综述形式，聚焦三种弱耦合理论框架：随机相位近似（RPA）、涨落交换近似（FLEX）和泛函重整化群（FRG），并以包含 Ni 的 dₓ²₋y² 和 d₃z²₋r² 轨道的双层双轨道 Hubbard 模型作为低能电子结构的常用建模工具，分析有效配对相互作用和涌现不稳定性。

Result: 该综述识别出三种理论方法各有侧重，共同用于揭示 La₃Ni₂O₇ 在压力下的配对机制与超导失稳条件，但摘要本身未报告具体数值结果或不同方法之间的对比结论。

Conclusion: 弱耦合理论方法为理解 La₃Ni₂O₇ 体系中涌现的超导配对提供了统一而互补的分析框架，该材料与铜氧化物、铁基超导体一起构成了非常规超导研究的重要理论试验场。

Abstract: This review provides a comprehensive survey of weak-coupling theoretical approaches applied to understand the emergent superconductivity in the pressurized nickelate bilayer system $La_{3}Ni_{2}O_{7}$. Following the landmark discovery of its high-Tc superconductivity under pressure, this material has rapidly become a new paradigmatic platform in the field of unconventional superconductivity, joining cuprates and iron-based systems. We focus on three pivotal theoretical frameworks: the random phase approximation (RPA), the fluctuation-exchange approximation (FLEX), and the functional renormalization group (FRG). These methods are deployed to analyze the effective pairing interactions and emergent instabilities arising from the low-energy electronic structure, which is commonly modeled by a bilayer two-orbital Hubbard Hamiltonian incorporating the Ni $d_{x^2-y^2}$ and $d_{3z^2-r^2}$ orbitals.

</details>


### [215] [Revised symmetry rule and intrinsically time-reversal symmetry breaking pairing in multi-orbital superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22902)
*Chang-Youn Moon*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该研究通过数值求解Sr₂RuO₄的线性化Eliashberg方程，发现多带超导配对态中电子置换（particle permutation）的基本对称性规则并非简单的正负号变换，即配对间隙函数在轨道（$\hat{O}$）或频率（$\hat{T}$）交换下并不单纯变为自身或负号。新发现的对称性规则允许线性化间隙方程出现复本征值，从而天然破缺时间反演对称性（time-reversal symmetry），并可能在Sr₂RuO₄中实现这种独特的配对态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前学术界普遍假设多带、含频超导（SC）配对间隙函数在配对电子间的轨道或频率交换下仅变换为自身或其负号，但该假设从未经过验证。作者旨在检验并修正这一基本对称性规则，以更准确地理解和解释多带超导态。

Method: 作者对Sr₂RuO₄求解线性化Eliashberg方程的数值解，系统考察了多带、频率依赖的间隙函数在轨道（$\hat{O}$）和频率（$\hat{T}$）交换操作下的变换行为，并分析了配对相互作用在这些操作下的不变性。

Result: 研究发现间隙函数在$\hat{O}$或$\hat{T}$交换下并不简单地变换为自身或其负号，原因在于配对相互作用在此类操作下并非不变。新规则允许线性化间隙方程出现复本征值，相应间隙函数在实频域中实部与虚部均非零，从本质上破缺时间反演对称性。数值结果表明Sr₂RuO₄可能具备这种奇特的配对态，其中材料的Hund耦合特性起关键作用。

Conclusion: 修正后的对称性规则不仅为已知超导态提供了更全面的理解，也为新型奇异超导态的可能性打开了大门。

Abstract: We investigate the basic symmetry rule for the particle permutation in superconducting (SC) pairing states by examining the numerical solution of the linearized Eliashberg equation for Sr$_2$RuO$_4$. We find that the general multi-band, frequency-dependent SC gap function does not simply transform to itself up to the minus sign with either orbital ($\hat{O}$) or frequency ($\hat{T}$) exchange between two pairing electrons, contradicting the common assumption which has been used without verification. It originates from the fact that paring interactions are not invariant under the $\hat{O}$ or $\hat{T}$ operation, and is demonstrated to be essential to correctly interpret and understand multi-band SC states. One of unique properties implied by our newly found symmetry rule is the possibility of the complex eigenvalue in the linearized gap equation where the corresponding gap functions $Δ$ always have real and imaginary components both non-zero in real frequencies, inherently breaking the time-reversal symmetry. Our numerical results suggest the possibility of this unique pairing in a real material, Sr$_2$RuO$_4$, for which the Hundness of the material is found to play a key role. The revised symmetry rule not only leads us to more comprehensive understanding of the known SC states, but also opens new possibilities into exotic and unique states.

</details>


### [216] [Three-dimensional Ising superconductors designed via inversion-symmetry breaking in intercalated NbSe$_2$ and NbTe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23083)
*Wenqian Tu,Run Lv,Xiaoying Li,Li'e Liu,Dingfu Shao,Yuping Sun,Wenjian Lu*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文通过插层（intercalation）策略系统设计了16种基于NbSe₂和NbTe₂的化合物，在非中心对称P̄6m2相中识别出4种有前景的三维伊辛超导体（3D Ising superconductors），其面内上临界场可达Pauli极限的4-7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
