Motivation: 二维单层过渡金属二硫化物（TMDs）中已确立伊辛超导的面内上临界场远超Pauli顺磁极限的特性，但三维体材料因平衡堆垛通常恢复反演对称性而罕有实现。作者旨在探索一种实用的对称性破缺工程策略，以在三维TMD材料中实现伊辛超导。

Method: 作者从四种插层剂（In、Sn、Pb、Bi）系统设计了16种NbSe₂基和NbTe₂基化合物，涵盖非中心对称P̄6m2和中心对称P6₃/mmc两种多型结构。通过第一性原理计算分析自旋轨道耦合（SOC）劈裂、自旋纹理（spin texture）及超导性质，并采用Bogoliubov-de Gennes（BdG）方程预测面内上临界场。

Result: P̄6m2相中的InNbSe₂、SnNbSe₂、PbNbSe₂和PbNbTe₂四种化合物被识别为有前景的三维伊辛超导体，具有80-100 meV的SOC劈裂、主导的面外自旋极化及各向异性超导性（T_c=2.6-5.4 K）。自旋纹理分析表明，伊辛保护的效率取决于费米面上的面外自旋纯度而非SOC劈裂大小。BdG计算预测面内上临界场达到Pauli极限的4-7倍。

Conclusion: 插层被确立为一种有前景的对称性工程策略，可用于在TMD材料中实现三维伊辛超导体。

Abstract: Ising superconductors exhibit in-plane upper critical fields far exceeding the Pauli paramagnetic limit, a hallmark first established in two-dimensional (2D) monolayer transition-metal dichalcogenides (TMDs). This field resilience requires the coexistence of strong spin-orbit coupling (SOC) and broken inversion symmetry, yet three-dimensional (3D) bulk realizations remain scarce because equilibrium stacking typically restores inversion symmetry. Here we demonstrate that intercalation provides a practical route to break this symmetry, systematically designing 16 NbSe$_2$- and NbTe$_2$-based compounds from four intercalants (In, Sn, Pb, Bi) across two polytypes: non-centrosymmetric $P\bar{6}m2$ and centrosymmetric $P6_3/mmc$. Four compounds in the $P\bar{6}m2$ phase, InNbSe$_2$, SnNbSe$_2$, PbNbSe$_2$, and PbNbTe$_2$, emerge as promising 3D Ising superconductors. They exhibit SOC splittings of 80-100 meV near the Fermi level, dominant out-of-plane spin polarization, and anisotropic superconductivity with $T_c=2.6$-$5.4$ K. Notably, spin-texture analysis reveals that the efficiency of Ising protection is governed not by the magnitude of SOC splitting alone but by the out-of-plane spin purity on the Fermi surface. Bogoliubov-de Gennes (BdG) calculations predict in-plane upper critical fields reaching 4-7 times the Pauli limit. These findings establish intercalation as a promising symmetry-engineering strategy for realizing 3D Ising superconductors in TMDs.

</details>


### [217] [Competing Extended-$s$- and $d$-Wave Pairing from Distinct Spin-Fluctuation Channels in Stoichiometric $\mathrm{FeTe}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23467)
*Yichen Hua,Wen-lin Yang,Jian-Jian Miao,Hu Xu,Changming Yue*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该论文通过第一性原理构建五轨道紧束缚模型，结合涨落交换近似（fluctuation-exchange approximation），研究化学计量比FeTe中超导配对的机制。线性化Eliashberg方程显示扩展s波与d波两种自旋单重态配对不稳定性，随填充和相互作用强度变化，扩展s波在化学计量填充下占优，电子掺杂则使d波成为主导不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学计量比FeTe中最近观察到的超导现象提出了一个问题：一旦间隙Fe被移除，配对如何在四方11型硫族化物中形成。理解这一配对机制对于解释该材料中的超导性至关重要。

Method: 从第一性原理计算构建实验约束的五轨道紧束缚模型（tight-binding model），在涨落交换近似框架内处理电子关联和配对，并通过求解线性化Eliashberg方程（linearized Eliashberg equation）分析配对不稳定性。

Result: 近化学计量比填充下，展开的一Fe布里渊区中(π,0)和(0,π)附近的自旋涨落连接Γ/M空穴口袋与X/Y电子口袋，有利于在空穴和电子片之间变号的扩展s波能隙。电子掺杂后空穴口袋耗尽，主导散射转向(π,π)附近的X–Y通道，使d_{x^2-y^2}波成为主导不稳定性，其节线避开大部分费米面。两种配对通道的相对强度随填充和相互作用强度变化；在匹配条件下扩展s波和d波特征值在FeTe中均大于FeSe。

Conclusion: 该研究给出了化学计量比FeTe的具体能隙结构，为光谱测量提供了可对比的理论预测。配对对称性依赖于填充和相互作用强度，扩展s波与d波之间的竞争由主导自旋涨落通道的改变所调控。

Abstract: The recent observation of superconductivity in stoichiometric $\mathrm{FeTe}$ raises the question of how pairing develops in this tetragonal 11-type chalcogenide once interstitial Fe is removed. We construct an experimentally constrained five-orbital tight-binding model from first-principles calculations and treat electronic correlations and pairing within the fluctuation-exchange approximation. The linearized Eliashberg equation yields competing extended-$s$- and $d_{x^2-y^2}$-wave spin-singlet pairing instabilities. Near stoichiometric filling, spin fluctuations near $(π,0)$ and $(0,π)$ in the unfolded one-Fe Brillouin zone connect the $Γ/M$ hole pockets with the $X/Y$ electron pockets and favor an extended-$s$ gap that changes sign between the hole and electron sheets. Upon electron doping, depletion of the hole pockets shifts the dominant scattering toward the $X$--$Y$ channel near $(π,π)$, making the $d_{x^2-y^2}$-wave state the leading instability, with nodal lines that avoid most of the Fermi surface. The relative strengths of the two pairing channels vary with filling and interaction strength as the dominant spin-fluctuation channel changes. Under matched interaction strength, temperature, and filling, the extended-$s$ eigenvalue is larger in $\mathrm{FeTe}$ than in $\mathrm{FeSe}$ throughout the range considered, while the $d_{x^2-y^2}$-wave eigenvalue is also generally larger, particularly under electron doping. These results give concrete gap structures against which spectroscopic measurements of stoichiometric $\mathrm{FeTe}$ can be compared.

</details>


<div id='gr-qc'></div>

### [218] [Testing f(R) gravity using gravitational-wave signals from binary mergers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21432)
*M. D. C. Torri*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究修正引力理论（modified gravity theories）中的f(R)引力理论，检验其对双致密天体并合过程引力波辐射的预言，并评估下一代引力波探测器（next-generation gravitational-wave detectors）对这些广义相对论扩展理论的检验能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来多项研究对广义相对论（General Relativity）预言的正确性进行检验，而引力波（gravitational waves）为在强场（strong-field）区域测试该理论提供了理想的探针。

Method: 本文考虑一类修正引力理论，即f(R)理论，并深入审视其对两个天体物理致密天体（compact objects）并合过程中的引力波辐射的预言。同时评估下一代引力波探测器对此类广义相对论扩展理论的检验能力。

Result: 研究给出了f(R)引力理论对双致密天体并合引力波辐射的预言结果，并评估了下一代探测器对该类理论的检验能力。

Conclusion: f(R)修正引力理论是广义相对论的有力替代候选，引力波观测结合下一代探测器有望为检验这些理论在强场区的有效性提供重要约束。

Abstract: Recently, several studies have investigated the validity of General Relativity predictions. Gravitational waves provide an ideal probe for testing the theory in the strong-field regime. In this work, we consider a class of modified-grevity theories, specifically f(R), and scrutinize their predictions for the gravitational-wave emission from the coalescence of two astrophysical compact objects. We also assess the impact of next-generation gravitational-wave detectors on the ability of test these estensions of General Relativity.

</details>


### [219] [Chiral symmetry and black hole isospectrality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21532)
*Colin Weller,Dongjun Li,Pratik Wagle,Andrew Laeuger,Yanbei Chen,Nicolás Yunes*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文证明：只要辐射度规（radiative metric）与物质微扰（matter perturbations）是由满足封闭、复线性系统及复线性边界条件的复主变量（complex master variables）重建而来，则线性无关解之间必然存在黑洞等谱性（black-hole isospectrality）。若相位旋转（phase rotation）将一个宇称扇区映射到另一个，则偶宇称与奇宇称的准正则模（quasinormal mode, QNM）谱重合，即宇称等谱。一大类手性对齐（chiral-aligned）理论满足这些判据。作为应用，本文证明次极端Kerr–de Sitter黑洞具有宇称等谱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞准正则模谱是引力波观测与基础物理检验的重要工具，但宇称扇区之间等谱性的成立条件此前缺乏一般性证明。作者旨在建立宇称等谱性成立的一般判据，并将其推广到更广的理论类别。

Method: 作者从复主变量出发，将辐射度规与物质微扰的重建过程形式化，要求微扰方程构成封闭的复线性系统并配以复线性边界条件。在此框架下，分析相位旋转对宇称扇区的作用，证明等谱成立的充分条件，并将其应用于手性对齐理论类别。

Result: 证明了复线性系统框架下宇称等谱性的一般性结论；识别出一大类满足判据的手性对齐理论；并具体验证了次极端Kerr–de Sitter黑洞的宇称等谱性。

Conclusion: 宇称等谱性并非特定理论的偶然性质，而是可由一般数学结构保证的系统性结果，手性对齐理论为此提供了广泛适用场景，Kerr–de Sitter黑洞作为具体实例支持了该结论。

Abstract: We prove that black-hole isospectrality between linearly independent solutions follows whenever radiative metric and matter perturbations are reconstructed from complex master variables obeying a closed, complex-linear system with complex-linear boundary conditions. If a phase rotation maps one parity sector to the other, then the even- and odd-parity QNM spectra coincide, and the perturbations are parity isospectral. A broad class of chiral-aligned theories satisfies these criteria. As an application, we demonstrate that subextremal Kerr--de Sitter black holes are parity isospectral.

</details>


### [220] [Electrically Charged Non-Abelian Black String in Anti-de Sitter Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21538)
*G. Alencar,R. N. Costa Filho,I. Jardim,Celio R. Muniz*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在四维爱因斯坦-杨-米尔斯理论（Einstein-Yang-Mills theory）中构造了一族新的静态、带电、柱对称黑弦（black string）解，由真正的非阿贝尔SU(2)涡旋场支撑。由于电场与涡旋场共存，标准单函数Lemos度量与爱因斯坦方程不一致，需要三函数度量。文中给出了完整的数值解，恢复了两类已知极限解，并发现非阿贝尔毛发（hair）在线性阶通过交换子场强出现，其背景反应在二次阶出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非阿贝尔规范场与引力相互作用下的新型黑洞解（black hole solution），特别是带有真正非阿贝尔毛发的黑弦解。此前类似的柱对称解要么是纯电学的（Lemos解），要么仅含阿贝尔规范场（Lemos-Zanchin解），缺乏由非阿贝尔涡旋场支撑的完整解。

Method: 构造三函数度量以与各向异性的杨-米尔斯应力张量相容；通过近眼界（near-horizon）与渐近AdS区域的正则展开提供数值积分边界数据；对嵌入的Lemos-Zanchin解进行微扰分析，分离非阿贝尔贡献的阶次；基于视界方程解析推导非退化（non-degeneracy）界限；并通过数值积分获得完全背景反应的对偶解。

Result: 恢复两个极限解：零电场视界数据对应中性Lemos黑弦（杨-米尔斯场强为零），关闭涡旋分量则回到嵌入的U(1)⊂SU(2)带电Lemos-Zanchin解。微扰分析表明非阿贝尔毛发在线性阶经交换子场强出现，其对电学剖面的背景反应始于二次阶。解析地获得局部非退化界限R_1^crit=s_0α√(3/(4πG))。渐近几何具有可吸收为有效阿贝尔荷的二次修正和给出非阿贝尔毛发首个不可约渐近特征的三次系数。熵满足贝肯斯坦-霍金面积律，温度差来自渐近lapse归一化。

Conclusion: 完全背景反应后的数值解构成该横向非阿贝尔形变的非线性实现。结果证明非阿贝尔毛发可以贯穿黑洞视界并以代数量化方式标记在渐近几何上，同时保持标准面积律熵。

Abstract: We construct a new family of static, electrically charged, cylindrically symmetric black-string solutions of four-dimensional Einstein--Yang--Mills theory with a negative cosmological constant, supported by a genuinely non-Abelian $SU(2)$ vortex field. The coexistence of electric and vortex sectors renders the standard single-function Lemos metric inconsistent with the Einstein equations, requiring a three-function metric compatible with the anisotropic Yang--Mills stress tensor. Regular near-horizon and asymptotic AdS expansions provide boundary data for numerical integration of the complete field equations. Two limiting sectors are recovered: vanishing electric horizon datum yields the neutral Lemos black string with zero Yang--Mills field strength, while switching off the vortex component gives the embedded $U(1)\subset SU(2)$ charged Lemos--Zanchin solution. Perturbations about the latter show that genuinely non-Abelian hair appears at linear order through the commutator field strength, whereas its backreaction on the electric profile and geometry begins at quadratic order. The horizon equations yield the analytic local non-degeneracy bound $R_1^{\rm crit}=s_0α\sqrt{3/(4πG)}$. The asymptotic geometry exhibits a quadratic correction absorbable into an effective Abelian charge and a cubic coefficient giving the first irreducible asymptotic signature of the non-Abelian hair. The entropy obeys the Bekenstein--Hawking area law, while the temperature difference from the embedded Abelian solution arises through the asymptotic lapse normalization. The fully backreacted numerical solutions are nonlinear realizations of this transverse non-Abelian deformation.

</details>


### [221] [Study of Dynamical Instability of Collapsing Charged Spherically Symmetric Anisotropic Matter Configurations within Non-Minimally Coupled Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21546)
*A. Rehman,M. Yousaf,Mohammed Zakarya,M. Aslam,Maram Ali*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了$f(R,\mathcal{L}_{m})$引力框架下带电、球对称、各向异性物质构型的动力学不稳定性和引力坍缩问题，通过微扰方案推导了牛顿和后牛顿近似的失稳条件，发现非最小物质-几何耦合产生的暗源项可以增强带电流体的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非最小耦合修正引力理论中的致密天体稳定性，综合考察各向异性压、电荷和修正引力源项对坍缩条件的影响。

Method: 采用微扰方案，使用绝热指数将静态与微扰变量关联，推导修正后的流体静力学平衡方程和坍缩方程，并在牛顿与后牛顿近似下获得失稳约束条件。

Result: 系统稳定性由内向引力吸引与外向压力支撑之间的竞争决定；非最小物质-几何耦合产生的暗源项可修改坍缩条件并增强带电流体的稳定性。

Conclusion: 该分析为理解非最小耦合引力中密集自引力致密天体的演化与坍缩提供了有用框架。

Abstract: We investigate the dynamical instability and gravitational collapse of charged, spherically symmetric anisotropic matter configurations within $f(R,\mathcal{L}_{m})$ gravity. The analysis focuses on the combined effects of anisotropic pressure, perturbations, electric charge, and modified-gravity source terms on the stability of compact objects. A specific equation of state is adopted to relate the static and perturbed variables through the adiabatic index. Using a perturbation scheme, we derive the modified hydrostatic equilibrium and collapse equations and obtain instability constraints in both Newtonian and post-Newtonian regimes. The results show that the stability of the system is governed by the competition between inward gravitational attraction and outward pressure support. In addition, the dark source terms generated by the non-minimal matter-geometry coupling modify the collapse conditions and can enhance the stability of the charged fluid. These findings provide a useful framework for understanding the evolution and collapse of dense self-gravitating compact objects in non-minimally coupled gravity.

</details>


### [222] [Black hole singularity is a surface not a point](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21590)
*Andrew J. S. Hamilton,Tyler McMaken*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文指出流行文献中"黑洞中心奇点是一个点"的说法是错误的。在广义相对论中，落入球对称黑洞的两条不同角向轨迹的观测者早在远离中心处就失去因果联系，因此奇点是一个曲面（surface）而非点。对于旋转黑洞，奇点几乎必然位于内视界（inner horizon），任何微小的经典或量子扰动都会触发指数质量膨胀不稳定性（mass inflation instability），导致坍缩为类空奇异曲面。文章进一步论证：无论终极量子引力理论为何，黑洞的量子态可能位于其本质上二维的奇异曲面上，并与事件视界内捕获的霍金辐射（Hawking radiation）热大气在热力学平衡中共演化且保持幺正性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对大众文献中广泛流传的"黑洞中心奇性是一个点"的错误认知进行纠正，并探讨奇点结构对量子引力理论的深层意涵。

Method: 基于广义相对论中自由下落观测者的世界线分析，论证球对称黑洞中不同角向轨迹的观测者在远离中心处即失去因果接触；对旋转黑洞情形，结合内视界处经典及量子扰动引发的指数质量膨胀不稳定性进行讨论，并由此推导量子引力的推论。

Result: 球对称黑洞的奇点是一个曲面而非点；旋转黑洞的奇异曲面几乎确定地位于内视界，微小扰动即可触发指数质量膨胀不稳定性并导致坍缩为类空奇异曲面。

Conclusion: 黑洞的量子态可能驻留于其本质上二维的奇异曲面，该曲面与事件视界内捕获的霍金辐射热大气处于热力学平衡，并以幺正方式共同演化——这一图景对任何终极量子引力理论均适用。

Abstract: It is widely repeated in the popular literature and elsewhere that the singularity at the center of a black hole is a point. It is not true. Two observers who free-fall into a spherical black hole along two different angular trajectories at the same time $t$ do not encounter each other at the central singularity; rather, they lose causal contact with each other already well away from the singularity. Counterintuitively, in general relativity two points can be spatially close yet causally distant. The singularity is a surface, not a point. The story for rotating black holes is more complicated, but the same conclusion holds. For a rotating black hole, the singular surface almost certainly resides at its inner horizon, where even the tiniest classical or quantum perturbations ignite the exponential mass inflation instability, precipitating collapse to a spacelike singular surface. There are implications for quantum gravity. We argue that, whatever the ultimate theory of quantum gravity may be, the quantum states of a black hole probably reside at its effectively 2-dimensional singular surface, which coevolves unitarily with, and in thermodynamic equilibrium with, the hot atmosphere of trapped Hawking radiation that the black hole generates within its event horizon.

</details>


### [223] [Dual entropy gain by radiation at the zero temperature limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21593)
*Koray Düztaş*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文在零温度极限下分析黑洞辐射过程中的熵变化，验证广义第二定律（GSL）的有效性。研究发现Kerr黑洞在零温度极限下，事件视界面积变化正定，同时外部区域冯·诺依曼熵对玻色和费米模式均增加，这种双重熵增益确保了GSL的稳健性，并同样适用于熵与视界面积不成正比且不解析可积的Kerr-Taub-NUT黑洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究表明黑洞辐射可以平滑地延伸到零温极限，本文旨在检查该极限下熵的变化，并评估广义第二定律是否仍然成立。通常验证GSL的方式是外部区域熵增益补偿黑洞蒸发导致的熵损失，但在零温极限下可能出现反常特征。

Method: 本文在零温度极限下分析Kerr黑洞辐射的熵变化。关键条件是发射被限制在模式ω<mΩ的范围内，这导致Kerr黑洞视界面积的变化恒为正定。同时考察外部区域关于玻色模式与费米模式的冯·诺依曼熵变化，并进一步将分析推广到Kerr-Taub-NUT黑洞。

Result: 在零温度极限下，Kerr黑洞视界面积变化为正定，外部区域的冯·诺依曼熵对玻色和费米模式均增加，形成双重熵增益。该双重增加也适用于Kerr-Taub-NUT黑洞，尽管其熵既不与视界面积成正比，也不解析可积。

Conclusion: 零温度极限下的双重熵增益标志着广义第二定律的稳健守恒，不仅适用于Kerr黑洞，也推广至结构更为复杂的Kerr-Taub-NUT黑洞。

Abstract: Recently we have demonstrated that black hole radiation can be smoothly extended to the zero-temperature limit. Here we analyse entropy variations at the zero temperature limit and evaluate the validity of the Generalized Second Law (GSL). Typically one verifies that the entropy gain in the exterior region compensates for the entropy loss of the black hole due to evaporation. However an anomalous feature emerges at the zero temperature limit. The fact that the emission is restricted to the modes $ω<mΩ$, renders the variation of the horizon area of a Kerr black hole positive definite. Simultaneously the von Neumann entropy of the exterior region increases both for bosonic and fermionic modes. The dual entropy gain at the zero temperature limit marks a robust conservation of the GSL. We demonstrate that the dual increase also applies to Kerr-Taub-NUT black holes whose entropy is neither directly proportional to the horizon area nor analytically integrable.

</details>


### [224] [Beyond de Sitter: Longitudinal-Mode Instabilities and Spectral Evolution of Massive Vector Fields with Non-Minimal Curvature Coupling during Inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21623)
*Ehsan Momeni,Fatimah Shojai*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究带有非最小曲率耦合的旁观者（spectator）大质量矢量场在德西特（de Sitter, dS）与准德西特（quasi-de Sitter, qdS）背景下的暴胀动力学，计算谱能量密度并量化精确dS近似与慢滚修正qdS演化之间的偏差，发现耦合参数xi对有效质量演化、质量穿越条件和纵向模行为有显著影响，并追踪模式进入辐射主导时期的后续演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对暴胀期间矢量场涨落的研究主要依赖于精确dS近似，但真实暴胀是准德西特背景，慢滚修正可能显著影响矢量场的涨落谱；同时非最小曲率耦合对矢量场动力学的影响尚需精确量化，这关系到后续辐射时代谱能量密度的预言。本文旨在系统评估dS近似的适用范围并确定慢滚修正何时变得重要。

Method: 在dS和qdS背景下，以Bunch-Davies初条件求解横向与纵向模的模方程（mode equations），计算谱能量密度（spectral energy densities），并比较精确dS近似与慢滚修正qdS演化之间的偏差。研究中追踪暴胀模式进入辐射主导时期的后续演化，以考察暴胀初条件如何决定后续谱能量密度。

Result: 耦合参数xi显著影响矢量场有效质量的演化、质量穿越条件（mass-crossing condition）以及纵向模的行为。增大xi会抑制矢量涨落并减小瞬态快子效应（transient tachyonic effects）。研究识别出dS近似能够提供准确描述的参数区间，并确定了慢滚修正变得相关的条件。

Conclusion: 非最小曲率耦合参数xi在矢量场暴胀动力学中扮演关键角色，其取值决定了dS近似是否足够精确。研究明确划分了dS近似有效与需要慢滚修正的参数区间，为后续辐射时代谱能量密度的准确预言提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We investigate the inflationary dynamics of a spectator massive vector field with a non-minimal curvature coupling in de Sitter (dS) and quasi-de Sitter (qdS) backgrounds, focusing on the transverse and longitudinal sectors. By solving the mode equations with Bunch-Davies initial conditions, we compute the spectral energy densities and quantify the deviations between the exact dS approximation and the slow-roll-corrected qdS evolution. We find that the coupling parameter xi significantly affects the evolution of the effective mass, the mass-crossing condition, and the behavior of the longitudinal mode. In particular, increasing xi suppresses the vector fluctuations and reduces transient tachyonic effects. We further follow the evolution of the modes into the radiation-dominated era, showing how the inflationary initial conditions determine the subsequent spectral energy densities. Our results identify the parameter regime in which the dS approximation provides an accurate description and determine when slow-roll corrections become relevant.

</details>


### [225] [Kerr-Newman and genuine Kerr black holes in the Bertotti-Robinson magnetic field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21672)
*Hryhorii Ovcharenko,Jiri Podolsky*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种描述外部磁场中旋转带电黑洞的精确时空解，该解是爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦方程组（Einstein-Maxwell equations）的精确解，形式简洁，可退化为标准Kerr-Newman度规和均匀Bertotti-Robinson磁宇宙，并分析了该族解的奇异性、视界、轴正则性、电荷、熵和温度等主要物理性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在构建一个能够统一描述黑洞在外部磁场中行为的新解族，并修正先前文献中Kerr-BR解的电荷限制，识别出真正的无电荷Kerr黑洞在磁场中的解。

Method: 通过构造爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦方程组的精确解，使新的时空解在极限情形下退化为已知的Kerr-Newman度规和Bertotti-Robinson磁宇宙，并在弱场极限下退化为Wald解，从而验证解的合理性。

Result: 作者证明了新解族在弱场极限下给出Wald解，识别了磁场中的无电荷Kerr黑洞，并分析了该族解的奇异性、视界、轴的规则性、电荷、熵和温度等物理性质。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个形式简洁且物理意义明确的精确解族，统一了旋转、电荷和外部磁场三种效应，为磁场中黑洞物理的深入研究提供了新的解析框架。

Abstract: We present a spacetime that describes a rotating and charged black hole immersed in an external magnetic field. This exact solution to the Einstein-Maxwell equations has a surprisingly simple form which reduces to standard Kerr-Newman metric, and to the uniform Bertotti-Robinson magnetic universe. We show that the Kerr-BR solution presented in [Phys. Rev. Lett. {\bf 135}, 181401 (2025)] has a special charge, and we identify a genuine Kerr black hole without any charge, in the magnetic field. In the weak-field limit it gives the Wald solution. We analyze main physical properties of the new family, namely singularities, horizons, regularity of axes, charges, entropy and temperature.

</details>


### [226] [Probing Variations of Newton's Constant in Strong Gravitational Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21758)
*T. D. Le*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 利用哈勃太空望远镜成像光谱仪（HST/STIS）获取白矮星G191-B2B的Ni V吸收线高分辨率紫外光谱，在强引力场（势能φ≈10⁴）下测得牛顿引力常数G的时间变化率为dG/G = (-0.014 ± 0.016) × 10⁻¹⁵ yr⁻¹，结果与常数G一致，为强场区域G的长期变化提供了严格约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 牛顿引力常数G的恒定性是广义相对论（general relativity）的基础预测，也是超越广义相对论的引力理论中的关键假设。在强引力场中高精度检验G的时间变化，有助于甄别和约束替代引力理论。

Method: 使用HST/STIS获取白矮星G191-B2B的Ni V吸收线高分辨率紫外光谱，将测得的谱线质心（line centroid）与实验室波长（laboratory wavelength）进行比较，并分离出引力红移（gravitational redshift）贡献，从而提取G的潜在时间变化信号。

Result: 在强场势能φ≈10⁴（对应吸收红移z_abs≈8.47×10⁻⁵）条件下，得到dG/G = (-0.014 ± 0.016) × 10⁻¹⁵ yr⁻¹。该结果与常数G一致，在强场区域对G的长期变化施加了严格约束。

Conclusion: 该测量证明了白矮星作为敏感探针（sensitive probe），可用于探测引力耦合（gravitational coupling）的时间变化，为未来在强引力场中检验引力常数演化提供了新途径。

Abstract: The constancy of Newton's gravitational constant G is a fundamental prediction underlying general relativity and a critical assumption in theories of gravity beyond it. We report a high-precision test of a possible temporal variation of G in a strong gravitational field, using high-resolution ultraviolet spectra of Ni V absorption lines in the white dwarf G191-B2B obtained with the Hubble Space Telescope Imaging Spectrograph (HST/STIS). By comparing the measured line centroids with laboratory wavelengths and isolating the gravitational-redshift contribution, we obtain dG/G = (-0.014 +/- 0.016) x 10^-15 yr^-1 at a strong-field potential phi approximately 10^4, corresponding to an absorption redshift z_abs approximately 8.47 x 10^-5. The result is consistent with a constant G and places a stringent constraint on its secular variation in the strong-field regime. Our measurement demonstrates the potential of white-dwarf stars as a sensitive probe of temporal variations in the gravitational coupling.

</details>


### [227] [The Schwarzschild Black Hole in an External Gravitational Tidal Field: the Quasinormal Spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21797)
*Dimitrios Giataganas,Alex Kehagias,Antonio Riotto*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究外部四极潮汐场如何修改 Schwarzschild 黑洞的准正则模（quasinormal mode）共振频率。通过在线性化潮汐形变背景上推导奇偶宇称扰动方程，计算了 Schwarzschild 共振的一阶位移，发现奇偶宇称位移一致（等谱性保持），而二次潮汐力可打破这种简并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞在现实宇宙中并非孤立存在，邻近伴星会施加外部引力潮汐。理解潮汐作用如何修改黑洞的共振（ringdown）频率，对引力波天文学中的黑洞观测与参数推断具有直接意义。

Method: 利用精确的潮汐畸变 Schwarzschild 解，将扰动方程按潮汐振幅 A 线性化，在形变背景上推导对角化的奇宇称与偶宇称扰动方程。将解析延拓的 Schwarzschild 共振代入，计算其一阶位移，并将位移分解为 Zeeman 型简并分裂模式与约化复系数（由围道积分之比给出）的乘积。

Result: 对角一阶位移因子化为 δω_{nℓm}=A c_{ℓm} κ_{nℓ}，其中约化系数仅依赖 (n,ℓ)，乘以普适 Zeeman 型模式分裂 2ℓ+1 个方位角多重态。对 ℓ=2 和 ℓ=3 明确分析发现奇偶宇称位移一致，即 Schwarzschild 等谱性（isospectrality）在一阶潮汐振幅下保持；但二次潮汐力可破坏该简并。天体物理上主导的 (0,2,2) 模式振荡更快、衰减更慢。此外，eikonal 位移可用潮汐形变光子环附近的 Penrose 极限做几何描述。

Conclusion: 外部四极潮汐会系统性改变黑洞共振频率，且其一阶效应保持奇偶宇称等谱性；高阶潮汐效应可打破此简并。这些结果对潮汐环境中的黑洞 ringdown 波形建模和引力波观测具有参考价值。

Abstract: A black hole need not ring down in isolation, since a nearby companion can subject it to an external gravitational tide. We calculate how a quadrupole tide modifies the Schwarzschild black hole resonances. Linearising the exact tidally distorted Schwarzschild solution in the tidal amplitude $A$, we derive the diagonal odd- and even-parity perturbation equations on the deformed background and compute the formal first order displacements of analytically continued Schwarzschild resonances. Each diagonal first order shift factorises as $δω_{n\ell m}=A\,c_{\ell m}κ_{n\ell}$, giving one reduced complex coefficient for each $(n,\ell)$, expressed as a ratio of contour integrals, multiplied by a universal Zeeman-like pattern that splits the $2\ell+1$ azimuthal multiplet. For the $\ell=2$ and $\ell=3$ sectors analysed explicitly, the odd- and even-parity shifts coincide, so Schwarzschild isospectrality survives at first order in the tidal amplitude. Quadratic tidal forces, by contrast, can break this degeneracy. Moreover, for a physical companion, the astrophysically dominant $(n,\ell,m)=(0,2,2)$ mode oscillates faster and decays more slowly. Finally, we show that the eikonal shifts admit a geometric description in terms of the Penrose limit about the tidally deformed photon ring.

</details>


### [228] [Discrete self-similarity imprints on primordial-black-hole mass functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21834)
*Luis E. Padilla,Tomohiro Harada,Hayami Iizuka*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本论文研究标量场坍缩的临界行为中离散自相似性（discrete self-similarity, DSS）如何印刻在原初黑洞（primordial black hole, PBH）质量函数上，利用宇宙学模拟中的DSS调制临界标度律，将近阈值质量映射传播到动能主导（kination）时期的归一化PBH质量函数中，发现即使无限窄的原初功率谱也会产生不可约的最小质量宽度，并在窄谱极限下质量峰值比满足m_{n+1}/m_n≃5.6。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连接宇宙学DSS坍缩模拟与PBH现象学预测，探究临界坍缩行为在PBH质量函数中的可观测印记。

Method: 采用DSS调制的临界标度律将近阈值质量映射传播到kination时期的PBH质量函数；系统比较高斯窗函数、k空间顶帽窗函数、实空间顶帽窗函数以及乘以kination传递函数的实空间顶帽窗函数对质量函数的影响，并分别考察窄谱与宽谱两种极限情形。

Result: 临界坍缩行为产生不可约的最小质量宽度；DSS调制度产生近似对数周期特征，等相位点满足Δln M_PBH=γP_ln，在窄谱极限下单峰间距比约为5.6；宽谱情形下卷积使DSS模式去相位并逐渐抹平临界子结构。

Conclusion: 该结果为宇宙学DSS坍缩模拟与PBH现象学（包括群体可观测量的预测）之间架起桥梁，并表明窄谱条件下DSS子结构有望在最终质量函数中存活。

Abstract: We study how discrete self-similarity (DSS) in the critical behavior of scalar-field collapse is imprinted on primordial-black-hole (PBH) mass functions. Using the DSS-modulated critical scaling law found in cosmological simulations, we propagate the near-threshold mass map into normalized PBH mass functions during a {kination} era. We compare Gaussian window function, $k$-space top-hat window function, and real-space top-hat window function, together with a real-space top-hat window function multiplied by a kination transfer function. We find that critical behavior in gravitational collapse produces an irreducible minimum width even for an infinitesimally narrow primordial spectrum. DSS then modulates this critical-scaling profile, generating approximately log-periodic features in mass. At fixed horizon mass, successive equal-phase points of the DSS-modulated critical mass map satisfy $Δ\ln M_{\rm PBH}=γP_{\rm ln}$. Consequently, in the narrow-spectrum limit, the corresponding structures in the final mass function are expected to satisfy $Δ\ln m\simeqγP_{\rm ln}$, or $m_{n+1}/m_n\simeq5.6$, for the fiducial Choptuik DSS parameters $γ$ and $P_{\rm ln}$. For broad primordial spectra, the convolution over horizon masses dephases the DSS pattern and progressively washes out the critical substructure. We normalize the mass functions so that we may primarily study the profile shape and the survival of DSS substructure, rather than the absolute PBH abundance. These results provide a bridge between cosmological DSS collapse simulations and PBH phenomenology including population observables.

</details>


### [229] [Learning Boson Star Solution Families with Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21845)
*Ao Liu,Chen-Hao Hao,Cuihong Wen,Shao-Jiang Wang,Jieci Wang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文开发了一种物理信息神经网络（physics-informed neural network, PINN）来求解玻色星（boson star）解的族集合，通过直接在平衡解流形上学习从物理参数和径向坐标到标量场与度规场的映射，从而以单次前向传播生成完整构型，并成功重建质量-频率螺旋线（mass-frequency spirals）及守恒量，包括传统求解器需要精细延拓的内支构型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统计算玻色星族系需要反复求解非线性特征值边值问题并进行数值延拓以穿越转择点（turning points），计算成本高且操作复杂。论文旨在用已训练的单一模型代替逐点求解，实现对非线性自引力解族的高效摊销式（amortized）探索。

Method: 构建一个PINN，学习从物理参数和径向坐标到标量场和度规场在平衡解流形上的映射。网络输出中嵌入正则性与渐近边界条件；训练目标综合了点式监督、爱因斯坦-克莱因-戈登方程残差，以及对Arnowitt-Deser-Misner质量和诺特荷（Noether charge）的曲线级约束。

Result: 在代表性的一支、两支和三支解族上，训练后的模型单次前向传播即生成完整构型，成功重建了质量-频率螺旋线和守恒量。即便对传统算法中需要精细延续处理的内支构型，该方法也能准确恢复。

Conclusion: 这些结果表明物理信息代理学习（physics-informed surrogate learning）是探索非线性自引力解族的一条实用途径，显著降低了传统求解所需的重复计算与数值操作复杂度。

Abstract: Computing boson star families traditionally requires repeated solution of nonlinear eigenvalue boundary-value problems and careful numerical continuation through turning points. We develop a physics-informed neural network (PINN) that learns the map from the physical parameters and radial coordinate directly to the scalar and metric fields over an equilibrium solution manifold. Regularity and asymptotic boundary conditions are incorporated into the network output, while the training objective combines pointwise supervision, Einstein-Klein-Gordon residuals, and curve-level constraints on the Arnowitt-Deser-Misner mass and Noether charge. A trained model generates a complete configuration in a single forward pass. Across representative one-, two-, and three-branch families, the method reconstructs the mass-frequency spirals and conserved quantities, including configurations on inner branches that require delicate continuation in conventional solvers. These results establish physics-informed surrogate learning as a practical route to amortized exploration of nonlinear self-gravitating solution families.

</details>


### [230] [Extreme mass-ratio inspirals around rotating accelerating black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22249)
*Xin-Dong Du,Tao Zhou,Wei Xiong,Tieguang Zi,Peng-Cheng Li*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了旋转黑洞加速度对极端质量比旋进（EMRI）引力波波形的影响。研究者通过构建一个中间克尔型波区并构造近赤道圆轨道，推导了自旋-2径向Teukolsky方程的分离点粒子源，利用Sasaki–Nakamura变换和格林函数方法求解径向方程，将地平线和远区通量与绝热演化耦合。结果表明加速度同时修正辐射反作用力和轨道频率，产生特征性的非单调累积失相（dephasing），在特定参数下一年内主导模的失相可略超1弧度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端质量比旋进（EMRI）能够将偏离Kerr动力学的微小偏差放大为可观测的引力波相位偏移，因此提供了一个高灵敏度的探测窗口。本文旨在利用这种敏感性，研究旋转黑洞的加速度（acceleration）对EMRI波形留下的印记，而旋转的C度规因不满足渐近平坦性和反射对称性，对该问题的标准框架构成障碍。

Method: 对于足够小的加速度AM，在满足M/r<<1和Ar<<1的中间Kerr型波区中表述计算，通过耦合径向—极向稳定性分析构造近赤道圆轨道。推导自旋-2径向Teukolsky方程的分离点粒子源，构建正则归一化角向解，采用Sasaki–Nakamura变换及格林函数方法求解径向方程，并将所得地平线和远区通量与稳定近赤道圆轨道的绝热演化相耦合。

Result: 该框架可恢复Kerr极限，并再现主导l=2 Kerr通量，相对误差约为10^{-7}量级。加速度同时修正辐射反作用力和轨道频率，产生非单调的累积失相行为。在M=10^6 M_⊙、m_s/M=10^{-5}、a/M=0.7、AM=3×10^{-7}的参数下，主导模一年内的失相略超过1弧度，显示弱加速度也能在长时程EMRI上产生量级为弧度的长期相位印记。

Conclusion: 即使弱加速度也能在受控近似范围内对长时程EMRI产生量级为弧度的累积相位印记，表明加速度效应是EMRI引力波建模中不可忽略的因素，为未来探测和参数提取提供了新的物理考量。

Abstract: Extreme mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs) can magnify small departures from Kerr dynamics into appreciable gravitational-wave phase shifts accumulated over many orbital cycles. We exploit this sensitivity to investigate the imprint of a rotating black hole's acceleration on an EMRI waveform. The spinning C metric poses two obstacles to the standard Kerr flux framework: the spacetime is not asymptotically flat, and the acceleration breaks the reflection symmetry that supports exactly equatorial circular timelike orbits. For sufficiently small acceleration $AM$, we therefore formulate the calculation in an intermediate Kerr-like wave zone satisfying $M/r\ll1$ and $Ar\ll1$, and construct a near-equatorial circular orbit by examining its coupled radial--polar stability. We derive the separated point-particle source for the spin$-2$ radial Teukolsky equation, construct a regular normalized angular solution, solve the radial equation using the Sasaki--Nakamura transformation and the Green function method, and couple the resulting horizon and far-zone fluxes to the adiabatic evolution of stable near-equatorial circular orbits. The framework recovers the Kerr limit and reproduces the dominant $l=2$ Kerr fluxes with relative errors of order $10^{-7}$. Acceleration modifies both radiation reaction and the orbital frequency, producing a characteristic nonmonotonic accumulated dephasing. For $M=10^6M_\odot$, $m_s/M=10^{-5}$, $a/M=0.7$, and $AM=3\times10^{-7}$, the dominant-mode dephasing slightly exceeds $1$ rad over one year. Thus even weak acceleration can generate an order-radian secular phase imprint on long-duration EMRIs within the controlled regime of the present approximation.

</details>


### [231] [Thermodynamics of Genuine Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22292)
*David Kubiznak,Hryhorii Ovcharenko,Jiri Podolsky*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种对真正 Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson（Kerr-BR）黑洞时空的热力学描述。与以往研究不同，该解不再带有电荷，因此其热力学由简单关系主导。通过引入合适的类时 Killing 矢量规范化因子，时空可由标准 Hawking 温度、Bekenstein 熵、Komar 质量和角动量及视界角速度表征，且质量服从标准 Christodoulou-Ruffini 公式及第一定律和 Smarr 关系。进一步通过坐标和解参数的适当重标度及非平凡参数空间约束，可形式上恢复真空 Kerr 解的热力学量，从而将时空转化为 Kerr 形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往关于 Kerr-BR 黑洞的研究涉及电荷，导致热力学关系复杂。本文旨在建立一种无电荷的"真正" Kerr-BR 时空的热力学描述，并揭示其与真空 Kerr 解之间潜在的结构联系。

Method: 通过引入合适的类时 Killing 矢量（timelike Killing vector）规范化因子，系统定义其 Hawking 温度、Bekenstein 熵、Komar 质量和角动量以及视界角速度；采用坐标和解参数的适当重标度，在满足非平凡参数空间约束的条件下，将热力学量映射至真空 Kerr 解的形式。

Result: 证明了 Kerr-BR 时空的质量满足标准 Christodoulou-Ruffini 公式，并满足标准第一定律（first law）与 Smarr 关系（Smarr relations）；通过重标度与参数约束，可形式上恢复真空 Kerr 解的热力学量，并将真正的 Kerr-BR 时空转化为 Kerr 类形式。

Conclusion: 本文表明，无电荷的真正 Kerr-BR 黑洞具有简洁且标准的热力学结构，并在参数空间约束下与真空 Kerr 解在形式上等价，从而为 Kerr-BR 时空的热力学理解提供了新的框架。

Abstract: We propose a thermodynamic description of the genuine Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson (Kerr-BR) black hole spacetimes. Contrary to previous studies, such solutions no longer have electric charge. Consequently, the thermodynamics is governed by simple relations. Namely, upon introducing a suitable normalization factor for the timelike Killing vector, these spacetimes are characterized by the standard Hawking temperature, Bekenstein's entropy, Komar mass and angular momentum, and horizon angular velocity. The corresponding mass is shown to obey the standard Christodoulou-Ruffini formula, along with the standard first law and Smarr relations. In fact, by a proper rescaling of coordinates and solution parameters, and up to a non-trivial parameter space constraint, we formally recover the thermodynamic quantities of the vacuum Kerr solution. This is used to bring the genuine Kerr-BR spacetime into a Kerr-like form.

</details>


### [232] [Correlated and coincident noise in Einstein Telescope's triangular configuration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22401)
*Jan Harms*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文针对爱因斯坦望远镜（Einstein Telescope, ET）的三角构型，利用候选选址的环境数据，首次引入环境噪声的时域模拟，分析相关环境噪声与瞬态毛刺（glitch）对观测能力的影响，发现这些特征严重限制了ET三角构型的观测能力及其零流（null stream）的效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前关于引力波（gravitational-wave, GW）探测器网络的分布优于单站三角构型的论证已有大量研究，但噪声相关性等关键因素的建模仅停留在表层处理，缺乏基于实际数据的深入评估。

Method: 利用ET候选选址的现有数据，构建ET三角构型的时域模拟框架，对相关环境噪声及环境场瞬变产生的重合毛刺进行仿真与分析。

Result: 模拟结果表明，相关环境噪声与重合毛刺对ET三角构型的观测能力构成重要限制，并削弱其零流在噪声抑制方面的实用价值。

Conclusion: ET三角构型的可行性需重新审视，相关环境噪声与瞬态毛刺是不可忽视的关键限制因素，可能动摇此前对三角构型优势的判断依据。

Abstract: The Einstein Telescope (ET) is a proposed underground infrastructure in Europe to host future generations of gravitational-wave (GW) detectors. Two configurations are under investigation. The first is the two-site solution with an L-shape detector at each site similar to the LIGO configuration. The second configuration is the single-site triangular detector. While numerous analyses over the last decade have already proven that the distributed network of GW detectors is the scientifically preferred solution, it was also argued that the advantage might not be decisive enough to dismiss ET's triangle configuration. The modeling of aspects such as noise correlations in these configurations has only been treated superficially in the past. In this article, we introduce a time-domain simulation of environmental noises in the ET triangle using available data from ET's candidate sites. We simulate and analyze correlated environmental noise as well as coincident glitches produced by transients in the environmental fields. We find that these features pose an important limitation to the observational capabilities of the ET triangle and limit the utility of its null stream.

</details>


### [233] [Cosmological extra dimensions can mimic dark matter I. Dark matter in galaxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22456)
*Mattia Villani*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出一个带有n+4维度的简单模型，其中额外维度被紧致化且其半径R具有空间依赖性，并展示了R的轮廓变化可由（可见）物质密度和牛顿势驱动，能够复现旋涡星系旋转曲线，同时提出了六个实验以检验该假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在通过额外维度的空间相关半径来解释星系旋转曲线等观测现象，以检验/替代传统暗物质（dark matter）解释框架。

Method: 作者构建了一个n+4维时空的简单模型，其中额外维度被紧致化并具有空间依赖的半径R，通过分析R的轮廓变化如何受物质密度和牛顿势（Newtonian potential）驱动，推导出R的表达式并复现旋涡星系旋转曲线。

Result: 该模型能够复现旋涡星系的旋转曲线，并为紧致化半径R提供了具体表达式；此外，作者提出了六项实验用于检验该假设。

Conclusion: 该模型为星系旋转曲线提供了一种基于额外维度几何的替代解释，但其可行性有待六项拟议实验的进一步验证。

Abstract: We present a simple model with a n + 4-dimensional metric with compactified extra dimensions with a space dependent radius R. We show that changes in the profile of R are sourced by (visible) matter density and Newtonian potential. We can reproduce rotation curves of spiral galaxies and provide an expression for R. In order to test our hypothesis, we propose six experiments.

</details>


### [234] [Killing tensors of Weyl's class](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22523)
*Dionysios Kokkinos*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文作为系统性识别代数一般型（Petrov type I）时空中隐藏对称性（hidden symmetries）的初始步骤，聚焦于Weyl类静态轴对称时空，利用二阶Killing张量的规范形式推导其承认真正隐藏对称性的约束条件，并对真空与电真空情形下的可积性障碍给出了解析刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代数一般型（Petrov type I）时空中的隐藏对称性缺乏系统性的识别框架，现有的研究多为零散的分析与数值探索，需要一种系统方法来判别这些时空是否具有额外的运动积分。

Method: 采用秩为2的Killing张量（Killing tensor）的规范形式（canonical forms），以Weyl类（提供一类简单但代数一般的静态轴对称几何）为研究对象，系统推导时空承认不可约Killing张量所需满足的约束条件。

Result: 在真空与电真空情形下，Weyl类中没有任何双Killing矢量成员承认可提供额外运动积分的不可约Killing张量；而具有第三个额外Killing矢量等距（即Levi-Civita）的Weyl类成员，其显式对称性本身已足以实现完全可积，仅承认可约Killing张量。

Conclusion: 该结果在解析层面补充了此前的分析与数值研究，系统性地建立了隐藏对称性的缺失与完全可积性破坏之间的联系；同时展示了Killing张量规范形式作为探测Petrov type I时空隐藏对称性系统工具的潜力。

Abstract: This work represents an initial step towards a systematic framework for identifying hidden symmetries in algebraically general (Petrov type I) spacetimes. Motivated by this, we focus on Weyl's class, which provides a simple yet a class of algebraically general solutions of static, axisymmetric geometries. Employing the canonical forms of rank-2 Killing tensors, we systematically derive the constraints that must be satisfied for these spacetimes to admit genuine hidden symmetries. Our analysis yields an analytical characterization of the obstructions to integrability within subclasses of Weyl's class, both in vacuum and electrovacuum. In particular, we find that no two-Killing-vector member of Weyl's class, in vacuum or electrovacuum, admits an irreducible Killing tensor capable of supplying the additional integral of motion required for complete integrability. Members of Weyl's class admitting a third additional Killing-vector isometry (i.e. Levi-Civita) are already completely integrable through their manifest symmetries alone admitting only reducible Killing tensors. This result provides an analytic complement to previous analytical and numerical investigations, and establishes a systematic connection between the absence of hidden symmetries and the breakdown of complete integrability. Beyond the specific classification obtained here, these results demonstrate the potential of our approach regarding the canonical forms of Killing tensor as a systematic tool for probing hidden symmetries in Petrov type I spacetimes.

</details>


### [235] [Comment on Stellar structure and stability of charged interacting quark stars and their scaling behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22653)
*Yann Bissessur,Michael Gammon,Robert B. Mann*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文是对带电相互作用夸克星文献中一处计算错误的评注，重新计算了广义相对论框架下带电夸克星的径向基频本征频率，发现修正后的结果中所有先前无稳定解的分支均存在稳定解，且带电夸克星的稳定范围比原研究更广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指认并纠正带电夸克星文献（Phys.Rev.D, 102(3):034031, 2020）中的错误，该错误导致后续研究（Phys.Rev.D, 104(12):123007, 2021）中径向稳定性方程的解存在偏差。

Method: 在广义相对论框架下重新计算带电相互作用夸克星的基频径向本征频率（fundamental radial eigenfrequencies），并与先前结果进行对比；同时纳入四项此前未包含的观测约束。

Result: 大多数定性趋势保持不变，但给定分支上最大质量与稳定性点之间的分离幅度可能发生显著变化；原研究[2]中所有不含稳定解的分支在修正后的版本中均出现稳定解；研究的全部案例中带电夸克星均表现出比原预测更大的稳定范围。

Conclusion: 修正后的结果表明带电夸克星的稳定性范围大于先前预测；所获得的质-半关系（mass-radius relations）中有子集可满足四项新的观测约束。

Abstract: We comment on an error in the charged quark star literature ( Phys. Rev. D, 102(3):034031, 2020 ) that resulted in erroneous solutions to the radial stability equations for charged interacting quark stars in ( Phys. Rev. D, 104(12):123007, 2021 )[2]. In this comment article we recalculate the fundamental radial eigenfrequencies for charged, interacting quark stars in general relativity and discuss any departure from previous results [2]. Most of the qualitative trends persist, but the magnitudes of the separation between the maximum mass and stability points on a given branch can change significantly. We also note that all branches in [2] that contained no stable solutions now have stable solutions in their corrected counterparts. The net effect is that in all of the studied cases charged quark stars have a greater range of stability than originally predicted. We also compare four recent observational constraints not included in previous studies, and find that these constraints are satisfied by a subset of the mass-radius relations we obtain.

</details>


### [236] [Joule Thomson expansion of $κ$-deformed Schwarzschild-AdS black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22791)
*Vishnu Rajagopal,Puxun Wu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个由Lie代数κ-形变（κ-deformed）时空描述的非对易Schwarzschild-AdS黑洞的焦耳-汤姆逊（Joule-Thomson）膨胀行为，发现κ-形变参数能够诱导无电荷AdS黑洞产生焦耳-汤姆逊膨胀，且最小反转温度与临界温度的比值约为0.493，与Reissner-Nordström黑洞的结果高度吻合。还发现存在一个依赖于κ-形变参数的最小黑洞质量，低于该质量后焦耳-汤姆逊膨胀消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 焦耳-汤姆逊膨胀是研究黑洞热力学的重要工具，先前的研究主要在带电或旋转黑洞中进行。本文旨在考察在非对易（non-commutative）κ-形变时空背景下，无电荷AdS黑洞是否也能表现出焦耳-汤姆逊膨胀，从而探索量子引力效应（quantum gravity effects）对黑洞热力学性质的影响。

Method: 采用Lie代数κ-形变时空来刻画黑洞的时空几何，对非对易Schwarzschild-AdS黑洞系统性地推导并分析焦耳-汤姆逊膨胀的相关热力学量，包括反转温度（inversion temperature）、临界温度（critical temperature）及最小黑洞质量等。

Result: κ-形变参数能够使无电荷AdS黑洞产生焦耳-汤姆逊膨胀；最小反转温度与临界温度的比值Ti^(min)/Tc≈0.493，该比值不依赖形变参数，且与Reissner-Nordström黑洞的值接近；此外存在一个取决于κ-形变参数的最小黑洞质量阈值，低于该阈值时焦耳-汤姆逊膨胀消失。

Conclusion: κ-形变时空的非对易效应为无电荷AdS黑洞引入了焦耳-汤姆逊膨胀机制，其关键比值与带电黑洞一致，表明这一特征可能具有普适性。最小黑洞质量的存在则体现了非对易几何对黑洞热力学行为的量子修正限制。

Abstract: We investigate the Joule-Thomson expansion of a non-commutative Schwarzschild-AdS black hole, whose space-time geometry is described by the Lie-algebraic $κ$-deformed space-time, and show that the $κ$-deformation parameter induces Joule-Thomson expansion in an uncharged-AdS black hole. We find the ratio of minimum inversion temperature to critical temperature $T_i^{(min)}/T_c \simeq 0.493$, is independent of the deformation parameter, and is in close agreement with the value for Reissner-Nordstrom black hole. Furthermore, we show the existence of a minimum black hole mass (depending on the $κ$-deformation parameter) below which the Joule-Thomson expansion vanishes.

</details>


### [237] [Complex frequency evolution of direct waves from binary black hole mergers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23209)
*Ling Sun,Sizheng Ma,Patrick P. Chen,Andrew Laeuger,Neil Lu,Yanbei Chen*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在响应滤波框架下研究黑洞渐变/形成过程中直接波（direct wave）的演化特性。通过有理滤波器从数值相对论波形中移除准正规模（quasinormal modes），发现直接波的瞬时复频率在晚期趋向于视界角速度2Ω_H和表面引力κ_H，其演化轨迹依赖于剩余自旋，且有限时间偏差承载了并合/坍缩动力学信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然黑洞事件视界发出的信号无法到达未来零无穷，但视界及其邻近区域的信息可以编码在落入渐增/形成视界的物质或场扰动所发射波的渐近性质中。研究这一信息编码机制，需要从黑洞响应中提取出跟随源演化、携带源信息的直接波成分。

Method: 采用响应滤波（response-filtered）框架区分直接波与静止阻尼正弦信号；使用有理滤波器（rational filters）从数值相对论（numerical relativity）波形中移除准正规模，从而研究直接波瞬时复频率（instantaneous complex frequency）的演化。该方法还利用Kerr响应中近似的极点-零点配对（pole-zero pairing）构建自旋依赖的最小滤波器组。

Result: 在数值污染之前，直接波轨迹依赖剩余自旋：低自旋（χ_f≲0.7）下实频率从上方趋向2Ω_H，高自旋（χ_f≳0.7）下从下方趋向；瞬时衰减率增加趋向约2κ_H，高自旋情形下可超越该值。视界控制值仅在晚期被接近，且对于χ_f~0.7的非进动、可比质量双星，早期实频率已接近2Ω_H。滤波器策略被证明可有效抑制准正规模特征并揭示源轨迹信息。

Conclusion: 直接波的有限时间偏差并非否定其与视界的联系，而是承载了并合/坍缩动力学的信息。完整直接波演化需要数值相对论标定；自旋依赖的最小滤波器组为提取源轨迹信息提供了有效工具。

Abstract: While no signal originating at a black hole's event horizon can reach future null infinity, information about the horizon and its immediate vicinity can be encoded in asymptotic properties of waves emitted by matter or field perturbations falling toward a growing/forming horizon. Within a response-filtered framework, "direct wave" denotes source-sensitive plunge and remnant-formation information revealed by filtering the black-hole response. Unlike a stationary damped sinusoid with a fixed complex frequency, the direct wave follows the evolving source and has an evolving instantaneous complex frequency tied at late times to the remnant horizon angular velocity $Ω_H$ and surface gravity $κ_H$. Using rational filters, we remove quasinormal modes from numerical-relativity waveforms to study this evolution. Before numerical contamination, trajectories depend on remnant spin: the real frequency evolves toward $2Ω_H$ from above for lower spins ($χ_f\lesssim0.7$) and from below for higher spins ($χ_f\gtrsim0.7$), while the instantaneous decay rate increases toward $\sim2κ_H$ and, in some high-spin cases, beyond it. As in particle-plunge results, the horizon-controlled value is approached only at late times, as frame dragging controls near-horizon motion. For $χ_f\sim0.7$ remnants of non-precessing, comparable-mass binaries, the early-time real frequency is close to $2Ω_H$ because the binary orbital frequency transitions smoothly to the remnant horizon frequency. Finite-time deviations therefore carry information about merger/collapse dynamics rather than undermining the horizon connection. Full direct-wave evolution requires numerical-relativity calibration. We further show that approximate pole-zero pairing in the Kerr response motivates a spin-dependent minimal filter set that suppresses quasinormal-mode features while revealing source-trajectory information.

</details>


### [238] [Gravitational lensing outside and inside of a marginally unstable photon sphere at a $Z_2$-symmetric wormhole throat in strong deflection limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23269)
*Naoki Tsukamoto*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究发现，在某些常见虫洞（wormhole）的Z₂对称喉部处的光子球（photon sphere）呈边缘不稳定状态，其附近光线的偏转角（deflection angle）并非按对数发散，而是按幂发散。作者通过数值方法研究了强偏转极限（strong deflection limit）下的引力透镜效应，并修正了此前的半解析结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光子球附近的光线偏转角通常以对数方式发散，这在黑洞阴影观测中具有重要意义。对于虫洞喉部处的边缘不稳定光子球，是否存在不同于对数发散的偏转角行为，是一个值得探究的问题。

Method: 作者选用Event Horizon Telescope合作组常研究的简单虫洞、Damour-Solodukhin虫洞和Simpson-Visser黑洞反弹（black-bounce）时空，在强偏转极限下对Z₂对称虫洞喉部边缘不稳定光子球内外的光线偏转角进行数值研究。

Result: 数值结果表明，在这些虫洞的Z₂对称喉部边缘不稳定光子球处，偏转角呈幂发散而非对数发散，且该发散行为具有普适性（universal property）。作者还修正了半解析方法中幂发散项系数。

Conclusion: 边缘不稳定的Z₂对称虫洞喉部光子球产生的幂发散偏转角，构成了与黑洞光子球对数发散偏转角不同的重要观测特征，可作为未来近空间观测区分虫洞与黑洞的潜在判据。

Abstract: The deflection angles of rays diverge logarithmically near outside and inside of a photon sphere and they could be detected by near-future space observations on black hole shadows. We show that the deflection angles of the rays near a photon sphere at the throat of an example among some commonly used types of wormholes or one of the simple wormholes, which is often studied by the Event Horizon Telescope collaborations, does diverge not logarithmically but in power. This is because it has a marginally unstable photon sphere at a $Z_2$-symmetric wormhole throat and the wormhole has not a common nor simple but a special property. We investigate numerically gravitational lensing of rays with the deflection angles diverging in power outside and inside of a marginally unstable photon sphere at $Z_2$-symmetric wormhole throat in strong deflection limits. We apply our numerical method to the commonly-used simple wormhole spacetime, a Damour-Solodukhin wormhole spacetime, and a Simpson-Visser black-bounce spacetime. Our numerical results imply universal property of the deflection angle by the marginally unstable photon sphere on $Z_2$-symmetric wormhole throat in the strong deflection limits as similar to the deflection angles of ray bent by a marginally unstable photon sphere off a throat in the strong deflection limits which have a different power. We also correct the coefficients of power-divergent terms of deflection angles of the rays bent nearly outside of the marginally unstable photon sphere at the $Z_2$-symmetric wormhole throat in a semi-analytic approach investigated by the author and others.

</details>


### [239] [Kerr Black Hole Uniqueness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23325)
*Qing Han,Marcus Khuri,Gilbert Weinstein,Jingang Xiong*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文证明了轴对称、平稳、真空背景下黑洞唯一性猜想（black hole uniqueness conjecture）：当每个退化黑洞分量具有非零角动量时，不存在具有多个视界分量（horizon component）的正则渐进平直（asymptotically flat）构型。证明基于奇异调和映射（singular harmonic maps）的精细渐近分析及Weyl共形因子的全局有界性，并推导出多动态黑洞的质量-角动量不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞唯一性猜想是广义相对论中的核心问题之一，此前在轴对称平稳真空情形下的多视界几何是否可能存在尚无定论。作者旨在严格证明在多退化视界情形下不存在正则多视界构型，并由此导出对应的极值质量-角动量不等式。

Method: 证明方法包括两部分：一是基于姊妹论文[16]对相关联的奇异调和映射进行精细渐近分析；二是利用由标量曲率相关微分不等式（scalar curvature related differential inequality）导出的Weyl共形因子（Weyl conformal factor）全局上界。通过结合这两个工具，分析每一有限轴杆（axis rod）上的对数角缺（logarithmic angle defect）。

Result: 在所有多视界构型中，沿每一有限轴杆的对数角缺严格为负，从而沿此类轴杆的相互作用力始终是吸引的。这一结果排除了多视界正则构型的存在，证明了黑洞唯一性猜想；作为极值情形的推论，利用文献[17]的主定理获得多个动力学黑洞的质量-角动量不等式（mass-angular momentum inequality）。

Conclusion: 本文完成了轴对称平稳真空情形下黑洞唯一性猜想的证明，确立了多退化视界（具有非零角动量）互相排斥于同一渐进平直时空之外这一结论，并为相关动力学黑洞质量-角动量不等式的猜想提供了确定性的证明路径。

Abstract: We prove the black hole uniqueness conjecture in the axially symmetric, stationary, vacuum setting: there is no regular asymptotically flat configuration with more than one horizon component when every degenerate component has nonzero angular momentum. More precisely, it is shown that in any such multi-horizon configuration, the logarithmic angle defect along every finite axis rod is strictly negative, and hence the interaction force along such axis rods is always attractive. The proof is based on a refined asymptotic analysis of the associated singular harmonic maps, in part from the companion paper [16], together with a global bound for the Weyl conformal factor arising from a certain scalar curvature related differential inequality. Furthermore, as a corollary of the extremal version of this uniqueness result, we obtain the conjectured mass-angular momentum inequality for multiple dynamical black holes using the main theorem of [17].

</details>


### [240] [Scalar charge bounds for extremal black hole formation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23355)
*Berend Schneider*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文证明了在Kehle-Unger框架下，标量荷电荷量$\mathfrak{e}$的坍缩标量场若要形成精确极端Reissner-Nordström黑洞（半径$r_+$），必须满足$|\mathfrak{e}|r_+ > \frac{1}{3}$；而亚极端坍缩则不受此约束。此外，若$|\mathfrak{e}|r_+ > \sqrt{\frac{3+\sqrt{33}}{3}}$，Schwarzschild黑洞可以变为极端黑洞，这暗示真空中的黑洞力学第三定律可能不成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究标量场坍缩形成极端黑洞的约束条件，并探讨黑洞力学第三定律（third law of black hole mechanics）在真空情形下是否成立。

Method: 通过解析论证，在Kehle-Unger构造的框架下分析带标量荷标量场的坍缩动力学，分别推导极端和亚极端Reissner-Nordström黑洞形成的充要条件。

Result: 极端情形下，形成精确极端黑洞需要对标量荷与半径的乘积满足严格下界$|\mathfrak{e}|r_+ > \frac{1}{3}$；而亚极端情形不存在类似界限，任意$\mathfrak{e} \neq 0$的标量场都可形成任意电荷质量比的亚极端黑洞。补充结果为Schwarzschild黑洞变为极端黑洞需满足$|\mathfrak{e}|r_+ > \sqrt{\frac{3+\sqrt{33}}{3}}$。

Conclusion: $|\mathfrak{e}|r_+$可取量级为1的值，这一事实可作为真空情形下黑洞力学第三定律为假的证据。

Abstract: I prove that a collapsing charged scalar field with scalar charge $\mathfrak{e}$ that forms an exactly extremal Reissner--Nordström black hole à la Kehle--Unger with radius $r_+$ must satisfy $|\mathfrak{e}|r_+ > \frac{1}{3}$. In contrast, I also show that no such bound exists for subextremal collapse: a scalar field with an arbitrary $\mathfrak{e} \ne 0$ can form a subextremal black hole with an arbitrary (subextremal) charge-to-mass ratio.
  Complementing the extremal bound, I prove that a Schwarzschild black hole can become extremal if $|\mathfrak{e}|r_+ > \sqrt{\frac{3 + \sqrt{33}}{3}}$. The fact that $|\mathfrak{e}|r_+$ can be taken to be of order unity could be considered evidence that the third law of black hole mechanics in vacuum is false.

</details>


### [241] [Action-selected currents and a singular conservation closure in two-fluid energy--momentum-squared cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23447)
*Özgür Akarsu,Bilal Bulduk,Nihan Katırcı*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了多流体物质型引力（multi-fluid, matter-type gravity）中，Bianchi恒等式对总有效应力张量散度的约束与各组成扇区电流分配之间的区分问题。基于尺度无关的扩展物质-几何引力（EMSG）背景，结合代数理想流体规定与收缩Hessian项消失的假设，构建了一个双流体有效背景闭合（effective background closure）系统，并分析其密度本征模、修正的Liénard方程及秩退化耦合性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多流体物质型引力中，Bianchi恒等式仅约束总有效应力张量的散度，而无法单独确定各组成扇区（sector）的电流（currents），这些电流只有在规定了离壳物质作用量（off-shell matter action）及运动方程或额外现象学闭合后才被选定。本文旨在明确区分这一结构差异，并通过一个具体的双流体背景案例检验该区分。

Method: 以一个受尺度无关EMSG启发的空间平坦（spatially flat）双流体背景为案例，将代数理想流体规定（algebraic perfect-fluid prescription）与收缩Hessian项（vanishing contracted-Hessian contribution）消失相结合，并假设总常规扇区与修正扇区各自独立守恒。基于此构建有效背景闭合系统，求解两个密度本征模（density eigenmodes），推导关于哈勃参数H的修正Liénard方程，并证明密度重构映射（density-reconstruction map）的判别式符号性质。

Result: 当两种流体的状态方程（EoS）不等且α≠0时，闭合系统退化为Barrow-Clifton系统，其传递系数依赖于(w1,w2)但不依赖于α，因此非零α族是奇异的：α→0极限无法恢复非耦合的广义相对论守恒律。密度重构映射的判别式对任意有限的w1≠w2严格为正，意味着每个真正的二次情形具有两个不同的实秩退化耦合。真空与刚性流体（stiff-fluid）族展示了模态消去（modal cancellation），当今日常规密度为正时，两者均保持为正的区间在显式分析的参数范围内通常终止于有限端点。

Conclusion: 这些结果提供了在多层流体物质型引力中区分作用量层面预测（action-level predictions）与闭合伪影（closure artifacts）的一致性诊断工具，但并未建立一个观测上可行的EMSG模型。

Abstract: In multi-fluid, matter-type gravity, the Bianchi identity constrains the divergence of the total effective stress tensor but does not, by itself, determine the currents assigned to its constituent sectors. Those currents are selected only after the off-shell matter action and equations, or an additional phenomenological closure, have been specified. We formulate this distinction and examine a spatially flat two-fluid background inspired by scale-independent EMSG. The case study combines an algebraic perfect-fluid prescription with a vanishing contracted-Hessian contribution, together with separate conservation of the total conventional and modification sectors. Because neither assumption is derived here from a concrete off-shell fluid action, the resulting system is an effective background closure rather than a microscopic EMSG model. For unequal EoS and every $α\ne0$, the closure yields a Barrow-Clifton system whose transfer coefficients depend on $(w_1,w_2)$ but not on $α$. Hence the nonzero-$α$ family is singular: its $α\to0$ limit does not recover the uncoupled GR conservation laws. We solve the two density eigenmodes and derive the associated modified Liénard equation for $H$. We also prove that the discriminant governing rank loss of the density-reconstruction map is strictly positive for every finite $w_1\ne w_2$. Thus each genuinely quadratic case has two distinct real rank-degenerate couplings. Exact vacuum and stiff-fluid families illustrate modal cancellation. When today's conventional densities are positive, the intervals on which both remain positive generally terminate at finite endpoints in the parameter ranges analyzed explicitly. These results provide a consistency diagnostic for separating action-level predictions from closure artifacts in multi-fluid, matter-type gravity; they do not establish an observationally viable EMSG model.

</details>

Zhang,T. Zhang,C. Zhao,J. Zhao,Yue Zhao,Yuhang Zhao,L. -M. Zheng,Y. Zheng,L. Zhizhong,H. Zhong,H. Zhou,H. O. Zhu,X. -J. Zhu,Z. -H. Zhu,Z. Zhu,D. Z. Zieba,A. B. Zimmerman,L. Zimmermann,M. E. Zucker*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本研究利用LIGO–Virgo–KAGRA合作组截至2025年4月1日第四次观测运行（O4）的数据，搜索各向同性随机引力波背景（stochastic gravitational-wave background）。通过引入新的频域截断以处理非平稳频谱噪声特征，重新处理了此前O4数据，未发现随机背景信号，并对引力波能量密度给出了95%置信度的上限约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前分析中，一类非平稳频谱噪声特征未被现有数据质量检查有效识别和缓解，可能影响随机引力波背景搜索的灵敏度与结果的可靠性，因此需要改进数据处理方法并重新分析已有数据。

Method: 采用LIGO–Virgo–KAGRA合作组在第一次观测运行至2025年4月1日期间积累的数据；在频域中实施新的截断（frequency-domain cuts）以针对非平稳频谱噪声；以对数均匀先验（log-uniform prior）在95%置信水平下对引力波能量密度设定上限；结合GWTC-5.0瞬变目录中的致密双星并合（compact binary coalescence）种群模型进行背景振幅预测。

Result: 未发现随机引力波背景信号。对谱指数为2/3的幂律背景（致密双星螺旋并合所预期），给出Ω_GW(25 Hz) ≤ 2.0×10⁻⁹；对尺度不变背景给出Ω_GW(25 Hz) ≤ 2.8×10⁻⁹，二者均在95%置信水平。这些限制相较重新计算后的先前数据结果改善1.4倍；同时更新了非标准偏振模式的替代引力理论约束，并验证相关磁噪声低于搜索灵敏度。预测致密双星背景振幅为Ω_CBC(25 Hz) = 6.3⁺⁵·⁰₋₂·₂ × 10⁻¹⁰（90%置信水平）。

Conclusion: 当前观测数据未提供随机引力波背景的探测证据，但新的频率域处理显著提升了约束灵敏度。基于最新GWTC-5.0目录的种群预测表明，致密双星背景的预期振幅仍在当前上限之下，后续观测运行有望实现对该背景的探测。

Abstract: We report results from a search for an isotropic stochastic gravitational-wave background using data collected by the LIGO--Virgo--KAGRA Collaboration. The analysis uses data from the first observing run through April 1, 2025, during the fourth observing run. New frequency-domain cuts are implemented to address a class of non-stationary spectral noise features that were not effectively identified and mitigated by existing data-quality checks in past analyses. Consequently, previously analyzed data from the fourth observing run are re-processed with the updated cuts. We find no evidence for a stochastic background signal and place upper limits on the gravitational-wave energy density. In particular, for a background following a power law with spectral index 2/3 as predicted by inspiralling compact binaries, we find $Ω_\mathrm{GW}(25\,\mathrm{Hz}) \leq 2.0 \times 10^{-9}$, while scale-invariant backgrounds are constrained to $Ω_\mathrm{GW}(25\,\mathrm{Hz}) \leq 2.8 \times 10^{-9}$, both at the 95\% credible level for a log-uniform prior on $Ω_\mathrm{GW}$. Relative to the constraints from previous data recomputed with the new frequency-domain cuts, these limits improve by a factor of 1.4. We also update bounds on alternative gravity scenarios predicting non-standard polarization modes, and we verify that correlated magnetic noise sources remain below the sensitivity of this search. Combining these observational constraints with population models of compact binary coalescences informed by the latest gravitational-wave transient catalog, GWTC-5.0, we predict the amplitude of the compact binary background to be $Ω_\mathrm{CBC}(25\,\mathrm{Hz}) = 6.3^{+5.0}_{-2.2} \times 10^{-10}$ at the 90\% credible level.

</details>


### [243] [Dynamical Love numbers of analogue rotating black and white holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23496)
*Satadal Datta,Wei-Can Syu,Da-Shin Lee*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文计算了由排水浴缸流和水泉浴缸流分别生成的2+1维类比黑洞与类比白洞的动力学潮汐响应系数（TRCs），发现其满足复函数形式，且类比白洞的TRC是类比黑洞TRC的复共轭。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究类比引力系统中边界对潮汐扰动的响应，为理解真实引力理论中的潮汐Love数和耗散系数提供流体力学类比框架。

Method: 利用排水泉浴缸流构造2+1维类比黑洞和类比白洞，通过Fourier模态的频率与方位角数以及旋转参数刻画参数空间，计算复数值的动力学潮汐响应系数，并将其实部和虚部分别定义为潮汐Love数（TLN）和潮汐耗散系数（TDC）。

Result: 类比黑洞的TRC在参数空间某些点（包括静态情况）TLN表现出对数跑动，TDC为零；不同于爱因斯坦引力，流体动力学允许类比白洞事件视界的物理存在，且ABH-AWH对共享相同的角动量和Bernoulli常数但质量流率相反；类比白洞的TRC是类比黑洞TRC的复共轭。

Conclusion: 流体力学系统能够同时容纳类比黑洞和类比白洞，且二者潮汐响应存在复共轭关系，为类比引力研究中对White Hole的需求提供了流体力学解决方案。

Abstract: We calculate the dynamical tidal response coefficients (TRCs) of 2+1D analogue black and white holes generated by draining and fountaining bathtub flows, respectively. The parameter space is characterized by the frequency and azimuthal number of the Fourier modes and the rotation of the analogue black or white hole. In general, the TRC is a complex-valued function of these parameters. Its real and imaginary parts are defined as the tidal Love number (TLN) and the tidal dissipation coefficient (TDC), respectively. The TRC of the analogue black hole (ABH) exhibits several interesting features. At certain points in the parameter space, including the static case of vanishing perturbation frequency, the TLN exhibits logarithmic running, while the TDC vanishes. Unlike in Einstein gravity, fluid dynamics allows for the physical existence of an analogue white hole (AWH) event horizon. Time-independent, torque-free, barotropic, inviscid, axisymmetric fluid dynamical equations can yield a pair of transonic background flow solutions. The solutions in the pair, corresponding to an ABH-AWH pair, share the same angular momentum and Bernoulli constant but have opposite mass flow rates, all of which are conserved quantities. For such an ABH-AWH pair, the TRC of the AWH is the complex conjugate of the TRC of the ABH.

</details>


### [244] [From arithmetic spectra to a quantum-corrected black hole geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23528)
*Kimet Jusufi,Ankit Anand*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究表明，黎曼ζ函数（Riemann zeta function）的欧拉乘积可视为自由玻色气体的配分函数，该"算术气体"与熵-几何对应关系的假设相结合，可推导出量子修正的黑洞度规。结果显示一个具有Reissner–Nordström视界结构但无Coulomb毛、满足零能量条件的各向异性源、软化中心奇点、有界Hawking温度以及终止蒸发的冷极值残余物的两视界黑洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索素数（primes）与黑洞热力学之间的深层联系，通过将黎曼ζ函数的算术结构与黑洞几何的熵-面积对应关系结合，构建一个具有物理意义的量子修正黑洞模型。

Method: 将黎曼ζ函数的欧拉乘积解释为自由玻色气体的配分函数（partition function），其模式能量（mode energies）为素数的对数；结合熵-几何对应假设，利用素数气体作为Hagedorn系统的性质，通过熵正比于能量（即熵正比于视界面积）的要求确定面积定律的对数修正系数，并在大半径处还原为Schwarzschild度规以固定从算术能量到视界面积的映射，最终得到闭式度规f(r)=1−2GMr/(r²+λ²)，其中λ²=αG/π。

Result: 得到量子修正的黑洞度规闭式解，描述两视界黑洞，具有Reissner–Nordström视界结构、无Coulomb毛、满足零能量条件的正能各向异性源、软化的中心奇点、有界Hawking温度以及终止蒸发的冷极值残余物。非平凡零点仅以指数抑制的对数周期波动形式出现在面积中，且同一长度尺度可独立地由第一定律的精确成立推导出来。

Conclusion: 该模型为黎曼假设（Riemann hypothesis）提供了物理诠释：黎曼假设等价于断言算术修正对黑洞热力学的影响尽可能小，即非平凡零点只会以指数抑制的微小波动形式影响黑洞面积。

Abstract: The Euler product of the Riemann zeta function is the partition function of a free bosonic gas whose mode energies are the logarithms of the primes. We show that this arithmetic gas, combined with the assumption that the entropy--geometry correspondence holds, leads to a quantum-corrected black-hole metric. The prime gas is a Hagedorn system. Its entropy is linear in the energy, which is exactly what an entropy linear in the horizon area requires, and the simple pole of the zeta function at $β=1$ fixes the coefficient of the logarithmic correction to the area law. The nontrivial zeros cannot play this role: their level density grows only logarithmically, and far too slowly to be extensive. Demanding that the reconstructed geometry reduce to Schwarzschild at large radius then fixes the map from arithmetic energy to horizon area and yields the closed-form metric $f(r)=1-2GMr/(r^{2}+\lz^{2})$ with $\lz^{2}=αG/π$. This describes a two-horizon black hole with Reissner--Nordström horizon structure but no Coulombic hair, a positive-energy anisotropic source obeying the null energy condition, a softened central singularity, a bounded Hawking temperature, and a cold extremal remnant that ends the evaporation. The same length scale follows independently from requiring that the first law hold exactly with the corrected entropy. The nontrivial zeros survive only as exponentially suppressed log-periodic ripples in the area, which suggests a physical interpretation of the Riemann hypothesis as the statement that arithmetic corrections to black-hole thermodynamics are as small as they can be.

</details>


### [245] [Geometric formulation for the relativistic kinetic theory of photons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22881)
*Yifan Cai,Long Cui,Bin Wu,Liu Zhao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文为光子的动力学理论（kinetic theory）提供了几何基础。光锥丛（light cone bundle）Γ₀⁺上的诱导度规（induced metric）是退化的，导致体积元为零，但作者借鉴霍奇对偶（Hodge dual）的方法构造了光锥丛的体积元，并在此几何结构上建立了完全协变的（fully covariant）光子玻尔兹曼方程（Boltzmann equation），所得流体力学量及其散度与有质量粒子情形具有相同形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光子的动力学理论需要一个自洽的几何框架。由于光锥丛上的诱导度规退化，标准体积元消失，无法直接沿用有质量粒子的协变公式，因此需要构造新的几何工具来支撑光子的动力学描述。

Method: 作者利用类似霍奇对偶的方法，在退化度规的条件下为光锥丛Γ₀⁺构造了非零的体积元η_{Γ₀⁺}，形成由度规与体积元共同构成的几何结构（Γ₀⁺, η_{Γ₀⁺}, ĥ），并在此基础上推导出光子的完全协变玻尔兹曼方程。

Result: 体积元与诱导度规之间存在非平凡的联系（nontrivial link），使物理分布（physical distributions）能够被一致地定义。由此得到的流体力学量（hydrodynamic quantities）及其散度（divergences）在形式上与有质量粒子的情形完全相同。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，尽管光锥丛的度规退化，仍可通过构造适当的体积元来建立光子的几何化动力学理论，且其宏观流体力学描述与有质量粒子保持统一的形式。

Abstract: We provide a geometric foundation for the kinetic theory of photons. Although the induced metric $\hat h$ on the light cone bundle $Γ_0^+$ is degenerate, which causes the corresponding volume element to vanish, we can still use a method similar to the Hodge dual to construct a volume element $η_{Γ_0^+}$ for the light cone bundle. Based on this geometric structure $(Γ^+_0,η_{Γ_0^+},\hat h)$, we establish the fully covariant Boltzmann equation for photons. More importantly, the volume element and the induced metric are linked in a nontrivial way, which allows the physical distributions to be defined consistently. This yields the corresponding hydrodynamic quantities and their divergences, which take the same form as in the case of massive particles.

</details>


### [246] [Curvature and Isocurvature Perturbations in multi-field Gauss-Bonnet inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22970)
*Seoktae Koh*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了多场暴胀（multi-field inflation）中标量场与高斯-博内项（Gauss-Bonnet term）通过一般耦合函数耦合时的宇宙学扰动。作者推导了空间平坦规范（spatially flat gauge）下完整的二次作用量，发现高斯-博内耦合引入了一个在爱因斯坦引力（Einstein gravity）中消失的反对称速度耦合项。利用曲率扰动与等曲率扰动（isocurvature mode）的分解，证明了曲率扰动的有效质量及非导数混合质量恒为零；并在两个极限下计算了曲率功率谱，分别得到修正了声速的有效单场理论以及由转向率（turn rate）控制并受高斯-博内修正的超视界转移行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多场暴胀模型是理解早期宇宙原初扰动起源的重要框架，而高斯-博内项作为修正引力（modified gravity）中的典型高阶曲率耦合形式，其对宇宙学扰动的影响尚未得到系统研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，推导出包含高斯-博内耦合的多场暴胀中完整的扰动作用量与功率谱。

Method: 作者在空间平坦规范下推导了场扰动的完整二次作用量，将场扰动分解为曲率模式和等曲率模式，并通过Weinberg绝热模（Weinberg's adiabatic mode）论证了两个质量项恒为零。随后在两个极限下计算曲率功率谱：（1）等曲率模重时对其全尺度积分得到含修正声速的有效单场理论；（2）耦合函数梯度与背景轨迹对齐（f_N=0）时导数混合消失，超视界转移由修正后的转向率控制。此外还在耦合线性阶给出了张量功率谱与张标比。

Result: 核心结果为：高斯-贝利耦合在作用量中引入反对称速度耦合项，该项在爱因斯坦引力中消失；曲率扰动的有效质量与混合质量严格为零（M_R^2 = M_mix^2 = 0），这与Weinberg绝热模一致；在两个极限下分别得到修正声速的有效单场暴胀，以及受高斯-博内修正的转向率控制的等曲率-曲率超视界转移；张量功率谱和张标比在耦合的一阶线性近似下得以呈现。

Conclusion: 高斯-博内耦合在多场暴胀中不仅修正了动能、梯度和质量矩阵，还引入了新的反对称速度耦合，从而改变曲率扰动的动力学性质。在等曲率模重或梯度对齐的极限下，系统分别简化为修正声速的单场暴胀或由修正转向率支配的转移过程，为检验此类修正引力模型的可观测信号（如张标比）提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We study cosmological perturbations in multi-field inflation in which the scalar fields couple to the Gauss-Bonnet terms through a general coupling function $f(φ^a)$. We derive the complete quadratic action for the field perturbations in spatially flat gauge and find that, besides corrections to the kinetic, gradient and mass matrices, the Gauss-Bonnet coupling induces an antisymmetric velocity coupling that vanishes in Einstein gravity. Decomposing the field perturbations into curvature and isocurvature modes, we show that the effective mass of the curvature perturbation and the non-derivative mixing mass vanish identically, $\mathcal{M}_{\mathcal{R}}^2 = \mathcal{M}_{\rm mix}^2 =0$ which are justified by the Weinberg's adiabatic mode. We then compute the curvature power spectrum in two limits. First, when the isocurvature mode is heavy, it can be integrated over all scales. This yields an effective single-field theory of the curvature perturbation with a modified sound speed. Second, when the gradient of the coupling function is aligned with the background trajectory ($f_N =0$), the derivative mixings disappear and the superhorizon transfer of isocurvature into curvature perturbations is governed by the turn rate, which is modified by the Gauss-Bonnet corrections. We also present the tensor power spectrum and the tensor-to-scalar ratio at linear order in the coupling.

</details>


### [247] [Remarks on quantum scalar field theory for a uniformly accelerated reference frame and a Schwarzschild black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23108)
*Mikhail N. Smolyakov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文从形式化角度探讨了匀加速参考系（均加速参考系）与二维和四维 Schwarzschild 黑洞背景下量子标量场论（quantum scalar field theory）的一致性问题，指出 Minkowski 时空中定义良好的某些量子态在 Rindler 时空中具有无限能量，并揭示了四维 Schwarzschild 黑洞背景下产生 Unruh 真空态的标准理论的不自洽性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在构建自洽的量子场论（quantum field theory）并突出那些在其他文献中未得到讨论的形式化问题，特别是在弯曲时空（curved spacetime）背景下量子标量场论的一致性问题。

Method: 本文采用形式化分析（formal analysis）方法，分别对匀加速参考系（Rindler 时空）和 Schwarzschild 黑洞（在二维与四维情形）下的量子标量场论进行考察，并通过具体论证指出标准理论中存在的病态（pathologies）与不一致性（inconsistency）。

Result: 结果表明：在匀加速参考系中存在一些量子态，它们在 Minkowski 时空中定义良好，但在 Rindler 时空中具有无限能量；四维 Schwarzschild 黑洞背景下提供 Unruh 真空态的标准理论是不自洽的；Kruskal-Szekeres 时空中量子标量场论的一致性也存在问题。

Conclusion: 作者总结认为，在匀加速参考系与 Schwarzschild 黑洞背景下，标准量子场论框架存在尚未被广泛认识的形式化缺陷，这些缺陷需要通过进一步研究加以解决。

Abstract: In the present paper, the cases of a uniformly accelerated reference frame and a Schwarzschild black hole in two and four dimensions are discussed focusing on formal aspects of the corresponding quantum scalar field theories. The main emphasis is made on building consistent quantum field theories and highlighting the problems that are not considered elsewhere. In particular, some pathologies in the case of a uniformly accelerated reference frame are discussed in detail: it is shown that some quantum states that are well defined in Minkowski spacetime possess an infinite energy when considered in Rindler spacetime. In the case of a Schwarzschild black hole in four dimensions, it is shown that the standard theory providing the Unruh vacuum state turns out to be inconsistent. The problem of consistency of quantum scalar field theory in Kruskal-Szekeres spacetime is also discussed.

</details>
