### [248] [Electrostatic Persistence Length Revisited. I. Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21617)
*Artem M. Rumyantsev,Alexey A. Gavrilov,Albert Johner*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该论文针对含盐稀溶液中聚电解质单链构象及静电持续长度（electrostatic persistence length）这一长期未解问题，发展了一套综合标度理论（scaling theory）并通过模拟加以验证。研究构建了涵盖柔性及半柔性/刚性聚电解质的统一标度相图，识别出九个不同的标度区域，并引入杆状静电团（rodlike electrostatic blob）等新概念来描述链构象随盐浓度增加而经历的五个连续转变阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚电解质在含盐稀溶液中的单链构象问题以及静电持续长度的概念数十年来悬而未决，缺乏统一的理论框架来同时描述柔性和半柔性/刚性聚电解质的构象行为。作者旨在构建一个全面的标度理论，以解决这一长期存在的物理挑战。

Method: 作者发展了一套综合性的标度理论框架，构建了同时涵盖柔性及本征半柔性/刚性聚电解质的统一标度相图。将描述柔性聚电解质的Gaussian静电团（electrostatic blob）概念推广至半柔性情形，并引入新的特征长度——杆状静电团（rodlike electrostatic blob）——以实现交叉区域的划分和柔性与半柔性链之间的类比。同时重新审视并推广了静电排除体积（electrostatic excluded volume）的概念。

Result: 研究识别出九个具有不同构象统计特征的标度区域。随盐浓度增加（德拜长度r_D减小），链构象依序经历五个演化阶段：杆状拉伸、Gaussian链、具有局域静电刚化的溶胀线圈、无刚化但有静电排除体积的溶胀线圈、以及准中性Gaussian线圈。在类型(ii)和(iii)区域中，静电持续长度与r_D的平方成正比，分别与OSF理论（半柔性链）和KK理论（柔性链）的预言一致。

Conclusion: 该标度理论为聚电解质单链构象问题提供了统一的描述框架，确立了柔性链和半柔性链之间的类比关系，并通过伴随论文中的粗粒化模拟（coarse-grained simulations）验证了OSF和KK标度关系的渐近行为，为理解聚电解质在盐溶液中的构象转变提供了系统的理论依据。

Abstract: The problem of single-chain conformations of polyelectrolytes in salt-added dilute solutions, and the associated concept of the electrostatic persistence length $\textbf{l}_\mathrm{e}$, has remained unresolved for decades. To address this challenge, we develop a comprehensive scaling theory and corroborate it with simulations. A unified scaling diagram is constructed that encompasses both flexible and intrinsically semiflexible/stiff polyelectrolytes. Nine distinct scaling regimes are identified, each characterized by different conformational statistics. The concept of the Gaussian electrostatic blob $ξ_\mathrm{e}$ used in the description of flexible polyelectrolytes is extended to the semiflexible case, and the new characteristic length $ξ_\mathrm{e}^{|}$ referred to as the rodlike electrostatic blob is introduced. This enables delineating the important crossovers and showcasing an analogy between semiflexible and flexible chains. The concept of electrostatic excluded volume is also reconsidered and generalized. Upon increasing the salt concentration, i.e., decreasing the Debye length $r_\mathrm{D}$, chain conformations evolve from (i) rodlike stretches to (ii) Gaussian and then (iii) swollen coils with local electrostatic stiffening characterized by $\textbf{l}_\mathrm{e}$, followed by (iv) swollen coils without stiffening but with electrostatic exclude volume, and finally to (v) quasi-neutral Gaussian coils. In regimes of type (ii) and (iii), the electrostatic persistence length scales quadratically with $r_\mathrm{D}$, in agreement with the OSF and KK predictions for semiflexible and flexible chains, respectively: $\textbf{l}_\mathrm{\mathrm{OSF}} \simeq r_\mathrm{D}^{2}/ξ_\mathrm{e}^{|}$ and $\textbf{l}_\mathrm{\mathrm{KK}} \simeq r_\mathrm{D}^{2}/ξ_\mathrm{e}$. These asymptotic scalings are confirmed by coarse-grained simulations presented in the accompanying article.

</details>


### [249] [Electrostatic Persistence Length Revisited. II. Simulations and Comparison to Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21627)
*Alexey A. Gavrilov,Albert Johner,Artem M. Rumyantsev*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模粗粒化蒙特卡洛模拟，验证了聚电解质（polyelectrolyte）的静电持续长度（electrostatic persistence length, EPL）遵循二次方OSF/KK标度定律（l_e ~ r_D²）而非线性BJ定律（l_e ~ r_D），并重新分析实验数据支持这一结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 围绕静电持续长度控制聚电解质局部刚性存在长期争议，核心矛盾集中在两种竞争性幂律预测上：线性BJ定律（l_e ~ r_D）与二次方OSF/KK标度（l_e ~ r_D²）。本工作旨在通过大规模模拟和实验数据重分析，明确判定哪种标度律才是正确的渐近行为。

Method: 基于伴随论文发展的渐近标度理论，对带荷电单体通过屏蔽库仑势相互作用的理想链，开展长达N≈10⁴–10⁵ Kuhn段的粗粒化蒙特卡洛模拟。追踪链尺寸R随德拜半径增长所对应的指数α（R ~ r_D^α），并分别考察末端距R_ee、回转半径R_g和流体力学半径R_h三种尺寸测量量，同时重新分析已有实验数据。

Result: 半柔性与柔性聚电解质的静电增韧均遵循相同的二次方OSF/KK定律；两种情况下α随链长增加越过3/5并缓慢趋近1，而BJ理论中α不可能超过2/5。三种尺寸测量量以末端距、回转半径、流体力学半径的顺序依次达到渐近行为，故表观指数满足α_ee ≥ α_g ≥ α_h。实验数据重分析排除了线性BJ定律，支持二次方KK标度。

Conclusion: 长链聚电解质静电刚性的真实渐近标度为二次方OSF/KK定律；先前实验中看似支持线性BJ定律的表观斜率，实则是短链条件下尚未达到渐近极限的有限尺寸效应。回转半径R_g比流体力学半径R_h更适合作为实验探针来测定真渐近指数。

Abstract: For decades, debate has surrounded the electrostatic persistence length (EPL) controlling local polyelectrolyte stiffening, centered on two competing power laws: the linear BJ prediction, $\textbf{l}_\mathrm{e} \sim r_\mathrm{D}$, and the quadratic OSF/KK scaling, $\textbf{l}_\mathrm{e} \sim r_\mathrm{D}^{2}$, where $r_\mathrm{D}$ is the Debye screening length. Building on the asymptotic scaling theory developed in the accompanying paper, we validate a complete diagram of limiting regimes using large-scale coarse-grained Monte Carlo simulations of ideal chains with charged monomers interacting through a screened Coulomb potential. By simulating long chains of up to $N \simeq 10^{4} - 10^{5}$ Kuhn segments, we demonstrate that the electrostatic stiffening of both semiflexible and flexible polyelectrolytes obeys the same quadratic OSF/KK law. We track the exponent $α$ which measures how the chain size $R$ grows with the Debye radius, $R \sim r_\mathrm{D}^α$. For both cases, in agreement with the OSF/KK theory, $α$ rises past 3/5 and slowly approaches 1 as the chain length increases, whereas within the BJ theory it can never exceed 2/5. We further find that three common size measures, the end-to-end distance $R_\mathrm{ee}$, radius of gyration $R_\mathrm{g}$, and hydrodynamic radius $R_\mathrm{h}$, reach this asymptotic behavior at progressively increasing chain lengths. Consequently, for any real polyelectrolyte, the apparent exponents obey $α_\mathrm{ee} \geq α_\mathrm{g} \geq α_\mathrm{h}$, making $R_\mathrm{g}$ a sharper experimental probe than $R_\mathrm{h}$. Finally, we re-analyze the available experimental data and show that they rule out the linear BJ law and support the quadratic KK scaling, thereby resolving contradictions that stem from mistaking the apparent slopes measured for short chains for the true asymptotic exponent.

</details>


### [250] [Pitch-controlled reorientational nonlinearity in chiral nematic liquid crystals: a reduced-order model for self-focusing and soliton formation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21751)
*Homa Saadatmand,M. Javad Zakeri,Ahmed Sameh Ahmed,Suraj Bhandari,Loubna Benkoula,Ameer B. Batarseh,Andrea Blanco-Redondo,Miroslaw Karpierz,Pawel S. Jung*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向手性向列液晶（chiral nematic liquid crystals）中重取向非线性的降阶半解析模型，证明手性螺距（chiral pitch）是决定非线性自聚焦与孤子（soliton）形成的主要物理长度尺度。该模型从完整的Frank-Oseen方程出发，推导出光诱导分子旋转的闭式表达式，在捕捉介质饱和非线性响应的同时，将计算成本比标准弛豫法求解器降低两个数量级以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值求解完整Frank-Oseen方程成本高昂，缺乏兼具物理洞察与计算效率的模型来理解手性向列液晶中的非线性自聚焦和孤子形成机制，难以支撑非线性光子器件的设计与优化。

Method: 从完整的Frank-Oseen方程出发，推导光诱导分子旋转的闭式解析表达式，构建一个捕捉介质饱和非线性响应的降阶半解析模型，并以弛豫法（relaxation method）求解结果为基准进行对比验证。

Result: 该模型在保持简单性的同时，成功复现了数值求解得到的非线性折射率、自局域化（self-localization）阈值以及一维和二维中离散到连续孤子的转变特征；还预测了完全局域化的像散型向列子（astigmatic nematicons）的形成，仅因忽略非局域效应而在自局域化阈值上产生微小偏移。

Conclusion: 该模型为软物质介质中的光-物质相互作用提供了直接的物理洞见，并为非线性光子器件的设计与优化提供了一种计算高效的工具，其计算成本较标准求解器降低两个数量级以上。

Abstract: We present a reduced-order semi-analytical model for reorientational nonlinearity in chiral nematic liquid crystals, showing that the chiral pitch acts as the dominant physical length scale governing the onset of nonlinear self-focusing and soliton formation. Starting from the full Frank-Oseen equation, we derive a closed-form expression for the optically induced molecular rotation that captures the essential saturable response of the medium while reducing computational cost by more than two orders of magnitude compared with standard relaxation-method solvers. Despite its simplicity, the model reproduces the essential features of the numerically obtained nonlinear refractive index, the onset of self-localization, and the transition from discrete to continuous solitons in one and two dimensions. It further predicts the formation of fully localized astigmatic nematicons with only minor shifts in the self-localization threshold due to the neglect of nonlocal effects. The proposed model provides direct physical insight into light-matter interactions with soft matter media and offers a computationally efficient tool for the design and optimization of nonlinear photonic devices.

</details>


### [251] [Voltage dynamics of spherical membranes from single ion channel currents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22121)
*Sirui Ning,Joshua B. Fernandes,Karthik Shekhar,Kranthi K. Mandadapu*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文结合理论与数值模拟，解析了球形膜囊泡上由局部电流驱动的电荷重组的时空动力学，揭示了跨膜电压从单极衰减到偶极尾部、再到空间均匀电容充电模态的演化过程，并推导了非局域电缆方程（nonlocal cable equation），为电紧张紧致细胞（electrotonically compact cell）的电生理简化提供了第一性原理基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离子通道和泵在细胞膜局部位点驱动离子选择性电流，但细胞的电学状态通常仅用一个跨膜电压（transmembrane voltage）概括。这种简化缺乏严格的物理基础，因此需要从第一性原理出发理解局部电流如何在球形膜上传播并最终趋于均匀化。

Method: 论文综合运用理论与数值模拟方法，分析球形膜囊泡上局部电流驱动的电荷重组时空动力学，将体相势（bulk potentials）分解为静电镜像电荷分量（electrostatic image-charge component）与空间均匀的电容模态（capacitive mode），并推导了控制跨膜电压动力学的非局域电缆方程。

Result: 早期阶段，响应与膜几何无关：跨膜电压以单极方式衰减，随距源距离的倒数变化，并交叉过渡到与距离三次方成反比的偶极尾部。在持续电流下，单极响应向外扩散；由于囊泡闭合，该响应不能无限持续。一旦单极前沿遍历整个囊泡，后续充电动力学由对应膜电容充电的空间均匀模态主导。均匀模态可表示为等效电路，且是所推导的非局域电缆方程的长时间解。

Conclusion: 该工作为电紧张紧致细胞中用电生理学简化描述跨膜电压提供了基于第一性原理的理论依据，阐明了局部电流如何通过时空演化最终收敛为空间均匀的电容充电行为。

Abstract: Ion channels and pumps drive ion-selective currents through cell membranes at localized sites, yet a cell's electrical state is routinely summarized by a single transmembrane voltage. Combining theory and numerical simulations, we resolve the spatiotemporal dynamics of charge reorganization driven by a localized current on a spherical membrane vesicle. At early times, the response is insensitive to membrane geometry: as in the case of a flat membrane (arXiv:2407.11947; arXiv:2508.14001), the transmembrane voltage decays in a monopolar fashion, varying inversely with distance from the source, and crosses over to a dipolar tail that scales as the inverse cube of distance. Under sustained current, this monopolar response spreads outward from the source. Because the vesicle is closed, this response cannot persist indefinitely; once the monopolar front traverses the entire vesicle, the subsequent charging dynamics is dominated by a spatially uniform mode corresponding to capacitive charging of the membrane. We further decompose the bulk potentials into an electrostatic image-charge component that generates the bulk electric fields and a spatially uniform capacitive mode that can be represented as an equivalent circuit. We also derive a nonlocal cable equation governing the transmembrane voltage dynamics and show that the uniform mode is its long-time solution. This work provides a first-principles basis for the electrophysiological simplification of an electrotonically compact cell.

</details>


### [252] [Beyond second-long trajectory of the Trp-cage peptide generated using a Kinetic Monte Carlo model derived from molecular dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21990)
*Abhijit Chatterjee,Rishav Deb,Gauri Thapa,Swati Bhattacharya*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文提出一种动力学蒙特卡洛（kinetic Monte Carlo, KMC）建模方法，用于描述肽分子从纳秒到秒时间尺度的随机构象动力学。以Trp-cage微小蛋白为例，模型从多条分子动力学（molecular dynamics, MD）轨迹中学习二面角跃迁，引入骨架相关性（backbone correlations），可在普通台式机数小时内达到秒级时间尺度，并广泛适用于蛋白质构象动力学模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质构象变化的随机动力学跨越纳秒到秒的时间尺度，传统分子动力学模拟难以在合理计算成本内覆盖如此长的时间范围。作者旨在开发一种能够跨越这一时间尺度鸿沟的建模方法。

Method: 采用KMC方法，将蛋白质构象变化的动力学在局部层面解释为二面角跃迁（dihedral transitions）事件。模型通过局部划分-求解（divide-and-conquer）策略，从多条MD轨迹中学习离散化的骨架二面角状态及其跃迁率。关键创新在于引入骨架相关性，使跃迁率依赖于局部环境和空间位阻耦合（steric coupling）。以Trp-cage微小蛋白为测试算例。

Result: 包含相关性信息的KMC模型与MD结果高度吻合，能够在普通台式机上以数小时CPU时间达到秒级时间尺度，并能轻松生成多条构象动力学的随机时间序列实现。

Conclusion: 该KMC建模方案具有广泛的蛋白质适用性，可以作为连接局部柔性与蛋白质整体动力学的桥梁，用于跨尺度的蛋白质构象动力学研究。

Abstract: We present a kinetic Monte Carlo (KMC) modeling approach to describe the stochastic dynamics of a peptide molecule spanning nanosecond to second timescales. The dynamics of protein conformational changes is interpreted at a local level in terms of dihedral transitions. Taking Trp-cage miniprotein as an example, the KMC model "learns" about the transitions from multiple MD trajectories. Training is based on local divide-and-conquer strategy that identifies the discretized backbone dihedral states as building blocks for the conformational space, along with associated transition rates of dihedral flips to describe the conformational state-to-state dynamics. A key feature in our approach is the incorporation of backbone correlations, such that rates are conditioned on the local environment and steric coupling. We show that with the correlations built-in, the KMC model closely matches MD. Such an approach is shown to reach second timescales in a few CPU hours on a standard desktop computer, and can easily yield multiple stochastic realizations of the conformational dynamics. Our KMC model construction scheme should be generally applicable to a wide range of proteins, and can be used for bridging local flexibility to protein-wide dynamics.

</details>


### [253] [Collective dynamics of chemo-mechanical colloidal chains with active tips](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22318)
*Arvin Gopal Subramaniam,Rajesh Singh*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文通过粗粒化模拟研究了二维半柔性链悬浮液中由尖端化学活性（tip chemical activity）产生的趋光场（phoretic field）所驱动的涌现动力学，系统绘制出包含瞬态混沌流、全局极性群体、介观湍流和仿两亲性胶束聚集等多重集体态的非平衡相图，并提出尖端趋光活性是实现这些集体态的最小实验可行机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有集体态（如群体运动和有源湍流）通常归因于水动力相互作用（hydrodynamic interactions）或立体对齐相互作用（steric alignment interactions），而化学活性尖端作为驱动机制尚未被系统探索。本文旨在建立趋光尖端活性作为产生多种集体态的最小统一机制的可行性。

Method: 通过改变链长（每链单体数N_pc）、面积分数φ和趋光耦合符号J_0，对半柔性链二维悬浮体系进行模拟，绘制非平衡相图；并发展了一个尖端发射型有源棒的粗粒化理论（coarse-grained theory）以预测二聚体群体的起始条件。

Result: 对于排斥性趋光相互作用（J_0 > 0），短链（N_pc = 2）表现出瞬态混沌流态，长时间后演化为具有超扩散均方位移和长程速度关联的全局极性群体（global polar flock），且出现密度涨落抑制的超均匀性（hyperuniformity）；中等链长（N_pc ≈ 4–8）则驱动介观尺度混沌流——即无需水动力或立体对齐相互作用的干态有源湍流（dry active turbulence）。对于吸引性相互作用（J_0 < 0），链自组织为头部成核、柔性尾向外辐射的刺猬状胶束聚集。

Conclusion: 趋光尖端活性为一系列此前归因于水动力或立体对齐相互作用的集体态提供了一种最小且实验上可实现的机制；粗粒化理论能预测二聚体群体行为的起始，但对长链极性有序的程度存在高估。

Abstract: We report a study of the emergent dynamics arising in two-dimensional suspensions of semi-flexible chains whose tip is chemically active, generating a phoretic field. By varying the chain length (number of monomers per chain $N_{pc}$), the area fraction $φ$, and the sign of the phoretic coupling $J_0$, we map out a rich non-equilibrium phase diagram in the presence of phoretic interactions. For repulsive phoretic interactions ($J_0 > 0$) between the chains, we find that short chains ($N_{pc} = 2$) develop a transient chaotic flow state that crosses over at long times to a global polar flock with super-diffusive mean-squared displacement and long-ranged velocity correlations. Surprisingly, we find this state to have suppressed density fluctuations, indicating the emergence of hyperuniformity. At intermediate chain lengths ($N_{pc} \sim 4$-$8$), the repulsive chemical field drives chaotic mesoscale flows -- a dry route to active turbulence -- without the need for hydrodynamic interactions or steric alignment interactions. For attractive phoretic interactions ($J_0 < 0$), chains self-organise into hedgehog-like micellar aggregates with heads forming the core and flexible tails radiating outward, in structural analogy with amphiphile micellisation but driven entirely by non-equilibrium self-propulsion. A coarse-grained theory of a tip-emitting active rod predicts the onset of the flocking of dimers, though overestimates the presence of polar order for longer chains. Our results establish phoretic tip activity as a minimal, experimentally realisable mechanism for a spectrum of collective states hitherto attributed to hydrodynamic interactions or steric alignment.

</details>


### [254] [Shapes, forces, and torques of compressed elastic fluid interfaces: beyond axisymmetric configurations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22567)
*Xinyi Liu,Neelesh A. Patankar,Leroy L. Jia*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文受经典肥皂膜 Plateau–Douglas 问题的启发，研究了固定表面积且具有抗面外弯曲能力的流体界面（fluid interface）的平衡问题。平面边界无需对称或同心，平衡面在最小化 Willmore 弯曲能的同时推广了悬链面（catenoid）等极小曲面，并展现出受限弹性界面（confined elastic interface）的屈曲特征。通过弱非线性分析和全非线性谱求解器，系统分类了所有可能的屈曲模态（buckling mode）和解分支，为稳定细胞膜等软材料的力与力矩需求提供了理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 Plateau–Douglas 问题描述了由两条闭合曲线界定的肥皂膜的极小曲面，但真实生物膜等流体界面不仅具有固定表面积，还抵抗面外弯曲。作者希望在更一般（非对称、非同心）的边界条件下求解此类界面的平衡形态，以填补现有极小曲面理论对弯曲刚度（bending stiffness）效应考虑不足的空白。

Method: 采用变分原理将平衡面定义为最小化 Willmore 弯曲能的曲面；通过弱非线性分析（weakly nonlinear analysis）研究系统在小扰动下的分岔行为，并开发了全非线性谱求解器（fully nonlinear spectral solver）以数值求解完整的非线性平衡方程，从而系统分类屈曲模态与解分支。

Result: 研究发现平衡曲面在推广极小曲面（如悬链面）的同时展示了受限弹性界面的典型屈曲特征；系统分类了所有可能的屈曲模态和解分支，并揭示了即使在完全对称的系统中，物理上相关的非对称状态（asymmetric state）也能够出现。

Conclusion: 该数学框架为理解细胞膜及其他软材料稳定所需的外力与力矩提供了定量洞见，表明弯曲刚度与表面积约束共同作用可诱导对称破缺的平衡构型，对生物膜力学和软材料设计具有指导意义。

Abstract: Inspired by the classical Plateau-Douglas problem for soap films bounded by two closed curves, we solve an analogous problem for fluid interfaces with fixed surface area and resistance to out-of-plane bending. The boundaries are planar but need not be symmetric or concentric. The equilibrium surfaces minimize the Willmore bending energy and generalize minimal surfaces such as the catenoid while also exhibiting characteristic features of confined elastic interfaces such as buckling. We systematically classify all possible buckling modes and solution branches by performing a weakly nonlinear analysis and developing a fully nonlinear spectral solver. Our mathematical framework provides insight into the forces and torques required to stabilize cellular membranes and other soft materials and shows that physically relevant asymmetric states can arise even in symmetric systems.

</details>


### [255] [Collective self-sorting on a chip](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22673)
*Emanuel F. Teixeira,Thieu van den Bergh,Arjen Klok,Tijn Heesakkers,Alexandre Morin*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文利用两种已知的集体去混合机制——差异速度和曲率——构建了一种自分类装置。通过粒子基模拟识别促进高效分离的几何条件，并将这些物理机制转化为一系列弯曲微流控单元，实验验证了二元Quincke转子混合物在该装置中逐步去混合并富集的现象，展示了将集体活性去混合转化为功能性连续分选策略的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已分别探索了活性粒子在二元混合物中的迁移性差异驱动自发空间分离，以及约束几何影响去混合发展速度与强度，但尚未将这些机制系统性地整合为实用的分选器件。作者旨在将集体活性去混合原理转化为一种基于粒子迁移率和约束几何的功能性连续微流控分选策略。

Method: 使用粒子基模拟（particle-based simulations）系统扫描几何条件以确定促进高效分离的设计参数，并据此设计有限分选架构；将物理机制转化为一系列弯曲微流控单元（curved microfluidic units），逐步放大两种粒子的分离效果并引导其进入不同收集区域；实验采用二元Quincke转子（Quincke rollers）混合物进行验证。

Result: 模拟确定了促进高效分离的几何条件并指导了有限分选架构的设计；实验证实初始混合的Quincke转子悬浮液在流经装置时逐步去混合，下游实现了显著的富集效果。

Conclusion: 研究证明集体活性去混合可以转化为一种功能性连续分选策略，为基于粒子迁移率和约束几何的自主微流控分离提供了新路径。

Abstract: We harness two established ingredients for collective demixing: differential speed and curvature to create a self-sorting device. In binary mixtures, motility differences drive spontaneous spatial segregation, while confinement geometry determines how rapidly and strongly this demixing develops. Using particle based simulations, we systematically identify the geometrical conditions that promote efficient segregation and use these results to guide the design of a finite sorting architecture. We then translate these physical mechanisms into a sequence of curved microfluidic units that progressively amplify the separation of the two species and direct them toward distinct collection regions. Experiments with binary Quincke-roller mixtures confirm that an initially mixed suspension progressively demixes as it propagates through the device, leading to strong enrichment downstream. Our results demonstrate how collective active demixing can be converted into a functional continuous sorting strategy, providing a route toward autonomous microfluidic separation based on particle motility and confinement geometry.

</details>


### [256] [Spontaneous currents determine capillary rise in active matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22878)
*Xinyi Dong,Yongfeng Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究从微观动力学出发，建立了主动流体的尤林定律（Jurin's law）主动形式，指出由粒子流产生的拖曳力是毛细上升的根源，并发现主动流体的毛细上升不仅取决于管径，还与管道的形状有关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统无源被动流体的毛细上升可用尤林定律描述，其受表面张力与重力之间的力平衡控制。而对于由成对相互作用的有源粒子组成的流体，机械表面张力为负值，但实验上仍观察到毛细上升现象，这一矛盾尚缺乏理论解释，构成了研究的出发点。

Method: 作者从微观动力学（microscopic dynamics）出发，推导出主动流体中尤林定律的有效形式。该形式中包含一个由粒子流（particle currents）产生的拖曳力项，被证实是主动流体中毛细上升现象的驱动因素。

Result: 研究发现，主动粒子流与负的表面张力共同作用，产生了平衡态下不可能出现的复杂且反直觉的毛细作用现象。具体而言，主动流体的毛细上升高度不仅取决于管道直径，还与管道的形状相关。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了主动流体的尤林定律主动形式，解释了有源粒子流体中毛细上升的来源，揭示了主动毛细作用与平衡态毛细作用的本质差别，并指出管道几何形状在主动流体毛细现象中的关键作用。

Abstract: The capillary rise of simple passive fluids in a tube is controlled by the force balance between surface tension and gravity --Jurin's law of capillary action. For fluids composed of active particles interacting via pairwise forces, mechanical surface tension is negative, but a capillary rise was nevertheless reported and remains unexplained. We establish the active form of Jurin's law from the microscopic dynamics. It includes a drag emerging from particle currents that we find responsible for capillary rise. These active currents, alongside negative surface tension, lead to complex and counterintuitive capillary action phenomena that are impossible in equilibrium. In particular the capillary rise of active fluids depends on the shape of the tube, not solely on the tube diameter.

</details>


### [257] [Comparison of mechanical and molecular measures of mobility during constant strain rate deformation of a PMMA glass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23434)
*Benjamin Bending,M. D. Ediger*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本研究在聚甲基丙烯酸甲酯（PMMA）玻璃处于玻璃化转变温度以下19 K的条件下进行恒应变速率变形与应力松弛实验，结合机械力与分子探针光学方法测量体系流动性，发现屈服前两种流动性测量结果相差两个数量级，而屈服后趋于一致，且动态异质性在变形过程中先降低后保持恒定，为聚合物玻璃变形理论模型提出了挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解聚合物玻璃（polymer glass）在外力作用下的变形机制对其理论建模至关重要，而现有文献中关于机械流动性（mechanical mobility）与链段流动性（segmental mobility）在变形过程中的关系尚不明确，尤其缺乏二者在屈服点前后行为差异的系统对比。

Method: 在Tg–19 K温度下对聚甲基丙烯酸甲酯（poly(methyl methacrylate)）玻璃施加三种不同应变速率的恒定应变率变形，并在大范围应变值下启动应力松弛实验；以松弛初始阶段应力衰减速率作为体系机械流动性的纯力学指标，同时利用分子探针的再取向（reorientation）进行光学测量以表征链段流动性。

Result: 机械方法测得的流动性在屈服前变化不超过3倍，而探针测得的流动性在屈服前增加可达100倍；屈服后两种方法测得的流动性非常接近且对应变速率的依赖性相似；动态异质性（dynamic heterogeneity）在恒应变率变形初期下降，屈服后保持恒定。

Conclusion: 机械流动性与探针流动性在屈服前存在巨大差异，说明屈服前两种测量反映的体系特征不同，而屈服后二者趋于一致；这些组合观测结果对现有聚合物玻璃变形的理论建模提出了挑战。

Abstract: We performed constant strain rate deformation and stress relaxation on a poly(methyl methacrylate) glass at Tg - 19 K, utilizing three strain rates and initiating the stress relaxation over a large range of strain values. Following previous workers, we interpret the initial rate of decay of the stress during the relaxation experiment as a purely mechanical measure of mobility for the system. In our experiments, the mechanical mobility obtained in this manner changes by less than a factor of 3 prior to yield. During these mechanical experiments, we also performed an optical measurement of segmental mobility based upon the reorientation of a molecular probe; we observe that the probe mobility increases up to a factor of 100 prior to yield. In the post-yield regime, in contrast, the mobilities determined mechanically and by probe reorientation are quite similar and show a similar dependence upon the strain rate. Dynamic heterogeneity is found to initially decrease during constant strain rate deformation and then remain constant in the post-yield regime. These combined observations of mechanical mobility, probe mobility, and dynamic heterogeneity present a challenge for theoretical modeling of polymer glass deformation.

</details>


### [258] [Highly Stable Glasses of cis-Decalin and cis/trans-Decalin Mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23443)
*Katherine R. Whitaker,Daniel J. Scifo,M. D. Ediger,Mathias Ahrenberg,Christoph Schick*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本研究利用原位交流纳米量热法（in situ AC nanocalorimetry）测量了气相沉积十氢化萘（decalin）玻璃的可逆热容，首次报道了分子混合物可形成高度稳定的玻璃，其中50/50顺反式十氢化萘混合物是已知形成超稳定玻璃（ultrastable glass）最高脆性（fragility）的材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索分子混合物是否能够像纯分子体系一样通过气相沉积形成超稳定玻璃，以扩展超稳定玻璃的研究范围并加深对其形成机理的理解。

Method: 采用原位交流纳米量热法（in situ AC nanocalorimetry）测量气相沉积十氢化萘玻璃的可逆热容，并与液体冷却玻璃进行比较，评估其动力学稳定性（kinetic stability）和热容特性。

Result: 由顺式十氢化萘及其与反式十氢化萘的多种混合物制备出的玻璃具有比液体冷却玻璃更低的热容和更高的动力学稳定性；50/50混合物在116 K（0.86 Tg）下沉积的约500 nm薄膜，其转化时间相当于退火温度下过冷液体结构松弛时间（structural relaxation time）的104.4倍；稳定玻璃的热容比液体冷却玻璃低达4.5%。

Conclusion: 这是首次报道分子混合物可形成高稳定玻璃，50/50顺反式十氢化萘混合物代表目前报道形成超稳定玻璃的最高脆性材料类别，拓宽了超稳定玻璃的适用范围。

Abstract: In situ AC nanocalorimetry was used to measure the reversing heat capacity of vapor-deposited glasses of decahydronaphthalene (decalin). Glasses with low heat capacity and high kinetic stability, as compared to the corresponding liquid-cooled glass, were prepared from cis-decalin and from several cis/trans-decalin mixtures. This is the first report of highly stable glass formation for molecular mixtures. The 50/50 cis/trans-decalin mixture is the highest fragility material reported to produce an ultrastable glass. The 50/50 mixture exhibited high kinetic stability, with an ~500 nm film deposited at 116 K (0.86 Tg,) displaying a transformation time equivalent to 104.4 times the structural relaxation time of the supercooled liquid at the annealing temperature. Cis-decalin and the decalin mixture formed stable glasses that had heat capacities as much as 4.5% lower than the liquid-cooled glass.

</details>


### [259] [One micron length scale controls kinetic stability of low energy glasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23454)
*Kenneth L. Kearns,M. D. Ediger,Heiko Huth,Christoph Schick*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文利用交流纳米量热法（AC nanocalorimetry）测量了低能态吲哚美辛（indomethacin）玻璃在等温转变为过冷液体过程中的可逆热容，发现薄膜厚度从75 nm增加到600 nm时转变时间增加一个数量级以上，而在1.4至30微米之间转变时间趋于恒定，表明存在截然不同的体相转变路径，并在1微米处观察到厚度依赖性的交叉，被解读为体相转变引发位点间的平均距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解低能态玻璃向过冷液体转变的动力学机制，尤其是转变是在表面引发还是体相引发的，以及薄膜厚度对转变动力学的影响，对于深化玻璃化转变（glass transition）理论具有重要意义。

Method: 采用交流纳米量热法（AC nanocalorimetry）测量低能态吲哚美辛玻璃在等温转化过程中可逆热容（reversing heat capacity）随时间的变化，并系统考察薄膜厚度从75 nm到30微米范围内转变动力学的尺寸依赖性。

Result: 当膜厚从75 nm增至600 nm时，转变时间增加超过一个数量级，符合表面引发转变机制；而在1.4至30微米范围内转变时间保持不变，表明存在独立的体相转变路径；对接近1微米尺度的玻璃样品观测到前所未有的尺寸依赖转变动力学。

Conclusion: 1微米处厚度依赖性的交叉预示着低能态玻璃体相转变引发位点间的平均距离约为1微米，这一发现为表面引发与体相引发转变机制的界限提供了定量判断。

Abstract: AC nanocalorimetry was used to measure the reversing heat capacity Cp of low energy indomethacin glasses as they isothermally transform into the supercooled liquid. As the film thickness increases from 75 to 600 nm, the transformation time increases by more than an order of magnitude, consistent with a surface-initiated transformation mechanism. Eventually, the transformation time becomes constant for films between 1.4 and 30 microns indicating a distinct bulk transformation pathway. The observation of size-dependent transformation kinetics for glass samples approaching 1 micron is unprecedented. We interpret the crossover in thickness dependence at 1 micron to signify the average distance between transformation initiation sites in the bulk low energy glass.

</details>


### [260] [Reaching the thermodynamic limit of wicking on textured surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23487)
*Zhaoyang Lv,Chen Ma,Li-Chen Huang,Yanshen Li*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文通过调控表面柱状结构的形状与排列方式，消除了纹理表面弱亲水液体芯吸（wicking）过程中由纹理引发的能量势垒，使芯吸所需的最小润湿性达到理论极限，并首次实现接触角高达82°的芯吸现象，同时该表面的芯吸系数甚至可超过相同孔隙率下的矩形沟槽表面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然能量最小化理论预测弱亲水液体可以在纹理化表面上自发渗入，但实际中因纹理引发的能量势垒（energy barriers）阻碍了此类液体的自发芯吸，这构成了一种理论与实验之间的差距。解决这一问题对于推动生物医学和热管理技术具有重要意义。

Method: 作者通过调整表面的柱状微结构（pillars）的形状与排列方式，设计出能够规避纹理诱导能量势垒的纹理化表面，从而将芯吸所需的润湿性要求推进至理论极限。

Result: 研究首次报道了芯吸接触角高达82°的弱亲水液体芯吸现象，该值远超传统认知。此外，该表面的芯吸系数（wicking coefficient）在相同孔隙率下可高于传统矩形沟槽（rectangular grooves）表面的对应值。

Conclusion: 通过合理设计纹理几何结构可消除能量势垒，使弱亲水液体在纹理化表面实现理论极限芯吸，该发现有望显著推动生物医学与热管理技术的发展。

Abstract: Wicking in a capillary tube could happen as long as the liquid contact angle is smaller than 90 degree, making it possible for weak hydrophilic liquids to spontaneously invade the tube. For textured surfaces, energy minimization argument predicts the same. However, wicking of weak hydrophilic liquids on textured surfaces has not been possible due to energy barriers induced by the textures. We demonstrate how these barriers could be avoided by adjusting the shape and arrangement of the pillars, thus the wettability required for wicking reaches the theoretical limit. An unprecedented wicking contact angle of 82 degree is reported. More surprisingly, wicking coefficients of such surfaces can be larger than that of rectangular grooves at the same porosity. These findings may significantly advance biomedical and thermal management technologies.

</details>


<div id='math.AP'></div>

# math.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [261] [A simple derivation of Anderson-Hartree equations with harmonic potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22437)
*Samaël Mackowiak*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文展示如何将Knowles和Pickl对Hartree方程的推导方法推广到哈密顿量为Anderson-Hermite算符（Anderson-Hermite operator）的情形（一维、二维及三维形式化情形），通过证明极限方程解的、在有界集内对噪声一致的能量估计，实现了双重极限——即平均场极限（mean-field limit）与噪声极限可以交换次序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KNOWles和Pickl针对Hartree方程已有的推导方法仅适用于通常情形，尚未覆盖哈密顿量涉及Anderson-Hermite算符（即带随机噪声的单粒子哈密顿量）时的场景。作者希望通过推广该推导，在噪声存在的情形下同样建立平均场极限的理论保证，并进一步考察两种极限的可交换性。

Method: 作者将Knowles-Pickl的Hartree方程推导方法适配于一维和二维的Anderson-Hermite算符情形，以及三维的形式化Anderson-Hermite算符情形。通过对极限方程解建立能量估计（energy estimates），并确保该估计在噪声参数的有界集上一致成立，从而构造出可交换的平均场极限与噪声极限。

Result: 作者成功论证了在噪声的有界集内一致有效的能量估计，由此实现了双重极限的交换，即平均场极限与噪声极限可以交换次序（commute）。

Conclusion: 本文的主要结论是：在含有Anderson-Hermite型噪声的单粒子哈密顿量系统中，平均场极限与噪声极限可以交换次序。这一结果为具有随机性单粒子算符的多体量子系统的平均场动力学分析提供了理论依据。

Abstract: In this paper, we show how to adapt a derivation of Hartree equations due to Knowles and Pickl in the case where the one-particle hamiltonian is an Anderson-Hermite operator in dimension 1 and 2 or a formal Anderson-Hermite operator in dimension 3. By proving energy estimates for solution of the limit equation, uniform in the noise in bounded sets, we achieve a double limit. Namely, we show that we can commute the mean-field limit with a limit in noise.

</details>


### [262] [The Wave Kinetic Theory for Quasilinear MMT Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22710)
*Huaxiang Lü*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究一维拟线性（quasilinear）Majda–McLaughlin–Tabak（MMT）方程在大环面上的适定性与动力学行为。在区域长度趋于无穷、非线性强度消失的极限下，作者证明解的适定性时间尺度与弥散指数σ之间存在二分性：σ∈(1,2]时仅在平凡共振下解存在至T0~α^{-5/4+}∧α^{-1/(1-β)+}，对应简并波动力学方程；σ∈(0,1)时解存在至T0~α^{-1-}，二阶统计量可由波动力学方程良好逼近。证明分两步：先建立截断方程的随机性传播以克服导数损失，再通过确定性高阶能量估计和自举论证延拓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 与半线性（semilinear）弥散模型不同，拟线性结构导致不可避免的导数损失，使得无法通过Duhamel公式迭代构造解，因此需要发展新方法研究此类方程的动力学行为与波湍流（wave turbulence）的涌现。

Method: 证明分为两大步骤：首先，对适当截断的方程建立随机性传播（propagation of randomness）性质，以克服拟线性结构带来的导数损失并恢复动力学描述；其次，进行确定性高阶能量估计并结合自举（bootstrap）论证，将解延拓至目标时间尺度T0。

Result: 对于σ∈(1,2]，作者以高概率证明解存在至T0~α^{-5/4+}∧α^{-1/(1-β)+}，且仅出现平凡共振，导致简并的波动率方程（degenerate wave kinetic equation）；对于σ∈(0,1)，解存在至T0~α^{-1-}，且二阶统计量可由波动率方程良好逼近。两种情形下解均保持光滑，在合适的L∞型范数下具有小性，尽管总能量很大。

Conclusion: 拟线性MMT方程的动力学行为依赖于弥散指数σ，存在清晰的二分性：较小σ对应非平凡的波动率动力学刻画，而较大σ导致简并情形。所提出的方法——随机性传播结合确定性能量估计与自举——为处理拟线性弥散方程的导数损失提供了有效路径。

Abstract: We study the one-dimensional quasilinear Majda--McLaughlin--Tabak (MMT) equation on a large torus $[0,L]$: \begin{align*}
  i \partial_t u +2π|\nabla|^σu +λ^{2}|\nabla|^β\left[ \left||\nabla|^βu\right|^{2} |\nabla|^βu\right]=0. \end{align*} Our focus is on the well-posedness of its dynamics and the emergence of kinetic behavior where the domain size $L$ tends to infinity and the nonlinearity $α=λ^2L^{-1}$ vanishes. In contrast to semilinear dispersive models, the quasilinear structure leads to unavoidable derivative loss, which prevents the construction of solutions via iteration of the Duhamel formula.
  Our results exhibit a dichotomy depending on the dispersion exponent $σ$. For $σ\in(1,2]$, we prove that, with high probability, solutions exist up to time scales $T_0 \sim α^{-\frac54+} \wedge α^{-\frac1{1-β}+}$, and that only trivial resonances occur, leading to a degenerate wave kinetic equation. For $σ\in(0,1)$, we prove the existence up to time scales $T_0 \sim α^{-1-}$ and show that the second-order statistics are well approximated by the wave kinetic equation. In both cases, the solutions remain smooth while exhibiting smallness in suitable $L^\infty$-based norms despite having large total energy. The proof proceeds in two main steps. First, we establish the propagation of randomness for a suitably truncated equation, which allows us to overcome the derivative loss and recover the kinetic description. Then, we perform deterministic high-order energy estimates and a bootstrap argument to extend the solution up to time $T_0$.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.quant-gas'></div>

# cond-mat.quant-gas [[Back]](#toc)

### [263] [Dynamical Signatures and Kibble-Zurek Scaling of Localization in Tilted Bose-Einstein Condensates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21492)
*Argha Debnath,Radheshyam Saha,Ravindra W. Chhajlany,Mariusz Gajda,Debraj Rakshit*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究了一维浅光学晶格中玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体（Bose-Einstein condensate）中倾斜（tilt）诱导局域化的非平衡特征，通过相互作用强度和外部倾斜的突然淬灭（quench）及有限速率扫描，结合局域化长度和Bogoliubov激发能隙进行Kibble-Zurek标度分析，确立淬灭响应和有限速率标度作为相互作用的玻色气体中局域化的互补动力学探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解倾斜光学晶格中玻色气体局域化（localization）的动力学特征，并探索局域化-去局域化交叉（localization-delocalization crossover）的非平衡探测手段。

Method: 以倾斜强度作为局域化-去局域化交叉的控制参数，在浅光学晶格中的一维玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体中，对相互作用强度和外部倾斜进行突然淬灭以表征局域化与去局域化区域，通过生存概率（survival probability）及其功率谱密度（power spectral density）分析动力学；随后进行倾斜强度的有限速率扫描（finite-rate ramps），利用局域化长度（localization length）和Bogoliubov激发能隙提取关键临界指数，并执行Kibble-Zurek标度（Kibble-Zurek scaling）分析。

Result: 局域化凝聚体表现出强记忆保持、显著回响（revivals）、规则动力学和窄谱响应；去局域化凝聚体则显示被抑制的重现、不规则动力学和更宽的频谱权重分布。此外，通过有限速率驱动提取了临界指数并完成了Kibble-Zurek标度分析。

Conclusion: 淬灭响应和有限速率标度是研究相互作用的玻色气体中局域化的互补动力学探针，其结果直接适用于倾斜光学晶格中的冷原子实验。

Abstract: We study nonequilibrium signatures of tilt-induced localization in a one-dimensional Bose-Einstein condensate loaded in a shallow optical lattice. The tilt strength acts as a control parameter for the localization-delocalization crossover. We also consider the effects of repulsive interactions, which tend to delocalize the condensate. We first characterize localized and delocalized regimes through sudden quenches of the interaction strength and the external tilt. The resulting dynamics is analyzed using the survival probability and its power spectral density. Localized condensates exhibit strong memory retention, pronounced revivals, regular dynamics and a narrow spectral response, whereas delocalized condensates show suppressed recurrences, irregular dynamics and a broader distribution of spectral weight over many frequencies. We then investigate finite-rate ramps of the tilt strength across the localization threshold. Using the localization length and the Bogoliubov excitation gap, we extract the relevant critical exponents and perform Kibble-Zurek scaling analysis in the driven dynamics. Our results establish quench response and finite-rate scaling as complementary dynamical probes of localization in interacting Bose gases, with direct relevance to cold-atom experiments in tilted optical lattices.

</details>


### [264] [Breakdown of Aharonov-Bohm cage in Rydberg synthetic lattices: the roles of inhomogeneity and long-range exchange](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21768)
*Li Pan,Xinlu Chen,Hong Zhang,Jian-Song Pan*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文利用演化路径对称性（evolution-path symmetry, EPS）框架，系统分析了Rydberg合成晶格中交换相互作用导致的Aharonov-Bohm（AB）笼破缺机制，揭示了均匀与不均匀交换相互作用对粒子输运的截然不同影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往认为相互诱导的AB笼破缺归因于均匀束缚对输运，但具有不均匀交换相互作用的Rydberg合成晶格系统表现出更复杂的动力学，需要更精确的理论框架来解析其输运机制。

Method: 采用近期发展的演化路径对称性（evolution-path symmetry）框架，在Fock空间中通过路径干涉分析双粒子动力学，并引入长程与最近邻交换相互作用的组合进行系统考察。

Result: 均匀的最近邻交换相互作用无论是否存在长程交换相互作用均不能破坏AB笼；相反，不均匀的最近邻交换相互作用破坏相消干涉的EPS，并解除多体紧致局域态的简并，生成非局域的色散本征态，使初始态获得非零重叠从而实现离域输运。此外，仅长程交换相互作用保留AB笼，但其与不均匀最近邻交换相互作用的耦合打开了不可抵消的路径，改变扩散轮廓。

Conclusion: 该工作将微观路径干涉与宏观谱重组联系起来，为Rydberg合成晶格中交换相互作用诱导输运的机制提供了解析理解。

Abstract: While the interaction-induced breakdown of Aharonov-Bohm (AB) cage is typically attributed to uniform bound-pair transport, systems with inhomogeneous exchange interactions realized with Rydberg synthetic lattices exhibit more complex dynamics. Employing the evolution-path symmetry (EPS) framework developed recently, we analyze the two-particle dynamics via path interference in Fock space. We find that a homogeneous nearest-neighbor exchange interaction cannot break the AB cage, regardless of whether the long-range exchange interaction is present or not. In contrast, we demonstrate that inhomogeneous nearest-neighbor exchange interaction breaks the destructive-interference EPS, and lifts the degeneracy of many-body compact localized states, thereby generating non-local dispersive eigenstates. Consequently, the initial state gains a non-zero overlap with these dispersive states, enabling delocalized transport. Furthermore, while long-range exchange interaction alone preserves the AB cage, its coupling with nearest-neighbor inhomogeneous exchange interaction opens non-canceling pathways that alter the diffusion profile. Our work connects microscopic path interference with macroscopic spectral reorganization, offering an analytical understanding of the mechanism underlying exchange-interaction-induced transport in Rydberg synthetic lattices.

</details>


### [265] [Odd-frequency pair correlations in the trapped fermionic Tonks-Girardeau gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21917)
*M. Wong,K. Thompson,M. Governale,J. Brand,U. Zuelicke*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文证明费米子Tonks-Girardeau气体在受到外部束缚势时，除了传统的p波库珀对凝聚外，还展现出非传统的奇数频率配对（odd-frequency pairing），即具有s波对称性且仅存在于费米子间存在有限时间延迟的配对关联。该效应是一种介观效应（mesoscopic effect），定位于系统边界，在热力学上不具广延性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索可精确求解模型中是否存在传统凝聚之外的非传统配对序，特别是具有固定粒子数系统中是否可能实现奇数频率配对这种难以捉摸的隐藏序。

Method: 将杨氏离域长程序理论（Yang's theory of off-diagonal long-range order）推广到定义依赖于时间延迟t的序参量，该序参量在联合时间反演与费米子对索引交换下反对称。利用该广义形式对固定粒子数的费米子Tonks-Girardeau气体进行定量计算。

Result: 在束缚势约束下，费米子Tonks-Girardeau气体中存在奇数频率配对，表现为s波对称且依赖有限时间延迟t。该效应被证明是一种介观效应，定位于系统边界，因此不具有热力学广延性（nonextensive thermodynamically）。

Conclusion: 奇数频率配对这一隐藏序可在具有固定粒子数的系统中存在，为其实验实现和更深入的研究提供了新的途径。

Abstract: The fermionic Tonks-Girardeau gas is an exactly solvable model realizing a conventional macroscopic condensate of p-wave Cooper pairs. Here we demonstrate that, when confined by a trapping potential, it also hosts unconventional odd-frequency pairing, i.e., pair correlations with s-wave symmetry that are only present with a finite time delay t between fermions forming a pair. Quantitative results for fixed particle number are obtained using a generalization of Yang's theory of off-diagonal long-range order to define a t-dependent order parameter that is antisymmetric under combined t inversion and exchange of fermion-pair indices. Odd-frequency pairing in the fermionic Tonks-Girardeau gas is found to be a mesoscopic effect, localized at the system's boundary and therefore nonextensive thermodynamically. Our results show that this elusive hidden order is accessible in systems having fixed particle number, suggesting new avenues towards its experimental realization and further detailed study.

</details>


### [266] [Spinor condensate persistent currents in an atomtronic Josephson necklace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23560)
*Sayan Chatterjee,Nalinikanta Pradhan,Rina Kanamoto,M. Bhattacharya,Pankaj Kumar Mishra*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了多约瑟夫森结环形势阱中自旋化原子玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体（Bose-Einstein condensate）的持续流稳定性与动力学，揭示了第二组分通过相滑移工程（phase-slip engineering）、稳定性控制、电流反转开关（current-inversion switching）和超流泵浦（supercurrent pumping）等机制，为二元环状凝聚体中持续流的工程调控提供了系统路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多结约瑟夫森电路中的超流研究是当前物理研究的前沿，涉及宏观相位相干的基本表现，并支撑量子模拟、计量学和计算等应用。本文旨在探索自旋化二元玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体在多约瑟夫森结环形结构中的持续流稳定性与动力学调控机制。

Method: 研究在带有多约瑟夫森结的环形势阱中，对自旋化原子玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体的两分量持续流进行广泛考察，系统分析正负种间相互作用（interspecies interaction）、同向与反向旋转、布居不对称性、Manakov不对称性以及拉比耦合（Rabi coupling）等因素对持续流的影响。

Result: 分析表明，第二组分的存在为环形超流的操控提供了多种前所未有的机制，包括相滑移工程（phase-slip engineering）、稳定性控制（stability control）、电流反转开关（current-inversion switching）和超流泵浦（supercurrent pumping）。

Conclusion: 本研究为珠链势（necklace potential）中二元环形凝聚体的持续流工程提供了系统路线图，对原子电子学（atomtronics）、物质波干涉测量和基于原子玻色气体的传感具有重要启示意义。

Abstract: The investigation of superflow in multi-junction Josephson circuits is currently a leading frontier of physics research, probing the fundamental manifestations of macroscopic phase coherence and enabling applications to quantum simulation, metrology and computing. In this work, we extensively investigate the stability and dynamics of persistent currents of a spinor atomic Bose-Einstein condensate on a ring with multiple Josephson junctions. Specifically, we examine the effects of positive and negative interspecies interactions, co- and counter-rotation, population and Manakov asymmetry, as well as Rabi coupling on the persistent currents carried by the two components. Our analysis reveals that the presence of a second species offers multiple mechanisms for unprecedented manipulation of supercurrents on the ring, including phase-slip engineering, stability control, current-inversion switching and supercurrent pumping. Our study provides a roadmap for the engineering of persistent currents in binary ring condensates in necklace potentials, with significant implications for atomtronics, matter-wave interferometry and sensing using atomic Bose gases.

</details>


<div id='physics.soc-ph'></div>

# physics.soc-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [267] [Earth as a Closed Life-Support System: Finite Buffers, Dynamical Thresholds, and Planetary Sustainability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21505)
*Jean-Pierre Gazeau*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 本文从非平衡动力学（nonequilibrium dynamics）视角，将行星可持续性（planetary sustainability）建模为有限资源库存与废物处理能力两个缓冲池的耦合最小问题，提出储备时间（reserve time）原则，区分软硬废物阈值，并论证承载能力（carrying capacity）是资源、废物、回收、技术及制度动态耦合下涌现的、随时间变化的属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球在物质上近乎封闭而在能量上开放，现有可持续性框架缺乏以运行性语言刻画这一非平衡系统的统一数学表述。作者希望通过借鉴封闭式生命支持系统（closed life-support systems）的工程语言，将行星可持续性形式化为一个最小的非平衡动力学问题。

Method: 框架围绕两个有限缓冲池构建：可耗竭的资源库存与可饱和的废物处理能力。以禁食与肾功能衰竭的生理对比启发储备时间原则——最先达到临界状态的缓冲池构成主导约束。模型为非线性动力学方程，耦合再生资源库存、累积废物库存与总活动量，并刻画软废物阈值（soft waste threshold）与硬废物阈值（hard waste threshold）两类失效机制。文中还扩展至回收（recycling）情景及多资源多废物类别，并给出数值算例。

Result: 模型展示了两种阈值行为：软阈值下废物积累抑制增长但处理能力仍可维持，硬阈值下废物产生速率超过最大处理能力导致系统失效。模型被推广至回收与多类别场景，并与Spaceship Earth、行星边界（planetary boundaries）、增长的极限（Limits to Growth）及存量—污染增长模型建立联系。作者还提出细胞—有机体—行星的多尺度类比，强调生命支持功能的分布式特性。

Conclusion: 核心主张是条件性的：承载能力并非固定常数，而是资源、废物、回收、技术与制度动态耦合下涌现的、随时间变化的属性；资源耗竭与消除失效（elimination failure）何者成为约束，取决于各自的特征储备时间。

Abstract: Earth is nearly closed with respect to matter but open with respect to energy. We formulate planetary sustainability as a minimal nonequilibrium dynamical problem for coupled finite stocks and fluxes, using the operational language of closed ecological and engineered life-support systems: inventories, flux balances, recycling efficiencies, processing capacities, reserve times, and failure thresholds. The framework is organized around two finite buffers: resource stocks that can be depleted and waste-processing capacities that can be saturated. The physiological contrast between fasting and renal failure motivates a reserve-time principle: the dominant constraint is the buffer that becomes critical first. The resulting nonlinear model couples a regenerative resource stock, an accumulated waste stock, and an aggregate activity variable. It exhibits a soft waste threshold, at which accumulated waste suppresses growth while processing remains capable, and a hard threshold, at which waste production exceeds maximal processing capacity. We extend the model to recycling and multiple resource and waste classes, give an explicit toy numerical example, and relate the approach to Spaceship Earth, planetary boundaries, Limits to Growth, and stock-pollution growth models. A multiscale analogy---cell, organism, planet---emphasizes that life-support functions are distributed across degradation, recycling, transport, regulation, and removal. The central claim is conditional: carrying capacity is an emergent, time-dependent property of coupled resource, waste, recycling, technological, and institutional dynamics; either resource depletion or elimination failure may become the binding constraint, depending on their characteristic reserve times.

</details>


### [268] [Evolution of cooperation with Q-learning: how much information do we need?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22705)
*Yile Ku,Xin Ou,Jiqiang Zhang,Shengfeng Deng,Huiji Yue,Li Chen*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 本研究采用强化学习框架（reinforcement learning framework），在结构化种群中通过Q-learning算法系统变化交互邻域大小（作为感知信息的代理），发现合作水平与邻域大小呈倒U型（inverted U-shaped）非单调关系，表明存在一个最优信息量使合作达到最高水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合作为自然与人类社会所普遍存在，但其演化基础仍是一个重大挑战。长期以来悬而未决的问题是：拥有更多信息是否导致更好的决策，从而带来更高水平的合作。本研究旨在通过强化学习框架系统探究信息量与合作水平之间的关系。

Method: 采用近年发展的强化学习框架，个体通过试错学习最大化累积奖励。具体而言，在结构化种群中装备Q-learning算法，并在二维方格子（square lattice）和Barabási-Albert无标度网络（scale-free network）两种拓扑上系统变化交互邻域大小，以其作为感知信息量的代理变量。

Result: 在两种网络拓扑中均观察到合作水平与邻域大小之间存在非单调关系，呈现倒U型依赖。机制分析表明，适中的邻域大小使个体能够在信息充分性与决策可处理性之间达到最优平衡，既能识别互惠机会，又避免因信息过载导致决策质量下降。

Conclusion: 研究发现挑战了日常直觉：适当而非更多的信息量才是合作涌现的最优条件。存在一个最优信息量使合作达到最高水平，这一发现为理解信息与合作的演化关系提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: Cooperation is ubiquitous in both natural and human societies, yet its evolutionary basis remains a major challenge. A long-standing puzzle is whether having more information leads to better decision-making and thus a higher level of cooperation. To address this question, we adopt a recently developed reinforcement learning framework in which individuals learn through trial and error to maximize cumulative rewards - a paradigm that has successfully explained diverse emergent patterns in human behaviors. Specifically, we equip a structured population with the Q-learning algorithm and systematically vary the size of the interactive neighborhood, which serves as a proxy for perceived information. Interestingly, we observe a non-monotonic relationship between cooperation prevalence and neighborhood size in both two-dimensional square lattices and Barabasi-Albert scale-free networks. This inverted U-shaped dependence reveals that an optimal amount of information exists, yielding the highest level of cooperation. Mechanistic analyses show that a moderate neighborhood size enables individuals to strike an optimal balance between information sufficiency and decision-making tractability. This balance allows them to detect reciprocal opportunities while avoiding the deterioration of decision quality due to information overload. Our findings challenge everyday intuition, suggesting that a proper amount of information - not more - is optimal for the emergence of cooperation.

</details>
